This is the sole change from v100.2: the previous 5px refinement was visibly too small, so the total shift is increased by 25px. Card sizes, player cards, board, history and the existing Undo/Next placement system remain untouched.

  function lang() {
    const l = state?.ui?.lang;
    return (l === "en" || l === "de") ? l : "hu";

  /* ================== CONSTANTS ================== */
  const WIN_URL = "https://github.com/Szala86/autodarts-audio/releases/download/v1/win.mp3";
  const TRIPLE_VALUES = ["T20","T19","T18","T17","T16","T15","T7","BULL","SBULL","DBULL","25","50"];

  const HOTKEY_PANEL = { shift: true, ctrl: false, alt: false, key: "f" };
  const HOTKEY_HELP  = { shift: true, ctrl: false, alt: false, key: "h" };
  const HOTKEY_SAFE  = { shift: true, ctrl: false, alt: false, key: "m" };
  const HOTKEY_PRESET_1 = { shift: true, ctrl: false, alt: false, key: "1" };
  const HOTKEY_PRESET_2 = { shift: true, ctrl: false, alt: false, key: "2" };

  const SAFE_LIMITS = { THROW_VAL_FONT_PX: 130, ORIG_FONT_PX: 38, TOTAL_FONT_PX: 130, CHECKOUT_FONT_PX: 130, ACTIVE_OUTLINE_PX: 6 };
  const EXT_LIMITS  = { THROW_VAL_FONT_PX: 220, ORIG_FONT_PX: 80, TOTAL_FONT_PX: 220, CHECKOUT_FONT_PX: 220, ACTIVE_OUTLINE_PX: 12 };

  // Layout OFF: méret/zone cache, hogy a név/átlag sor ki-be kapcsolása
  // ne tudjon új, kisebb score/history profilt számoltatni ugyanahhoz a kártyamérethez.
  const AD_CORE_OFF_ZONE_CACHE = new Map();
  const AD_CORE_OFF_SIZE_CACHE = new Map();

  // Layout ON v8: a panel csúszkák maximuma is kövesse a tényleges
  // kártyaszélességi fit-limitet. A score slidernél a 100% mindig
  // használható alap marad; csak a 100% fölötti tartományt vágjuk a
  // tényleges szélességi limitnél.
  const AD_CORE_LAYOUT_ON_WIDTHFIT_LIMITS = {
    scoreMaxPct: 320,
    nameMaxScale: 2.60,
    avgMaxScale: 2.60,
    collecting: false,
    nextScoreMaxPct: Infinity,
    nextNameMaxScale: Infinity,
    nextAvgMaxScale: Infinity,
  };

  // v9: a Layout ON panel csúszkákat húzás közben nem rendereljük újra,
  // mert a pontszám szélességi limitje mozgás közben változhat, és ez
  // megszakíthatja a böngésző natív range-drag állapotát.
  let AD_CORE_LAYOUT_SLIDER_ACTIVE = false;

  function bindLayoutPanelRangeDrag(slider) {
    if (!slider) return;
    slider.dataset.adCoreLayoutRange = "1";
    slider.style.setProperty("touch-action", "none");
    slider.style.setProperty("pointer-events", "auto", "important");
    slider.addEventListener("pointerdown", (e) => {
      AD_CORE_LAYOUT_SLIDER_ACTIVE = true;
      e.stopPropagation();
    });
    slider.addEventListener("mousedown", (e) => {
      AD_CORE_LAYOUT_SLIDER_ACTIVE = true;
      e.stopPropagation();
    });
    slider.addEventListener("touchstart", (e) => {
      AD_CORE_LAYOUT_SLIDER_ACTIVE = true;
      e.stopPropagation();
    }, { passive: true });
    slider.addEventListener("focus", () => {
      AD_CORE_LAYOUT_SLIDER_ACTIVE = true;
    });
    const finish = () => {
      if (!AD_CORE_LAYOUT_SLIDER_ACTIVE) return;
      AD_CORE_LAYOUT_SLIDER_ACTIVE = false;
      renderPanelIfOpen();
    };
    slider.addEventListener("change", finish);
    slider.addEventListener("blur", finish);
    window.addEventListener("pointerup", finish, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener("mouseup", finish, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener("touchend", finish, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener("touchcancel", finish, { passive: true });
  }

  function floorToStep(v, step, min, max) {
    const n = Number(v);
    if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return max;
    const floored = Math.floor((n + 1e-9) / step) * step;
    return clamp(Number(floored.toFixed(4)), min, max);
  }

  function resetLayoutOnWidthFitLimitCollection() {
    AD_CORE_LAYOUT_ON_WIDTHFIT_LIMITS.collecting = true;
    AD_CORE_LAYOUT_ON_WIDTHFIT_LIMITS.nextScoreMaxPct = Infinity;
    AD_CORE_LAYOUT_ON_WIDTHFIT_LIMITS.nextNameMaxScale = Infinity;
    AD_CORE_LAYOUT_ON_WIDTHFIT_LIMITS.nextAvgMaxScale = Infinity;
  }

  function collectLayoutOnWidthFitLimit(kind, value) {
    const lim = AD_CORE_LAYOUT_ON_WIDTHFIT_LIMITS;
    if (!lim.collecting) return;
    const v = Number(value);
    if (!Number.isFinite(v) || v <= 0) return;
    if (kind === "score") lim.nextScoreMaxPct = Math.min(lim.nextScoreMaxPct, v);
    else if (kind === "name") lim.nextNameMaxScale = Math.min(lim.nextNameMaxScale, v);
    else if (kind === "avg") lim.nextAvgMaxScale = Math.min(lim.nextAvgMaxScale, v);
  }

  function publishLayoutOnWidthFitLimits() {
    const lim = AD_CORE_LAYOUT_ON_WIDTHFIT_LIMITS;
    if (!lim.collecting) return;
    lim.collecting = false;

    const scoreMax = Number.isFinite(lim.nextScoreMaxPct)
      ? floorToStep(Math.max(100, lim.nextScoreMaxPct), 1, 100, 320)
      : 320;
    const nameMax = Number.isFinite(lim.nextNameMaxScale)
      ? floorToStep(lim.nextNameMaxScale, 0.02, 0.60, 2.60)
      : 2.60;
    const avgMax = Number.isFinite(lim.nextAvgMaxScale)
      ? floorToStep(lim.nextAvgMaxScale, 0.02, 0.60, 2.60)
      : 2.60;

    const changedLimit =
      scoreMax !== lim.scoreMaxPct ||
      nameMax !== lim.nameMaxScale ||
      avgMax !== lim.avgMaxScale;

    lim.scoreMaxPct = scoreMax;
    lim.nameMaxScale = nameMax;
    lim.avgMaxScale = avgMax;

    const c = cfg();
    let changedCfg = false;
    if (c.LAYOUT_ENABLED) {
      const score = Number(c.LAYOUT_SCORE_FONT_PX ?? 100);
      const name = Number(c.LAYOUT_NAME_ROW_SCALE ?? 1);
      const avg = Number(c.LAYOUT_AVG_ROW_SCALE ?? 1);

      if (Number.isFinite(score) && score > scoreMax) { c.LAYOUT_SCORE_FONT_PX = scoreMax; changedCfg = true; }
      if (Number.isFinite(name) && name > nameMax) { c.LAYOUT_NAME_ROW_SCALE = nameMax; changedCfg = true; }
      if (Number.isFinite(avg) && avg > avgMax) { c.LAYOUT_AVG_ROW_SCALE = avgMax; changedCfg = true; }
    }

    if (changedCfg) {
      saveStateDebounced();
      dirtyPlayers();
      scheduleUpdate();
    }
    if (changedLimit || changedCfg) {
      if (!AD_CORE_LAYOUT_SLIDER_ACTIVE) renderPanelIfOpen();
    }
  }
  const STYLE_ID = "ad-style-core-v245";
  const UI_STYLE_ID = "ad-core-ui-style-v245";
  const EXTRA_STYLE_ID = "ad-style-core-skin-v245";

  const ACTIVE_CLASS = "ad-active-player";
  const TRIPLE_CLASS = "ad-triple-hit";

  const SDT_RE = /^([SDT])(\d{1,2})$/i;
  const CHECKOUT_TOKEN_RE = /^(?:[SDT](?:[1-9]|1\d|20)|BULL|SBULL|DBULL|25|50)$/i;

  // ✅ Sticky selection (kijelölt dobáskártya indexe a turn-ön)
  const TURN_SEL_ATTR = "data-ad-sel-throw-idx";

  /* ================== UTILS ================== */
  function clamp(n, min, max) { return Math.min(Math.max(n, min), max); }
  function sanitizeHex(v, fallback) {
    if (typeof v !== "string") return fallback;
    return /^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(v) ? v : fallback;
  }
  function hexToRgbString(hex) {
    hex = sanitizeHex(hex, "#ffffff");
    const r = parseInt(hex.slice(1,3),16);
    const g = parseInt(hex.slice(3,5),16);
    const b = parseInt(hex.slice(5,7),16);
    return `${r}, ${g}, ${b}`;
  }
  function hexToRgba(hex, alpha) {
    hex = sanitizeHex(hex, "#000000");
    alpha = clamp(Number(alpha ?? 0.85), 0, 1);
    const r = parseInt(hex.slice(1,3),16);
    const g = parseInt(hex.slice(3,5),16);
    const b = parseInt(hex.slice(5,7),16);
    return `rgba(${r},${g},${b},${alpha})`;
  }
  function ensureHead(cb) {
    if (document.head) return cb();
    const obs = new MutationObserver(() => { if (document.head) { obs.disconnect(); cb(); } });
    obs.observe(document.documentElement, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  }
  function ensureBody(cb) {
    if (document.body) return cb();
    const obs = new MutationObserver(() => { if (document.body) { obs.disconnect(); cb(); } });
    obs.observe(document.documentElement, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  }

  function matchHotkey(e, def) {
    if (!def) return false;
    if (!!def.shift !== e.shiftKey) return false;
    if (!!def.ctrl  !== e.ctrlKey)  return false;
    if (!!def.alt   !== e.altKey)   return false;
    return (e.key || "").toLowerCase() === def.key.toLowerCase();
  }
  function ensureVisible(x, y, w, h, pad = 8) {
    return { x: clamp(x, pad, window.innerWidth - w - pad), y: clamp(y, pad, window.innerHeight - h - pad) };
  }
  function tryParseJSON(raw) { try { return JSON.parse(raw); } catch { return null; } }

    function sanitizeUrl(u, fallback) {
    try{
      const s = String(u || "").trim();
      if (!s) return fallback;
      const U = new URL(s, location.href);
      if (U.protocol !== "http:" && U.protocol !== "https:") return fallback;
      return U.href;
    }catch{
      return fallback;
    }
  }
  function cssUrl(u) {
    // minimál védelem: ne tudjon idézőjelet / sortörést “kiszúrni” a CSS-be
    return String(u || "").replace(/["\\\n\r]/g, "");
  }
  function sanitizeFontDataUrl(u) {
    const s = String(u || "").trim().replace(/[\n\r]/g, "");
    if (s.length > 5_500_000) return "";
    if (!/^data:/i.test(s)) return "";
    const head = s.slice(0, 96).toLowerCase();
    if (!/font|woff|ttf|otf|octet-stream|sfnt/.test(head)) return "";
    return s;
  }
  function sanitizeFontFamilyName(f) {
    return String(f || "").trim().replace(/['"<>;{}:\\]/g, "").slice(0, 120);
  }

function getPlayerCardResolvedFontStack() {
  const fallback = "'Barlow Condensed', system-ui, sans-serif";
  const c = cfg();
  const url = sanitizeFontDataUrl(c.PLAYER_CARD_FONT_DATA_URL);

  if (c.PLAYER_CARD_CUSTOM_FONT && url) {
    const fam = sanitizeFontFamilyName(c.PLAYER_CARD_FONT_FAMILY) || "AD Core Player Card";
    return `'${fam.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}', ${fallback}`;
  }

  return fallback;
}

function getTurnCardResolvedFontStack() {
  const fallback = "'Barlow Condensed', system-ui, sans-serif";
  const c = cfg();
  const url = sanitizeFontDataUrl(c.TURN_CARD_FONT_DATA_URL);

  if (c.TURN_CARD_CUSTOM_FONT && url) {
    const fam = sanitizeFontFamilyName(c.TURN_CARD_FONT_FAMILY) || "AD Core Turn Card";
    return `'${fam.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}', ${fallback}`;
  }

  return fallback;
}

    /* ================== SCOPE (cleanup for listeners/timers) ================== */
  function makeScope(){
    const off = [];
    const timers = new Set();
    return {
      on(target, type, handler, options){
        target.addEventListener(type, handler, options);
        off.push(() => {
          try { target.removeEventListener(type, handler, options); } catch {}
        });
      },
      setTimeout(fn, ms){
        const id = window.setTimeout(() => { timers.delete(id); fn(); }, ms);
        timers.add(id);
        return id;
      },
      setInterval(fn, ms){
        const id = window.setInterval(fn, ms);
        timers.add(id);
        return id;
      },
      abort(){
        // remove listeners
        while (off.length) { try { off.pop()(); } catch {} }
        // clear timers
        for (const t of timers) { clearTimeout(t); clearInterval(t); }
        timers.clear();
      }
    };
  }

  let scopeMain = null; // resize/fullscreen stb.
  let scopeWin  = null; // win-music stop hookok

  /* ================== STATE LOAD/MIGRATE ================== */
  function normalizeState(st) {
    const out = clone(DEFAULT_STATE);
    out.activePreset = clamp(Number(st?.activePreset ?? out.activePreset), 0, 2);

    out.ui = { ...out.ui, ...(st?.ui || {}) };
    out.ui.clock = { ...clone(DEFAULT_CLOCK), ...(st?.ui?.clock || {}) };
    out.ui.lang = (out.ui.lang === "en" || out.ui.lang === "de") ? out.ui.lang : "hu";

out.presets = (Array.isArray(st?.presets) && st.presets.length === 3)
  ? st.presets.map(p => {
      const merged = { ...clone(DEFAULT_CFG), ...(p || {}) };

      if (p && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(p, "SKIN_CSS")) {
        if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(p, "SKIN_ENABLED")) {
          merged.SKIN_ENABLED = !!p.SKIN_CSS;
        }
        if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(p, "LAYOUT_ENABLED")) {
          merged.LAYOUT_ENABLED = !!p.SKIN_CSS;
        }
      }

      return merged;
    })
  : [clone(DEFAULT_CFG), clone(DEFAULT_CFG), clone(DEFAULT_CFG)];

    out.schemaVersion = STATE_SCHEMA_VERSION;
    return out;
  }

  function migrateToState(obj) {
    if (obj && obj.state) return migrateToState(obj.state);
    if (obj && typeof obj === "object" && Array.isArray(obj.presets) && obj.presets.length === 3 && obj.ui) return normalizeState(obj);
    if (obj && typeof obj === "object" && !obj.presets) {
      const base = { ...clone(DEFAULT_CFG), ...obj };
      const st = clone(DEFAULT_STATE);
      st.presets = [clone(base), clone(base), clone(base)];
      return normalizeState(st);
    }
    return clone(DEFAULT_STATE);
  }

  function loadState() {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORE_KEY_STATE);
    if (raw) return migrateToState(tryParseJSON(raw));
    for (const k of LEGACY_KEYS) {
      const r = localStorage.getItem(k);
      if (!r) continue;
      const st = migrateToState(tryParseJSON(r));
      try { localStorage.setItem(STORE_KEY_STATE, JSON.stringify(st)); } catch {}
      return st;
    }
    return clone(DEFAULT_STATE);
  }

  let state = loadState();
  state.schemaVersion = STATE_SCHEMA_VERSION;
  const cfg = () => state.presets[state.activePreset];

  const LAYOUT_ON_100_REBASE_FLAG = "ad_core_v43_layout_on_100_rebase_v5";
  function rebaseLayoutOnControlsTo100Once() {
    try {
      if (localStorage.getItem(LAYOUT_ON_100_REBASE_FLAG) === "1") return;

      let changed = false;
      for (const p of state.presets || []) {
        if (!p) continue;

        // A v4 tesztben kb. 190px / 180% / 116% volt a szemre jó Layout ON méret.
        // v5-ben ez legyen a 100%-os alap. A régi, kézzel feltolt tesztértékeket
        // egyszer visszaállítjuk 100-ra, hogy a panelen is az új alap jelenjen meg.
        const sc = Number(p.LAYOUT_SCORE_FONT_PX);
        if (Number.isFinite(sc) && sc > 130) {
          p.LAYOUT_SCORE_FONT_PX = 100;
          changed = true;
        }

        const ns = Number(p.LAYOUT_NAME_ROW_SCALE);
        if (Number.isFinite(ns) && ns > 1.30) {
          p.LAYOUT_NAME_ROW_SCALE = 1;
          changed = true;
        }

        const av = Number(p.LAYOUT_AVG_ROW_SCALE);
        if (Number.isFinite(av) && av > 1.08) {
          p.LAYOUT_AVG_ROW_SCALE = 1;
          changed = true;
        }
      }

      localStorage.setItem(LAYOUT_ON_100_REBASE_FLAG, "1");
      if (changed) localStorage.setItem(STORE_KEY_STATE, JSON.stringify(state));
    } catch (_) {}
  }
  rebaseLayoutOnControlsTo100Once();

  function saveStateNow(){
    try {
      state.schemaVersion = STATE_SCHEMA_VERSION;
      localStorage.setItem(STORE_KEY_STATE, JSON.stringify(state));
    } catch {}
  }
  let saveTimer = null;
  function saveStateDebounced() {
    if (saveTimer) clearTimeout(saveTimer);
    saveTimer = setTimeout(() => { saveStateNow(); saveTimer = null; }, 250);
  }

  // import legacy clock settings once
  (function importLegacyClockOnce(){
    try{
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(LEGACY_CLOCK_KEY);
      if(!raw) return;
      const legacy = tryParseJSON(raw);
      if(!legacy || typeof legacy !== "object") return;

      const c = state.ui.clock || (state.ui.clock = clone(DEFAULT_CLOCK));
      const looksDefault = (c.x == null && c.y == null && c.scale === 1 && c.bgHex === DEFAULT_CLOCK.bgHex && c.textHex === DEFAULT_CLOCK.textHex);
      if(!looksDefault) return;

      c.enabled     = !!legacy.enabled;
      c.x           = (typeof legacy.x === "number") ? legacy.x : null;
      c.y           = (typeof legacy.y === "number") ? legacy.y : null;
      c.scale       = clamp(Number(legacy.scale ?? 1), 0.6, 2.0);
      c.format24    = (legacy.format24 !== undefined) ? !!legacy.format24 : true;
      c.showSeconds = (legacy.showSeconds !== undefined) ? !!legacy.showSeconds : true;
      c.bgHex       = sanitizeHex(legacy.bgHex, c.bgHex);
      c.bgAlpha     = clamp(Number(legacy.bgAlpha ?? c.bgAlpha), 0, 1);
      c.textHex     = sanitizeHex(legacy.textHex, c.textHex);

      saveStateNow();
    }catch{}
  })();

  /* ================== SAFE MODE HELPERS ================== */
  function getMaxFor(key) {
    const safeMax = SAFE_LIMITS[key];
    const extMax = EXT_LIMITS[key];
    if (safeMax == null || extMax == null) return null;
    return state.ui.safeMode ? safeMax : extMax;
  }
  function clampIfSafe(key, value) {
    const safeMax = SAFE_LIMITS[key];
    if (safeMax == null) return value;
    if (!state.ui.safeMode) return value;
    return Math.min(value, safeMax);
  }
  function pillLevel(key, value) {
    const safeMax = SAFE_LIMITS[key];
    if (safeMax == null) return "ok";
    if (value <= safeMax) return "ok";
    if (value <= safeMax * 1.15) return "warn";
    return "danger";
  }
  function applySafeClampsToCfg() {
    if (!state.ui.safeMode) return;
    const c = cfg();
    c.THROW_VAL_FONT_PX = clampIfSafe("THROW_VAL_FONT_PX", c.THROW_VAL_FONT_PX);
    c.ORIG_FONT_PX = clampIfSafe("ORIG_FONT_PX", c.ORIG_FONT_PX);
    c.TOTAL_FONT_PX = clampIfSafe("TOTAL_FONT_PX", c.TOTAL_FONT_PX);
    c.CHECKOUT_FONT_PX = clampIfSafe("CHECKOUT_FONT_PX", c.CHECKOUT_FONT_PX);
    c.ACTIVE_OUTLINE_PX = clampIfSafe("ACTIVE_OUTLINE_PX", c.ACTIVE_OUTLINE_PX);
  }

  /* ================== UI INDICATOR (sliders) ================== */
  function slidersTinySvg(size = 14) {
    return `
      <svg width="${size}" height="${size}" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none"
           xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true">
        <path d="M4 7h9" stroke="white" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round"/>
        <path d="M17 7h3" stroke="white" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round"/>
        <circle cx="15" cy="7" r="2" stroke="white" stroke-width="2"/>
        <path d="M4 17h3" stroke="white" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round"/>
        <path d="M11 17h9" stroke="white" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round"/>
        <circle cx="9" cy="17" r="2" stroke="white" stroke-width="2"/>
      </svg>
    `;
  }

  function ensureUIStyle() {
    ensureHead(() => {
      if (document.getElementById(UI_STYLE_ID)) return;
      const st = document.createElement("style");
      st.id = UI_STYLE_ID;
      st.textContent = `
        #ad-core-panel .ad-mod-row{
          transition: transform .16s ease, background .16s ease, box-shadow .16s ease, border-color .16s ease;
        }
        #ad-core-panel .ad-mod-row:hover{ border-color: rgba(255,255,255,0.18) !important; }
        #ad-core-panel .ad-mod-row.is-config:hover{
          transform: translateX(2px);
          background: rgba(255,255,255,0.10) !important;
          box-shadow: 0 10px 26px rgba(0,0,0,0.35);
        }
        #ad-core-panel .ad-mod-icon{
          opacity:.65;
          transform: translateY(1px);
          transition: opacity .16s ease, transform .16s ease, filter .16s ease;
          display:inline-flex;
          align-items:center;
        }
        #ad-core-panel .ad-mod-row.is-config:hover .ad-mod-icon{
          opacity:1;
          transform: translateY(1px) rotate(-6deg) scale(1.05);
          filter: drop-shadow(0 0 10px rgba(255,255,255,0.15));
        }
        #ad-core-panel .ad-mod-hint{
          opacity:0; transform: translateX(-4px);
          transition: opacity .16s ease, transform .16s ease;
          font-size:11px; font-weight:800; padding:4px 8px;
          border-radius:999px; border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.14);
          background: rgba(0,0,0,.35); color:#fff; white-space:nowrap;
        }
        #ad-core-panel .ad-mod-row.is-config:hover .ad-mod-hint{ opacity:.85; transform: translateX(0); }
      `;
      document.head.appendChild(st);
    });
  }

  /* ================== CORE CSS ================== */
  function ensureFontLink() {
    const c = cfg();
    const needsFont = c.THROWS_TO_POINTS || c.TOTAL_VIEW || c.CHECKOUT_VIEW;
    if (!needsFont) return;
    if (!document.getElementById(FONT_LINK_ID)) {
      const link = document.createElement("link");
      link.id = FONT_LINK_ID;
      link.rel = "stylesheet";
      link.href = "https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Barlow+Condensed:wght@700;800;900&display=swap";
      document.head.appendChild(link);
    }
  }

  function renderCss() {
    ensureHead(() => {
      ensureFontLink();
      ensureTurnCardFontFace();
      const c = cfg();

      let style = document.getElementById(STYLE_ID);
      if (!style) {
        style = document.createElement("style");
        style.id = STYLE_ID;
        document.head.appendChild(style);
      }

      const activeRGB = hexToRgbString(c.ACTIVE_COLOR_HEX);
      const throwRGB  = hexToRgbString(c.THROW_VAL_COLOR_HEX);
      const origRGB   = hexToRgbString(c.ORIG_COLOR_HEX);
      const totalRGB  = hexToRgbString(c.TOTAL_COLOR_HEX);
      const chkRGB    = hexToRgbString(c.CHECKOUT_COLOR_HEX);
      const throwBgRGB      = hexToRgbString(c.THROW_BG_HEX);
      const throwHoverBgRGB = hexToRgbString(c.THROW_HOVER_BG_HEX);
      const totalBgRGB      = hexToRgbString(c.TOTAL_BG_HEX);
      const turnCardFontStack = getTurnCardResolvedFontStack();
      const css = [];

      css.push(`
:root{
  --ad-active-rgb: ${activeRGB};
  --ad-active-outline: ${clamp(+c.ACTIVE_OUTLINE_PX || 3, 0, 12)}px;
  --ad-active-glow: ${clamp(+c.ACTIVE_GLOW || 0.42, 0, 1)};

  --ad-throw-font: ${clamp(+c.THROW_VAL_FONT_PX || 100, 20, 220)}px;
  --ad-throw-font-scale-factor: ${(clamp(+c.THROW_VAL_FONT_PX || 100, 20, 220) / 100).toFixed(4)};
  --ad-throw-miss-scale: ${c.TURN_CARD_CUSTOM_FONT ? "0.62" : "0.72"};
  --ad-throw-rgb: ${throwRGB};
  --ad-throw-op: ${clamp(+c.THROW_VAL_OPACITY ?? 1, 0, 1)};

  --ad-throw-bg-rgb: ${throwBgRGB};
  --ad-throw-bg-op: ${clamp(+c.THROW_BG_OPACITY ?? 1, 0, 1)};
  --ad-throw-hover-bg-rgb: ${throwHoverBgRGB};
  --ad-throw-hover-bg-op: ${clamp(+c.THROW_HOVER_BG_OPACITY ?? 1, 0, 1)};

  --ad-total-bg-rgb: ${totalBgRGB};
  --ad-total-bg-op: ${clamp(+c.TOTAL_BG_OPACITY ?? 0, 0, 1)};

  --ad-orig-font: ${clamp(+c.ORIG_FONT_PX || 30, 10, 80)}px;
  --ad-orig-font-scale-factor: ${(clamp(+c.ORIG_FONT_PX || 30, 10, 80) / 30).toFixed(4)};
  --ad-orig-rgb: ${origRGB};
  --ad-orig-op: ${clamp(+c.ORIG_OPACITY ?? 0.45, 0, 1)};

  --ad-total-font: ${clamp(+c.TOTAL_FONT_PX || 100, 20, 220)}px;

    // ================== SKIN – selector health-check ==================
  const SKIN_HEALTH_SSKEY = () => `ad_core_skin_sel_warned_${SCRIPT_VERSION}`;
  let skinHealthTimer = 0;
  let skinHealthAttempts = 0;

  function runSkinHealthCheck() {
    const c = cfg();
    if (!c || !c.LAYOUT_ENABLED) return;
    if (!location.pathname.startsWith("/matches/")) return;

    const turn = document.querySelector("#ad-ext-turn");
    const players = document.querySelector("#ad-ext-player-display");

    // ha még nem töltött be a meccs UI, próbáljuk újra párszor
    if (!turn || !players) {
      if (skinHealthAttempts++ < 15) scheduleSkinHealthCheck();
      return;
    }

    // Ha egyik ismert Chakra selector sincs, gyanús: Autodarts update / selector drift
    const anyKnown = document.querySelector(
      ".css-tkevr6, .css-19lo6pj, .css-1omnor5, .css-ul22ge, .css-1dkgpmk, .css-1wlduvp, .css-sm8wdq, .css-881tme, .css-rrf7rv, .css-1cdcn26, .css-jbngkd, .css-1emway5"
    );
    if (anyKnown) return;

    // toast csak egyszer / session / verzió
    try {
      if (sessionStorage.getItem(SKIN_HEALTH_SSKEY()) === "1") return;
      sessionStorage.setItem(SKIN_HEALTH_SSKEY(), "1");
    } catch {}

    console.warn("[AD-CORE] Skin health-check: likely selector mismatch after update.");
    const L = T();
    const msg = (L && L.toasts && L.toasts.layoutWarn) ? L.toasts.layoutWarn : "Layout: selector mismatch";
    if (typeof showToast === "function") showToast(msg);

    // optional auto-disable
    if (c.SKIN_AUTO_DISABLE_ON_MISMATCH) {
      c.LAYOUT_ENABLED = false;
      dirtySkin();
      saveStateDebounced();
      scheduleUpdate();

      const L2 = T();
      if (typeof showToast === "function") {
        showToast(L2?.toasts?.layoutAutoOff || "Layout AUTO-OFF");
      }
    }
  }

  function scheduleSkinHealthCheck() {
    if (skinHealthTimer) return;
    skinHealthTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      skinHealthTimer = 0;
      runSkinHealthCheck();
    }, 1500);
  }

  function ensureSkinCss() {
    ensureHead(() => {
      const c = cfg();
      const skinOn = !!c.SKIN_ENABLED;
      const layoutOn = !!c.LAYOUT_ENABLED;

      let st = document.getElementById(EXTRA_STYLE_ID);
      if (!skinOn && !layoutOn) {
        if (st) st.remove();
        return;
      }

      if (!st) {
        st = document.createElement("style");
        st.id = EXTRA_STYLE_ID;
      }

const scale = clamp(Number(c.SKIN_UI_SCALE ?? 1), 0.85, 1.15);
const spacing = clamp(Number(c.SKIN_SPACING_PLAYER ?? 20), 0, 80);
const alpha = clamp(Number(c.SKIN_BG_OVERLAY_ALPHA ?? 0.55), 0, 1);
const url = sanitizeUrl(c.SKIN_BG_URL, DEFAULT_CFG.SKIN_BG_URL);
const overlayRgb = hexToRgbString(sanitizeHex(c.SKIN_OVERLAY_HEX, "#081a28"));
const pbgRGB = hexToRgbString(sanitizeHex(c.SKIN_PLAYER_BG_HEX, DEFAULT_CFG.SKIN_PLAYER_BG_HEX));
const pbgOp  = clamp(Number(c.SKIN_PLAYER_BG_OPACITY ?? DEFAULT_CFG.SKIN_PLAYER_BG_OPACITY), 0, 1);

const dynLayout = layoutOn ? String.raw`
:root{
  --ad-ui-scale: ${scale};
  --spacing-player: ${spacing}px;
}

  (layoutOn ? (LAYOUT_CSS + "\n" + dynLayout) : "");

      const core = document.getElementById(STYLE_ID);
      if (core && core.parentNode) {
        if (!st.parentNode) core.parentNode.appendChild(st);
        if (core.nextSibling !== st) core.after(st);
      } else if (!st.parentNode) {
        document.head.appendChild(st);
        }
        skinHealthAttempts = 0;
        scheduleSkinHealthCheck();
    });

  function ensurePlayerCardFontFace() {
    const c = cfg();
    ensureHead(() => {
      let st = document.getElementById(PLAYER_CARD_FONT_FACE_ID);
      if (!st) {
        st = document.createElement("style");
        st.id = PLAYER_CARD_FONT_FACE_ID;
        document.head.appendChild(st);
      }
      const url = sanitizeFontDataUrl(c.PLAYER_CARD_FONT_DATA_URL);
      const use = !!(c.PLAYER_CARD_CUSTOM_FONT && url);
      if (!use) {
        st.textContent = "";
        return;
      }
      const fam = sanitizeFontFamilyName(c.PLAYER_CARD_FONT_FAMILY) || "AD Core Player Card";
      const safeUrl = cssUrl(url);
      st.textContent = `@font-face{font-family:'${fam.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}';src:url("${safeUrl}") format("woff2"),url("${safeUrl}") format("woff"),url("${safeUrl}") format("truetype");font-display:swap;}`;
    });
  }

  function ensureTurnCardFontFace() {
    const c = cfg();
    ensureHead(() => {
      let st = document.getElementById(TURN_CARD_FONT_FACE_ID);
      if (!st) {
        st = document.createElement("style");
        st.id = TURN_CARD_FONT_FACE_ID;
        document.head.appendChild(st);
      }
      const url = sanitizeFontDataUrl(c.TURN_CARD_FONT_DATA_URL);
      const use = !!(c.TURN_CARD_CUSTOM_FONT && url);
      if (!use) {
        st.textContent = "";
        return;
      }
      const fam = sanitizeFontFamilyName(c.TURN_CARD_FONT_FAMILY) || "AD Core Turn Card";
      const safeUrl = cssUrl(url);
      st.textContent = `@font-face{font-family:'${fam.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}';src:url("${safeUrl}") format("woff2"),url("${safeUrl}") format("woff"),url("${safeUrl}") format("truetype");font-display:swap;}`;
    });
  }

function buildLayoutPlayerExtrasCss() {
  const c = cfg();
  const fontStack = getPlayerCardResolvedFontStack();
  const offNameScale = clamp(Number(c.LAYOUT_NAME_ROW_SCALE ?? 1), 0.60, 1.80);
  const offAvgScale  = clamp(Number(c.LAYOUT_AVG_ROW_SCALE ?? 1), 0.60, 1.80);
  const offNameGapPx = clamp(Math.round(Math.max(0, offNameScale - 1) * 8), 0, 12);
  const offAvgGapPx  = clamp(Math.round(Math.max(0, offAvgScale - 1) * 7), 0, 10);

  // v16 OFF native DOM fix:
  // Layout OFF-ban a dump alapján az aktív és inaktív kártya eltérő natív DOM-ot használ.
  // Ezért itt nem overlayre támaszkodunk, hanem a közös natív elemeket célozzuk:
  // .ad-ext-player-score, .css-y3hfdd > .css-xsngok, .css-y3hfdd > .css-37hv00,
  // .css-y3hfdd > .css-1igwmid. Így minden player card ugyanarra a panelállításra reagál.
  const offScorePct = clamp(Number(c.LAYOUT_SCORE_FONT_PX ?? 100), 40, 260) / 100;
  const offScorePx = clamp(Math.round(92 * offScorePct), 34, 210);
  const offNamePx = clamp(Math.round(16 * offNameScale), 10, 42);
  const offAvgPx = clamp(Math.round(14 * offAvgScale), 9, 36);
  const nameAbove = c.LAYOUT_NAME_PLACEMENT === "above";
  const avgAbove = c.LAYOUT_AVG_PLACEMENT === "above";
  const orderName = nameAbove ? 10 : 30;
  const orderAvg = avgAbove ? 11 : 31;
  const orderScore = 20;

  if (!c.LAYOUT_ENABLED) {
    return `
#ad-ext-player-display{
  --ad-core-player-card-font: ${fontStack};
  font-family: var(--ad-core-player-card-font) !important;
}

#ad-ext-player-display canvas{
  font-family: inherit !important;
}

#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-player-name *,
#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-player-avg,
#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-player-avg *,
#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-player-name-row,
#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-player-name-row *,
#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-player-avg-row,
#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-player-avg-row *,
#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-visible-meta-row,
#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-visible-meta-row *,
#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-visible-avg-row,
#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-visible-avg-row *{
  font-family: var(--ad-core-player-card-font) !important;
}

}

}

function applyLayoutPlayerHtmlClasses() {
  const c = cfg();
  const root = document.documentElement;

  root.classList.toggle("ad-core-layout-off", !c.LAYOUT_ENABLED);
  root.classList.toggle("ad-core-layout-on", !!c.LAYOUT_ENABLED);

  if (!c.LAYOUT_ENABLED) {
    root.classList.toggle("ad-core-hide-layout-name", !c.LAYOUT_SHOW_PLAYER_NAME);
    root.classList.toggle("ad-core-hide-layout-avg", !c.LAYOUT_SHOW_PLAYER_AVG);
    root.classList.toggle("ad-core-layout-name-above", c.LAYOUT_NAME_PLACEMENT === "above");
    root.classList.toggle("ad-core-layout-name-below", c.LAYOUT_NAME_PLACEMENT !== "above");
    root.classList.toggle("ad-core-layout-avg-above", c.LAYOUT_AVG_PLACEMENT === "above");
    root.classList.toggle("ad-core-layout-avg-below", c.LAYOUT_AVG_PLACEMENT !== "above");
    return;
  }

  root.classList.toggle("ad-core-hide-layout-name", !c.LAYOUT_SHOW_PLAYER_NAME);
  root.classList.toggle("ad-core-hide-layout-avg", !c.LAYOUT_SHOW_PLAYER_AVG);

  root.classList.toggle("ad-core-layout-name-above", c.LAYOUT_NAME_PLACEMENT === "above");
  root.classList.toggle("ad-core-layout-name-below", c.LAYOUT_NAME_PLACEMENT !== "above");

  root.classList.toggle("ad-core-layout-avg-above", c.LAYOUT_AVG_PLACEMENT === "above");
  root.classList.toggle("ad-core-layout-avg-below", c.LAYOUT_AVG_PLACEMENT !== "above");
}

  function playerCardLooksLikeAvgLine(tRaw) {
    const s = String(tRaw || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    if (!s || s.length > 96) return false;
    if (/^\d{2,3}\.\d{1,2}$/.test(s)) return true;
    if ((/ø|Ø/u).test(s) && /\d/.test(s)) return true;
    if (/#[0-9]+\s*[|｜]/.test(s)) return true;
    if (/\d+\.\d+\s*\/\s*\d+\.\d/.test(s)) return true;
    return false;
  }

  function playerCardTreeDepth(el) {
    let d = 0;
    for (let n = el; n; n = n.parentElement) d++;
    return d;
  }

  function playerCardDirectChild(el, root) {
    if (!el || !root || !root.contains(el)) return null;
    let n = el;
    while (n && n.parentElement !== root) n = n.parentElement;
    return n;
  }

  function playerCardPickRowFromRoot(leaf, playerRoot) {
  if (!leaf || !playerRoot || !playerRoot.contains(leaf)) return leaf || null;

  const row = playerCardDirectChild(leaf, playerRoot);
  if (!row) return leaf || null;

  // avataros blokk nem lehet
  if (playerCardNodeContainsAvatar(row)) return leaf || null;

  const txt = playerCardTextNorm(row.textContent);
  if (!txt) return leaf || null;

  // alsó stat sor ne legyen
  if (
    /#\d+\s*[|｜]/.test(txt) ||
    /\d+\.\d+\s*\/\s*\d+\.\d+/.test(txt) ||
    /Ø/.test(txt)
  ) {
    return leaf || null;
  }

  return row;
}

  function playerCardNodeContainsAvatar(el) {
  if (!el || !el.querySelector) return false;
  return !!(
    el.querySelector(".chakra-avatar") ||
    el.querySelector('[data-part="root"].chakra-avatar') ||
    el.querySelector("div.chakra-stack.css-1psdi5l") ||
    el.querySelector("img.chakra-image.css-6t0bzd")
  );
}

function playerCardLooksLikeStatsLine(txt) {
  const s = playerCardTextNorm(txt);
  return (
    /Ø/.test(s) ||
    /#\d+\s*[|｜]/.test(s) ||
    /\d+\.\d+\s*\/\s*\d+\.\d+/.test(s)
  );
}

function playerCardPickChipRowFromLeaf(leaf, stopRoot) {
  if (!leaf) return null;

  const leafTxt = playerCardTextNorm(leaf.textContent);
  let n = leaf;
  let best = null;

  while (n && n.parentElement && n !== stopRoot) {
    const p = n.parentElement;
    if (!p || p === stopRoot) break;

    if (playerCardNodeContainsAvatar(p)) break;

    const txt = playerCardTextNorm(p.textContent);
    if (!txt) break;

    const r = p.getBoundingClientRect();
    const leaves = playerCardTextLeaves(p)
      .map(x => playerCardTextNorm(x.textContent))
      .filter(Boolean);

    const hasLeaf = leaves.includes(leafTxt);
    const hasStats = leaves.some(playerCardLooksLikeStatsLine);
    const shortLeaves = leaves.filter(t => t.length <= 24);

    const compactEnough =
      r.height <= 72 &&
      r.width <= 340;

    if (
      hasLeaf &&
      !hasStats &&
      shortLeaves.length >= 2 &&
      shortLeaves.length <= 5 &&
      compactEnough
    ) {
      best = p;
      n = p;
      continue;
    }

    break;
  }

  return best;
}

function playerCardPickOffNameBandFromChipRow(chipRow, stopRoot) {
  if (!chipRow || !stopRoot || !stopRoot.contains(chipRow)) return null;

  let n = chipRow;
  let best = null;

  while (n && n.parentElement && n !== stopRoot) {
    const p = n.parentElement;
    if (!p || p === stopRoot) break;

    const txt = playerCardTextNorm(p.textContent);
    if (!txt) break;
    if (playerCardLooksLikeStatsLine(txt)) break;

    const r = p.getBoundingClientRect();
    const leaves = playerCardTextLeaves(p)
      .map((x) => playerCardTextNorm(x.textContent))
      .filter(Boolean);

    const hasName = leaves.some((t) => playerCardLooksLikeNameLine(t));
    const hasAvatar = playerCardNodeContainsAvatar(p);
    const digitLeafCount = leaves.filter((t) => /^\d{1,3}$/.test(t)).length;
    const shortLeafCount = leaves.filter((t) => t.length <= 24).length;

    const looksLikeWholeNameBand =
      hasName &&
      (hasAvatar || digitLeafCount >= 1) &&
      shortLeafCount >= 2 &&
      r.width >= 120 &&
      r.width <= 380 &&
      r.height >= 24 &&
      r.height <= 84;

    if (looksLikeWholeNameBand) {
      best = p;
    }

    n = p;
  }

  return best;
}

function playerCardPickCompactRow(el, root) {
  if (!el) return null;
  if (!root || !root.contains(el)) return el;

  let n = el;
  let best = el;

  while (n && n.parentElement && n !== root) {
    const p = n.parentElement;
    if (!p || p === root) break;

    const txt = playerCardTextNorm(p.textContent);
    const r = p.getBoundingClientRect();

    // FONTOS:
    // ha a szülő már avatart is tartalmaz, ne másszunk feljebb,
    // mert akkor a "név" kapcsoló az avatart is elrejti
    if (playerCardNodeContainsAvatar(p)) break;

    if (txt && txt.length <= 80 && r.height <= 64) {
      best = p;
      n = p;
      continue;
    }
    break;
  }

  return best;
}

  function playerCardPickLiveNameRow(nameLeaf, playerRoot) {
  if (!nameLeaf || !playerRoot || !playerRoot.contains(nameLeaf)) return nameLeaf || null;

  let n = nameLeaf;
  let best = null;

  while (n && n.parentElement && n !== playerRoot) {
    const p = n.parentElement;
    if (!p || p === playerRoot) break;

    const txt = playerCardTextNorm(p.textContent);
    if (!txt) break;
    if (playerCardLooksLikeStatsLine(txt)) break;

    const r = p.getBoundingClientRect();
    const leaves = playerCardTextLeaves(p)
      .map(x => playerCardTextNorm(x.textContent))
      .filter(Boolean);

    const hasAvatar = playerCardNodeContainsAvatar(p);
    const hasNameText = leaves.some(t => playerCardLooksLikeNameLine(t));
    const shortLeafCount = leaves.filter(t => t.length <= 24).length;

    const looksLikeLiveRow =
      r.height <= 72 &&
      r.width <= 360 &&
      hasNameText &&
      (hasAvatar || shortLeafCount >= 2);

    if (looksLikeLiveRow) {
      best = p;
    }

    n = p;
  }

  return best || nameLeaf;
}

function playerCardCommonAncestorWithinRoot(a, b, root) {
  if (!a || !b || !root) return null;
  if (!root.contains(a) || !root.contains(b)) return null;

  const seen = new Set();
  let n = a;
  while (n) {
    seen.add(n);
    if (n === root) break;
    n = n.parentElement;
  }

  n = b;
  while (n) {
    if (seen.has(n)) return n === root ? null : n;
    if (n === root) break;
    n = n.parentElement;
  }

  return null;
}

function playerCardPickNativeMetaRow(nameLeaf, avatarNode, playerRoot) {
  if (!nameLeaf || !playerRoot || !playerRoot.contains(nameLeaf)) return nameLeaf || null;

  const rootRect = playerRoot.getBoundingClientRect();
  const candidates = new Set();
  const avatarAnchor =
    avatarNode && playerRoot.contains(avatarNode)
      ? avatarNode
      : null;

  function pushCandidate(el) {
    if (!el || el === playerRoot) return;
    if (!playerRoot.contains(el)) return;
    candidates.add(el);
  }

  const chipRow = playerCardPickChipRowFromLeaf(nameLeaf, playerRoot);
  const lca = avatarAnchor
    ? playerCardCommonAncestorWithinRoot(nameLeaf, avatarAnchor, playerRoot)
    : null;

  if (chipRow) {
    pushCandidate(chipRow);
    let n = chipRow;
    while (n && n.parentElement && n !== playerRoot) {
      const p = n.parentElement;
      if (!p || p === playerRoot) break;
      pushCandidate(p);
      n = p;
    }
  }

  if (lca) {
    pushCandidate(lca);
    let n = lca;
    while (n && n.parentElement && n !== playerRoot) {
      const p = n.parentElement;
      if (!p || p === playerRoot) break;
      pushCandidate(p);
      n = p;
    }
  }

  let n = nameLeaf;
  while (n && n.parentElement && n !== playerRoot) {
    const p = n.parentElement;
    if (!p || p === playerRoot) break;
    pushCandidate(p);
    n = p;
  }

  if (avatarAnchor) {
    n = avatarAnchor;
    while (n && n.parentElement && n !== playerRoot) {
      const p = n.parentElement;
      if (!p || p === playerRoot) break;
      pushCandidate(p);
      n = p;
    }
  }

  Array.from(playerRoot.children || []).forEach((ch) => {
    if (!ch || ch.nodeType !== 1) return;
    if (ch.contains(nameLeaf) || (avatarAnchor && ch.contains(avatarAnchor))) {
      pushCandidate(ch);
    }
  });

  let best = null;
  let bestScore = -1e9;

  for (const p of candidates) {
    const txt = playerCardTextNorm(p.textContent);
    if (!txt) continue;
    if (playerCardLooksLikeStatsLine(txt)) continue;

    const r = p.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (r.width < 40 || r.height < 16) continue;
    if (r.height > 84) continue;
    if (r.width > Math.min(Math.round(rootRect.width * 0.82), 420)) continue;
    if ((r.top - rootRect.top) > Math.max(220, Math.round(rootRect.height * 0.74))) continue;

    const leaves = playerCardTextLeaves(p)
      .map((x) => playerCardTextNorm(x.textContent))
      .filter(Boolean);

    const hasAvatar = playerCardNodeContainsAvatar(p);
    const hasNameText = leaves.some((t) => playerCardLooksLikeNameLine(t));
    const shortLeafCount = leaves.filter((t) => t.length <= 28).length;
    const digitChipCount = leaves.filter((t) => /^\d{1,3}$/.test(t)).length;
    const directChildren = p.children ? p.children.length : 0;

    if (!hasNameText) continue;
    if (leaves.length === 1 && !hasAvatar) continue;

    let score = 0;

    if (hasAvatar) score += 90;
    if (digitChipCount >= 1) score += 120;
    if (chipRow && p === chipRow) score += 24;
    if (chipRow && p.contains(chipRow) && p !== chipRow) score += 46;
    if (avatarAnchor && p.contains(avatarAnchor)) score += 30;
    if (lca && p === lca) score += 22;
    if (directChildren >= 2 && directChildren <= 6) score += 20;
    if (shortLeafCount >= 2 && shortLeafCount <= 6) score += 12;

    if (r.width >= 180 && r.width <= 360) score += 80;
    else if (r.width >= 140 && r.width < 180) score += 20;
    else if (r.width > 360) score -= 25;

    score += Math.min(r.width, 360) * 0.12;
    score -= r.height * 0.22;

    if (!hasAvatar && digitChipCount === 0) score -= 100;
    if (p === nameLeaf) score -= 260;

    if (score > bestScore) {
      bestScore = score;
      best = p;
    }
  }

  return best || playerCardPickLiveNameRow(nameLeaf, playerRoot) || nameLeaf;
}

function restoreOffNameBandHidden(root) {
  if (!root || !root.querySelectorAll) return;
  root.querySelectorAll("[data-ad-core-off-name-band-hidden='1']").forEach((el) => {
    el.style.removeProperty("display");
    el.style.removeProperty("visibility");
    el.style.removeProperty("opacity");
    el.style.removeProperty("pointer-events");
    delete el.dataset.adCoreOffNameBandHidden;
  });
}

function applyOffNameBandHide(playerRoot, scoreEl, nameLeaf, avgLeaf, metaRow) {
  restoreOffNameBandHidden(playerRoot);

  const c = cfg();
  if (!playerRoot || c.LAYOUT_ENABLED || c.LAYOUT_SHOW_PLAYER_NAME) return;

  const all = [playerRoot, ...playerRoot.querySelectorAll("div, span, p, img, svg")];

  const seedRow =
    (metaRow && playerRoot.contains(metaRow) ? metaRow : null) ||
    (nameLeaf ? playerCardPickChipRowFromLeaf(nameLeaf, playerRoot) : null) ||
    nameLeaf ||
    null;

  const seedNodes = [];
  if (seedRow && playerRoot.contains(seedRow)) seedNodes.push(seedRow);
  if (nameLeaf && nameLeaf !== seedRow && playerRoot.contains(nameLeaf)) seedNodes.push(nameLeaf);
  if (metaRow && metaRow !== seedRow && playerRoot.contains(metaRow)) seedNodes.push(metaRow);

  const avatarNode =
    playerRoot.querySelector(".chakra-avatar") ||
    playerRoot.querySelector('[data-part="root"].chakra-avatar') ||
    playerRoot.querySelector("div.chakra-stack.css-1psdi5l") ||
    playerRoot.querySelector("img.chakra-image.css-6t0bzd");

  if (avatarNode) seedNodes.push(avatarNode);

  let bandTop = Infinity;
  let bandBottom = -Infinity;

  for (const el of seedNodes) {
    if (!el || !playerRoot.contains(el)) continue;
    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (r.width < 4 || r.height < 4) continue;
    bandTop = Math.min(bandTop, r.top);
    bandBottom = Math.max(bandBottom, r.bottom);
  }

  const scoreRect = scoreEl ? scoreEl.getBoundingClientRect() : null;
  const avgRect = avgLeaf ? avgLeaf.getBoundingClientRect() : null;

  // Fallback: ha a név/meta row már display:none lett, akkor a score és avg közti kompakt sávból indulunk.
  if (!Number.isFinite(bandTop) || !Number.isFinite(bandBottom)) {
    if (scoreRect && scoreRect.width > 0 && scoreRect.height > 0) {
      bandTop = scoreRect.bottom + 6;
      bandBottom = avgRect && avgRect.width > 0 && avgRect.height > 0 && avgRect.top > bandTop
        ? Math.min(avgRect.top - 6, bandTop + 72)
        : bandTop + 72;

      for (const el of all) {
        if (!el || !el.isConnected || el === playerRoot) continue;
        if (scoreEl && (el === scoreEl || el.contains(scoreEl) || scoreEl.contains(el))) continue;
        if (avgLeaf && (el === avgLeaf || el.contains(avgLeaf) || avgLeaf.contains(el))) continue;

        const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
        if (cs.display === "none" || cs.visibility === "hidden") continue;

        const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
        if (r.width < 4 || r.height < 4) continue;
        if (r.width > 220 || r.height > 72) continue;
        if (r.right < scoreRect.left - 40 || r.left > scoreRect.right + 80) continue;

        const cy = (r.top + r.bottom) / 2;
        if (cy < bandTop - 18 || cy > bandBottom + 18) continue;

        const txt = playerCardTextNorm(el.textContent);
        const hasAvatarish =
          playerCardNodeContainsAvatar(el) ||
          el.matches?.(".chakra-avatar, [data-part='root'].chakra-avatar, img, svg");
        const isDigitChip = /^\d{1,3}$/.test(txt);
        const isNameish = playerCardLooksLikeNameLine(txt);
        const isShortEmptyBand = !txt && r.width <= 96 && r.height <= 40;

        if (hasAvatarish || isDigitChip || isNameish || isShortEmptyBand) {
          bandTop = Math.min(bandTop, r.top);
          bandBottom = Math.max(bandBottom, r.bottom);
        }
      }
    } else {
      return;
    }
  }

  if (scoreRect && scoreRect.width > 0 && scoreRect.height > 0) {
    bandTop = Math.max(bandTop - 6, scoreRect.bottom + 6);
  }

  if (avgRect && avgRect.width > 0 && avgRect.height > 0 && avgRect.top > bandTop) {
    bandBottom = Math.min(bandBottom + 10, avgRect.top - 6);
  } else {
    bandBottom += 10;
  }

  // Sáv bővítése: minden név/avatar/0-chip jellegű kis elem, ami ebben a környezetben van
  for (const el of all) {
    if (!el || !el.isConnected) continue;

    const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
    if (cs.display === "none" || cs.visibility === "hidden") continue;

    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (r.width < 4 || r.height < 4) continue;
    if (r.height > 90 || r.width > 420) continue;

    const cy = (r.top + r.bottom) / 2;
    if (cy < bandTop - 24 || cy > bandBottom + 24) continue;

    const txt = playerCardTextNorm(el.textContent);
    const hasAvatarish =
      playerCardNodeContainsAvatar(el) ||
      el.matches?.(".chakra-avatar, [data-part='root'].chakra-avatar, img, svg");

    const isDigitChip = /^\d{1,3}$/.test(txt);
    const isNameish = playerCardLooksLikeNameLine(txt);
    const isShortEmptyBand = !txt && r.width <= 96 && r.height <= 40;

    if (hasAvatarish || isDigitChip || isNameish || isShortEmptyBand) {
      bandTop = Math.min(bandTop, r.top);
      bandBottom = Math.max(bandBottom, r.bottom);
    }
  }

  // Teljes névsáv elrejtése
  for (const el of all) {
    if (!el || !el.isConnected) continue;
    if (el === playerRoot) continue;
    if (el === scoreEl || el === avgLeaf) continue;
    if (scoreEl && (el.contains(scoreEl) || scoreEl.contains(el))) continue;
    if (avgLeaf && (el.contains(avgLeaf) || avgLeaf.contains(el))) continue;

    const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
    if (cs.display === "none" || cs.visibility === "hidden") continue;

    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (r.width < 4 || r.height < 4) continue;
    if (r.height > Math.max(96, (bandBottom - bandTop) + 24)) continue;
    if (r.width > 420) continue;

    const cy = (r.top + r.bottom) / 2;
    if (cy < bandTop || cy > bandBottom) continue;

    const txt = playerCardTextNorm(el.textContent);
    if (playerCardLooksLikeStatsLine(txt)) continue;

    el.dataset.adCoreOffNameBandHidden = "1";
    el.style.setProperty("display", "none", "important");
    el.style.setProperty("visibility", "hidden", "important");
    el.style.setProperty("opacity", "0", "important");
    el.style.setProperty("pointer-events", "none", "important");
  }
}

function playerCardTextNorm(v) {
  return String(v || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
}

function playerCardTextLeaves(root) {
  if (!root) return [];
  const all = [root, ...root.querySelectorAll("div, span, p")];
  return all.filter((el) => {
    if (!el || !el.isConnected) return false;
    if (el.closest?.(".ad-core-player-top-overlay")) return false;
    if (el.dataset?.adCoreOverlayHidden === "1" || el.dataset?.adCoreExtraHidden === "1") return false;
    if (el.children && el.children.length > 0) return false;
    if (el.closest?.("button, a")) return false;

    const txt = playerCardTextNorm(el.textContent);
    if (!txt || txt.length > 96) return false;

    const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
    if (cs.display === "none" || cs.visibility === "hidden") return false;

    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (r.width < 4 || r.height < 4) return false;

    return true;
  });
}

function playerCardLooksLikeNameLine(tRaw) {
  const s = playerCardTextNorm(tRaw);
  if (!s || s.length > 40) return false;
  if (playerCardLooksLikeAvgLine(s)) return false;
  if (/^\d{1,4}$/.test(s)) return false;
  if (/^(undo|next|start|reset|abort|surrender)$/i.test(s)) return false;
  if (!/[A-Za-zÀ-ÿ]/.test(s)) return false;
  return true;
}

function playerCardPickTopNonStatsAncestor(leaf, stopRoot) {
  if (!leaf) return null;

  let n = leaf;
  let best = leaf;

  while (n && n.parentElement && n !== stopRoot) {
    const p = n.parentElement;
    if (!p || p === stopRoot) break;

    const txt = playerCardTextNorm(p.textContent);
    if (!txt) break;

    // Amint elérjük a stat sort / meta blokkot, megállunk.
    // A best az utolsó még "tiszta" sor lesz.
    if (playerCardLooksLikeStatsLine(txt)) break;

    best = p;
    n = p;
  }

  return best;
}

function playerCardPickAvatarKeepBlock(avNode, stopRoot) {
  if (!avNode) return null;

  let n = avNode;
  let best = avNode;

  while (n && n.parentElement && n !== stopRoot) {
    const p = n.parentElement;
    if (!p || p === stopRoot) break;

    const leaves = playerCardTextLeaves(p)
      .map(x => playerCardTextNorm(x.textContent))
      .filter(Boolean);

    // Amíg a blokkban nincs valódi szöveg (név / 0 / 45+ / stat),
    // addig mehetünk feljebb. Így elkapjuk az avatar + zászló közös wrappert.
    if (leaves.length > 0) break;

    best = p;
    n = p;
  }

  return best;
}

function markAvatarKeepElements(container) {
  if (!container) return;

  const media = Array.from(container.querySelectorAll(
    "img, svg, .chakra-avatar, [data-part='root'].chakra-avatar, [style*='background-image']"
  ));

  if (!media.length) return;

  let avatarMain = null;
  let bestArea = -1;

  for (const el of media) {
    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    const area = r.width * r.height;
    if (area > bestArea) {
      bestArea = area;
      avatarMain = el;
    }
  }

  if (avatarMain) {
    avatarMain.classList.add("ad-core-keep-avatar-main");
  }

  for (const el of media) {
    if (el === avatarMain) continue;

    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();

    // kis overlay / zászló jelölés
    if (r.width <= 48 && r.height <= 36) {
      el.classList.add("ad-core-keep-flag-on-name-hide");

      if (el.parentElement && el.parentElement !== container) {
        el.parentElement.classList.add("ad-core-keep-flag-on-name-hide");
      }
    }
  }
}

function playerCardPickMetaWrap(panel) {
  if (!panel) return null;

  const playerRoot =
    panel.querySelector(".ad-ext-player") ||
    panel.querySelector(".css-17xejub") ||
    panel.querySelector(".css-1qox1u6");

  const scoreEl = playerCardPickMainScoreEl(panel);
  const baseRoot = playerRoot || panel;
  const br = baseRoot.getBoundingClientRect();
  const baseArea = Math.max(1, br.width * br.height);

  const candidates = new Set();

  panel.querySelectorAll(".css-y3hfdd").forEach((el) => candidates.add(el));
  if (playerRoot) {
    Array.from(playerRoot.children).forEach((el) => candidates.add(el));
  }

  let best = null;
  let bestScore = -1e9;

  for (const el of candidates) {
    if (!el || !el.isConnected) continue;
    if (scoreEl && (el === scoreEl || el.contains(scoreEl))) continue;

    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (r.width < 8 || r.height < 8) continue;

    const areaRatio = (r.width * r.height) / baseArea;
    if (areaRatio > 0.72) continue;

    const leaves = playerCardTextLeaves(el);
    if (!leaves.length) continue;

    let score = 0;

    if (leaves.some((n) => playerCardLooksLikeAvgLine(n.textContent))) score += 80;
    if (leaves.some((n) => playerCardLooksLikeNameLine(n.textContent))) score += 45;
    if (playerCardNodeContainsAvatar(el)) score += 10;

    // túl score-szerű candidate-eket büntessük
    if (leaves.some((n) => /^\d{1,3}$/.test(playerCardTextNorm(n.textContent)))) score -= 30;

    score -= areaRatio * 80;
    score += Math.min(leaves.length, 8) * 2;

    if (score > bestScore) {
      bestScore = score;
      best = el;
    }
  }

  return best;
}

function playerCardPickAvgLeaf(root) {
  if (!root) return null;

  const cands = playerCardTextLeaves(root).filter((el) =>
    playerCardLooksLikeAvgLine(el.textContent)
  );
  if (!cands.length) return null;

  return cands.sort((a, b) => {
    const da = playerCardTreeDepth(a);
    const db = playerCardTreeDepth(b);
    if (db !== da) return db - da;

    const ta = playerCardTextNorm(a.textContent).length;
    const tb = playerCardTextNorm(b.textContent).length;
    return ta - tb;
  })[0];
}

function playerCardPickNameLeaf(root, avgLeaf, scoreEl) {
  if (!root) return null;

  const cands = playerCardTextLeaves(root).filter((el) => {
    if (avgLeaf && (el === avgLeaf || avgLeaf.contains(el) || el.contains(avgLeaf))) return false;
    if (scoreEl && (el === scoreEl || scoreEl.contains(el) || el.contains(scoreEl))) return false;

    const txt = playerCardTextNorm(el.textContent);
    return playerCardLooksLikeNameLine(txt);
  });

  if (!cands.length) return null;

  return cands.sort((a, b) => {
    const fa = parseFloat(getComputedStyle(a).fontSize) || 0;
    const fb = parseFloat(getComputedStyle(b).fontSize) || 0;
    if (fb !== fa) return fb - fa;

    const ta = playerCardTextNorm(a.textContent);
    const tb = playerCardTextNorm(b.textContent);

    const sa = (ta.length <= 24 ? 10 : 0) + (/[A-ZÀ-Ÿ]/.test(ta) ? 4 : 0) - Math.abs(ta.length - 10) * 0.2;
    const sb = (tb.length <= 24 ? 10 : 0) + (/[A-ZÀ-Ÿ]/.test(tb) ? 4 : 0) - Math.abs(tb.length - 10) * 0.2;

    return sb - sa;
  })[0];
}

function playerCardPickMainScoreEl(panel) {
  if (!panel) return null;

  const panelRect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
  const panelArea = Math.max(1, panelRect.width * panelRect.height);

  let pointBoardHost = null;
  let historyHost = null;
  let fireworksHost = null;

  try { pointBoardHost = findPlayerPointBoard(panel); } catch {}
  try { historyHost = phFindHistoryHost(panel); } catch {}
  try { fireworksHost = panel.querySelector(".fireworks"); } catch {}

  function isSafeHistoryHost(host) {
    if (!host || !host.isConnected) return false;
    const r = host.getBoundingClientRect();

    const relTop = r.top - panelRect.top;
    const relBottom = r.bottom - panelRect.top;

    // csak akkor tekintsük valódi history blokknak,
    // ha tényleg a panel alsó részén van
    return (
      r.width >= 60 &&
      r.height >= 80 &&
      relTop >= panelRect.height * 0.30 &&
      relBottom >= panelRect.height * 0.55
    );
  }

  const blockedHosts = [
    pointBoardHost,
    isSafeHistoryHost(historyHost) ? historyHost : null,
    fireworksHost
  ].filter(Boolean);

  const isBlocked = (el) => {
    if (!el) return true;
    // Saját overlay-klónokat soha nem szabad újra natív score-ként felismerni,
    // mert ettől a név/átlag kapcsolók váltásakor kaszkád-szerűen csökken a méret.
    if (el.closest?.(".ad-core-player-top-overlay")) return true;
    if (el.classList?.contains("ad-core-overlay-score") || el.classList?.contains("ad-core-overlay-meta")) return true;
    if (el.dataset?.adCoreOverlayHidden === "1" || el.dataset?.adCoreExtraHidden === "1") return true;
    if (el.closest?.(".fireworks")) return true;
    if (el.classList?.contains("animate180Text")) return true;
    return blockedHosts.some((host) => host && (el === host || host.contains(el)));
  };

  function scoreCandidateLooksLikeTopBadge(el, txt, r, fs) {
    const relTop = r.top - panelRect.top;
    const relLeft = r.left - panelRect.left;
    const relRight = panelRect.right - r.right;
    const nearTop = relTop <= panelRect.height * 0.24;
    const nearSide = relLeft <= panelRect.width * 0.22 || relRight <= panelRect.width * 0.22;
    const badgeSized =
      r.width <= panelRect.width * 0.28 &&
      r.height <= panelRect.height * 0.12;
    const tinyish = fs <= Math.max(22, Math.min(34, panelRect.height * 0.065));

    let metaishAncestor = false;
    for (let n = el; n && n !== panel; n = n.parentElement) {
      const nr = n.getBoundingClientRect();
      const nRelBottom = nr.bottom - panelRect.top;
      if (nRelBottom > panelRect.height * 0.28) break;

      const nt = playerCardTextNorm(n.textContent);
      if (!nt) continue;

      if (
        playerCardLooksLikeAvgLine(nt) ||
        playerCardLooksLikeNameLine(nt) ||
        nt.includes("#") ||
        /[A-Za-z]/.test(nt)
      ) {
        metaishAncestor = true;
        break;
      }
    }

    return (
      (nearTop && nearSide && badgeSized) ||
      (nearTop && tinyish && metaishAncestor && txt.length <= 2)
    );
  }

  function scoreCandidateScore(el) {
    const txt = playerCardTextNorm(el.textContent);
    if (!/^\d{1,3}$/.test(txt)) return -Infinity;

    const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
    if (cs.display === "none" || cs.visibility === "hidden") return -Infinity;
    if ((parseFloat(cs.opacity) || 0) <= 0) return -Infinity;

    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (r.width < 18 || r.height < 18) return -Infinity;
    if (r.left < -5000 || r.top < -5000) return -Infinity;

    const relTop = r.top - panelRect.top;
    if (relTop > panelRect.height * 0.72) return -Infinity;

    const fs = parseFloat(cs.fontSize) || 0;
    if (scoreCandidateLooksLikeTopBadge(el, txt, r, fs)) return -Infinity;

    const area = r.width * r.height;
    const cx = r.left + r.width / 2;
    const panelCx = panelRect.left + panelRect.width / 2;
    const widthRatio = clamp(r.width / Math.max(1, panelRect.width), 0, 1);
    const heightRatio = clamp(r.height / Math.max(1, panelRect.height), 0, 1);
    const centeredness = 1 - clamp(Math.abs(cx - panelCx) / Math.max(1, panelRect.width * 0.5), 0, 1);

    let score = 0;
    score += fs * 26;
    score += area * 0.05;
    score -= Math.abs(cx - panelCx) * 0.22;
    score += widthRatio * 220;
    score += heightRatio * 260;
    score += centeredness * 160;

    if (relTop < panelRect.height * 0.42) score += 45;
    if (txt === "0") score += 60; // win state-ben ez legyen erősen preferált

    if (txt === "0") {
      const zeroLooksLikeMainScore =
        centeredness >= 0.70 &&
        (
          widthRatio >= 0.22 ||
          heightRatio >= 0.14 ||
          area >= panelArea * 0.035
        );

      if (!zeroLooksLikeMainScore) score -= 320;
    }

    return score;
  }

  // 1) Első kör: text-leaf keresés
  const leafCandidates = playerCardTextLeaves(panel).filter((el) => {
    if (!el || !el.isConnected) return false;
    if (isBlocked(el)) return false;
    if (el.closest(".ad-ext-turn-throw")) return false;
    if (el.closest(".ad-total-cell")) return false;
    if (el.closest(".ad-total-overlay")) return false;
    if (el.closest(".ad-native-history-floating")) return false;
    if (el.closest("button, a")) return false;
    return /^\d{1,3}$/.test(playerCardTextNorm(el.textContent));
  });

  let best = null;
  let bestScore = -Infinity;

  for (const el of leafCandidates) {
    const s = scoreCandidateScore(el);
    if (s > bestScore) {
      bestScore = s;
      best = el;
    }
  }

  if (best) return best;

  // 2) Fallback: minden p/span/div közül
  const fallback = Array.from(panel.querySelectorAll("p, span, div")).filter((el) => {
    if (!el || !el.isConnected) return false;
    if (isBlocked(el)) return false;
    if (el.closest(".ad-ext-turn-throw, .ad-total-cell, .ad-total-overlay, .ad-native-history-floating")) return false;
    if (el.closest(".fireworks")) return false;
    if (el.closest("button, a")) return false;

    const txt = playerCardTextNorm(el.textContent);
    if (!/^\d{1,3}$/.test(txt)) return false;

    const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
    if (cs.display === "none" || cs.visibility === "hidden") return false;

    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (r.width < 18 || r.height < 18) return false;
    if ((r.top - panelRect.top) > panelRect.height * 0.72) return false;

    return true;
  });

  let fb = null;
  let fbScore = -Infinity;

  for (const el of fallback) {
    const s = scoreCandidateScore(el);
    if (s > fbScore) {
      fbScore = s;
      fb = el;
    }
  }

  return fb;
}

function playerCardPickStatsRowFromLeaf(leaf, stopRoot, nameLeaf) {
  if (!leaf) return null;

  const nameTxt = playerCardTextNorm(nameLeaf?.textContent || "");
  let n = leaf;
  let best = leaf;

  while (n && n.parentElement && n !== stopRoot) {
    const p = n.parentElement;
    if (!p || p === stopRoot) break;

    const txt = playerCardTextNorm(p.textContent);
    if (!txt) break;

    const leaves = playerCardTextLeaves(p)
      .map(x => playerCardTextNorm(x.textContent))
      .filter(Boolean);

    const hasStats =
      playerCardLooksLikeStatsLine(txt) ||
      leaves.some(playerCardLooksLikeStatsLine);

    if (!hasStats) break;
    if (nameTxt && txt.includes(nameTxt)) break;
    if (playerCardNodeContainsAvatar(p)) break;

    best = p;
    n = p;
  }

  return best;
}

function ensurePlayerMetaBlock(playerRoot, scoreZone, nameRowEl, statsRowEl) {
  if (!playerRoot) return null;

  let block = Array.from(playerRoot.children).find(
    el => el.classList && el.classList.contains("ad-core-zone-meta-block")
  );

  if (!block) {
    block = document.createElement("div");
    block.className = "ad-core-zone-meta-block";
  }

  if (block.parentElement !== playerRoot) {
    if (scoreZone && scoreZone.parentElement === playerRoot) {
      playerRoot.insertBefore(block, scoreZone.nextSibling);
    } else {
      playerRoot.appendChild(block);
    }
  } else if (scoreZone && scoreZone.parentElement === playerRoot) {
    if (block.previousSibling !== scoreZone) {
      playerRoot.insertBefore(block, scoreZone.nextSibling);
    }
  }

  if (nameRowEl && nameRowEl.parentElement !== block) {
    block.appendChild(nameRowEl);
  }

  if (statsRowEl && statsRowEl !== nameRowEl && statsRowEl.parentElement !== block) {
    block.appendChild(statsRowEl);
  }

  return block;
}

function applyPlayerMetaPlacement(panel, scoreEl, metaRow, avgRow) {
  if (!panel || !scoreEl) return;

  const c = cfg();
  const layoutOn = !!c.LAYOUT_ENABLED;
  const fontStack = getPlayerCardResolvedFontStack();
  const showName = !!c.LAYOUT_SHOW_PLAYER_NAME;
  const showAvg = !!c.LAYOUT_SHOW_PLAYER_AVG;
  const nameAbove = c.LAYOUT_NAME_PLACEMENT === "above";
  const avgAbove = c.LAYOUT_AVG_PLACEMENT === "above";

  const panelRect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
  if (!panelRect.width || !panelRect.height) return;

  const playerRoot =
    panel.querySelector(".ad-ext-player") ||
    panel.querySelector(".css-17xejub") ||
    panel.querySelector(".css-1qox1u6") ||
    panel;

  const panelCs = getComputedStyle(panel);
  if (panelCs.position === "static") panel.style.position = "relative";

  function norm(v) {
    return playerCardTextNorm(v || "");
  }

  function restoreOverlayManagedOriginals() {
    panel.querySelectorAll(":scope > .ad-core-player-top-overlay").forEach((el) => el.remove());

    panel.querySelectorAll("[data-ad-core-overlay-hidden='1'], [data-ad-core-extra-hidden='1']").forEach((el) => {
      el.style.removeProperty("visibility");
      el.style.removeProperty("pointer-events");
      el.style.removeProperty("opacity");
      el.style.removeProperty("display");
      delete el.dataset.adCoreOverlayHidden;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreExtraHidden;
    });

    if (panel.dataset && panel.dataset.adCoreInfoBottom) {
      delete panel.dataset.adCoreInfoBottom;
    }
  }

  function softHide(el) {
    if (!el || !el.isConnected || !panel.contains(el)) return;
    el.dataset.adCoreOverlayHidden = "1";
    el.style.setProperty("visibility", "hidden", "important");
    el.style.setProperty("opacity", "0", "important");
    el.style.setProperty("pointer-events", "none", "important");
  }

  function makeOverlay(className) {
    const overlay = document.createElement("div");
    overlay.className = className || "ad-core-player-top-overlay";
    overlay.style.setProperty("position", "absolute", "important");
    overlay.style.setProperty("inset", "0", "important");
    overlay.style.setProperty("pointer-events", "none", "important");
    overlay.style.setProperty("overflow", "visible", "important");
    overlay.style.setProperty("z-index", "70", "important");
    panel.appendChild(overlay);
    return overlay;
  }

  function scoreCenterX() {
    const sr = scoreEl.getBoundingClientRect();
    return clamp(
      Math.round(sr.left - panelRect.left + sr.width / 2),
      32,
      Math.max(32, panelRect.width - 32)
    );
  }

  function nativeScoreTop() {
    const sr = scoreEl.getBoundingClientRect();
    return clamp(
      Math.round(sr.top - panelRect.top),
      4,
      Math.max(4, panelRect.height - 34)
    );
  }

  function nativeScoreBottom() {
    const sr = scoreEl.getBoundingClientRect();
    return Math.round(sr.bottom - panelRect.top);
  }

  function findHistoryTop() {
    let top = panelRect.height - 8;

    document.querySelectorAll(".ad-native-history-floating").forEach((box) => {
      if (!box || !box.isConnected) return;
      if (box.closest?.(".ad-core-player-top-overlay")) return;

      const cs = getComputedStyle(box);
      if (cs.display === "none" || cs.visibility === "hidden") return;

      const r = box.getBoundingClientRect();
      const intersects = !(
        r.right < panelRect.left ||
        r.left > panelRect.right ||
        r.bottom < panelRect.top ||
        r.top > panelRect.bottom
      );
      if (!intersects) return;

      const relTop = Math.round(r.top - panelRect.top);
      if (relTop > 20) top = Math.min(top, relTop);
    });

    return clamp(top, Math.round(panelRect.height * 0.34), panelRect.height - 6);
  }

  function lineHeight(el) {
    return Math.max(0, Math.ceil(el?.getBoundingClientRect?.().height || 0));
  }

  function blockHeight(items, gap) {
    if (!items.length) return 0;
    return items.reduce((sum, item, i) => sum + lineHeight(item.el || item) + (i ? gap : 0), 0);
  }

  function fitElementToWidth(el, maxWidth, minScale, desiredScale) {
    if (!el) return desiredScale || 1;

    const wanted = Number(desiredScale || el.dataset.adCoreBaseScale || 1) || 1;
    const limitW = Math.max(1, Number(maxWidth || 0));

    // v6 Layout ON width-fit: a nyers szélességet mindig a transform nélküli
    // tartalomból becsüljük. A scrollWidth nem tartalmazza a CSS transform scale-t,
    // a boundingClientRect viszont igen, ezért csak tartalék mérésként használjuk.
    const rectW = Math.max(0, el.getBoundingClientRect?.().width || 0);
    const scrollW = Math.max(0, el.scrollWidth || 0, el.offsetWidth || 0);
    const rawW = Math.max(1, scrollW || (rectW / Math.max(wanted, 0.01)) || rectW);

    const maxAllowedScale = limitW / rawW;
    const fit = clamp(
      Math.min(wanted, maxAllowedScale),
      minScale || 0.24,
      Math.max(wanted, maxAllowedScale, 1)
    );

    el.dataset.adCoreFittedScale = String(fit);
    el.dataset.adCoreMaxAllowedScale = String(maxAllowedScale);
    return fit;
  }

  function placeRowsBottomAnchored(entries, zoneTop, zoneBottom, gapBase = 5) {
    if (!entries.length) return 0;

    zoneTop = Math.round(zoneTop);
    zoneBottom = Math.round(Math.max(zoneTop + 1, zoneBottom));

    let gap = gapBase;
    let total = blockHeight(entries, gap);
    let guard = 0;

    while (total > zoneBottom - zoneTop && guard < 8) {
      for (const entry of entries) {
        const el = entry.el || entry;
        const cur = Number(el.dataset.adCoreFittedScale || entry.scale || 1) || 1;
        const next = clamp(cur * 0.92, entry.minScale || 0.22, entry.scale || cur);
        el.dataset.adCoreFittedScale = String(next);
        el.style.setProperty("transform", `translateX(-50%) scale(${next})`, "important");
      }
      gap = Math.max(2, gap - 1);
      total = blockHeight(entries, gap);
      guard += 1;
    }

    let y = Math.max(zoneTop, zoneBottom - total);
    for (let i = 0; i < entries.length; i++) {
      const el = entries[i].el || entries[i];
      el.style.setProperty("top", `${Math.round(y)}px`, "important");
      y += lineHeight(el) + (i < entries.length - 1 ? gap : 0);
    }

    return Math.round(y);
  }

  function placeRowsTopAnchored(entries, zoneTop, zoneBottom, gapBase = 5) {
    if (!entries.length) return 0;

    zoneTop = Math.round(zoneTop);
    zoneBottom = Math.round(Math.max(zoneTop + 1, zoneBottom));

    let gap = gapBase;
    let total = blockHeight(entries, gap);
    let guard = 0;

    while (total > zoneBottom - zoneTop && guard < 8) {
      for (const entry of entries) {
        const el = entry.el || entry;
        const cur = Number(el.dataset.adCoreFittedScale || entry.scale || 1) || 1;
        const next = clamp(cur * 0.92, entry.minScale || 0.22, entry.scale || cur);
        el.dataset.adCoreFittedScale = String(next);
        el.style.setProperty("transform", `translateX(-50%) scale(${next})`, "important");
      }
      gap = Math.max(2, gap - 1);
      total = blockHeight(entries, gap);
      guard += 1;
    }

    let y = zoneTop;
    for (let i = 0; i < entries.length; i++) {
      const el = entries[i].el || entries[i];
      el.style.setProperty("top", `${Math.round(y)}px`, "important");
      y += lineHeight(el) + (i < entries.length - 1 ? gap : 0);
    }

    return Math.round(y);
  }

  function reactKeys(el) {
    if (!el) return [];
    return Object.keys(el).filter((k) =>
      k.startsWith("__reactFiber$") ||
      k.startsWith("__reactProps$") ||
      k.startsWith("__reactContainer$")
    );
  }

  function looksLikeFullName(v) {
    const s = norm(v);
    if (!s || s.length < 2 || s.length > 48) return false;
    if (s.endsWith("..") || s.endsWith("…")) return false;
    if (/^\d+$/.test(s)) return false;
    if (playerCardLooksLikeAvgLine(s)) return false;
    return /[A-Za-zÀ-ÿ]/.test(s);
  }

  function shortPrefix(v) {
    return norm(v).replace(/[.…]+$/g, "").toLowerCase();
  }

  function findReactFullName(startEl, shortText) {
    const prefix = shortPrefix(shortText);
    if (!startEl || !prefix || prefix.length < 2) return "";

    const queueRoots = [];
    let n = startEl;
    for (let i = 0; n && i < 10; i++, n = n.parentElement) queueRoots.push(n);

    let best = "";
    let bestScore = -Infinity;

    for (const el of queueRoots) {
      for (const rk of reactKeys(el)) {
        const rootObj = el[rk];
        if (!rootObj || typeof rootObj !== "object") continue;

        const seen = new WeakSet();
        const q = [{ obj: rootObj, depth: 0 }];

        while (q.length) {
          const cur = q.shift();
          const obj = cur?.obj;
          if (!obj || typeof obj !== "object" || seen.has(obj)) continue;
          seen.add(obj);

          for (const [key, val] of Object.entries(obj)) {
            if (typeof val === "string") {
              const s = norm(val);
              if (!looksLikeFullName(s)) continue;
              if (!s.toLowerCase().startsWith(prefix)) continue;

              const keyBonus = key === "label" ? 90 : key === "name" ? 65 : key === "children" ? 40 : 0;
              const score = keyBonus + s.length - cur.depth * 8 + (s === s.toUpperCase() ? 10 : 0);
              if (score > bestScore) {
                bestScore = score;
                best = s;
              }
            } else if (val && typeof val === "object" && cur.depth < 10) {
              q.push({ obj: val, depth: cur.depth + 1 });
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }

    return best;
  }

  function pickNameLeaf() {
    const direct = panel.querySelector(".ad-core-player-name");
    if (direct) return direct;

    const fromMeta = metaRow ? playerCardPickNameLeaf(metaRow, null, scoreEl) : null;
    if (fromMeta) return fromMeta;

    return playerCardPickNameLeaf(playerRoot, null, scoreEl);
  }

  function pickAvgLeaf() {
    const direct = panel.querySelector(".ad-core-player-avg");
    if (direct) return direct;

    const fromAvgRow = avgRow ? playerCardPickAvgLeaf(avgRow) : null;
    if (fromAvgRow) return fromAvgRow;

    return playerCardPickAvgLeaf(playerRoot);
  }

  function resolveNameText(sourceNameLeaf) {
    const raw = norm(sourceNameLeaf?.textContent || "");
    const fromReact = findReactFullName(sourceNameLeaf || metaRow, raw || norm(metaRow?.textContent || ""));
    if (fromReact) return fromReact;
    if (playerCardLooksLikeNameLine(raw)) return raw;

    const leaves = playerCardTextLeaves(playerRoot)
      .filter((el) => el !== scoreEl)
      .map((el) => norm(el.textContent))
      .filter((t) => playerCardLooksLikeNameLine(t));

    return leaves[0] || "";
  }

  function resolveAvgText(sourceAvgLeaf) {
    const raw = norm(sourceAvgLeaf?.textContent || avgRow?.textContent || "");
    if (playerCardLooksLikeAvgLine(raw) || /#\s*\d+/.test(raw)) return raw;

    const leaves = playerCardTextLeaves(playerRoot)
      .map((el) => norm(el.textContent))
      .filter((t) => playerCardLooksLikeAvgLine(t) || /#\s*\d+/.test(t));

    return leaves[0] || "";
  }

  function makeTextLine(overlay, text, kind, fontPx, weight, centerX) {
    const line = document.createElement("div");
    line.className = `ad-core-overlay-${kind} ad-core-overlay-meta ad-core-overlay-offline-v21`;
    line.textContent = text;
    line.style.setProperty("position", "absolute", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("left", `${centerX}px`, "important");
    line.style.setProperty("top", "0px", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("transform", "translateX(-50%)", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("transform-origin", "top center", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("margin", "0", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("padding", "0 6px", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("width", "max-content", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("max-width", "none", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("white-space", "nowrap", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("text-align", "center", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("overflow", "visible", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("font-family", fontStack, "important");
    line.style.setProperty("font-size", `${fontPx}px`, "important");
    line.style.setProperty("font-weight", String(weight), "important");
    line.style.setProperty("line-height", "1.05", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("letter-spacing", "0", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("color", "inherit", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("visibility", "visible", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("opacity", "1", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("z-index", "86", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("pointer-events", "none", "important");
    overlay.appendChild(line);
    return line;
  }

  function fitTextLineToWidth(line, maxWidth) {
    if (!line) return;
    let guard = 0;
    while (guard < 8) {
      const w = Math.max(line.scrollWidth || 0, line.getBoundingClientRect().width || 0);
      if (!w || w <= maxWidth) break;
      const cur = parseFloat(line.style.fontSize) || 14;
      const next = clamp(Math.floor(cur * (maxWidth / w) * 0.98), 9, cur);
      if (next >= cur) break;
      line.style.setProperty("font-size", `${next}px`, "important");
      guard += 1;
    }
  }

  // =========================================================
  // LAYOUT OFF (v44 native measured)
  // =========================================================
  // OFF módban már nem építünk overlay-t a kártya fölé. A natív player card
  // saját DOM-jában rendezzük el a score / name / avg sorokat, mért slotokkal,
  // stabil parent magassággal és jobb oldali history dokkal.
  //
  // A v43-ig használt clone-overlay logika a player card / nativeStack
  // magasságát véletlenül 0..4 px-re tudta omlasztani, ezért a kártyák néha
  // egy vékony csíkká szűkültek. Ezt elkerüljük: az alábbi pipeline csak a
  // natív elemekre tesz CSS-t, és nem hoz létre újabb absolute DOM réteget.
  if (!layoutOn) {
    restoreOverlayManagedOriginals();

    // Visszatakarítjuk a régebbi verziók által rátett inline stílusokat,
    // hogy az új natív mérés tiszta lapról induljon.
    [metaRow, avgRow].filter(Boolean).forEach((row) => {
      row.classList.remove(
        "ad-core-visible-meta-row",
        "ad-core-visible-avg-row"
      );
      [
        "position", "left", "right", "top", "bottom", "transform", "transform-origin",
        "margin", "width", "max-width", "display", "text-align", "pointer-events",
        "visibility", "z-index", "line-height", "white-space", "padding", "opacity", "zoom"
      ].forEach((prop) => row.style.removeProperty(prop));
    });

    if (typeof applyOffNativeMeasuredLayout === "function") {
      try { applyOffNativeMeasuredLayout(panel); } catch (_) {}
    }

    return;
  }


  // =========================================================
  // LAYOUT ON
  // =========================================================
  // Ez külön ág, hogy az OFF javítás ne tudja elrontani a korábban működő Layout ON logikát.
  restoreOverlayManagedOriginals();

  const root = document.documentElement;
  if (root.classList.contains("ad-core-hide-layout-name")) metaRow = null;
  if (root.classList.contains("ad-core-hide-layout-avg")) avgRow = null;

  // Akkor is kell a kontrollált score-overlay, ha a név és az átlag sor ki van kapcsolva.
  // Különben a natív score/total blokk visszaugrik a kártya közepére/aljára,
  // és ráfedhet a historyra.
  const overlay = makeOverlay("ad-core-player-top-overlay ad-core-player-top-overlay-on-v23");

  function layoutOnInfoZone() {
    const panelW = Math.max(1, Math.round(panelRect.width));
    const panelH = Math.max(1, Math.round(panelRect.height));
    const pad = clamp(Math.round(panelW * 0.028), 8, 18);
    const gap = clamp(Math.round(panelW * 0.030), 8, 18);

    let best = null;
    panel.querySelectorAll(":scope > .ad-native-history-floating").forEach((box) => {
      if (!(box instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
      try {
        const cs = getComputedStyle(box);
        if (cs.display === "none" || cs.visibility === "hidden" || Number(cs.opacity || 1) <= 0.02) return;
      } catch (_) {}
      const r = box.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (r.width < 40 || r.height < 44) return;
      const relLeft = Math.round(r.left - panelRect.left);
      const relRight = Math.round(r.right - panelRect.left);
      const relTop = Math.round(r.top - panelRect.top);
      const relBottom = Math.round(r.bottom - panelRect.top);
      if (relRight <= 0 || relLeft >= panelW || relBottom <= 0 || relTop >= panelH) return;
      const cand = {
        left: clamp(relLeft, 0, panelW),
        right: clamp(relRight, 0, panelW),
        top: clamp(relTop, 0, panelH),
        bottom: clamp(relBottom, 0, panelH),
        w: Math.round(r.width),
        h: Math.round(r.height),
      };
      if (!best || cand.w * cand.h > best.w * best.h) best = cand;
    });

    let left = pad;
    let right = panelW - pad;
    let side = "none";

    if (best) {
      const histCenterX = (best.left + best.right) / 2;
      const isSideHistory = best.h > panelH * 0.42 && best.w < panelW * 0.55;
      if (isSideHistory && histCenterX >= panelW * 0.54 && best.left - gap - pad >= 110) {
        right = clamp(best.left - gap, pad + 110, panelW - pad);
        side = "right";
      } else if (isSideHistory && histCenterX <= panelW * 0.46 && panelW - (best.right + gap) - pad >= 110) {
        left = clamp(best.right + gap, pad, panelW - pad - 110);
        side = "left";
      }
    }

    const width = Math.max(96, right - left);
    const center = clamp(Math.round(left + width / 2), 32, Math.max(32, panelW - 32));
    return { left, right, width, center, side };
  }

  const onInfoZone = layoutOnInfoZone();
  const centerX = onInfoZone.center;
  const historyTop = findHistoryTop();
