  const panelCount = (typeof getVisiblePlayerPanels === "function") ? getVisiblePlayerPanels().length : 0;
  const isTwoPlayerCompact = panelCount > 0 && panelCount <= 2;
  const cardScale = clamp(Math.min(panelRect.width / 411, panelRect.height / 620), 0.48, 1);
  const widthFactor = clamp(panelRect.width / 360, 0.50, 1);
  const heightFactor = clamp(panelRect.height / 520, 0.50, 1);
  const vpH = window.innerHeight || panelRect.height || 0;
  const lowHFactor = vpH <= 620 ? 0.68 : vpH <= 760 ? 0.82 : 1;
  const panelFactor = panelCount >= 5 ? 0.82 : panelCount >= 3 ? 0.90 : (isTwoPlayerCompact ? (vpH <= 620 ? 0.86 : vpH <= 760 ? 0.92 : 0.97) : 1);

  // Layout ON v6: a 100-as érték marad a v5-ben elfogadott vizuális alap.
  // 100% fölött a kívánt méret ténylegesen nőhet, de később soronként
  // a kártya aktuális belső szélességéhez lesz limitálva.
  const onScoreControl = clamp(Number(c.LAYOUT_SCORE_FONT_PX ?? 100), 40, 320);
  const onScoreBasePx = 190;
  const userScorePx = clamp(Math.round(onScoreBasePx * (onScoreControl / 100)), 40, 640);
  const scorePx = clamp(
    Math.round(userScorePx * Math.min(lowHFactor, panelFactor, widthFactor, heightFactor, cardScale, 1)),
    panelRect.width <= 220 ? 18 : panelRect.width <= 260 ? 22 : panelRect.width <= 320 ? 26 : 30,
    userScorePx
  );

  function buildScoreClone(src) {
    const cloneEl = document.createElement("div");
    cloneEl.className = "ad-core-overlay-score";
    cloneEl.textContent = norm(src?.textContent || "");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("position", "absolute", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("left", `${centerX}px`, "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("top", "0px", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("transform", "translateX(-50%)", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("margin", "0", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("padding", "0", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("width", "max-content", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("max-width", "none", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("white-space", "nowrap", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("text-align", "center", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("font-family", fontStack, "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("font-size", `${scorePx}px`, "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("font-weight", "800", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("letter-spacing", "0", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("line-height", "1", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("color", "inherit", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("z-index", "84", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("pointer-events", "none", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("visibility", "visible", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("opacity", "1", "important");
    return cloneEl;
  }

  function patchFullNameInClone(cloneEl, sourceRow) {
    if (!cloneEl || !sourceRow) return;
    const nameLeaf = playerCardPickNameLeaf(sourceRow, null, null);
    const raw = norm(nameLeaf?.textContent || sourceRow.textContent || "");
    const full = findReactFullName(sourceRow, raw);
    if (!full) return;

    const target =
      cloneEl.querySelector?.('.ad-ext-player-name p.chakra-text, .ad-ext-player-name > p') ||
      cloneEl.querySelector?.('.ad-ext-player-name') ||
      playerCardPickNameLeaf(cloneEl, null, null) ||
      cloneEl;

    if (!target) return;
    target.textContent = full;
    target.setAttribute("title", full);
    target.dataset.adCoreOverlayFullName = full;
  }

  function normalizeMetaClone(cloneEl, displayMode) {
    if (!cloneEl) return;

    cloneEl.style.setProperty("position", "absolute", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("left", `${centerX}px`, "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("top", "0px", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("margin", "0", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("padding", "0", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("width", "max-content", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("max-width", "none", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("display", displayMode, "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("text-align", "center", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("white-space", "nowrap", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("overflow", "visible", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("text-overflow", "clip", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("font-family", fontStack, "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("z-index", "86", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("pointer-events", "none", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("visibility", "visible", "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("opacity", "1", "important");

    cloneEl.querySelectorAll("*").forEach((el) => {
      if (!el || !el.style) return;
      el.style.setProperty("font-family", fontStack, "important");
      el.style.setProperty("white-space", "nowrap", "important");
      el.style.setProperty("text-overflow", "clip", "important");
      el.style.setProperty("min-width", "0", "important");
      el.style.setProperty("max-width", "none", "important");
      el.style.setProperty("flex-wrap", "nowrap", "important");
    });

    if (displayMode === "inline-flex") {
      cloneEl.style.setProperty("align-items", "center", "important");
      cloneEl.style.setProperty("justify-content", "center", "important");
      cloneEl.style.setProperty("column-gap", "6px", "important");
      cloneEl.style.setProperty("row-gap", "0", "important");

      cloneEl.querySelectorAll('.chakra-avatar, [data-part="root"].chakra-avatar').forEach((el) => {
        if (!el || !el.style) return;
        el.style.setProperty("display", "inline-flex", "important");
        el.style.setProperty("align-items", "center", "important");
        el.style.setProperty("justify-content", "center", "important");
        el.style.setProperty("margin-right", "6px", "important");
        el.style.setProperty("border", "none", "important");
        el.style.setProperty("outline", "none", "important");
        el.style.setProperty("box-shadow", "none", "important");
        el.style.setProperty("background", "transparent", "important");
        el.style.setProperty("padding", "0", "important");
        el.style.setProperty("overflow", "hidden", "important");
        el.style.setProperty("flex", "0 0 auto", "important");
      });

      cloneEl.querySelectorAll("img, svg").forEach((el) => {
        if (!el || !el.style) return;
        el.style.setProperty("border", "none", "important");
        el.style.setProperty("outline", "none", "important");
        el.style.setProperty("box-shadow", "none", "important");
        el.style.setProperty("background", "transparent", "important");
        el.style.setProperty("object-fit", "cover", "important");
        el.style.setProperty("object-position", "center center", "important");
      });
    }
  }

  function buildMetaClone(sourceRow, scale, displayMode) {
    if (!sourceRow) return null;
    const cloneEl = sourceRow.cloneNode(true);
    cloneEl.classList.add("ad-core-overlay-meta");
    cloneEl.dataset.adCoreBaseScale = String(scale);
    normalizeMetaClone(cloneEl, displayMode);
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("transform", `translateX(-50%) scale(${scale})`, "important");
    cloneEl.style.setProperty("transform-origin", "top center", "important");
    if (displayMode === "inline-flex") patchFullNameInClone(cloneEl, sourceRow);
    return cloneEl;
  }

  function buildAvgClone(sourceRow, scale) {
    if (!sourceRow) return null;
    const txt = norm(sourceRow.textContent || "");
    if (!txt) return null;

    const line = document.createElement("div");
    line.className = "ad-core-overlay-avg";
    line.textContent = txt;
    line.dataset.adCoreBaseScale = String(scale);
    line.style.setProperty("position", "absolute", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("left", `${centerX}px`, "important");
    line.style.setProperty("top", "0px", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("transform", `translateX(-50%) scale(${scale})`, "important");
    line.style.setProperty("transform-origin", "top center", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("margin", "0", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("padding", "0", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("white-space", "nowrap", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("text-align", "center", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("font-family", fontStack, "important");
    line.style.setProperty("font-size", "26px", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("line-height", "1.05", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("z-index", "86", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("pointer-events", "none", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("visibility", "visible", "important");
    line.style.setProperty("opacity", "1", "important");
    return line;
  }

  const scoreClone = buildScoreClone(scoreEl);
  overlay.appendChild(scoreClone);
  softHide(scoreEl);

  const narrowFactor = clamp(Math.min(widthFactor, heightFactor, cardScale * 1.02), 0.48, 1);
  const twoPlayerMetaFactor = isTwoPlayerCompact
    ? (panelRect.width <= 240 ? 0.70 : panelRect.width <= 280 ? 0.76 : panelRect.width <= 320 ? 0.82 : panelRect.width <= 380 ? 0.88 : 0.94)
    : 1;

  // Layout ON v6: a név/átlag 100%-os alapja marad a v5-ben elfogadott méret,
  // de a felső limitet nem fix 180%-nál vágjuk el, hanem a sor tényleges
  // kártyaszélesség-fit számítása állítja meg.
  const onNameControl = clamp(Number(c.LAYOUT_NAME_ROW_SCALE ?? 1), 0.60, 2.60);
  const onAvgControl = clamp(Number(c.LAYOUT_AVG_ROW_SCALE ?? 1), 0.60, 2.60);
  const nameBaseFitFactor = 1.80 * narrowFactor * twoPlayerMetaFactor;
  const avgBaseFitFactor = 1.16 * Math.min(1, narrowFactor + 0.10) * Math.min(1, twoPlayerMetaFactor + 0.06);
  const nameScale = clamp(onNameControl * nameBaseFitFactor, 0.18, 5.20);
  const avgScale = clamp(onAvgControl * avgBaseFitFactor, 0.20, 5.20);

  const metaClone = (metaRow && showName) ? buildMetaClone(metaRow, nameScale, "inline-flex") : null;
  const avgClone = (avgRow && showAvg) ? buildAvgClone(avgRow, avgScale) : null;

  if (metaClone) {
    overlay.appendChild(metaClone);
    softHide(metaRow);
  }
  if (avgClone) {
    overlay.appendChild(avgClone);
    softHide(avgRow);
  }

  const zoneLeft = Math.round(onInfoZone.left || 8);
  const zoneRight = Math.round(onInfoZone.right || (panelRect.width - 8));
  const zoneWidth = Math.max(96, zoneRight - zoneLeft);
  const safeSidePad = isTwoPlayerCompact
    ? clamp(Math.round(panelRect.width * 0.025), 6, 12)
    : clamp(Math.round(panelRect.width * 0.020), 8, 16);
  const maxRowWidth = Math.max(92, zoneWidth - (safeSidePad * 2));

  if (scoreClone) {
    const minScorePx = panelRect.width <= 340 ? 26 : 32;
    const currentPx = parseFloat(scoreClone.style.fontSize) || scorePx;
    const rawWAtCurrent = Math.max(
      1,
      Math.ceil(scoreClone.scrollWidth || 0),
      Math.ceil(scoreClone.getBoundingClientRect().width || 0)
    );
    const widthPerPx = rawWAtCurrent / Math.max(1, currentPx);
    const maxScorePxByWidth = Math.floor(maxRowWidth / Math.max(0.01, widthPerPx));
    const fittedPx = clamp(
      Math.min(userScorePx, maxScorePxByWidth),
      minScorePx,
      userScorePx
    );
    scoreClone.style.setProperty("font-size", `${fittedPx}px`, "important");
    scoreClone.dataset.adCoreMaxAllowedPx = String(maxScorePxByWidth);
    collectLayoutOnWidthFitLimit("score", Math.max(100, (maxScorePxByWidth / Math.max(1, onScoreBasePx)) * 100));
  }

  if (metaClone) {
    const s = fitElementToWidth(metaClone, maxRowWidth, isTwoPlayerCompact ? 0.20 : 0.45, nameScale);
    metaClone.style.setProperty("transform", `translateX(-50%) scale(${s})`, "important");
    const maxAllowed = Number(metaClone.dataset.adCoreMaxAllowedScale || 0);
    if (Number.isFinite(maxAllowed) && maxAllowed > 0) {
      collectLayoutOnWidthFitLimit("name", maxAllowed / Math.max(0.01, nameBaseFitFactor));
    }
  }
  if (avgClone) {
    const s = fitElementToWidth(avgClone, maxRowWidth, isTwoPlayerCompact ? 0.22 : 0.45, avgScale);
    avgClone.style.setProperty("transform", `translateX(-50%) scale(${s})`, "important");
    const maxAllowed = Number(avgClone.dataset.adCoreMaxAllowedScale || 0);
    if (Number.isFinite(maxAllowed) && maxAllowed > 0) {
      collectLayoutOnWidthFitLimit("avg", maxAllowed / Math.max(0.01, avgBaseFitFactor));
    }
  }

  const above = [];
  const below = [];

  if (metaClone) {
    (nameAbove ? above : below).push({ el: metaClone, scale: nameScale, minScale: isTwoPlayerCompact ? 0.20 : 0.45 });
  }
  if (avgClone) {
    (avgAbove ? above : below).push({ el: avgClone, scale: avgScale, minScale: isTwoPlayerCompact ? 0.22 : 0.45 });
  }

  const padTop = clamp(Math.round(panelRect.height * 0.020), 8, 16);
  const gap = clamp(Math.round(panelRect.height * 0.010), 5, 9);
  const sideHistoryMode = !!(onInfoZone && onInfoZone.side && onInfoZone.side !== "none");
  const bottomPad = clamp(Math.round(panelRect.height * 0.025), 8, 18);

  // v4 fullscreen fix:
  // Ha a history oldalra kerül (a full Layout ON kártyán ez a jellemző),
  // akkor a név/átlag/pontszám blokkot nem a history teteje korlátozza.
  // Ilyenkor a blokk a kártya fő info-zónájában függőlegesen középre kerül,
  // a history pedig csak vízszintes helyet vesz el.
  const contentBottom = sideHistoryMode
    ? Math.max(padTop + 54, Math.round(panelRect.height - bottomPad))
    : Math.max(padTop + 54, historyTop - clamp(Math.round(panelRect.height * 0.020), 10, 18));

  let baseAboveHeight = blockHeight(above, 6);
  let baseBelowHeight = blockHeight(below, 6);
  let scoreH = lineHeight(scoreClone);
  let availableBlockH = Math.max(54, contentBottom - padTop);

  let totalBlockH =
    baseAboveHeight +
    (above.length ? gap : 0) +
    scoreH +
    (below.length ? gap : 0) +
    baseBelowHeight;

  if (totalBlockH > availableBlockH) {
    let guard = 0;
    while (totalBlockH > availableBlockH && guard < 10) {
      const currentPx = parseFloat(scoreClone.style.fontSize) || scorePx;
      const newPx = clamp(
        Math.floor(currentPx * 0.90),
        panelRect.width <= 340 ? 22 : 28,
        currentPx
      );
      if (newPx < currentPx) {
        scoreClone.style.setProperty("font-size", `${newPx}px`, "important");
      }

      for (const entry of [...above, ...below]) {
        const el = entry.el || entry;
        const cur = Number(el.dataset.adCoreFittedScale || entry.scale || 1) || 1;
        const next = clamp(cur * 0.96, entry.minScale || 0.20, entry.scale || cur);
        el.dataset.adCoreFittedScale = String(next);
        el.style.setProperty("transform", `translateX(-50%) scale(${next})`, "important");
      }

      baseAboveHeight = blockHeight(above, 5);
      baseBelowHeight = blockHeight(below, 5);
      scoreH = lineHeight(scoreClone);
      totalBlockH =
        baseAboveHeight +
        (above.length ? gap : 0) +
        scoreH +
        (below.length ? gap : 0) +
        baseBelowHeight;

      if (newPx >= currentPx) break;
      guard += 1;
    }
  }

  // A teljes blokk szemre a kijelölt fő info-terület közepére kerül.
  const blockTop = Math.round(
    padTop + Math.max(0, (availableBlockH - totalBlockH) / 2)
  );

  let scoreTop = blockTop + baseAboveHeight + (above.length ? gap : 0);
  scoreTop = clamp(
    scoreTop,
    padTop,
    Math.max(padTop, contentBottom - scoreH - (below.length ? baseBelowHeight + gap : 0))
  );

  scoreClone.style.setProperty("top", `${Math.round(scoreTop)}px`, "important");

  scoreH = lineHeight(scoreClone);
  const scoreBottom = scoreTop + scoreH;

  const aboveZoneTop = blockTop;
  const aboveZoneBottom = Math.max(aboveZoneTop + 1, scoreTop - gap);
  const belowZoneTop = scoreBottom + gap;
  const belowZoneBottom = Math.max(belowZoneTop + 1, contentBottom);

  placeRowsBottomAnchored(above, aboveZoneTop, aboveZoneBottom, 5);
  const belowEnd = placeRowsTopAnchored(below, belowZoneTop, belowZoneBottom, 5);

  let infoBottom = scoreBottom;
  for (const entry of below) {
    const top = parseFloat(entry.el.style.top) || 0;
    infoBottom = Math.max(infoBottom, top + lineHeight(entry.el));
  }
  if (belowEnd) infoBottom = Math.max(infoBottom, belowEnd);

  // Layout ON: a history következő frissítése ezt az alsó határt használja,
  // így a név/átlag sor nem csúszik rá a history boxra.
  panel.dataset.adCoreInfoBottom = String(Math.round(infoBottom + clamp(Math.round(panelRect.height * 0.018), 8, 16)));
  panel.dataset.adCoreOnInfoZoneCenter = String(Math.round(centerX));
  panel.dataset.adCoreOnInfoZoneWidth = String(Math.round(maxRowWidth));
}

function getVisiblePlayerPanels() {
  const host = document.querySelector("#ad-ext-player-display");
  if (!host) return [];

  return Array.from(host.children)
    .filter((el) => {
      if (!el || el.nodeType !== 1) return false;
      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      return r.width > 80 && r.height > 120;
    })
    .sort((a, b) => a.getBoundingClientRect().left - b.getBoundingClientRect().left);
}

function getVisibleGlobalChalkboards() {
  return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(".css-1u90hiz"))
    .filter((el) => {
      if (!el || !el.isConnected) return false;
      const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
      if (cs.display === "none" || cs.visibility === "hidden") return false;
      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      return r.width > 80 && r.height > 80;
    })
    .sort((a, b) => a.getBoundingClientRect().left - b.getBoundingClientRect().left);
}

function rememberGlobalBoardStyle(el) {
  if (!el) return;
  if (!("adCoreBoardOrigPosition" in el.dataset)) el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigPosition = el.style.position || "";
  if (!("adCoreBoardOrigLeft" in el.dataset)) el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigLeft = el.style.left || "";
  if (!("adCoreBoardOrigTop" in el.dataset)) el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigTop = el.style.top || "";
  if (!("adCoreBoardOrigRight" in el.dataset)) el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigRight = el.style.right || "";
  if (!("adCoreBoardOrigBottom" in el.dataset)) el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigBottom = el.style.bottom || "";
  if (!("adCoreBoardOrigWidth" in el.dataset)) el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigWidth = el.style.width || "";
  if (!("adCoreBoardOrigHeight" in el.dataset)) el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigHeight = el.style.height || "";
  if (!("adCoreBoardOrigTransform" in el.dataset)) el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigTransform = el.style.transform || "";
}

function clearOneGlobalChalkboardAutoFit(board) {
  if (!board) return;
  if (board.dataset.adCoreBoardGlobalFit !== "1") return;

  board.style.position = board.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigPosition || "";
  board.style.left = board.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigLeft || "";
  board.style.top = board.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigTop || "";
  board.style.right = board.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigRight || "";
  board.style.bottom = board.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigBottom || "";
  board.style.width = board.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigWidth || "";
  board.style.height = board.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigHeight || "";
  board.style.transform = board.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigTransform || "";

  delete board.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigPosition;
  delete board.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigLeft;
  delete board.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigTop;
  delete board.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigRight;
  delete board.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigBottom;
  delete board.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigWidth;
  delete board.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigHeight;
  delete board.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigTransform;
  delete board.dataset.adCoreBoardGlobalFit;
}

function clearGlobalChalkboardAutoFit() {
  document
    .querySelectorAll('.css-1u90hiz[data-ad-core-board-global-fit="1"]')
    .forEach(clearOneGlobalChalkboardAutoFit);
}

function fitOneGlobalChalkboardToPanel(board, panel) {
  if (!board || !panel) return;

  const panelRect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
  if (panelRect.width < 80 || panelRect.height < 120) return;

  const prev = {
    position: board.style.position,
    left: board.style.left,
    top: board.style.top,
    width: board.style.width,
    height: board.style.height,
    maxWidth: board.style.maxWidth,
    maxHeight: board.style.maxHeight,
    zIndex: board.style.zIndex,
  };

  // ideiglenesen natív állapotban mérünk
  board.style.position = "";
  board.style.left = "";
  board.style.top = "";
  board.style.width = "";
  board.style.height = "";
  board.style.maxWidth = "";
  board.style.maxHeight = "";
  board.style.zIndex = "";

  const nativeRect = board.getBoundingClientRect();

  const nativeTop = Math.round(nativeRect.top - panelRect.top);
  const nativeWidth = nativeRect.width;
  const nativeHeight = nativeRect.height;

  if (nativeWidth < 20 || nativeHeight < 40) {
    board.style.position = prev.position;
    board.style.left = prev.left;
    board.style.top = prev.top;
    board.style.width = prev.width;
    board.style.height = prev.height;
    board.style.maxWidth = prev.maxWidth;
    board.style.maxHeight = prev.maxHeight;
    board.style.zIndex = prev.zIndex;
    return;
  }

  const ratio = 0.43;

  const sideGap = 18;
  const bottomGap = 18;

  const freeTop = Math.max(12, nativeTop);
  const freeBottom = Math.round(panelRect.height - bottomGap);
  const freeHeight = freeBottom - freeTop;

  if (freeHeight < 120) {
    board.style.position = prev.position;
    board.style.left = prev.left;
    board.style.top = prev.top;
    board.style.width = prev.width;
    board.style.height = prev.height;
    board.style.maxWidth = prev.maxWidth;
    board.style.maxHeight = prev.maxHeight;
    board.style.zIndex = prev.zIndex;
    return;
  }

  const maxWidth = Math.max(80, panelRect.width - sideGap * 2);

  let targetHeight = freeHeight;
  let targetWidth = Math.round(targetHeight * ratio);

  if (targetWidth > maxWidth) {
    targetWidth = maxWidth;
    targetHeight = Math.round(targetWidth / ratio);
  }

  const left = Math.round(panelRect.left + (panelRect.width - targetWidth) / 2);
  const top = Math.round(panelRect.top + freeTop + Math.max(0, (freeHeight - targetHeight) / 2));

  board.style.position = "fixed";
  board.style.left = left + "px";
  board.style.top = top + "px";
  board.style.width = targetWidth + "px";
  board.style.height = targetHeight + "px";
  board.style.maxWidth = "none";
  board.style.maxHeight = "none";
  board.style.zIndex = "6";

  board.dataset.adAutoFit = "1";
}

function fitGlobalChalkboardToPanel() {
  const c = cfg();
  if (!c.LAYOUT_ENABLED) {
    clearGlobalChalkboardAutoFit();
    return;
  }

  const panels = getVisiblePlayerPanels();
  const boards = getVisibleGlobalChalkboards();

  if (!panels.length || !boards.length) {
    clearGlobalChalkboardAutoFit();
    return;
  }

  const freePanels = new Set(panels);
  const usedBoards = new Set();

  const centerX = (el) => {
    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    return r.left + (r.width / 2);
  };

  for (const board of boards) {
    let bestPanel = null;
    let bestDist = Infinity;

    for (const panel of freePanels) {
      const dist = Math.abs(centerX(board) - centerX(panel));
      if (dist < bestDist) {
        bestDist = dist;
        bestPanel = panel;
      }
    }

    if (bestPanel) {
      fitOneGlobalChalkboardToPanel(board, bestPanel);
      freePanels.delete(bestPanel);
      usedBoards.add(board);
    }
  }

  boards.forEach((board) => {
    if (!usedBoards.has(board)) {
      clearOneGlobalChalkboardAutoFit(board);
    }
  });
}

function clearAutoBoardStyle(root) {
  if (!root || !root.querySelectorAll) return;

  root.querySelectorAll(".ad-core-auto-board").forEach((el) => {
    if ("adCoreBoardOrigPosition" in el.dataset) {
      el.style.position = el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigPosition;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigPosition;
    }
    if ("adCoreBoardOrigLeft" in el.dataset) {
      el.style.left = el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigLeft;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigLeft;
    }
    if ("adCoreBoardOrigTop" in el.dataset) {
      el.style.top = el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigTop;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigTop;
    }
    if ("adCoreBoardOrigBottom" in el.dataset) {
      el.style.bottom = el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigBottom;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigBottom;
    }
    if ("adCoreBoardOrigHeight" in el.dataset) {
      el.style.height = el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigHeight;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigHeight;
    }
    if ("adCoreBoardOrigTransform" in el.dataset) {
      el.style.transform = el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigTransform;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigTransform;
    }

    el.classList.remove("ad-core-auto-board");
  });
}

function rememberBoardInlineStyle(el) {
  if (!("adCoreBoardOrigPosition" in el.dataset)) el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigPosition = el.style.position || "";
  if (!("adCoreBoardOrigLeft" in el.dataset)) el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigLeft = el.style.left || "";
  if (!("adCoreBoardOrigTop" in el.dataset)) el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigTop = el.style.top || "";
  if (!("adCoreBoardOrigBottom" in el.dataset)) el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigBottom = el.style.bottom || "";
  if (!("adCoreBoardOrigHeight" in el.dataset)) el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigHeight = el.style.height || "";
  if (!("adCoreBoardOrigTransform" in el.dataset)) el.dataset.adCoreBoardOrigTransform = el.style.transform || "";
}

function playerCardPickBoardCandidateFromRoot(root, panel, blockedEls) {
  if (!root || !panel) return null;

  const panelRect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
  const blocked = (blockedEls || []).filter(Boolean);

  const numericLeaves = playerCardTextLeaves(root).filter((el) => {
    if (!el || !el.isConnected) return false;
    if (blocked.some((b) => b === el || b.contains(el) || el.contains(b))) return false;
    if (el.closest(".ad-ext-turn-throw, .ad-total-cell, .ad-total-overlay")) return false;

    const txt = playerCardTextNorm(el.textContent);
    if (!/^\d{1,4}$/.test(txt)) return false;

    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (r.top < panelRect.top + panelRect.height * 0.34) return false;

    return true;
  });

  let best = null;
  let bestScore = -1e9;

  for (const leaf of numericLeaves) {
    let cand = leaf;
    let n = leaf;

    while (n && n.parentElement && n !== root) {
      const p = n.parentElement;
      if (!p || p === root) break;
      if (blocked.some((b) => b === p || b.contains(p) || p.contains(b))) break;

      const r = p.getBoundingClientRect();

      if (r.width > panelRect.width * 0.90) break;
      if (r.height > panelRect.height * 0.82) break;
      if (r.top < panelRect.top + panelRect.height * 0.22) break;

      cand = p;
      n = p;
    }

    const r = cand.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (r.width < 110 || r.height < 120) continue;

    const panelCenterX = panelRect.left + panelRect.width / 2;
    const candCenterX = r.left + r.width / 2;

    const lowerBonus = Math.max(0, r.top - (panelRect.top + panelRect.height * 0.34));
    const sizeBonus = Math.min(r.height, 420) * 0.55;
    const centerPenalty = Math.abs(candCenterX - panelCenterX) * 0.35;

    const score = lowerBonus + sizeBonus - centerPenalty;

    if (score > bestScore) {
      bestScore = score;
      best = cand;
    }
  }

  return best;
}

function playerCardPickBoardEl(panel, scoreEl, metaRow, avgRow) {
  const playerRoot =
    panel.querySelector(".ad-ext-player") ||
    panel.querySelector(".css-17xejub") ||
    panel.querySelector(".css-1qox1u6");

  const blocked = [scoreEl, metaRow, avgRow].filter(Boolean);

  return (
    playerCardPickBoardCandidateFromRoot(playerRoot || panel, panel, blocked) ||
    playerCardPickBoardCandidateFromRoot(panel, panel, blocked) ||
    null
  );
}

function autoFitPlayerBoard(panel, scoreEl, metaRow, avgRow) {
  if (!panel || !scoreEl) return;

  clearAutoBoardStyle(panel);

  const boardEl = playerCardPickBoardEl(panel, scoreEl, metaRow, avgRow);
  if (!boardEl) return;

  const panelRect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();

  const usedBottom = [scoreEl, metaRow, avgRow]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .reduce((max, el) => {
      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      return Math.max(max, r.bottom - panelRect.top);
    }, 0);

  const GAP_TOP = 18;
  const GAP_BOTTOM = 22;
  const MIN_HEIGHT = 140;

  let top = Math.round(usedBottom + GAP_TOP);
  let height = Math.round(panelRect.height - top - GAP_BOTTOM);

  if (height < MIN_HEIGHT) {
    height = MIN_HEIGHT;
    top = Math.max(10, Math.round(panelRect.height - GAP_BOTTOM - height));
  }

  rememberBoardInlineStyle(boardEl);
  boardEl.classList.add("ad-core-auto-board");

  boardEl.style.position = "absolute";
  boardEl.style.left = "50%";
  boardEl.style.transform = "translateX(-50%)";
  boardEl.style.top = `${top}px`;
  boardEl.style.bottom = "auto";
  boardEl.style.height = `${height}px`;
}

function annotatePlayerCardInfo() {
  const c = cfg();
  const host = document.querySelector("#ad-ext-player-display");

  const CLEAR_CLASSES = [
    "ad-core-player-name",
    "ad-core-player-name-row",
    "ad-core-player-avg",
    "ad-core-player-avg-row",
    "ad-core-player-score",
    "ad-core-player-meta-wrap",
    "ad-ext-zone-meta-inner",
    "ad-core-zone-avatar",
    "ad-core-zone-score",
    "ad-core-zone-meta",
    "ad-core-zone-meta-block",
    "ad-core-keep-avatar-on-name-hide",
    "ad-core-keep-avatar-main",
    "ad-core-keep-flag-on-name-hide",
    "ad-core-visible-meta-row",
    "ad-core-visible-avg-row",
  ];

  function removeSyntheticZones(root) {
    if (!root) return;
    root.querySelectorAll(".ad-core-zone-name, .ad-core-zone-avg, .ad-core-zone-meta-block").forEach((el) => el.remove());
  }

  function clearManagedInlineStyle(el) {
    if (!el || !el.style) return;
    [
      "position",
      "left",
      "right",
      "top",
      "bottom",
      "transform",
      "margin",
      "width",
      "max-width",
      "display",
      "text-align",
      "pointer-events",
      "visibility",
      "transform-origin",
      "font-family",
      "z-index",
      "line-height",
      "white-space",
      "padding",
      "opacity"
    ].forEach((prop) => el.style.removeProperty(prop));
  }

  function restoreOverlayManagedOriginals(root) {
    if (!root) return;

    root.querySelectorAll(".ad-core-player-top-overlay").forEach((el) => el.remove());

    root.querySelectorAll("[data-ad-core-overlay-hidden='1'], [data-ad-core-extra-hidden='1']").forEach((el) => {
      el.style.removeProperty("display");
      el.style.removeProperty("visibility");
      el.style.removeProperty("pointer-events");
      el.style.removeProperty("opacity");
      delete el.dataset.adCoreOverlayHidden;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreExtraHidden;
    });

    if (root.dataset && root.dataset.adCoreInfoBottom) {
      delete root.dataset.adCoreInfoBottom;
    }
  }

  function restoreLikelyManagedHidden(root) {
    if (!root || !root.querySelectorAll) return;

    root.querySelectorAll("[style]").forEach((el) => {
      if (!el || !el.isConnected) return;
      if (el.closest(".ad-native-history-floating")) return;
      if (el.closest(".ad-ext-turn-throw")) return;
      if (el.closest(".ad-total-cell")) return;
      if (el.closest(".ad-total-overlay")) return;
      if (el.closest("button, a, input, select, textarea")) return;
      if (el.dataset?.adTotalHidden === "1") return;
      if (el.dataset?.adHistoryHidden === "1") return;

      const hasInlineHidden =
        el.style.display === "none" ||
        el.style.visibility === "hidden" ||
        el.style.opacity === "0";

      if (!hasInlineHidden) return;

      const txt = playerCardTextNorm(el.textContent);
      const hasAvatarish =
        !!el.querySelector?.(".chakra-avatar, [data-part='root'].chakra-avatar, img, svg");

      if (!txt && !hasAvatarish) return;
      if (txt && txt.length > 120) return;

      el.style.removeProperty("display");
      el.style.removeProperty("visibility");
      el.style.removeProperty("opacity");
      el.style.removeProperty("pointer-events");
    });
  }

  function clearAdCoreClasses(root) {
    if (!root) return;

    restoreOverlayManagedOriginals(root);
    restoreOffNameBandHidden(root);
    restoreLikelyManagedHidden(root);

    root.querySelectorAll(
      ".ad-core-visible-meta-row, .ad-core-visible-avg-row, .ad-core-player-score"
    ).forEach((el) => {
      clearManagedInlineStyle(el);
    });

    root.querySelectorAll(
      ".ad-core-player-name, .ad-core-player-name-row, .ad-core-player-avg, .ad-core-player-avg-row, .ad-core-player-score, .ad-core-player-meta-wrap, .ad-ext-zone-meta-inner, .ad-core-zone-avatar, .ad-core-zone-score, .ad-core-zone-meta, .ad-core-zone-meta-block, .ad-core-keep-avatar-on-name-hide, .ad-core-keep-avatar-main, .ad-core-keep-flag-on-name-hide, .ad-core-visible-meta-row, .ad-core-visible-avg-row"
    ).forEach((el) => {
      CLEAR_CLASSES.forEach((cls) => el.classList.remove(cls));
    });
  }

  function isUsableRow(el) {
    if (!el || !el.isConnected) return false;
    const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
    if (cs.display === "none" || cs.visibility === "hidden") return false;
    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    return r.width >= 8 && r.height >= 8;
  }

  function rowHasStats(el) {
    if (!el) return false;
    const txt = playerCardTextNorm(el.textContent);
    if (!txt) return false;
    if (playerCardLooksLikeStatsLine(txt)) return true;

    const leaves = playerCardTextLeaves(el)
      .map((x) => playerCardTextNorm(x.textContent))
      .filter(Boolean);

    return leaves.some(playerCardLooksLikeStatsLine);
  }

  if (!host) {
    return;
  }

  if (!c.LAYOUT_ENABLED) {
    clearAutoBoardStyle(host);
  }

  const panels = Array.from(host.children).filter((n) => n && n.nodeType === 1);

  for (const panel of panels) {
    removeSyntheticZones(panel);
    clearAdCoreClasses(panel);

    const playerRoot =
      panel.querySelector(".ad-ext-player") ||
      panel.querySelector(".css-17xejub") ||
      panel.querySelector(".css-1qox1u6") ||
      panel;

    const scoreEl = playerCardPickMainScoreEl(panel);
    if (!scoreEl) continue;

    scoreEl.classList.add("ad-core-player-score");

    const avgLeaf = playerCardPickAvgLeaf(playerRoot);
    const nameLeaf = playerCardPickNameLeaf(playerRoot, avgLeaf, scoreEl);

    if (nameLeaf) nameLeaf.classList.add("ad-core-player-name");
    if (avgLeaf) avgLeaf.classList.add("ad-core-player-avg");

    const avNode =
      panel.querySelector(".chakra-avatar") ||
      panel.querySelector('[data-part="root"].chakra-avatar') ||
      panel.querySelector("div.chakra-stack.css-1psdi5l") ||
      panel.querySelector("img.chakra-image.css-6t0bzd");

    const zAv = avNode
      ? (playerCardPickAvatarKeepBlock(avNode, playerRoot) || playerCardDirectChild(avNode, playerRoot) || avNode)
      : null;

    const nameChipRow = nameLeaf ? playerCardPickChipRowFromLeaf(nameLeaf, playerRoot) : null;
    const offMetaLca =
      !c.LAYOUT_ENABLED && nameChipRow && zAv
        ? playerCardCommonAncestorWithinRoot(nameChipRow, zAv, playerRoot)
        : null;

    if (avNode) avNode.classList.add("ad-core-keep-avatar-on-name-hide");
    if (zAv) zAv.classList.add("ad-core-zone-avatar", "ad-core-keep-avatar-on-name-hide");
    markAvatarKeepElements(zAv || avNode);

    const pickCleanMetaRow = () => {
  const chipRow = nameLeaf ? playerCardPickChipRowFromLeaf(nameLeaf, playerRoot) : null;
  const offNameBand =
    !c.LAYOUT_ENABLED && chipRow
      ? playerCardPickOffNameBandFromChipRow(chipRow, playerRoot)
      : null;

  return (
    offNameBand ||
    (!c.LAYOUT_ENABLED ? playerCardPickNativeMetaRow(nameLeaf, avNode, playerRoot) : null) ||
    playerCardPickLiveNameRow(nameLeaf, playerRoot) ||
    chipRow ||
    (nameLeaf ? playerCardPickCompactRow(nameLeaf, playerRoot) : null) ||
    playerCardPickCompactRow(zAv || avNode, playerRoot) ||
    (nameLeaf ? playerCardPickTopNonStatsAncestor(nameLeaf, playerRoot) : null) ||
    (nameLeaf ? playerCardDirectChild(nameLeaf, playerRoot) : null)
  );
};

    const pickCleanAvgRow = () =>
      playerCardPickStatsRowFromLeaf(avgLeaf, playerRoot, nameLeaf) ||
      (avgLeaf ? playerCardPickCompactRow(avgLeaf, playerRoot) : null) ||
      (avgLeaf ? playerCardDirectChild(avgLeaf, playerRoot) : null);

    let metaRow = pickCleanMetaRow();
    let avgRow = pickCleanAvgRow();

    if (metaRow && rowHasStats(metaRow)) {
      const cleanMeta =
        ((!c.LAYOUT_ENABLED && offMetaLca && !rowHasStats(offMetaLca)) ? offMetaLca : null) ||
        (!c.LAYOUT_ENABLED ? playerCardPickNativeMetaRow(nameLeaf, zAv || avNode, playerRoot) : null) ||
        playerCardPickLiveNameRow(nameLeaf, playerRoot) ||
        nameChipRow ||
        (nameLeaf ? playerCardDirectChild(nameLeaf, playerRoot) : null);

      if (cleanMeta && cleanMeta !== metaRow) {
        metaRow = cleanMeta;
      }
    }

    if (avgRow && nameLeaf && avgRow.contains(nameLeaf)) {
      avgRow = null;
    }

    if (metaRow && avgRow) {
      if (metaRow === avgRow || metaRow.contains(avgRow) || avgRow.contains(metaRow)) {
        const cleanMeta = pickCleanMetaRow();
        const cleanAvg = pickCleanAvgRow();

        if (cleanMeta && cleanMeta !== cleanAvg) metaRow = cleanMeta;
        if (cleanAvg && cleanAvg !== metaRow) avgRow = cleanAvg;
      }
    }

    if (!isUsableRow(metaRow)) metaRow = null;
    if (!isUsableRow(avgRow)) avgRow = null;

    if (metaRow) metaRow.classList.add("ad-core-player-name-row");
    if (avgRow) avgRow.classList.add("ad-core-player-avg-row");
    if (metaRow) metaRow.classList.add("ad-core-visible-meta-row");
    if (avgRow) avgRow.classList.add("ad-core-visible-avg-row");

    applyOffNameBandHide(playerRoot, scoreEl, nameLeaf, avgLeaf, metaRow);

    applyPlayerMetaPlacement(panel, scoreEl, metaRow, avgRow);
  }

  forceOffLayoutDirectOverlayV15();
}


function forceOffLayoutDirectOverlayV15() {
  // v44: ez a függvény régen egy clone-overlay-t (ad-core-off-v40-overlay) készített
  // a player kártya tetejére. Az új natív mért layout (applyOffNativeMeasuredLayout)
  // ezt a réteget feleslegessé teszi, sőt az overlay-ek többszöröződése korábban a
  // kártya magasságát is 0..4 px-re tudta összeomlasztani. Ezért itt már csak
  // takarítunk: kivesszük a régi overlay DOM-ot és visszaoldjuk a régi rejtéseket.
  const host = document.querySelector("#ad-ext-player-display");
  if (!host) return;

  host.querySelectorAll(
    ".ad-core-off-v15-overlay, .ad-core-off-v40-overlay, " +
    ".ad-core-player-top-overlay-off-v43, .ad-core-off-v12-overlay"
  ).forEach((el) => {
    try { el.remove(); } catch (_) {}
  });

  host.querySelectorAll(
    "[data-ad-core-off-v15-hidden='1'], " +
    "[data-ad-core-off-v40-hidden='1'], " +
    "[data-ad-core-off-v12-hidden='1']"
  ).forEach((el) => {
    try {
      el.style.removeProperty("visibility");
      el.style.removeProperty("opacity");
      el.style.removeProperty("pointer-events");
      el.style.removeProperty("display");
      delete el.dataset.adCoreOffV15Hidden;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreOffV40Hidden;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreOffV12Hidden;
    } catch (_) {}
  });
}


/* =========================================================================
   v53 célzott history-duplikáció javítás
   =========================================================================
   A v49-es globális CSS-felülírás visszavonása:
     - kétjátékos lowh/ultralow nézetben újra a natív Autodarts-szabály
       rejti a `.ad-ext-player` alatti másodlagos history-példányt;
     - nincs DOM-mozgatás, nincs remove(), nincs Layout ON-specifikus
       JavaScript-módosítás;
     - a v52 OFF fallback/logika változatlan marad.
   ========================================================================= */

/* =========================================================================
   v52 OFF native measured layout
   =========================================================================
   BUG-JAVÍTÁS a v50/v51-hez képest: DUPLIKÁLT HISTORY kis felbontáson

   A CSS-ből (1365. sor) kiderült, hogy az Autodarts a history-t felbontás
   szerint kétféle DOM-helyre teszi:
     - nagy felbontáson: a `.ad-ext-player`-en belül
       (> div > .ad-ext-player > .css-1qox1u6)
     - kis felbontáson: a panel direkt gyermekeként
       (> div > .css-1qox1u6)
   Kis felbontáson néha MINDKÉT példány jelen van a DOM-ban. Az eredeti
   `vp-lowh` CSS a player-belit visibility: hidden-re rejtette, így csak a
   panel-szintű látszott. A v49-ben no-op-ra állítottam ezt a CSS-t → mindkét
   history látszik → DUPLIKÁCIÓ.

   A v52 megoldás:
     - NEM remove()-ol semmilyen history-t (a v51 remove-ja React-crash
       veszélyes lett volna a natív panel-szintű history-n).
     - Csak display: none + visibility: hidden a NEM választott history-
       példányokra (React-biztos).
     - A választott (látható + stilizált) history: a `.ad-ext-player`-beli,
       ha van; egyébként a panel-szintű.
     - A calc-eltolás a választott history TÉNYLEGES offsetParent-jéhez
       viszonyít (lehet player vagy panel), így mindkét helyen jól pozícionál.

   Egyéb változatlan a v50-ből:
     - NINCS panel.appendChild DOM-mozgatás (React-crash elkerülése)
     - playerRoot overflow: visible, panel overflow: hidden
     - diagnosztikai console.log + window.__AD_CORE_VERSION__
     - history-méret kompakt panelnél (180-220 px) + dinamikus font
     - köztes wrapperek csak width-stabilizálás (csík bug ellen)
     - name / avg sor transform: scale-el
     - layout magasság-kalkuláció: naturalH × scale
     - auto-scroll bottom history-ban új sor érkezésekor
========================================================================= */

function applyOffNativeMeasuredLayout(panel) {
  if (!panel || !(panel instanceof HTMLElement)) return;

  const c = cfg();
  if (c.LAYOUT_ENABLED) return;

  // ------ 1) Régi overlay-ek takarítása ------------------------------
  panel.querySelectorAll(
    ":scope > .ad-core-off-v12-overlay, " +
    ":scope > .ad-core-off-v15-overlay, " +
    ":scope > .ad-core-off-v40-overlay, " +
    ":scope > .ad-core-player-top-overlay-off-v43, " +
    ":scope > .ad-core-player-top-overlay"
  ).forEach((el) => { try { el.remove(); } catch (_) {} });

  panel.querySelectorAll(
    "[data-ad-core-off-v12-hidden='1'], " +
    "[data-ad-core-off-v15-hidden='1'], " +
    "[data-ad-core-off-v40-hidden='1'], " +
    "[data-ad-core-overlay-hidden='1'], " +
    "[data-ad-core-extra-hidden='1']"
  ).forEach((el) => {
    try {
      el.style.removeProperty("visibility");
      el.style.removeProperty("opacity");
      el.style.removeProperty("pointer-events");
      el.style.removeProperty("display");
      delete el.dataset.adCoreOffV12Hidden;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreOffV15Hidden;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreOffV40Hidden;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreOverlayHidden;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreExtraHidden;
    } catch (_) {}
  });

  // ------ 1.5) DUPLIKÁLT HISTORY-elemek kezelése (display: none) -----
  // v52: az Autodarts a history-t felbontás szerint kétféle helyre teszi:
  //   - nagy felbontáson: a `.ad-ext-player`-en belül
  //   - kis felbontáson: a panel direkt gyermekeként (> div > .css-1qox1u6)
  // Kis felbontáson néha MINDKETTŐ jelen van a DOM-ban, és duplikálódik a
  // history. A v49-ben no-op-ra állított vp-lowh CSS miatt ez most látszik.
  //
  // FONTOS: NEM remove()-oljuk a duplikátumot (azt a React kezelheti →
  // DOMException crash). Csak display: none-nal rejtjük el a NEM választott
  // példányt. A választott (látható) history: a `.ad-ext-player`-beli, ha
  // van; egyébként a panel-szintű.
  let chosenHistory = null;
  try {
    const playerRootForHist = panel.querySelector(".ad-ext-player");
    const playerHist = playerRootForHist
      ? playerRootForHist.querySelector(".css-1qox1u6")
      : null;
    const panelLevelHist = panel.querySelector(":scope > .css-1qox1u6");
    chosenHistory = playerHist || panelLevelHist || null;

    // Minden NEM választott history-példányt elrejtünk (display: none).
    panel.querySelectorAll(".css-1qox1u6").forEach((el) => {
      if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
      if (el === chosenHistory) return;
      setImp(el, "display", "none");
      setImp(el, "visibility", "hidden");
      setImp(el, "opacity", "0");
      setImp(el, "pointer-events", "none");
    });
  } catch (_) {}

  // ------ 2) Panel mérés + alapstabilizálás --------------------------
  const panelRect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
  if (panelRect.width < 120 || panelRect.height < 80) return;

  const playerRoot = panel.querySelector(".ad-ext-player");
  if (!(playerRoot instanceof HTMLElement)) return;

  function setImp(el, prop, val) {
    if (el && el.style) el.style.setProperty(prop, val, "important");
  }

  if (getComputedStyle(panel).position === "static") {
    setImp(panel, "position", "relative");
  }
  setImp(panel, "overflow", "hidden");
  setImp(panel, "padding", "0");

  // KÖZTES WRAPPEREK (.css-rtn29s, .css-hjw8x4 stb.) szélesség-stabilizálás:
  // ezek a panel és a .ad-ext-player között lévő Chakra wrapperek. CSAK
  // szélesség + padding (NEM HEIGHT), hogy a frissítés utáni init állapotban
  // a Chakra szülő explicit magasság hiányában a `height: 100%` ne essen 0-ra
  // és ne omlassza össze a kártyát egy csíkká.
  {
    let cur = playerRoot.parentElement;
    let guard = 0;
    while (cur && cur !== panel && guard < 10) {
      setImp(cur, "width", "100%");
      setImp(cur, "max-width", "100%");
      setImp(cur, "min-width", "0");
      setImp(cur, "padding", "0");
      setImp(cur, "margin", "0");
      setImp(cur, "box-sizing", "border-box");
      // NEM állítjuk: height, min-height, max-height, position, overflow
      // — ezeket hagyjuk a Chakra-nak, hogy az init állapotban se omoljon össze.
      cur = cur.parentElement;
      guard++;
    }
  }

  setImp(playerRoot, "position", "relative");
  setImp(playerRoot, "width", "100%");
  setImp(playerRoot, "height", "100%");
  setImp(playerRoot, "min-height", "0");
  setImp(playerRoot, "max-height", "100%");
  setImp(playerRoot, "overflow", "visible");  // v50: a calc-eltolt history kívülre nyúlhat a `.ad-ext-player`-en — a panel hidden levágja
  setImp(playerRoot, "box-sizing", "border-box");
  setImp(playerRoot, "padding", "0");
  setImp(playerRoot, "margin", "0");

  // ------ 3) Natív elemek megtalálása --------------------------------
  const nativeStack =
    playerRoot.querySelector(":scope > .css-y3hfdd") ||
    playerRoot.querySelector(".css-y3hfdd");

  const scoreEl =
    playerRoot.querySelector(".ad-ext-player-score") ||
    playerRoot.querySelector(".ad-core-player-score");

  const scoreBand = nativeStack
    ? (nativeStack.querySelector(":scope > .css-xsngok") ||
       nativeStack.querySelector(".css-xsngok"))
    : (scoreEl ? scoreEl.closest(".css-xsngok") : null);

  const nameBand = nativeStack
    ? (nativeStack.querySelector(":scope > .css-37hv00") ||
       nativeStack.querySelector(".css-37hv00"))
    : null;

  const avgBand = nativeStack
    ? (nativeStack.querySelector(":scope > .css-1igwmid") ||
       nativeStack.querySelector(".css-1igwmid"))
    : null;

  // v52: a már a takarításban kiválasztott látható history-példányt használjuk
  // (chosenHistory), hogy biztosan ne a rejtett duplikátumot stilizáljuk.
  let history =
    chosenHistory ||
    playerRoot.querySelector(":scope > .css-1qox1u6") ||
    playerRoot.querySelector(".css-1qox1u6") ||
    panel.querySelector(".css-1qox1u6");

  // ------ 4) HISTORY: CALC-ELTOLÁS (NINCS DOM-mozgatás!) -------------
  // v50 KRITIKUS JAVÍTÁS: a v46-v49-ben a panel.appendChild(history) áthelyezte
  // a history-t a panel direkt gyermekévé. EZT NEM SZABAD! A React komponens-
  // fája a history-t a `.ad-ext-player`-ben tartja számon, és amikor legközelebb
  // re-render következik (pl. egy új dobás), megpróbálja onnan kitörölni:
  //   DOMException: Node.removeChild: The node to be removed is not a child...
  // A React komponensfa crash-el → page disappears → cards collapse to stripe.
  //
  // A v50 NEM mozgatja a DOM-ot. A history a `.ad-ext-player`-en belül marad,
  // és a calc-eltolás kompenzálja a Chakra-padding miatti elcsúszást. Ehhez a
  // playerRoot overflow: visible kell, hogy a kifelé nyúló history látható
  // maradjon (a panel overflow: hidden levágja a panel-keretnél).
  let histW = 0;
  if (history instanceof HTMLElement && !history.closest(".ad-core-off-v40-overlay")) {

    // 1. Kompakt panel: nagyobb history-doboz a kis felbontáson.
    const isCompactPanel = panelRect.width < 480;
    const histRatio = isCompactPanel ? 0.40 : 0.28;
    const histMinW  = isCompactPanel ? 180  : 160;
    const histMaxW  = 220;
    histW = Math.round(clamp(panelRect.width * histRatio, histMinW, histMaxW));

    // 2. CALC-ELTOLÁS: a history vagy a `.ad-ext-player`-en belül van, vagy a
    //    panel direkt gyermeke (kis felbontás). Mindkét esetben a tényleges
    //    offsetParent-jéhez viszonyítva számoljuk az eltolást, hogy a látszó
    //    pozíció a panel látható kerethez igazodjon.
    const histOffsetParent = (history.offsetParent instanceof HTMLElement)
      ? history.offsetParent
      : playerRoot;
    const refRect = histOffsetParent.getBoundingClientRect();
    const panelOffsetRight  = panelRect.right  - refRect.right;
    const panelOffsetTop    = refRect.top    - panelRect.top;
    const panelOffsetBottom = panelRect.bottom - refRect.bottom;

    setImp(history, "position", "absolute");
    setImp(history, "right",  `${Math.round(14 - panelOffsetRight)}px`);
    setImp(history, "top",    `${Math.round(14 - panelOffsetTop)}px`);
    setImp(history, "bottom", `${Math.round(14 - panelOffsetBottom)}px`);
    setImp(history, "left", "auto");
    setImp(history, "flex", "0 0 auto");
    setImp(history, "flex-shrink", "0");
    setImp(history, "width", `${histW}px`);
    setImp(history, "min-width", `${histW}px`);
    setImp(history, "max-width", `${histW}px`);
    setImp(history, "height", "auto");
    setImp(history, "min-height", "0");
    setImp(history, "max-height", "none");
    setImp(history, "overflow-y", "auto");
    setImp(history, "overflow-x", "hidden");
    setImp(history, "scrollbar-width", "thin");
    setImp(history, "box-sizing", "border-box");
    setImp(history, "z-index", "6");
    setImp(history, "visibility", "visible");
    setImp(history, "opacity", "1");
    setImp(history, "display", "block");
    setImp(history, "pointer-events", "auto");

    // 3. HISTORY belső font-size DINAMIKUS — a histW alapján, hogy a "501"
    //    típusú 3 jegyű szám mindig elférjen a doboz szélességébe.
    const histFontPx = Math.round(clamp(histW * 0.24, 12, 44));
    try {
      history.querySelectorAll("p, span, div").forEach((el) => {
        if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
        setImp(el, "font-size", `${histFontPx}px`);
        setImp(el, "line-height", "1.1");
      });
    } catch (_) {}

    // 4. Auto-scroll bottom új sor érkezésekor
    try {
      const prevH = Number(history.dataset.adCoreHistoryPrevHeight || 0);
      const curH = history.scrollHeight;
      if (curH > prevH + 2) {
        history.scrollTop = curH;
      }
      history.dataset.adCoreHistoryPrevHeight = String(curH);
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  // ------ 5) Content slot calc ---------------------------------------
  const leftPad   = 18;
  const rightPad  = histW ? (histW + 14 + 14) : 18;
  const topPad    = Math.round(clamp(panelRect.height * 0.075, 12, 18));
  const bottomPad = topPad;
  const usableW   = Math.max(60, panelRect.width  - leftPad  - rightPad);
  const usableH   = Math.max(40, panelRect.height - topPad   - bottomPad);
  const gapPx     = Math.round(clamp(panelRect.height * 0.045, 8, 14));

  // ------ 6) nativeStack: block container (NEM flex), child-jai absolute --
  if (nativeStack instanceof HTMLElement) {
    setImp(nativeStack, "position", "absolute");
    setImp(nativeStack, "inset", "0");
    setImp(nativeStack, "left", "0");
    setImp(nativeStack, "right", "0");
    setImp(nativeStack, "top", "0");
    setImp(nativeStack, "bottom", "0");
    setImp(nativeStack, "width", "100%");
    setImp(nativeStack, "height", "100%");
    setImp(nativeStack, "min-height", "0");
    setImp(nativeStack, "max-height", "100%");
    setImp(nativeStack, "display", "block");
    setImp(nativeStack, "padding", "0");
    setImp(nativeStack, "gap", "0");
    setImp(nativeStack, "overflow", "hidden");
    setImp(nativeStack, "z-index", "4");
    setImp(nativeStack, "visibility", "visible");
    setImp(nativeStack, "opacity", "1");
  }

  // ------ 7) User beállítások (100% = Chakra natural ETALON) ---------
  const showName  = !!c.LAYOUT_SHOW_PLAYER_NAME;
  const showAvg   = !!c.LAYOUT_SHOW_PLAYER_AVG;
  const nameAbove = c.LAYOUT_NAME_PLACEMENT === "above";
  const avgAbove  = c.LAYOUT_AVG_PLACEMENT  === "above";

  const userScorePct  = clamp(Number(c.LAYOUT_SCORE_FONT_PX ?? 100), 40, 260) / 100;
  const userNameScale = clamp(Number(c.LAYOUT_NAME_ROW_SCALE  ?? 1), 0.4, 2.4);
  const userAvgScale  = clamp(Number(c.LAYOUT_AVG_ROW_SCALE   ?? 1), 0.4, 2.4);

  const BASE_SCORE_PX = 92; // 100% etalon score font-size

  // ------ 8) NAME / AVG: TELJES sor transform-skálázás ----------------
  // A child-elemek font-size / avatar-méret BEÁLLÍTÁSÁT NEM piszkáljuk —
  // hagyjuk a Chakra natural értékeket. A teljes wrappert transform: scale-el
  // skálázzuk, így a zöld leg-jelző, avatar, zászló, név, chip mind együtt
  // nő/csökken.
  function prepareRowForScale(rowEl) {
    if (!(rowEl instanceof HTMLElement)) return;

    setImp(rowEl, "display", "inline-flex");
    setImp(rowEl, "flex-direction", "row");
    setImp(rowEl, "align-items", "center");
    setImp(rowEl, "justify-content", "center");
    setImp(rowEl, "flex-wrap", "nowrap");
    setImp(rowEl, "padding", "0");
    setImp(rowEl, "margin", "0");
    setImp(rowEl, "overflow", "visible");
    setImp(rowEl, "white-space", "nowrap");
    setImp(rowEl, "transform-origin", "top center");
    setImp(rowEl, "min-height", "0");

    // Töröljük a korábbi inline child-stiláinkat (v45-ben az itt állított
    // font-size / avatar pixel méretek megmaradhatnak, ezért takarítunk).
    rowEl.querySelectorAll("p, span, div").forEach((el) => {
      if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
      el.style.removeProperty("font-size");
      el.style.removeProperty("font-weight");
      el.style.removeProperty("line-height");
      el.style.removeProperty("white-space");
    });
    rowEl.querySelectorAll(".chakra-avatar, [data-part='root'].chakra-avatar, img, svg").forEach((el) => {
      try {
        el.style.removeProperty("width");
        el.style.removeProperty("height");
        el.style.removeProperty("min-width");
        el.style.removeProperty("min-height");
        el.style.removeProperty("max-width");
        el.style.removeProperty("max-height");
      } catch (_) {}
    });
  }

  // Natural méret mérése a row-on (transform NÉLKÜL)
  function measureRowNatural(rowEl) {
    if (!(rowEl instanceof HTMLElement)) return { w: 0, h: 0 };
    const prevTransform = rowEl.style.getPropertyValue("transform");
    rowEl.style.removeProperty("transform");
    void rowEl.offsetWidth;
    const r = rowEl.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (prevTransform) {
      rowEl.style.setProperty("transform", prevTransform, "important");
    }
    return {
      w: Math.max(8, Math.round(r.width)),
      h: Math.max(8, Math.round(r.height))
    };
  }

  let nameNat = { w: 0, h: 0 };
  let avgNat  = { w: 0, h: 0 };

  if (showName && nameBand) {
    prepareRowForScale(nameBand);
    nameNat = measureRowNatural(nameBand);
  }
  if (showAvg && avgBand) {
    prepareRowForScale(avgBand);
    avgNat = measureRowNatural(avgBand);
  }

  // ------ 9) SCORE font-size dinamikus (figyelembe veszi name/avg sort) --
  // Az effektív magasságot már a user-scale-el számoljuk: ha a user a name-et
  // 50%-ra állítja, a score-nak több hely jut.
  const nameEffScaleForHeight = (showName && nameBand)
    ? Math.min(userNameScale, nameNat.w > 0 ? usableW / nameNat.w : userNameScale)
    : 0;
  const avgEffScaleForHeight = (showAvg && avgBand)
    ? Math.min(userAvgScale, avgNat.w > 0 ? usableW / avgNat.w : userAvgScale)
    : 0;

  const nameScaledH = (showName && nameBand) ? Math.round(nameNat.h * nameEffScaleForHeight) : 0;
  const avgScaledH  = (showAvg  && avgBand)  ? Math.round(avgNat.h  * avgEffScaleForHeight)  : 0;

  const visibleRowCount = 1 + (showName && nameBand ? 1 : 0) + (showAvg && avgBand ? 1 : 0);
  const gapsCount = Math.max(0, visibleRowCount - 1);
  const scoreAvailH = Math.max(28, usableH - nameScaledH - avgScaledH - gapsCount * gapPx);

  const desiredScorePx = Math.max(28, Math.round(BASE_SCORE_PX * userScorePct));

  let finalScorePx = desiredScorePx;
  if (scoreEl instanceof HTMLElement) {
    const txt = (scoreEl.textContent || "0").replace(/\s+/g, "").trim() || "0";
    const charCount = Math.max(1, txt.length);
    const aspectFactor = charCount * 0.62 + 0.10;
    const widthLimitPx  = Math.max(28, Math.floor(usableW / aspectFactor));
    const heightLimitPx = Math.max(28, Math.floor(scoreAvailH * 0.96));
    finalScorePx = Math.max(28, Math.min(desiredScorePx, widthLimitPx, heightLimitPx));

    setImp(scoreEl, "font-size", `${finalScorePx}px`);
    setImp(scoreEl, "line-height", "0.92");
    setImp(scoreEl, "font-weight", "900");
    setImp(scoreEl, "white-space", "nowrap");
    setImp(scoreEl, "margin", "0");
    setImp(scoreEl, "padding", "0");
    setImp(scoreEl, "text-align", "center");
    setImp(scoreEl, "max-width", "100%");
    setImp(scoreEl, "overflow", "visible");
    setImp(scoreEl, "transform", "none");
    setImp(scoreEl, "visibility", "visible");
    setImp(scoreEl, "opacity", "1");

    try {
      panel.dataset.adCoreOffScorePxLimit = String(Math.min(widthLimitPx, heightLimitPx));
      panel.dataset.adCoreOffScoreFinalPx = String(finalScorePx);
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  // ------ 10) SCORE band wrapper -------------------------------------
  if (scoreBand instanceof HTMLElement) {
    setImp(scoreBand, "transform-origin", "top center");
    setImp(scoreBand, "display", "inline-flex");
    setImp(scoreBand, "align-items", "center");
    setImp(scoreBand, "justify-content", "center");
    setImp(scoreBand, "padding", "0");
    setImp(scoreBand, "margin", "0");
    setImp(scoreBand, "overflow", "visible");
    setImp(scoreBand, "white-space", "nowrap");
    setImp(scoreBand, "min-height", "0");
    setImp(scoreBand, "max-width", `${Math.round(usableW)}px`);
    setImp(scoreBand, "visibility", "visible");
    setImp(scoreBand, "opacity", "1");
  }

  void playerRoot.offsetHeight;
  function realHeight(el) {
    if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return 0;
    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    return Math.round(r.height);
  }
  const scoreSlotH = scoreBand ? realHeight(scoreBand) : finalScorePx;

  // ------ 11) Slot order list ----------------------------------------
  const slots = [];
  if (showName && nameBand && nameAbove) {
    slots.push({ el: nameBand, type: "name", baseH: nameNat.h, baseW: nameNat.w, userScale: userNameScale });
  }
  if (showAvg && avgBand && avgAbove) {
    slots.push({ el: avgBand, type: "avg", baseH: avgNat.h, baseW: avgNat.w, userScale: userAvgScale });
  }
  if (scoreBand) {
    slots.push({ el: scoreBand, type: "score", baseH: scoreSlotH, baseW: usableW, userScale: 1 });
  }
  if (showName && nameBand && !nameAbove) {
    slots.push({ el: nameBand, type: "name", baseH: nameNat.h, baseW: nameNat.w, userScale: userNameScale });
  }
  if (showAvg && avgBand && !avgAbove) {
    slots.push({ el: avgBand, type: "avg", baseH: avgNat.h, baseW: avgNat.w, userScale: userAvgScale });
  }

  // ------ 12) Effektív scale és magasság -----------------------------
  for (const slot of slots) {
    if (slot.type === "score") {
      slot.effScale = 1;
      slot.effH = slot.baseH;
    } else {
      const widthFit = slot.baseW > 0 ? usableW / slot.baseW : slot.userScale;
      slot.effScale = clamp(Math.min(slot.userScale, widthFit), 0.3, 2.4);
      slot.effH = Math.round(slot.baseH * slot.effScale);
    }
  }

  // ------ 13) Túllógás esetén közös globalScale ----------------------
  let totalH = slots.reduce((s, x) => s + x.effH, 0);
  if (slots.length > 1) totalH += (slots.length - 1) * gapPx;

  let globalScale = 1;
  let startY;
  if (totalH > usableH) {
    globalScale = clamp(usableH / totalH, 0.4, 1);
    startY = topPad;
  } else {
    startY = topPad + Math.max(0, (usableH - totalH) / 2);
  }

  // ------ 14) Slot pozícionálás (absolute, manual top) ---------------
  const centerX = leftPad + usableW / 2;
  let curY = startY;
  for (const slot of slots) {
    const el = slot.el;
    const effEffectiveScale = (slot.type === "score" ? 1 : slot.effScale) * globalScale;

    setImp(el, "position", "absolute");
    setImp(el, "left", `${Math.round(centerX)}px`);
    setImp(el, "top", `${Math.round(curY)}px`);
    setImp(el, "right", "auto");
    setImp(el, "bottom", "auto");
    setImp(el, "margin", "0");
    setImp(el, "z-index", "5");
    setImp(el, "transform-origin", "top center");
    setImp(el, "visibility", "visible");
    setImp(el, "opacity", "1");

    if (slot.type === "score") {
      if (globalScale < 1) {
        setImp(el, "transform", `translateX(-50%) scale(${globalScale.toFixed(3)})`);
      } else {
        setImp(el, "transform", "translateX(-50%)");
      }
      setImp(el, "width", "max-content");
      setImp(el, "max-width", `${Math.round(usableW)}px`);
    } else {
      // Name / Avg: transform: scale a TELJES sorra (egész sor együtt)
      setImp(el, "transform", `translateX(-50%) scale(${effEffectiveScale.toFixed(3)})`);
      setImp(el, "width", "max-content");
      const maxNaturalW = effEffectiveScale > 0.01
        ? Math.round(usableW / effEffectiveScale)
        : Math.round(usableW);
      setImp(el, "max-width", `${maxNaturalW}px`);
    }

    curY += slot.effH * globalScale + gapPx * globalScale;
  }

  // ------ 15) Hide-elt sorok elrejtése -------------------------------
  if (!showName && nameBand) {
    setImp(nameBand, "display", "none");
    setImp(nameBand, "visibility", "hidden");
  }
  if (!showAvg && avgBand) {
    setImp(avgBand, "display", "none");
    setImp(avgBand, "visibility", "hidden");
  }

  // ------ 16) Min-height védelem a panelnek --------------------------
  if (panelRect.height >= 140) {
    setImp(panel, "min-height", `${Math.round(panelRect.height)}px`);
  }
}


function cleanupOffLayoutV12OverlayResidue() {
  const host = document.querySelector("#ad-ext-player-display");
  if (!host) return;
  host.querySelectorAll(".ad-core-off-v12-overlay").forEach((el) => {
    try { el.remove(); } catch (_) {}
  });
  host.querySelectorAll("[data-ad-core-off-v12-hidden='1']").forEach((el) => {
    try {
      el.style.removeProperty("visibility");
      el.style.removeProperty("opacity");
      el.style.removeProperty("pointer-events");
      delete el.dataset.adCoreOffV12Hidden;
    } catch (_) {}
  });
}

function forceOffLayoutPipelineV12() {
  // v44: ez a függvény régen egy ad-core-off-v12-overlay alapú DOM réteget
  // tett a player kártyára, és elrejtette a natív score/name/avg elemeket.
  // Ez a réteg konfliktált a v40 overlay-jel és az applyPlayerMetaPlacement
  // OFF-overlay-ével — három overlay egymáson —, ami a player kártya
  // összeomlásához vezetett (0..4 px height).
  //
  // Az új natív mért layout (applyOffNativeMeasuredLayout) ezt feleslegessé
  // teszi, ezért itt már csak az esetleg ottragadt régi DOM-ot takarítjuk
  // ki, és visszaoldjuk a régi rejtéseket. A tényleges OFF layoutot az
  // annotatePlayerCardInfo → applyPlayerMetaPlacement útvonalon a panelra
  // hívott applyOffNativeMeasuredLayout végzi.
  const c = cfg();
  const host = document.querySelector("#ad-ext-player-display");
  if (!host) return;

  host.querySelectorAll(
    ".ad-core-off-v12-overlay, " +
    ".ad-core-off-v15-overlay, " +
    ".ad-core-off-v40-overlay, " +
    ".ad-core-player-top-overlay-off-v43"
  ).forEach((el) => { try { el.remove(); } catch (_) {} });

  host.querySelectorAll(
    "[data-ad-core-off-v12-hidden='1'], " +
    "[data-ad-core-off-v15-hidden='1'], " +
    "[data-ad-core-off-v40-hidden='1'], " +
    "[data-ad-core-overlay-hidden='1'], " +
    "[data-ad-core-extra-hidden='1']"
  ).forEach((el) => {
    try {
      el.style.removeProperty("visibility");
      el.style.removeProperty("opacity");
      el.style.removeProperty("pointer-events");
      el.style.removeProperty("display");
      delete el.dataset.adCoreOffV12Hidden;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreOffV15Hidden;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreOffV40Hidden;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreOverlayHidden;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreExtraHidden;
    } catch (_) {}
  });

  // Ha mégis Layout ON-ban hívnák valahonnan, takarítás után kilépünk.
  if (c.LAYOUT_ENABLED) return;

  // Layout OFF-ban a panelekre is alkalmazzuk az új natív layoutot, hogy
  // a már látható kártyák a render loop pulzusai között is stabilak
  // legyenek (resize, fullscreen-toggle, two-player váltás stb.).
  const panels = Array.from(host.children || []).filter((el) => {
    if (!el || el.nodeType !== 1) return false;
    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    return r.width > 120 && r.height > 80;
  });
  for (const panel of panels) {
    try { applyOffNativeMeasuredLayout(panel); } catch (_) {}
  }
}

  function refreshLayoutPlayerExtras() {
    ensureHead(() => {
      ensurePlayerCardFontFace();
      let st = document.getElementById(PLAYER_LAYOUT_EXTRAS_ID);
      if (!st) {
        st = document.createElement("style");
        st.id = PLAYER_LAYOUT_EXTRAS_ID;
        document.head.appendChild(st);
      }
      st.textContent = buildLayoutPlayerExtrasCss();
      applyLayoutPlayerHtmlClasses();
      const extraSkin = document.getElementById(EXTRA_STYLE_ID);
      const core = document.getElementById(STYLE_ID);
      if (extraSkin && extraSkin.parentNode) {
        if (st.parentNode !== extraSkin.parentNode) extraSkin.parentNode.appendChild(st);
        if (extraSkin.nextSibling !== st) extraSkin.after(st);
      } else if (core && core.parentNode) {
        if (st.parentNode !== core.parentNode) core.parentNode.appendChild(st);
        if (core.nextSibling !== st) core.after(st);
      }
    });
  }

  /* ================== PLAYER POINTBOARD AUTO LAYOUT ================== */
function resetManagedPointBoard(el) {
  if (!el) return;
  [
    "position",
    "left",
    "top",
    "right",
    "bottom",
    "width",
    "height",
    "transform",
    "margin",
    "maxHeight",
    "minHeight",
    "boxSizing"
  ].forEach((k) => { el.style[k] = ""; });
  delete el.dataset.adAutoPointboard;
}

function clearManagedPointBoards(panel) {
  if (!panel) return;
  panel.querySelectorAll("[data-ad-auto-pointboard='1']").forEach(resetManagedPointBoard);
}

function findPlayerPointBoard(panel) {
  if (!panel) return null;

  const playerRoot =
    panel.querySelector(".ad-ext-player") ||
    panel.querySelector(".css-17xejub") ||
    panel.querySelector(".css-1qox1u6") ||
    panel;

  const roots = [playerRoot, panel].filter(Boolean);
  const seen = new Set();

  let best = null;
  let bestScore = -1e9;

  for (const root of roots) {
    const lvl1 = Array.from(root.children || []);
    const lvl2 = lvl1.flatMap((el) => Array.from(el.children || []));
    const candidates = [...lvl1, ...lvl2];

    for (const el of candidates) {
      if (!el || seen.has(el)) continue;
      seen.add(el);

      if (el === panel || el === playerRoot) continue;
      if (el.classList?.contains("fireworks")) continue;
      if (el.querySelector?.(".fireworks")) continue;
      if (el.querySelector?.(".ad-core-player-score")) continue;
      if (el.classList?.contains("ad-core-visible-meta-row")) continue;
      if (el.classList?.contains("ad-core-visible-avg-row")) continue;
      if (el.closest?.(".ad-core-visible-meta-row, .ad-core-visible-avg-row")) continue;

      const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
      if (cs.display === "none" || cs.visibility === "hidden") continue;

      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (r.width < 70 || r.height < 120) continue;

      const aspect = r.height / Math.max(1, r.width);
      if (aspect < 1.2 || aspect > 6) continue;

      let borderSides = 0;
      for (const side of ["top", "right", "bottom", "left"]) {
        const w = parseFloat(cs.getPropertyValue(`border-${side}-width`)) || 0;
        const st = cs.getPropertyValue(`border-${side}-style`) || "none";
        if (w > 0.5 && st !== "none") borderSides += 1;
      }

      const numericLeaves = playerCardTextLeaves(el).filter((n) => {
        const txt = playerCardTextNorm(n.textContent);
        return /^\d{1,4}$/.test(txt);
      }).length;

      const score =
        borderSides * 220 +
        numericLeaves * 30 +
        (r.width * r.height) / 300 -
        Math.abs(aspect - 2.45) * 90;

      if (score > bestScore) {
        bestScore = score;
        best = el;
      }
    }
  }

  return best;
}

function layoutSinglePlayerPointBoard(panel) {
  if (!panel) return;

  if (!cfg().LAYOUT_ENABLED) {
    clearManagedPointBoards(panel);
    return;
  }

  const board = findPlayerPointBoard(panel);
  if (!board) {
    clearManagedPointBoards(panel);
    return;
  }

  const scoreEl =
    panel.querySelector(".ad-core-player-score") ||
    playerCardPickMainScoreEl(panel);

  if (!scoreEl) {
    clearManagedPointBoards(panel);
    return;
  }

  const panelRect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
  if (!panelRect.width || !panelRect.height) return;

  const metaRow = panel.querySelector(".ad-core-visible-meta-row");
  const avgRow  = panel.querySelector(".ad-core-visible-avg-row");

  const isShown = (el) =>
    !!el &&
    getComputedStyle(el).display !== "none" &&
    getComputedStyle(el).visibility !== "hidden";

  let anchorBottom = scoreEl.getBoundingClientRect().bottom - panelRect.top;

  if (isShown(metaRow)) {
    anchorBottom = Math.max(
      anchorBottom,
      metaRow.getBoundingClientRect().bottom - panelRect.top
    );
  }
  if (isShown(avgRow)) {
    anchorBottom = Math.max(
      anchorBottom,
      avgRow.getBoundingClientRect().bottom - panelRect.top
    );
  }

  const sidePad = 14;
  const topGap = 18;
  const bottomPad = 14;

  let top = Math.round(anchorBottom + topGap);
  let availH = Math.floor(panelRect.height - top - bottomPad);

  if (availH < 120) {
    top = Math.max(10, Math.floor(panelRect.height * 0.45));
    availH = Math.max(120, Math.floor(panelRect.height - top - bottomPad));
  }

  const naturalRect = board.getBoundingClientRect();
  let ratio = naturalRect.height / Math.max(1, naturalRect.width);

  if (!Number.isFinite(ratio) || ratio < 1.8 || ratio > 3.4) {
    ratio = 2.45;
  }

  const maxW = Math.max(90, Math.floor(panelRect.width - sidePad * 2));

  let targetH = availH;
  let targetW = Math.round(targetH / ratio);

  if (targetW > maxW) {
    targetW = maxW;
    targetH = Math.floor(targetW * ratio);
  }

  const left = Math.round((panelRect.width - targetW) / 2);
  const clampedTop = Math.max(
    10,
    Math.min(top, Math.floor(panelRect.height - bottomPad - targetH))
  );

  Object.assign(board.style, {
    position: "absolute",
    left: `${left}px`,
    top: `${clampedTop}px`,
    right: "auto",
    bottom: "auto",
    width: `${Math.round(targetW)}px`,
    height: `${Math.round(targetH)}px`,
    transform: "none",
    margin: "0",
    maxHeight: "none",
    minHeight: "0",
    boxSizing: "border-box",
  });

  board.dataset.adAutoPointboard = "1";

  // FONTOS:
  // a natív ponttábla belső tartalmát itt már nem formázzuk át,
  // mert a full-history overlay ebből olvassa ki a sorokat.
}

function isPointBoardNumberLeaf(el) {
  if (!el || !el.isConnected) return false;
  const txt = playerCardTextNorm(el.textContent);
  if (!/^\d{1,4}$/.test(txt)) return false;

  const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
  if (r.width < 6 || r.height < 6) return false;

  return true;
}

function pickPointBoardCellForLeaf(leaf, board) {
  if (!leaf || !board) return null;

  let n = leaf;
  let best = leaf.parentElement || null;

  while (n && n.parentElement && n.parentElement !== board) {
    const p = n.parentElement;
    const nums = playerCardTextLeaves(p).filter(isPointBoardNumberLeaf);

    if (nums.length !== 1) break;

    const r = p.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (r.width < 10 || r.height < 10) break;

    best = p;
    n = p;
  }

  return best;
}

function tunePointBoardContent(board, boardW, boardH) {
  if (!board) return;

  const ip = (el, prop, val) => {
    if (!el || !el.style) return;
    el.style.setProperty(prop, String(val), "important");
  };

  const norm = (v) => String(v || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  const isNum = (v) => /^\d{1,4}$/.test(norm(v));

  // itt áll a méret
  const fontPx = Math.max(34, Math.min(52, Math.round(boardW * 0.18)));

  ip(board, "text-align", "center");
  ip(board, "overflow", "hidden");
  ip(board, "box-sizing", "border-box");

  const table = board.querySelector("table");
  if (table) {
    ip(table, "width", "100%");
    ip(table, "height", "auto");
    ip(table, "table-layout", "fixed");
    ip(table, "border-collapse", "collapse");
    ip(table, "border-spacing", "0");
    ip(table, "margin", "0");
  }

  board.querySelectorAll("tbody, thead, tfoot").forEach((el) => {
    ip(el, "height", "auto");
  });

  board.querySelectorAll("tr").forEach((el) => {
    ip(el, "height", "auto");
  });

  board.querySelectorAll("td, th").forEach((cell) => {
    ip(cell, "width", "50%");
    ip(cell, "text-align", "center");
    ip(cell, "vertical-align", "top");
    ip(cell, "padding", "2px 0 0 0");
    ip(cell, "line-height", "1.05");

    // ha a szám közvetlenül a td/th-ben van
    if (isNum(cell.textContent) && (!cell.children || cell.children.length === 0)) {
      ip(cell, "font-size", `${fontPx}px`);
      ip(cell, "font-weight", "700");
    }
  });

  // belső wrap-ek
  board.querySelectorAll("td > *, th > *, p, span, div").forEach((el) => {
    const txt = norm(el.textContent);
    if (!isNum(txt)) return;

    ip(el, "display", "block");
    ip(el, "width", "100%");
    ip(el, "text-align", "center");
    ip(el, "margin", "0 auto");
    ip(el, "padding", "0");
    ip(el, "line-height", "1.05");
    ip(el, "font-size", `${fontPx}px`);
    ip(el, "font-weight", "700");
  });
}

function layoutAllPlayerPointBoards() {
  const host = document.querySelector("#ad-ext-player-display");
  if (!host) return;

  const panels = Array.from(host.children).filter((n) => n && n.nodeType === 1);
  for (const panel of panels) {
    layoutSinglePlayerPointBoard(panel);
  }
}

  /* ================== BOARD MARKER ================== */
  function isBoardSvg(svg) {
    if (!svg) return false;
    const pathCount = svg.querySelectorAll("path").length;
    if (pathCount < 40) return false;

    const has20Text = Array.from(svg.querySelectorAll("text")).some(t => (t.textContent || "").trim() === "20");
    const vb = (svg.getAttribute("viewBox") || "").split(/\s+/).map(Number);
    const looksSquareVB = vb.length === 4 && Math.abs((vb[2] || 0) - (vb[3] || 0)) < 5 && (vb[2] || 0) > 300;

    return has20Text || (looksSquareVB && pathCount > 120);
  }

function applyBoardMarkerNow() {
  const c = cfg();
  const svgs = document.querySelectorAll("svg");
  for (const svg of svgs) {
    const isBoard = isBoardSvg(svg);
    const has = svg.classList.contains("ad-board-svg");
    if (c.BOARD_MARKER) {
      if (isBoard && !has) svg.classList.add("ad-board-svg");
    } else {
      if (has) svg.classList.remove("ad-board-svg");
    }
  }
}

  let boardMarkScheduled = false;
  function scheduleBoardMark() {
    if (boardMarkScheduled) return;
    boardMarkScheduled = true;
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      boardMarkScheduled = false;
      applyBoardMarkerNow();
    });
  }

  /* ================== BOARD MANAGER BACK BUTTON ================== */
  const BM_BTN_ID = "ad-bm-back-btn";
  function isBoardsPage() { return location.pathname.startsWith("/boards"); }

  function removeBmBackButton() {
    const el = document.getElementById(BM_BTN_ID);
    if (el) el.remove();
  }

  function ensureBmBackButton() {
    const c = cfg();
    if (!isBoardsPage()) { removeBmBackButton(); return; }
    if (!c.BM_BACK_BUTTON) { removeBmBackButton(); return; }

    const label = T().bmBackLabel;

    const existing = document.getElementById(BM_BTN_ID);
    if (existing) {
      const span = existing.querySelector("span");
      if (span && span.textContent !== label) span.textContent = label; // ✅ nyelvváltásnál is frissül
      return;
    }

    const candidates = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("a.chakra-button, button.chakra-button"));
    if (!candidates.length) return;

    const anchor = candidates.reverse().find(el => el && el.offsetParent !== null);
    if (!anchor) return;

    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.id = BM_BTN_ID;
    a.href = "https://play.autodarts.io/";
    a.target = "_self";
    a.rel = "noopener";
    Object.assign(a.style, {
      display: "inline-flex",
      alignItems: "center",
      gap: "8px",
      padding: "8px 12px",
      borderRadius: "10px",
      border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.16)",
      background: "rgba(255,255,255,0.08)",
      color: "#fff",
      textDecoration: "none",
      fontWeight: "800",
      whiteSpace: "nowrap",
    });

    a.addEventListener("mouseenter", () => a.style.filter = "brightness(1.12)");
    a.addEventListener("mouseleave", () => a.style.filter = "none");

    a.innerHTML = `
      <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20"
           viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-width="2"
           stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">
        <path d="M10 3H6a2 2 0 0 0-2 2v14c0 1.1.9 2 2 2h4"/>
        <path d="M16 17l5-5-5-5"/>
        <path d="M19.8 12H9"/>
      </svg>
      <span>${label}</span>
    `;

    anchor.insertAdjacentElement("afterend", a);
  }

  /* ================== THROWS -> POINTS ================== */
  function parseThrow(raw) {
    const t = (raw || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    const mm = t.match(/^M(\d{1,2})$/i);
    if (mm) return { points: 0, orig: null, missLabel: true };
    if (t === "MISS" || t === "0") return { points: 0, orig: null, missLabel: true };
    if (t === "BULL" || t === "DBULL" || t === "50") return { points: 50, orig: raw };
    if (t === "SBULL" || t === "25") return { points: 25, orig: raw };

    const m = t.match(SDT_RE);
    if (m) {
      const ch = m[1];
      const num = parseInt(m[2], 10);
      const mult = ch === "S" ? 1 : (ch === "D" ? 2 : 3);
      return { points: num * mult, orig: raw };
    }
    return { points: null, orig: raw };
  }

  function isPlaceholderRaw(raw) {
    const t = (raw || "").trim();
    return (t === "" || t === "..." || t.includes("•") || t === "…" || t.toLowerCase() === "null");
  }

  let __adStickyInit = false;

  function initStickyThrowSelectOnce(){
  if (__adStickyInit) return;
  __adStickyInit = true;

  document.addEventListener("pointerdown", (e) => {
    if (e.button != null && e.button !== 0) return;

    // 1) keressük meg a kártyát a composedPath-ban (stabilabb)
    let card = null;
    const path = (typeof e.composedPath === "function") ? e.composedPath() : null;
    if (path) {
      for (const n of path) {
        if (n && n.classList && n.classList.contains("ad-ext-turn-throw")) { card = n; break; }
      }
    }
    // 2) fallback: closest
    if (!card) card = e.target?.closest?.(".ad-ext-turn-throw");
    if (!card) return;

    const turn = card.closest("#ad-ext-turn") || document.querySelector("#ad-ext-turn");
    if (!turn) return;

    const cards = Array.from(turn.querySelectorAll(".ad-ext-turn-throw"));
    const idx = cards.indexOf(card);
