    if (idx < 0) return;

    // fontos: itt MOST NEM szűrünk ad-has-throw / data-adval alapján,
    // mert pont ez szokott “nem kész lenni” kattintáskor.
    // Csak a teljesen üres/placeholdert dobjuk ki:
    const p = card.querySelector("p");
    const raw = (p?.textContent || "").trim();
    if (isPlaceholderRaw(raw)) return;

    const curRaw = turn.getAttribute(TURN_SEL_ATTR);
    const cur = (curRaw == null) ? -1 : (parseInt(curRaw, 10) | 0);
    const next = (cur === idx) ? -1 : idx;

    if (next < 0) turn.removeAttribute(TURN_SEL_ATTR);
    else turn.setAttribute(TURN_SEL_ATTR, String(next));

    applyStickyThrowSelection(turn);

    dirtyTurn();
    scheduleUpdate();

    if (toastEl) showToast(next < 0 ? "Select OFF" : `Select ${next + 1}`);
  }, true);
}

  function updateThrowGroup(parent) {
    const c = cfg();
    const throwDivs = Array.from(parent.children).filter((el) => el.classList?.contains("ad-ext-turn-throw"));
    if (!throwDivs.length) return;

    const ps = throwDivs.map((d) => d.querySelector("p")).filter(Boolean);
    if (!ps.length) return;

    const raws = ps.map((p) => (p.textContent || "").trim());
    const parsed = raws.map((raw) => isPlaceholderRaw(raw) ? { _placeholder: true } : parseThrow(raw));

    parsed.forEach((it, i) => {
      const p = ps[i];
      const card = p.closest(".ad-ext-turn-throw");

      if (it._placeholder) {
        if (card) {
          card.classList.remove("ad-has-throw");
          card.classList.remove("ad-click-selected");
        }
        if ("adval" in p.dataset) delete p.dataset.adval;
        if ("adorig" in p.dataset) delete p.dataset.adorig;
        return;
      }

      if (card) card.classList.add("ad-has-throw");

      const shown = it.missLabel ? "MISS" : (typeof it.points === "number" ? String(it.points) : raws[i]);
      const orig  = it.missLabel ? null : (it.orig ?? raws[i]);

      p.dataset.adval = shown;

      if (c.SHOW_ORIG_IN_CORNER) {
        if (orig) p.dataset.adorig = orig;
        else if ("adorig" in p.dataset) delete p.dataset.adorig;
      } else {
        if ("adorig" in p.dataset) delete p.dataset.adorig;
      }
    });
  }

  function updateAllThrowGroups(turn) {
    const throwEls = turn.querySelectorAll(".ad-ext-turn-throw");
    const parents = new Set();
    for (const t of throwEls) if (t.parentElement) parents.add(t.parentElement);
    for (const p of parents) updateThrowGroup(p);
  }

function applyStickyThrowSelection(turn){
  if (!turn) return;

  const rawAttr = turn.getAttribute(TURN_SEL_ATTR);
  let idx = (rawAttr == null) ? -1 : (parseInt(rawAttr, 10) | 0);

  const cards = Array.from(turn.querySelectorAll(".ad-ext-turn-throw"));
  if (!cards.length) return;

  // ha rossz index (pl. kevesebb kártya lett), töröljük az attribútumot
  if (idx < 0 || idx >= cards.length) {
    if (rawAttr != null) turn.removeAttribute(TURN_SEL_ATTR);
    cards.forEach(c => c.classList.remove("ad-click-selected"));
    return;
  }

  // ha a kiválasztott kártya placeholder lett -> töröljük a kijelölést
  const p = cards[idx].querySelector("p");
  const txt = (p?.textContent || "").trim();
  if (isPlaceholderRaw(txt)) {
    turn.removeAttribute(TURN_SEL_ATTR);
    idx = -1;
  }

  cards.forEach((c, i) => c.classList.toggle("ad-click-selected", idx >= 0 && i === idx));
}

  /* ================== TOTAL OVERLAY FIX ================== */
  function restoreTotalOverlays(root = document) {
    root.querySelectorAll(".ad-total-overlay").forEach(el => el.remove());
    root.querySelectorAll("[data-ad-total-hidden='1']").forEach(el => {
      el.removeAttribute("data-ad-total-hidden");
      el.style.position = "";
      el.style.left = "";
      el.style.top = "";
      el.style.width = "";
      el.style.height = "";
      el.style.overflow = "";
      el.style.opacity = "";
      el.style.pointerEvents = "";
    });
    root.querySelectorAll(".ad-total-cell").forEach(el => el.classList.remove("ad-total-cell"));
    root.querySelectorAll(".ad-total-native-cell").forEach(el => el.classList.remove("ad-total-native-cell"));
  }

  function unhideTotalLeaf(el) {
    if (!el) return;
      el.removeAttribute("data-ad-total-hidden");
      el.style.position = "";
      el.style.left = "";
      el.style.top = "";
      el.style.width = "";
      el.style.height = "";
      el.style.overflow = "";
      el.style.opacity = "";
      el.style.pointerEvents = "";
  }

  function findNumericLeaf(container) {
    if (!container) return null;
    const all = [container, ...container.querySelectorAll("*")];
    for (const el of all) {
      const txt = (el.textContent || "").trim();
      if (!/^\d{1,4}$/.test(txt)) continue;
      if (el.children && el.children.length > 0) continue;
      return el;
    }
    return null;
  }

  function isTurnThrowCell(el) {
    if (!el || el.nodeType !== 1) return false;
    return !!(
      el.classList?.contains("ad-ext-turn-throw") ||
      el.matches?.(".score.css-156dsds")
    );
  }

  function findTurnTotalCellAndLeaf(turn) {
    if (!turn) return null;

    const directCells = Array.from(turn.children).filter((el) => {
      if (!el || el.nodeType !== 1) return false;
      if (el.matches?.("button, [role='button']")) return false;
      const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
      if (cs.display === "none" || cs.visibility === "hidden") return false;
      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      return r.width >= 24 && r.height >= 24;
    });

    if (!directCells.length) return null;

    const firstThrowIdx = directCells.findIndex((el) => isTurnThrowCell(el));
    const searchCells = firstThrowIdx > 0
      ? directCells.slice(0, firstThrowIdx)
      : directCells.filter((el) => !isTurnThrowCell(el));

    const preferredCells = [
      ...searchCells.filter((el) => el.classList?.contains("ad-total-cell")),
      ...searchCells.filter((el) => el.matches?.(".css-rrf7rv")),
      ...searchCells.filter((el) => !isTurnThrowCell(el)),
    ];

    const seen = new Set();
    for (const cell of preferredCells) {
      if (!cell || seen.has(cell)) continue;
      seen.add(cell);

      const leaf = findNumericLeaf(cell);
      if (!leaf) continue;
      if (leaf.classList?.contains("ad-total-overlay")) continue;
      if (leaf.closest(".ad-ext-turn-throw")) continue;

      return { cell, leaf };
    }

    return null;
  }

function forceCenterTotalOverlay(turn) {
  if (!turn) return;

  const hasOverlayManagedTotal =
    !!turn.querySelector(".ad-total-overlay") ||
    !!turn.querySelector("[data-ad-total-hidden='1']");
  const hasManagedTotal =
    hasOverlayManagedTotal ||
    !!turn.querySelector(".ad-total-cell") ||
    !!turn.querySelector(".ad-total-native-cell");
  const layoutOn = !!cfg().LAYOUT_ENABLED;
  const totalPick = findTurnTotalCellAndLeaf(turn);
  const leaf = totalPick?.leaf || null;
  const cell = totalPick?.cell || null;

  // Bust / üres / nem található total -> takarítás, hogy ne ragadjon bent
  if (!leaf) {
    if (hasManagedTotal) restoreTotalOverlays(turn);
    turn.querySelectorAll(".ad-ext-turn-total-value").forEach(el => el.classList.remove("ad-ext-turn-total-value"));
    return;
  }

  const value = (leaf.textContent || "").trim();
  if (!/^\d{1,4}$/.test(value)) {
    if (hasManagedTotal) restoreTotalOverlays(turn);
    turn.querySelectorAll(".ad-ext-turn-total-value").forEach(el => el.classList.remove("ad-ext-turn-total-value"));
    return;
  }

  if (!cell || cell === turn) {
    if (hasManagedTotal) restoreTotalOverlays(turn);
    turn.querySelectorAll(".ad-ext-turn-total-value").forEach(el => el.classList.remove("ad-ext-turn-total-value"));
    return;
  }

  if (!cfg().TOTAL_VIEW) {
    if (hasManagedTotal) restoreTotalOverlays(turn);
    turn.querySelectorAll(".ad-ext-turn-total-value").forEach(el => {
      if (el !== leaf) el.classList.remove("ad-ext-turn-total-value");
    });
    turn.querySelectorAll(".ad-total-cell").forEach(el => {
      if (el !== cell) el.classList.remove("ad-total-cell");
    });
    turn.querySelectorAll(".ad-total-native-cell").forEach(el => el.classList.remove("ad-total-native-cell"));
    cell.classList.add("ad-total-cell");
    leaf.classList.add("ad-ext-turn-total-value");
    return;
  }

  if (!layoutOn) {
    if (hasOverlayManagedTotal) restoreTotalOverlays(turn);
    turn.querySelectorAll(".ad-ext-turn-total-value").forEach(el => {
      if (el !== leaf) el.classList.remove("ad-ext-turn-total-value");
    });
    turn.querySelectorAll(".ad-total-cell").forEach(el => {
      if (el !== cell) el.classList.remove("ad-total-cell");
    });
    turn.querySelectorAll(".ad-total-native-cell").forEach(el => {
      if (el !== cell) el.classList.remove("ad-total-native-cell");
    });
    cell.classList.add("ad-total-cell");
    cell.classList.add("ad-total-native-cell");
    leaf.classList.add("ad-ext-turn-total-value");
    return;
  }

  // Régi hidden leaf-ek visszaállítása, ha már nem ez az aktuális
  turn.querySelectorAll("[data-ad-total-hidden='1']").forEach(el => {
    if (el !== leaf) unhideTotalLeaf(el);
  });

  // Idegen overlay-ek törlése
  turn.querySelectorAll(".ad-total-overlay").forEach(el => {
    if (!cell.contains(el)) el.remove();
  });

  // Régi total-cell class levétele más cellákról
  turn.querySelectorAll(".ad-total-cell").forEach(el => {
    if (el !== cell) el.classList.remove("ad-total-cell");
  });
  turn.querySelectorAll(".ad-total-native-cell").forEach(el => el.classList.remove("ad-total-native-cell"));

  cell.classList.add("ad-total-cell");

  let overlay = Array.from(cell.children).find(
    el => el.classList && el.classList.contains("ad-total-overlay")
  );

  if (!overlay) {
    overlay = document.createElement("div");
    overlay.className = "ad-total-overlay ad-ext-turn-total-value";
    cell.appendChild(overlay);
  }

  if (overlay.textContent !== value) overlay.textContent = value;
  overlay.classList.remove("ad-ext-turn-checkout-value");
  overlay.classList.add("ad-ext-turn-total-value");

  if (leaf.dataset.adTotalHidden !== "1") {
    leaf.dataset.adTotalHidden = "1";
    leaf.style.position = "absolute";
    leaf.style.left = "-9999px";
    leaf.style.top = "-9999px";
    leaf.style.width = "1px";
    leaf.style.height = "1px";
    leaf.style.overflow = "hidden";
    leaf.style.opacity = "0";
    leaf.style.pointerEvents = "none";
  }
}

  /* ================== CHECKOUT MARK ================== */
function isInButton(el) { return !!el.closest?.("button"); }
function markCheckoutInTurnBar(turn) {
  if (!turn) return;

  turn.querySelectorAll(".ad-ext-turn-checkout-value").forEach(el => {
    el.classList.remove("ad-ext-turn-checkout-value");
  });
  turn.querySelectorAll(".ad-ext-turn-checkout-points").forEach(el => {
    el.classList.remove("ad-ext-turn-checkout-points");
  });
  turn.querySelectorAll(".ad-ext-turn-checkout-card").forEach(el => {
    el.classList.remove("ad-ext-turn-checkout-card");
  });

  // ✅ Biztonság: a Total környékén soha ne legyen checkout class
  turn.querySelectorAll(".ad-total-cell .ad-ext-turn-checkout-value, .ad-total-overlay.ad-ext-turn-checkout-value")
      .forEach(el => el.classList.remove("ad-ext-turn-checkout-value"));

  const layoutOn = !!cfg().LAYOUT_ENABLED;

  const nodes = turn.querySelectorAll(".chakra-text, p, span, div");

  for (const el of nodes) {
    if (el.closest(".ad-ext-turn-throw")) continue;    // dobáskártyákon ne
    if (el.closest(".ad-total-cell")) continue;        // total cellen belül ne
    if (el.closest(".ad-total-overlay")) continue;     // total overlayen ne
    if (isInButton(el)) continue;                      // gombokon ne

    // ✅ KRITIKUS: csak LEAF elemet jelöljünk (különben a * selector mindent elvisz)
    if (el.children && el.children.length > 0) continue;

    const txt = (el.textContent || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    if (!txt || txt.length > 10) continue;

    if (!CHECKOUT_TOKEN_RE.test(txt)) continue;

    el.classList.add("ad-ext-turn-checkout-value");
    if (!layoutOn) continue;

    let card = el;
    while (card && card.parentElement && card.parentElement !== turn) {
      card = card.parentElement;
    }
    if (!card || card === turn) continue;

    card.classList.add("ad-ext-turn-checkout-card");

    card.querySelectorAll("p, span, div").forEach((leaf) => {
      if (!leaf || leaf === el) return;
      if (leaf.closest(".ad-ext-turn-throw")) return;
      if (leaf.closest(".ad-total-cell, .ad-total-overlay")) return;
      if (isInButton(leaf)) return;
      if (leaf.children && leaf.children.length > 0) return;

      const leafTxt = (leaf.textContent || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
      if (!/^\d{1,3}$/.test(leafTxt)) return;

      leaf.classList.add("ad-ext-turn-checkout-points");
    });
  }
}

/* ================== FULL HISTORY PLAYER CARDS ================== */
const PH_STYLE_ID = "ad-history-only-style-v130-core";
const PH_CONNECTOR_EXTEND_FACTOR = 0.85;

const PH_STORAGE_PREFIX = "ad-history-only-v130-core";
let phStorageLoaded = false;
let phRouteKey = location.pathname;
let phHostMemo = new WeakMap();

let phHistoryMem = {
  key: location.pathname,
  left: [],
  right: [],
  leftTurn: -1,
  rightTurn: -1,
};

function phIsEl(n) {
  return !!(n && n.nodeType === 1 && n instanceof HTMLElement);
}

function phRowsEqual(a, b) {
  if (!Array.isArray(a) || !Array.isArray(b)) return false;
  if (a.length !== b.length) return false;
  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
    if ((a[i]?.a || "") !== (b[i]?.a || "")) return false;
    if ((a[i]?.b || "") !== (b[i]?.b || "")) return false;
  }
  return true;
}

function phEnsureMem() {
  const key = location.pathname;
  if (phHistoryMem.key !== key) {
    phHistoryMem = {
      key,
      left: [],
      right: [],
      leftTurn: -1,
      rightTurn: -1,
    };
    phHostMemo = new WeakMap();
  }
}

function phGetHistoryStorageKey() {
  return `${PH_STORAGE_PREFIX}|${location.pathname}`;
}

function phSaveHistoryMem() {
  try {
    const payload = {
      key: phHistoryMem.key,
      left: Array.isArray(phHistoryMem.left) ? phHistoryMem.left : [],
      right: Array.isArray(phHistoryMem.right) ? phHistoryMem.right : [],
      leftTurn: Number.isFinite(phHistoryMem.leftTurn) ? phHistoryMem.leftTurn : -1,
      rightTurn: Number.isFinite(phHistoryMem.rightTurn) ? phHistoryMem.rightTurn : -1,
      ts: Date.now(),
    };
    sessionStorage.setItem(phGetHistoryStorageKey(), JSON.stringify(payload));
  } catch (_) {}
}

function phLoadHistoryMem() {
  try {
    const raw = sessionStorage.getItem(phGetHistoryStorageKey());
    if (!raw) return;

    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== "object") return;

    phHistoryMem = {
      key: location.pathname,
      left: Array.isArray(parsed.left) ? parsed.left : [],
      right: Array.isArray(parsed.right) ? parsed.right : [],
      leftTurn: Number.isFinite(parsed.leftTurn) ? parsed.leftTurn : -1,
      rightTurn: Number.isFinite(parsed.rightTurn) ? parsed.rightTurn : -1,
    };
  } catch (_) {}
}

function phClearHistoryMemStorage() {
  try {
    sessionStorage.removeItem(phGetHistoryStorageKey());
  } catch (_) {}
}

function phRestoreAll(root = document) {
  root.querySelectorAll(".ad-native-history-floating").forEach(el => el.remove());

  root.querySelectorAll("[data-ad-history-hidden='1']").forEach(phRestoreHistoryHost);

  root.querySelectorAll("[data-ad-history-panel-patched='1']").forEach(el => {
    el.style.position = "";
    el.removeAttribute("data-ad-history-panel-patched");
  });
}

function phCollectNumericLeaves(root, includeVisibilityHidden = false) {
  if (!phIsEl(root)) return [];

  const out = [];
  const seen = new Set();

  for (const el of root.querySelectorAll("*")) {
    if (!phIsEl(el)) continue;
    if (el.closest(".ad-native-history-floating")) continue;
    if (el.children.length > 0) continue;

    const txt = (el.textContent || "").trim();
    if (!/^\d{1,3}$/.test(txt)) continue;

    const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
    if (cs.display === "none") continue;
    if (!includeVisibilityHidden && cs.visibility === "hidden") continue;

    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (r.width <= 0 || r.height <= 0) continue;

    const key = [
      txt,
      Math.round(r.left),
      Math.round(r.top),
      Math.round(r.width),
      Math.round(r.height)
    ].join("|");

    if (seen.has(key)) continue;
    seen.add(key);

    out.push({
      el,
      text: txt,
      left: r.left,
      top: r.top,
      width: r.width,
      height: r.height,
      cx: r.left + r.width / 2,
      cy: r.top + r.height / 2,
    });
  }

  return out;
}

function phFindHistoryHost(panel) {
  if (!phIsEl(panel)) return null;

  const memo = phHostMemo.get(panel);
  if (memo && memo.isConnected && panel.contains(memo)) return memo;

  const direct =
    panel.querySelector(":scope > .ad-ext-player > .css-1qox1u6") ||
    panel.querySelector(".ad-ext-player > .css-1qox1u6") ||
    panel.querySelector(".css-1qox1u6");

  if (direct && phIsEl(direct)) {
    phHostMemo.set(panel, direct);
    return direct;
  }

  const strict =
    panel.querySelector(".css-1qox1u6 table") ||
    panel.querySelector(".css-1qox1u6 .chakra-container.css-1u90hiz") ||
    panel.querySelector(".css-1qox1u6 .css-1y542hf") ||
    panel.querySelector(".css-1qox1u6 .css-1u90hiz");

  if (strict && phIsEl(strict)) {
    const host =
      strict.closest(".css-1qox1u6") ||
      strict.closest(".css-1y542hf") ||
      strict.closest(".css-1u90hiz") ||
      strict;
    phHostMemo.set(panel, host);
    return host;
  }

  return null;
}

function phReadRowsFromHost(host) {
  if (!phIsEl(host)) return [];

  const root =
    (host.matches && host.matches(".css-1qox1u6") ? host : null) ||
    host.closest?.(".css-1qox1u6") ||
    host;

  if (!phIsEl(root)) return [];

  const table =
    (root.matches && root.matches("table") ? root : null) ||
    root.querySelector("table");

  if (table && phIsEl(table)) {
    const rows = [];

    for (const tr of table.querySelectorAll("tr")) {
      const nums = Array.from(tr.querySelectorAll("td, th"))
        .map((el) => String(el.textContent || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim())
        .filter((t) => /^\d{1,4}$/.test(t));

      if (!nums.length) continue;

      if (nums.length === 1) {
        rows.push({ a: "", b: nums[0] });
      } else {
        rows.push({ a: nums[0] || "", b: nums[1] || "" });
      }
    }

    if (rows.length) return rows;
  }

  const hr = root.getBoundingClientRect();

  let cells = phCollectNumericLeaves(root, true).filter((c) => (
    /^\d{1,4}$/.test(c.text) &&
    c.left >= hr.left - 2 &&
    c.left + c.width <= hr.right + 2 &&
    c.top >= hr.top - 2 &&
    c.top + c.height <= hr.bottom + 2
  ));

  if (!cells.length) return [];

  const seen = new Set();
  cells = cells.filter((c) => {
    const key = `${c.text}|${Math.round(c.left)}|${Math.round(c.top)}`;
    if (seen.has(key)) return false;
    seen.add(key);
    return true;
  });

  const midX = hr.left + hr.width / 2;

  const left = cells
    .filter((c) => c.cx < midX)
    .sort((a, b) => a.cy - b.cy)
    .map((c) => c.text);

  const right = cells
    .filter((c) => c.cx >= midX)
    .sort((a, b) => a.cy - b.cy)
    .map((c) => c.text);

  const rows = [];

  if (right.length === left.length + 1) {
    rows.push({ a: "", b: right[0] });
    for (let i = 0; i < left.length; i++) {
      rows.push({ a: left[i] || "", b: right[i + 1] || "" });
    }
    return rows;
  }

  const len = Math.max(left.length, right.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
    rows.push({ a: left[i] || "", b: right[i] || "" });
  }

  return rows;
}

function phMergeRows(stored, visible) {
  const clean = (rows) => Array.isArray(rows)
    ? rows
        .map((r) => ({ a: String(r?.a || "").trim(), b: String(r?.b || "").trim() }))
        .filter((r) => r.a || r.b)
    : [];

  const saved = clean(stored);
  const live = clean(visible);

  if (!live.length) return saved;
  if (!saved.length) return live.slice();
  if (phRowsEqual(saved, live)) return saved;

  // Native chalkboards can expose either chronological order (old → new)
  // or reversed order (new → old), depending on the active DOM branch.
  // First preserve the existing complete list whenever the live window is
  // already contained in it.
  if (live.length <= saved.length) {
    for (let start = 0; start <= saved.length - live.length; start++) {
      if (phRowsEqual(saved.slice(start, start + live.length), live)) return saved;
    }
  }

  const max = Math.min(saved.length, live.length);

  // Chronological window: saved tail = live head → append new rows.
  for (let k = max; k >= 1; k--) {
    if (phRowsEqual(saved.slice(-k), live.slice(0, k))) {
      return saved.concat(live.slice(k));
    }
  }

  // Reverse chronological window: live tail = saved head → prepend new rows.
  for (let k = max; k >= 1; k--) {
    if (phRowsEqual(live.slice(-k), saved.slice(0, k))) {
      return live.concat(saved.slice(k));
    }
  }

  // A larger live set that fully contains the stored sequence is authoritative.
  if (live.length > saved.length) {
    for (let start = 0; start <= live.length - saved.length; start++) {
      if (phRowsEqual(live.slice(start, start + saved.length), saved)) return live.slice();
    }
  }

  // No reliable overlap: do not discard the remembered full history.
  return saved;
}

function phParseTurn(panel) {
  if (!panel) return -1;

  const candidates = [
    ...panel.querySelectorAll(".ad-core-visible-avg-row"),
    ...panel.querySelectorAll("[data-ad-history-hidden='1']"),
    ...panel.querySelectorAll("[data-ad-core-overlay-hidden='1']"),
  ];

  for (const el of candidates) {
    const txt = String(el.textContent || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    const m = txt.match(/#\s*(\d+)/);
    if (m) return Number(m[1]);
  }

  const allText = String(panel.textContent || panel.innerText || "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();

  const m = allText.match(/#\s*(\d+)/);
  return m ? Number(m[1]) : -1;
}

const PH_BOGEY_SCORES = new Set([159, 162, 163, 165, 166, 168, 169]);

function phIsCheckoutScore(v) {
  const n = Number(v);
  return Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 2 && n <= 170 && !PH_BOGEY_SCORES.has(n);
}

function phRenderHistoryRow(r, i) {
  return `
    <div class="ad-native-history-row" data-row-index="${i}">
      <div class="ad-native-history-cell">${r?.a || ""}</div>
      <div class="ad-native-history-cell">${r?.b || ""}</div>
    </div>
  `;
}

function phRestoreHistoryHost(host) {
  if (!host) return;

  host.style.visibility = host.dataset.phHistOrigVisibility || "";
  host.style.pointerEvents = host.dataset.phHistOrigPointerEvents || "";
  host.style.opacity = host.dataset.phHistOrigOpacity || "";

  delete host.dataset.phHistOrigVisibility;
  delete host.dataset.phHistOrigPointerEvents;
  delete host.dataset.phHistOrigOpacity;

  host.removeAttribute("data-ad-history-hidden");
  delete host.dataset.adCoreOwnHistorySource;
}

function phHideNativeHost(host) {
  if (!host) return;

  if (!("phHistOrigVisibility" in host.dataset)) host.dataset.phHistOrigVisibility = host.style.visibility || "";
  if (!("phHistOrigPointerEvents" in host.dataset)) host.dataset.phHistOrigPointerEvents = host.style.pointerEvents || "";
  if (!("phHistOrigOpacity" in host.dataset)) host.dataset.phHistOrigOpacity = host.style.opacity || "";

  host.setAttribute("data-ad-history-hidden", "1");
  host.dataset.adCoreOwnHistorySource = "1";
  host.style.visibility = "hidden";
  host.style.pointerEvents = "none";
  host.style.opacity = "0";
}

function phEnsurePanelPosition(panel) {
  if (!panel) return;
  const cs = getComputedStyle(panel);
  if (cs.position === "static") {
    panel.style.position = "relative";
    panel.setAttribute("data-ad-history-panel-patched", "1");
  }
}

function phGetTextMetrics(rows, fontSize, colGap, padX) {
  const maxLeftLen = Math.max(1, ...(rows || []).map(r => String(r?.a || "").length));
  const maxRightLen = Math.max(1, ...(rows || []).map(r => String(r?.b || "").length));
  const digitW = fontSize * 0.60;

  const contentW = Math.ceil(
    (maxLeftLen * digitW) +
    (maxRightLen * digitW) +
    colGap +
    (padX * 2) +
    24
  );

  return { maxLeftLen, maxRightLen, contentW };
}

function phFindInfoBlockBottom(panel, host) {
  if (!phIsEl(panel)) return 0;

  const panelRect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
  const panelArea = Math.max(1, panelRect.width * panelRect.height);

  const fromDataset = Number(panel.dataset.adCoreInfoBottom);
  if (Number.isFinite(fromDataset) && fromDataset > 0) {
    return clamp(
      Math.round(fromDataset),
      Math.round(panelRect.height * 0.28),
      Math.round(panelRect.height * 0.62)
    );
  }

  const priority = [];
  const fallback = [];

  for (const el of panel.querySelectorAll("*")) {
    if (!phIsEl(el)) continue;
    if (el === host) continue;
    if (host && host.contains(el)) continue;
    if (el.closest(".ad-native-history-floating")) continue;

    const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
    if (cs.display === "none" || cs.visibility === "hidden") continue;

    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (r.width < 8 || r.height < 8) continue;
    if (r.bottom <= panelRect.top || r.top >= panelRect.bottom) continue;

    const relTop = r.top - panelRect.top;
    const relBottom = r.bottom - panelRect.top;
    if (relTop > panelRect.height * 0.72) continue;

    const area = r.width * r.height;
    if (area > panelArea * 0.35) continue;

    const txt = (el.textContent || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    const cx = r.left + r.width / 2;
    const relCx = cx - panelRect.left;

    if (relCx < panelRect.width * 0.12 || relCx > panelRect.width * 0.88) continue;

    const looksImportant =
      txt.includes("Ø") ||
      txt.includes("#") ||
      /[A-Za-zÁÉÍÓÖŐÚÜŰáéíóöőúüű]/.test(txt) ||
      el.tagName === "IMG" ||
      el.tagName === "SVG" ||
      el.tagName === "CANVAS";

    const pureScore = /^\d{1,3}$/.test(txt);

    if (looksImportant && !pureScore) {
      priority.push(relBottom);
    } else {
      fallback.push(relBottom);
    }
  }

  const best = priority.length
    ? Math.max(...priority)
    : fallback.length
      ? Math.max(...fallback)
      : panelRect.height * 0.36;

  return clamp(
    Math.round(best),
    Math.round(panelRect.height * 0.28),
    Math.round(panelRect.height * 0.62)
  );
}

function phGetFloatingBoxGeometry(panel, host, rows) {
  const panelRect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
  const panelW = Math.round(panelRect.width);
  const panelH = Math.round(panelRect.height);

  const dsStableH = Number(panel.dataset?.adCoreOffStablePanelH);
  const dsStableW = Number(panel.dataset?.adCoreOffStablePanelW);
  const stablePanelH = Math.max(panelH, Number.isFinite(dsStableH) && dsStableH > 0 ? Math.round(dsStableH) : 0);
  const stablePanelW = Math.max(panelW, Number.isFinite(dsStableW) && dsStableW > 0 ? Math.round(dsStableW) : 0);
  const cardScale = clamp(Math.min(stablePanelW / 411, stablePanelH / 620), 0.48, 1);

  const isWideCard = panelW > stablePanelH * 1.18;
  const rowCount = Math.max(1, Array.isArray(rows) ? rows.length : 0);
  const visibleRows = clamp(rowCount, 7, 14);

  let left, top, width, height;
  let fontSize, colGap, padX, rowGap, padTop, padBottom;

  if (isWideCard) {
    left = Math.round(panelW * 0.74);
    top = Math.round(panelH * 0.06);
    width = Math.round(panelW * 0.22);
    height = Math.round(panelH * 0.88);

    const stableHeightForFont = Math.max(height, Math.round(stablePanelH * 0.88));
    fontSize = clamp(((stableHeightForFont + 4) / visibleRows) * 1.08, 28, 52);
    colGap = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * 0.12), 7, 11);
    padX = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * 0.18), 7, 11);
    rowGap = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * 0.10), 5, 9);
    padTop = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * 0.14), 7, 11);
    padBottom = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * 0.18), 7, 12);
  } else {
    const infoBottom = phFindInfoBlockBottom(panel, host);
    const gapBelowInfo = Math.round(clamp(10 + (cardScale * 8), 10, 18));
    const bottomPad = Math.max(8, Math.round(panelH * 0.035));
    const sideGap = clamp(
      Math.round(panelW * (cardScale <= 0.60 ? 0.11 : cardScale <= 0.74 ? 0.09 : 0.08)),
      12,
      22
    );

    width = Math.round(
      clamp(
        panelW - (sideGap * 2),
        112,
        panelW - 12
      )
    );

    // OFF v10: kikapcsolt elrendezésnél legyen kicsit nagyobb és kényelmesebben görgethető.
    try {
      const layoutOnHistory = !!cfg()?.LAYOUT_ENABLED && document.documentElement.classList.contains("ad-core-two-player");
      if (layoutOnHistory) {
        // Layout ON v5: full képernyős kétjátékos nézetben a history box ne foglalja el
        // majdnem a teljes kártyaszélességet. A feljelölt cél kb. a player kártya 2/3-a.
        width = Math.round(clamp(panelW * 0.66, 112, panelW - 12));
      } else {
        width = Math.round(clamp(width * 1.12, 126, panelW - 10));
      }
    } catch (_) {}

    left = Math.round((panelW - width) / 2);

    top = Math.max(
      infoBottom + gapBelowInfo,
      Math.round(panelH * (cardScale <= 0.58 ? 0.48 : cardScale <= 0.72 ? 0.50 : 0.52))
    );

    height = Math.max(72, panelH - top - bottomPad);
    const stableHeightForFont = Math.max(height, stablePanelH - top - bottomPad);

    fontSize = clamp(
      ((stableHeightForFont - 6) / visibleRows) * (cardScale <= 0.68 ? 0.72 : 0.84),
      cardScale <= 0.60 ? 17 : cardScale <= 0.72 ? 18 : 20,
      38
    );
    colGap = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * 0.12), 5, 12);
    padX = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * 0.18), 5, 12);
    rowGap = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * 0.10), 4, 9);
    padTop = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * 0.12), 5, 10);
    padBottom = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * 0.16), 5, 12);
  }

  const metrics = phGetTextMetrics(rows, fontSize, colGap, padX);
  const fontFromWidth =
    (width - (padX * 2) - colGap - 24) /
    ((metrics.maxLeftLen + metrics.maxRightLen) * 0.60);

  const widthSafeFont = isWideCard ? (fontFromWidth * 1.02) : (fontFromWidth * 0.96);

  fontSize = clamp(
    Math.min(fontSize, widthSafeFont),
    isWideCard ? 28 : (cardScale <= 0.60 ? 17 : cardScale <= 0.72 ? 18 : 20),
    isWideCard ? 52 : 38
  );

  // A history betűmérete se függjön a név/átlag sor láthatóságától. Ugyanabban
  // a viewport/panel bucketben megtartjuk a legnagyobb már kiszámolt fontot.
  const histKey = [
    "ph-geom-v42",
    Math.round((window.innerWidth || 0) / 24) * 24,
    Math.round((window.innerHeight || 0) / 24) * 24,
    Math.round(panelW / 8) * 8,
    Math.round(stablePanelH / 8) * 8,
    isWideCard ? "wide" : "tall",
    visibleRows,
    metrics.maxLeftLen,
    metrics.maxRightLen
  ].join("|");

  const cached = AD_CORE_PH_GEOMETRY_CACHE.get(histKey);
  if (cached && Number.isFinite(cached.font)) {
    fontSize = clamp(
      Math.max(fontSize, cached.font),
      isWideCard ? 36 : (cardScale <= 0.60 ? 20 : cardScale <= 0.72 ? 22 : 24),
      isWideCard ? 66 : 52
    );
  }

  AD_CORE_PH_GEOMETRY_CACHE.set(histKey, { font: Math.round(fontSize) });

  colGap = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * (isWideCard ? 0.14 : 0.12)), isWideCard ? 8 : 5, isWideCard ? 14 : 12);
  padX = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * (isWideCard ? 0.22 : 0.18)), isWideCard ? 8 : 5, isWideCard ? 14 : 12);
  rowGap = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * (isWideCard ? 0.12 : 0.10)), isWideCard ? 6 : 4, isWideCard ? 10 : 9);
  padTop = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * (isWideCard ? 0.16 : 0.12)), isWideCard ? 8 : 5, isWideCard ? 12 : 10);
  padBottom = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * (isWideCard ? 0.22 : 0.16)), isWideCard ? 8 : 5, isWideCard ? 14 : 12);

  return {
    left: Math.round(left),
    top: Math.round(top),
    width: Math.round(width),
    height: Math.round(height),
    font: Math.round(fontSize),
    rowGap,
    colGap,
    padX,
    padTop,
    padBottom,
    centerX: !isWideCard,
  };
}

function phDrawHistoryConnectors(box, rows) {
  const list = box.querySelector(".ad-native-history-list");
  if (!list) return;

  let layer = list.querySelector(":scope > .ad-native-history-connectors");
  if (!layer) {
    layer = document.createElement("div");
    layer.className = "ad-native-history-connectors";
    list.appendChild(layer);
  }

  layer.innerHTML = "";

  const rowEls = Array.from(list.querySelectorAll(":scope > .ad-native-history-row"));
  if (rowEls.length < 2) return;

  const listRect = list.getBoundingClientRect();

  for (let i = 1; i < rowEls.length; i++) {
    const prevRemain = Number(rows?.[i - 1]?.b);
    if (!phIsCheckoutScore(prevRemain)) continue;

    const prevRow = rowEls[i - 1];
    const currRow = rowEls[i];

    const prevRight = prevRow.children[1];
    const currLeft = currRow.children[0];
    if (!prevRight || !currLeft) continue;

    const r1 = prevRight.getBoundingClientRect();
    const r2 = currLeft.getBoundingClientRect();

    const x1 = r1.left + r1.width * 0.54 - listRect.left;
    const y1 = r1.top + r1.height * 0.56 - listRect.top;

    const x2 = r2.left + r2.width * 0.46 - listRect.left;
    const y2 = r2.top + r2.height * 0.44 - listRect.top;

    const dx = x2 - x1;
    const dy = y2 - y1;
    const baseLen = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    if (baseLen < 1) continue;

    const ux = dx / baseLen;
    const uy = dy / baseLen;

    const extendStart = Math.max(12, Math.min(r1.width, r1.height) * PH_CONNECTOR_EXTEND_FACTOR);
    const extendEnd = Math.max(12, Math.min(r2.width, r2.height) * PH_CONNECTOR_EXTEND_FACTOR);

    const sx = x1 - ux * extendStart;
    const sy = y1 - uy * extendStart;
    const ex = x2 + ux * extendEnd;
    const ey = y2 + uy * extendEnd;

    const finalDx = ex - sx;
    const finalDy = ey - sy;
    const finalLen = Math.hypot(finalDx, finalDy);
    const angle = Math.atan2(finalDy, finalDx) * 180 / Math.PI;

    const line = document.createElement("div");
    line.className = "ad-native-history-connector";
    line.style.left = `${sx}px`;
    line.style.top = `${sy}px`;
    line.style.width = `${finalLen}px`;
    line.style.transform = `rotate(${angle}deg)`;

    layer.appendChild(line);
  }
}


/* v78: This is the user's own all-throws history renderer. Its box must
   occupy the same physical rectangle as the original native chalkboard.
   We use the native host only as a measurement anchor; the React node remains
   in place and is merely visibility-hidden while the custom renderer is shown. */
function phGetOwnHistoryNativeSizedGeometry(panel, host, rows) {
  const fallback = phGetFloatingBoxGeometry(panel, host, rows);
  if (!phIsEl(panel) || !phIsEl(host)) return fallback;

  const panelRect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
  const hostRect = host.getBoundingClientRect();
  const panelW = Math.round(panelRect.width);
  const panelH = Math.round(panelRect.height);

  const rectIsUsable =
    panelW > 0 && panelH > 0 &&
    hostRect.width >= 48 && hostRect.height >= 48 &&
    hostRect.left >= panelRect.left - 6 &&
    hostRect.top >= panelRect.top - 6 &&
    hostRect.right <= panelRect.right + 6 &&
    hostRect.bottom <= panelRect.bottom + 6;

  if (!rectIsUsable) return fallback;

  // The native host is the size reference, but it can touch the edge of the
  // player card. Keep the custom box fully inside the card with the same
  // visual inset on every side.
  const inset = clamp(Math.round(Math.min(panelW, panelH) * 0.035), 8, 14);
  const width = clamp(Math.round(hostRect.width), 48, Math.max(48, panelW - inset * 2));
  const height = clamp(Math.round(hostRect.height), 64, Math.max(64, panelH - inset * 2));
  const rawLeft = Math.round(hostRect.left - panelRect.left);
  const rawTop = Math.round(hostRect.top - panelRect.top);
  const left = clamp(rawLeft, inset, Math.max(inset, panelW - width - inset));
  const top = clamp(rawTop, inset, Math.max(inset, panelH - height - inset));

  // Always reserve the same number of readable rows. Extra throws make the
  // list taller than the viewport and are reached by native scrolling; they
  // must never reduce the text size.
  const targetVisibleRows = 7;
  const baseFont = ((height - 14) / targetVisibleRows) * 0.84;
  const font = clamp(Math.round(baseFont), 14, Math.max(18, Math.round(width * 0.23)));
  const colGap = clamp(Math.round(font * 0.12), 4, 10);
  const padX = clamp(Math.round(font * 0.16), 4, 10);
  const rowGap = clamp(Math.round(font * 0.08), 3, 8);
  const padTop = clamp(Math.round(font * 0.12), 4, 9);
  const padBottom = clamp(Math.round(font * 0.14), 4, 10);

  return {
    left,
    top,
    width,
    height,
    font,
    rowGap,
    colGap,
    padX,
    padTop,
    padBottom,
    centerX: false,
  };
}

function phMountFloatingOverlay(panel, host, rows) {
  if (!panel || !host) return;

  phEnsurePanelPosition(panel);
  phHideNativeHost(host);

  const geom = phGetOwnHistoryNativeSizedGeometry(panel, host, rows);

  const boxes = Array.from(panel.querySelectorAll(":scope > .ad-native-history-floating"));
  let box = boxes.shift() || null;
  boxes.forEach(el => el.remove());
  if (!box) {
    box = document.createElement("div");
    box.className = "ad-native-history-floating";
    box.dataset.adCoreOwnHistory = "1";
    panel.appendChild(box);
  }

  box.dataset.adCoreOwnHistory = "1";
  box.dataset.adCoreOwnHistoryVersion = "80";
  delete box.dataset.adUserScrolled;

  box.style.left = geom.centerX ? "50%" : `${geom.left}px`;
  box.style.top = `${geom.top}px`;
  box.style.width = `${geom.width}px`;
  box.style.height = `${geom.height}px`;
  box.style.transform = geom.centerX ? "translateX(-50%)" : "";
  box.style.setProperty("--ad-hist-font", `${geom.font}px`);
  box.style.setProperty("--ad-hist-row-gap", `${geom.rowGap}px`);
  box.style.setProperty("--ad-hist-col-gap", `${geom.colGap}px`);
  box.style.setProperty("--ad-hist-pad-x", `${geom.padX}px`);
  box.style.setProperty("--ad-hist-pad-t", `${geom.padTop}px`);
  box.style.setProperty("--ad-hist-pad-b", `${geom.padBottom}px`);

  let scroll = box.querySelector(":scope > .ad-native-history-scroll");
  const prevScrollTop = scroll ? scroll.scrollTop : 0;

  const sig = JSON.stringify(rows || []);
  const geomSig = `${geom.left}|${geom.top}|${geom.width}|${geom.height}|${geom.font}|${geom.rowGap}|${geom.colGap}|${geom.padX}|${geom.padTop}|${geom.padBottom}`;
  const contentChanged = box.dataset.adSig !== sig;
  const geomChanged = box.dataset.adGeomSig !== geomSig;

  if (!scroll || contentChanged) {
    box.innerHTML = `
      <div class="ad-native-history-scroll">
        <div class="ad-native-history-list">
          ${(rows || []).map((r, i) => phRenderHistoryRow(r, i)).join("")}
          <div class="ad-native-history-connectors"></div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
    scroll = box.querySelector(":scope > .ad-native-history-scroll");
    box.dataset.adSig = sig;
  }

  box.dataset.adGeomSig = geomSig;

  requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    phDrawHistoryConnectors(box, rows || []);

    // v80: whenever the content or the chalkboard geometry refreshes, always
    // return to the latest throw. A second frame handles browser layout that
    // settles after the connector drawing pass.
    if (scroll && (contentChanged || geomChanged)) {
      const scrollToLatest = () => {
        scroll.scrollTop = Math.max(0, scroll.scrollHeight - scroll.clientHeight);
      };
      scrollToLatest();
      requestAnimationFrame(scrollToLatest);
      setTimeout(scrollToLatest, 0);
    }
  });

  if (scroll && !scroll.dataset.adWheelBound) {
    scroll.dataset.adWheelBound = "1";
    scroll.addEventListener("wheel", e => {
      // Keep the wheel event local to the history box. The next history refresh
      // intentionally returns this box to the newest row.
      e.stopPropagation();
    }, { passive: true });
  }
}

function syncFullHistoryOverlaysOwnHistory() {
  phEnsureStyle();

  if (location.pathname !== phRouteKey) {
    phRouteKey = location.pathname;
    phStorageLoaded = false;
    phEnsureMem();
    phRestoreAll();
  }

  const display = document.querySelector("#ad-ext-player-display");
  if (!display) {
    phRestoreAll();
    return;
  }

  const panels = Array.from(display.children).filter(phIsEl);
  if (panels.length < 2) {
    phRestoreAll(display);
    return;
  }

  // FONTOS: a régi CORE-os pointboard auto-fit ne szóljon bele
  for (const panel of panels) {
    try {
      clearManagedPointBoards(panel);
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  phEnsureMem();

  if (!phStorageLoaded) {
    phLoadHistoryMem();
    phStorageLoaded = true;
  }

  const leftPanel = panels[0];
  const rightPanel = panels[1];

  const leftHost = phFindHistoryHost(leftPanel);
  const rightHost = phFindHistoryHost(rightPanel);

  if (!leftHost || !rightHost) {
    phRestoreAll(display);
    return;
  }

  const leftVisible = phReadRowsFromHost(leftHost);
  const rightVisible = phReadRowsFromHost(rightHost);

  const leftTurn = phParseTurn(leftPanel);
  const rightTurn = phParseTurn(rightPanel);

  const leftReset = (
    leftTurn >= 0 &&
    (
      leftTurn < phHistoryMem.leftTurn ||
      (leftTurn === 0 && leftVisible.some(r => (r.b || "") === "501"))
    )
  );

  const rightReset = (
    rightTurn >= 0 &&
    (
      rightTurn < phHistoryMem.rightTurn ||
      (rightTurn === 0 && rightVisible.some(r => (r.b || "") === "501"))
    )
  );

  if (leftReset || rightReset) {
    phClearHistoryMemStorage();
  }

  if (leftReset) phHistoryMem.left = leftVisible.slice();
  else phHistoryMem.left = phMergeRows(phHistoryMem.left, leftVisible);

  if (rightReset) phHistoryMem.right = rightVisible.slice();
  else phHistoryMem.right = phMergeRows(phHistoryMem.right, rightVisible);

  phHistoryMem.leftTurn = Math.max(phHistoryMem.leftTurn, leftTurn);
  phHistoryMem.rightTurn = Math.max(phHistoryMem.rightTurn, rightTurn);

  phSaveHistoryMem();

  phMountFloatingOverlay(leftPanel, leftHost, phHistoryMem.left);
  phMountFloatingOverlay(rightPanel, rightHost, phHistoryMem.right);
}
  /* ================== ACTIVE PLAYER DETECT ================== */
  function parseRGBA(str) {
    const m = String(str || "").match(/rgba?\(\s*(\d+)\s*,\s*(\d+)\s*,\s*(\d+)(?:\s*,\s*([0-9.]+))?\s*\)/i);
    if (!m) return null;
    return { r: +m[1], g: +m[2], b: +m[3], a: (m[4] == null ? 1 : +m[4]) };
  }
  function brightness01(rgba) { return (rgba.r + rgba.g + rgba.b) / 3 / 255; }
  function bestWhiteFromShadow(shadowStr) {
    const s = String(shadowStr || "");
    let best = 0;
    const re = /rgba?\([^)]+\)/ig;
    let m;
    while ((m = re.exec(s)) !== null) {
      const rgba = parseRGBA(m[0]);
      if (!rgba) continue;
      const b = brightness01(rgba);
      if (b < 0.78 || rgba.a < 0.12) continue;
      best = Math.max(best, b * rgba.a);
    }
    if (best > 0 && s.toLowerCase().includes("inset")) best *= 1.25;
    return best;
  }
  function styleFrameScore(cs) {
    if (!cs) return 0;
    let best = 0;
    const sides = ["top","right","bottom","left"];
    for (const side of sides) {
      const w = parseFloat(cs.getPropertyValue(`border-${side}-width`)) || 0;
      const st = cs.getPropertyValue(`border-${side}-style`) || "none";
      if (w < 0.6 || st === "none") continue;
      const col = cs.getPropertyValue(`border-${side}-color`);
      const rgba = parseRGBA(col);
      if (!rgba) continue;
      const b = brightness01(rgba);
      if (b < 0.78 || rgba.a < 0.12) continue;
      best = Math.max(best, w * b * rgba.a);
    }
    best = Math.max(best, bestWhiteFromShadow(cs.getPropertyValue("box-shadow")) * 1.5);
    return best;
  }
  function elementFrameScore(el) { return styleFrameScore(getComputedStyle(el)); }
  function panelWhiteFrameScore(panel) {
    let best = elementFrameScore(panel);
    const kids = panel.querySelectorAll("*");
    const limit = Math.min(kids.length, 24);
    for (let i = 0; i < limit; i++) {
      const el = kids[i];
      const tn = el.tagName;
      if (tn !== "DIV" && tn !== "SPAN") continue;
      best = Math.max(best, elementFrameScore(el));
    }
    return best;
  }
  function clearActiveClasses() {
    const host = document.querySelector("#ad-ext-player-display");
    if (!host) return;
    Array.from(host.children).forEach(p => p.classList?.remove(ACTIVE_CLASS));
  }
  function updateActivePlayerHighlight() {
    const c = cfg();
    if (!c.ACTIVE_PLAYER_HIGHLIGHT) return;

    const host = document.querySelector("#ad-ext-player-display");
    if (!host) return;

    const panels = Array.from(host.children).filter((n) => n && n.nodeType === 1);
    if (!panels.length) return;

    /*
      v81 – The v67 skin gives every card the same white border. Therefore the
      earlier "brightest frame wins" heuristic no longer identifies the active
      player reliably; it can permanently prefer the first card. Autodarts
      already exposes the real state on the native player node as
      .ad-ext-player-active, so use that authoritative marker first. The old
      visual scoring remains only as a compatibility fallback for a future DOM
      variant that does not expose the class.
    */
    const nativeMarked = panels.filter((panel) => {
      const directPlayer = Array.from(panel.children || []).find((child) =>
        child?.classList?.contains("ad-ext-player")
      );
      const player = directPlayer || panel.querySelector?.(".ad-ext-player");
      return !!(
        panel.classList?.contains("ad-ext-player-active") ||
        panel.getAttribute?.("data-active") === "true" ||
        player?.classList?.contains("ad-ext-player-active") ||
        player?.getAttribute?.("data-active") === "true"
      );
    });

    let bestPanel = null;
    let source = "none";

    if (nativeMarked.length === 1) {
      bestPanel = nativeMarked[0];
      source = "native-class";
    } else {
      let bestScore = 0;
      for (const p of panels) {
        const score = panelWhiteFrameScore(p);
        if (score > bestScore) {
          bestScore = score;
          bestPanel = p;
        }
      }
      if (!(bestScore > 0.02)) bestPanel = null;
      if (bestPanel) source = "frame-fallback";
    }

    for (const panel of panels) {
      const isActive = panel === bestPanel;
      panel.classList.toggle(ACTIVE_CLASS, isActive);
      if (isActive) panel.dataset.adCoreActiveSource = source;
      else delete panel.dataset.adCoreActiveSource;
    }
  }

  function panelHasNativeStateBg(panel) {
  if (!panel) return false;

  const rgba = parseRGBA(getComputedStyle(panel).backgroundColor);
  if (!rgba || rgba.a < 0.20) return false;

  const max = Math.max(rgba.r, rgba.g, rgba.b);
  const min = Math.min(rgba.r, rgba.g, rgba.b);
  const spread = max - min;

  if (spread < 45) return false;

  const isGreen = (rgba.g >= rgba.r + 28) && (rgba.g >= rgba.b + 20);
  const isRed   = (rgba.r >= rgba.g + 28) && (rgba.r >= rgba.b + 20);

  return isGreen || isRed;
}

function updatePlayerStateOverlay() {
  const host = document.querySelector("#ad-ext-player-display");
  if (!host) return;

  const panels = Array.from(host.children).filter(n => n && n.nodeType === 1);
  for (const panel of panels) {
    panel.classList.toggle("ad-native-state-bg", panelHasNativeStateBg(panel));
  }
}

  function nodeIsFireworksRelated(n) {
  if (!n || n.nodeType !== 1) return true;
  const el = n;
  return !!(
    el.classList?.contains("fireworks") ||
    el.closest?.(".fireworks") ||
    el.querySelector?.(".fireworks")
  );
}

function mutationsOnlyFireworks(muts) {
  return Array.isArray(muts) && muts.length > 0 && muts.every((m) => {
    const nodes = [
      ...(m.addedNodes ? Array.from(m.addedNodes) : []),
      ...(m.removedNodes ? Array.from(m.removedNodes) : []),
    ];

    if (nodes.length) {
      return nodes.every(nodeIsFireworksRelated);
    }

    return !!m.target?.closest?.(".fireworks");
  });
}

  /* ================== TRIPLE ================== */
function clearTripleClasses() {
  document.querySelectorAll(".ad-ext-turn-throw").forEach(card => {
    card.classList.remove(TRIPLE_CLASS);
    if (card.dataset) delete card.dataset.adTripleToken;
  });
}

function restartTriple(card) {
  card.classList.remove(TRIPLE_CLASS);
  void card.offsetWidth;
  card.classList.add(TRIPLE_CLASS);
}

function updateTripleHighlight(turn) {
  const c = cfg();
  if (!c.TRIPLE_ANIM || !turn) return;

  const allow = new Set(TRIPLE_VALUES.map(v => String(v).toUpperCase().trim()));
  const cards = turn.querySelectorAll(".ad-ext-turn-throw");

  for (const card of cards) {
    const p = card.querySelector("p");
    const raw = (p?.textContent || "").trim().toUpperCase();

    if (!raw || raw === "..." || raw === "…" || raw.includes("•")) {
      card.classList.remove(TRIPLE_CLASS);
      if (card.dataset) delete card.dataset.adTripleToken;
      continue;
    }

    const isTriple = allow.has(raw);
    const prev = (card.dataset && card.dataset.adTripleToken) ? card.dataset.adTripleToken : "";

    if (!isTriple) {
      card.classList.remove(TRIPLE_CLASS);
      if (card.dataset) delete card.dataset.adTripleToken;
      continue;
    }

    if (prev !== raw) {
      if (card.dataset) card.dataset.adTripleToken = raw;
      restartTriple(card);
    } else {
      if (!card.classList.contains(TRIPLE_CLASS)) card.classList.add(TRIPLE_CLASS);
    }
  }
}

  /* ================== WIN MUSIC ================== */
let winAudio = null;
let winUnlocked = false;
let winArmed = true;
let winLastPlay = 0;
let winPrevFinishPresent = false;

// ✅ ha a win UI eltűnik (next leg/set vagy auto progress), ennyi ideig várunk, hogy ne villogásra álljon le
let winUiAbsentSince = 0;
const WIN_STOP_ABSENT_MS = 450;

const WIN_PLAY_COOLDOWN_MS = 2500;
const RE_FINISH = /(finish|befejez|beenden)/i;

// ✅ ezekre a gombokra azonnal álljon le
const RE_STOP_BTN = /(finish|befejez|beenden|next\s*leg|következő\s*leg|nächste\s*leg|naechste\s*leg|next\s*set|következő\s*set|nächste\s*set|naechste\s*set)/i;

function safe(fn) { try { return fn(); } catch {} }

function stopWinAudio() {
  if (!winAudio) return;
  safe(() => { winAudio.pause(); winAudio.currentTime = 0; });
}

function installWinStopHooks() {
  if (scopeWin) scopeWin.abort();
  scopeWin = makeScope();

  // ✅ Stop gombokra
  scopeWin.on(document, "click", (e) => {
    const btn = e.target?.closest?.("button, a");
    if (!btn) return;
    const txt = ((btn.textContent || "") + " " + (btn.getAttribute("aria-label") || "")).trim();
    if (RE_STOP_BTN.test(txt)) stopWinAudio();
  }, true); // capture

  // ✅ Navigáció / oldalváltás esetén is álljon le
  const onNav = () => stopWinAudio();
  scopeWin.on(window, "popstate", onNav, true);
  scopeWin.on(window, "hashchange", onNav, true);

  // SPA route váltás (history patch) – ezt nem lehet “unpatch”-elni, ezért csak egyszer
  if (!installWinStopHooks._patched) {
    installWinStopHooks._patched = true;

    const _ps = history.pushState;
    history.pushState = function () {
      const r = _ps.apply(this, arguments);
      onNav();
      return r;
    };
    const _rs = history.replaceState;
    history.replaceState = function () {
      const r = _rs.apply(this, arguments);
      onNav();
      return r;
    };
  }
}

function initWinMusicOnce() {
  if (winAudio) return;

  winAudio = new Audio(WIN_URL);
  winAudio.preload = "auto";
  winAudio.volume = clamp(Number(cfg().WIN_VOLUME ?? 1.0), 0, 1);

  installWinStopHooks();

  const unlock = () => {
    if (winUnlocked || !winAudio) return;
    winUnlocked = true;

    const oldVol = winAudio.volume;
    winAudio.volume = 0;
    safe(() => { winAudio.pause(); winAudio.currentTime = 0; });

    const p = winAudio.play();
    if (p && typeof p.then === "function") {
      p.then(() => {
        safe(() => winAudio.pause());
        safe(() => (winAudio.currentTime = 0));
        winAudio.volume = oldVol;
      }).catch(() => { winAudio.volume = oldVol; winUnlocked = false; });
    } else {
      winAudio.volume = oldVol;
    }
  };

  document.addEventListener("pointerdown", unlock, true);
  document.addEventListener("keydown", unlock, true);
  document.addEventListener("click", unlock, true);
}

function hadThrowInThisTurn() {
  const turn = document.querySelector("#ad-ext-turn");
  const t = (turn?.innerText || "").toUpperCase();
  return /\b[SDT](?:[1-9]|1\d|20)\b/.test(t) || /\bSBULL\b|\bDBULL\b|\bBULL\b|\b25\b|\b50\b/.test(t);
}

function findFinishButton() {
  const btns = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("button"));
  for (const b of btns) {
    const txt = ((b.textContent || "") + " " + (b.getAttribute("aria-label") || "")).trim();
    if (RE_FINISH.test(txt)) return b;
  }
  return null;
}

function findStopButtonPresent() {
  const btns = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("button"));
  for (const b of btns) {
    const txt = ((b.textContent || "") + " " + (b.getAttribute("aria-label") || "")).trim();
    if (RE_STOP_BTN.test(txt)) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

let adCelebrationWasActive = false;
let adDeferredPlayersRefresh = false;
let adCelebrationReleaseTimer = 0;
const AD_CELEBRATION_UNFREEZE_DELAY_MS = 220;

function isCelebrationActive() {
  return !!findFinishButton() || findStopButtonPresent();
}

function queueCelebrationRelease() {
  if (adCelebrationReleaseTimer) clearTimeout(adCelebrationReleaseTimer);

  adCelebrationReleaseTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    adCelebrationReleaseTimer = 0;

    if (isCelebrationActive()) return;
    if (!adDeferredPlayersRefresh) return;

    adDeferredPlayersRefresh = false;
    dirtyPlayers();
    scheduleUpdate();
  }, AD_CELEBRATION_UNFREEZE_DELAY_MS);
}

function handleCelebrationFreezeEdge() {
  const now = isCelebrationActive();

  if (now && adCelebrationReleaseTimer) {
    clearTimeout(adCelebrationReleaseTimer);
    adCelebrationReleaseTimer = 0;
  }

  if (!adCelebrationWasActive && now) {
    if (typeof scheduleSync === "function") scheduleSync();
  }

  if (adCelebrationWasActive && !now) {
    queueCelebrationRelease();
    if (typeof scheduleSync === "function") scheduleSync();
  }

  adCelebrationWasActive = now;
}

function scanWinMusic() {
  const c = cfg();
  if (!c.WIN_MUSIC) return;

  if (!winAudio) initWinMusicOnce();
  if (winAudio) winAudio.volume = clamp(Number(c.WIN_VOLUME ?? 1.0), 0, 1);

  const finishPresent = !!findFinishButton();
  const stopUiPresent = finishPresent || findStopButtonPresent();

  // ha még nem volt dobás ebben a turnben, újra “élesítjük”
  if (!hadThrowInThisTurn()) winArmed = true;

  // ✅ START: finish megjelent ÉS volt dobás
  if (finishPresent && !winPrevFinishPresent && hadThrowInThisTurn()) {
    const t = Date.now();
    if (winArmed && winUnlocked && t - winLastPlay > WIN_PLAY_COOLDOWN_MS) {
      winArmed = false;
      winLastPlay = t;
      winUiAbsentSince = 0;

      safe(() => { winAudio.pause(); winAudio.currentTime = 0; });
      const pr = winAudio.play();
      if (pr && typeof pr.catch === "function") pr.catch(() => {});
    }
  }

  // ✅ STOP: csak akkor álljon meg, ha a win UI eltűnt (auto new leg/set, auto exit, stb.)
  // (nem időre, nem gif hosszra)
  if (winAudio && !winAudio.paused) {
    if (stopUiPresent) {
      winUiAbsentSince = 0;
    } else {
      if (!winUiAbsentSince) winUiAbsentSince = Date.now();
      else if (Date.now() - winUiAbsentSince >= WIN_STOP_ABSENT_MS) {
        stopWinAudio();
        winUiAbsentSince = 0;
      }
    }
  }

  winPrevFinishPresent = finishPresent;
}

  /* ================== FLOATING CLOCK ================== */
  let clockEl = null;
  let clockTimeEl = null;
  let clockDrag = null;
  let clockTicker = null;

  const CLOCK_SCALE_MIN = 0.6;
  const CLOCK_SCALE_MAX = 2.0;
  const CLOCK_SCALE_STEP = 0.05;

  function buildClockIfNeeded() {
    if (clockEl) return;

    clockEl = document.createElement("div");
    clockEl.id = "ad-core-floating-clock";
    Object.assign(clockEl.style, {
      position: "fixed",
      zIndex: 2147483647,
      left: "0px",
      top: "0px",
      padding: "10px 12px",
      borderRadius: "12px",
      boxShadow: "0 6px 18px rgba(0,0,0,.55)",
      userSelect: "none",
      pointerEvents: "auto",
      transformOrigin: "top left",
      display: "flex",
      alignItems: "center",
      gap: "10px",
      cursor: "move"
    });

    clockTimeEl = document.createElement("span");
    Object.assign(clockTimeEl.style, {
      fontSize: "22px",
      fontWeight: "800",
      fontFamily: "Arial, sans-serif",
      letterSpacing: "0.3px",
      whiteSpace: "nowrap"
    });

    clockEl.appendChild(clockTimeEl);
    document.body.appendChild(clockEl);

    clockEl.addEventListener("pointerdown", (e) => {
      const cs = state.ui.clock;
      if (!cs.enabled) return;
      if (e.button !== undefined && e.button !== 0) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      clockEl.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);
      clockDrag = { dx: e.clientX - clockEl.offsetLeft, dy: e.clientY - clockEl.offsetTop };
      clockEl.style.opacity = "0.95";
    });

    window.addEventListener("pointermove", (e) => {
      if (!clockDrag || !clockEl) return;
      const cs = state.ui.clock;
      const r = clockEl.getBoundingClientRect();
      const nx = e.clientX - clockDrag.dx;
      const ny = e.clientY - clockDrag.dy;
      const safe = ensureVisible(nx, ny, r.width, r.height);
      cs.x = Math.round(safe.x);
      cs.y = Math.round(safe.y);
      cs.blL = cs.x;
      cs.blB = Math.round(window.innerHeight - (cs.y + r.height));
      applyClockPosition();
    }, { passive: true });

    window.addEventListener("pointerup", () => {
      if (!clockDrag) return;
      clockDrag = null;
      clockEl.style.opacity = "";
      saveStateDebounced();
      showToast(T().toasts.clockSaved);
    });

    clockEl.addEventListener("wheel", (e) => {
      if (!e.ctrlKey) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      const dir = e.deltaY > 0 ? -1 : 1;
      const cs = state.ui.clock;
      cs.scale = clamp(Math.round((cs.scale + dir * CLOCK_SCALE_STEP) * 100) / 100, CLOCK_SCALE_MIN, CLOCK_SCALE_MAX);
      applyClockScale();
      applyClockPosition();
      saveStateDebounced();
      renderPanelIfOpen();
    }, { passive: false });

    clockEl.addEventListener("dblclick", (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      const cs = state.ui.clock;
      if (e.shiftKey) cs.showSeconds = !cs.showSeconds;
      else cs.format24 = !cs.format24;
      saveStateDebounced();
      renderClockTime();
      renderPanelIfOpen();
    });

    if (!clockTicker) {
      clockTicker = setInterval(() => {
        if (!clockEl) return;
        if (!state.ui.clock.enabled) return;
        renderClockTime();
      }, 250);
    }

    applyClockStyle();
    applyClockScale();
    applyClockPosition();
    applyClockEnabled();
    renderClockTime();
  }

  function getClockViewportMetrics() {
    if (adCoreViewportMode === "ad-core-vp-ultralow") {
      return { scale: 0.78, pad: 8, fontPx: 18, py: 7, px: 9, radius: 10, gap: 8 };
    }
    if (adCoreViewportMode === "ad-core-vp-lowh") {
      return { scale: 0.90, pad: 10, fontPx: 20, py: 8, px: 10, radius: 11, gap: 9 };
    }
    return { scale: 1, pad: 16, fontPx: 22, py: 10, px: 12, radius: 12, gap: 10 };
  }

  function applyClockEnabled() {
    if (!clockEl) return;
    clockEl.style.display = state.ui.clock.enabled ? "flex" : "none";
  }
  function applyClockScale() {
    if (!clockEl) return;
    const vm = getClockViewportMetrics();
    const userScale = Number(state.ui.clock.scale || 1);
    clockEl.style.transform = `scale(${clamp(userScale * vm.scale, 0.4, 3)})`;
  }
  function applyClockStyle() {
    if (!clockEl) return;
    const cs = state.ui.clock;
    const vm = getClockViewportMetrics();
    clockEl.style.background = hexToRgba(cs.bgHex, cs.bgAlpha);
    clockEl.style.color = sanitizeHex(cs.textHex, "#ffffff");
    clockEl.style.padding = `${vm.py}px ${vm.px}px`;
    clockEl.style.borderRadius = `${vm.radius}px`;
    clockEl.style.gap = `${vm.gap}px`;
    if (clockTimeEl) clockTimeEl.style.fontSize = `${vm.fontPx}px`;
  }

  function applyClockPosition() {
    if (!clockEl) return;
    const cs = state.ui.clock;

    const r = clockEl.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (typeof cs.blL === "number" && typeof cs.blB === "number") {
     cs.x = Math.round(cs.blL);
     cs.y = Math.round(window.innerHeight - cs.blB - r.height);
    }
    let x = cs.x;
    let y = cs.y;

    const vm = getClockViewportMetrics();

    // default: jobb felül
    if (typeof x !== "number") x = Math.round(window.innerWidth - r.width - vm.pad);
    if (typeof y !== "number") y = vm.pad;

    const safe = ensureVisible(x, y, r.width, r.height, vm.pad);

    clockEl.style.left = Math.round(safe.x) + "px";
    clockEl.style.top  = Math.round(safe.y) + "px";
  }

  // ✅ locale követi a nyelvet (hu-HU / en-US / de-DE)
  function renderClockTime() {
    if (!clockTimeEl) return;
    const cs = state.ui.clock;

    const opts = {
      hour: "2-digit",
      minute: "2-digit",
      second: cs.showSeconds ? "2-digit" : undefined,
      hour12: !cs.format24
    };
    if (!cs.showSeconds) delete opts.second;

    const loc = (state.ui.lang === "en") ? "en-US" : (state.ui.lang === "de" ? "de-DE" : "hu-HU");
    clockTimeEl.textContent = new Date().toLocaleTimeString(loc, opts);
  }

  function resetClockLook() {
    state.ui.clock = { ...clone(DEFAULT_CLOCK), enabled: state.ui.clock.enabled, x: state.ui.clock.x, y: state.ui.clock.y };
    applyClockStyle(); applyClockScale(); applyClockPosition(); renderClockTime();
    saveStateDebounced();
  }
  function resetClockPosition() {
    state.ui.clock.x = null;
    state.ui.clock.y = null;
    state.ui.clock.blL = null;
    state.ui.clock.blB = null;
    applyClockPosition();
    saveStateDebounced();
  }

  /* ================== PERF: DIRTY FLAGS ================== */
  const DIRTY = {
    turn: true,
    players: true,
    board: true,
    bm: true,
    skin: true,
  };
  let suppressTurnObserverDepth = 0;
  function dirtyTurn(){ DIRTY.turn = true; }
  function dirtyPlayers(){ DIRTY.players = true; }
  function dirtyBoard(){ DIRTY.board = true; }
  function dirtyBm(){ DIRTY.bm = true; }
  function dirtySkin(){ DIRTY.skin = true; }

  function withSuppressedTurnObserver(fn) {
    suppressTurnObserverDepth++;
    try {
      return fn();
    } finally {
      window.setTimeout(() => {
        suppressTurnObserverDepth = Math.max(0, suppressTurnObserverDepth - 1);
      }, 0);
    }
  }

  let adCoreViewportMode = "";

  function getAdCoreViewportMode() {
    const w = window.innerWidth || 0;
    const h = window.innerHeight || 0;
    const twoPlayer = document.documentElement.classList.contains("ad-core-two-player");
    if (twoPlayer) {
      if (w <= 1080 || h <= 560 || (w <= 1220 && h <= 680)) return "ad-core-vp-ultralow";
      if (w <= 1320 || h <= 700 || (w <= 1500 && h <= 820)) return "ad-core-vp-lowh";
      return "";
    }
    if (w <= 1180 || h <= 620 || (w <= 1320 && h <= 760)) return "ad-core-vp-ultralow";
    if (w <= 1420 || h <= 760 || (w <= 1680 && h <= 900)) return "ad-core-vp-lowh";
    return "";
  }

  function applyAdCoreViewportMode(force = false) {
    const html = document.documentElement;
    if (!html) return false;
    const next = getAdCoreViewportMode();
    if (!force && next === adCoreViewportMode) return false;
    html.classList.remove("ad-core-vp-lowh", "ad-core-vp-ultralow");
    if (next) html.classList.add(next);
    adCoreViewportMode = next;
    return true;
  }

  function applyHudMenuFit() {
    const selectors = [".css-19lo6pj", ".css-tkevr6"];
    const avail = Math.max(320, (window.innerWidth || 0) - 24);

    selectors.forEach((sel) => {
      document.querySelectorAll(sel).forEach((el) => {
        if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return;

        try {
          el.style.setProperty("flex-wrap", "nowrap", "important");
          el.style.setProperty("justify-content", "center", "important");
          el.style.setProperty("align-items", "center", "important");
          el.style.setProperty("overflow", "visible", "important");
          Array.from(el.children || []).forEach((child) => {
            if (!(child instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
            child.style.setProperty("flex", "0 0 auto", "important");
          });

          el.style.setProperty("--ad-hud-fit-scale", "1");
          const raw = Math.max(
            el.scrollWidth || 0,
            Array.from(el.children || []).reduce((sum, child) => {
              if (!(child instanceof HTMLElement)) return sum;
              const r = child.getBoundingClientRect();
              return sum + Math.ceil(r.width || 0);
            }, 0) + Math.max(0, (el.children?.length || 0) - 1) * 8
          );

          const fit = raw > avail ? clamp((avail - 4) / Math.max(1, raw), 0.66, 1) : 1;
          el.style.setProperty("--ad-hud-fit-scale", String(Math.round(fit * 1000) / 1000));
        } catch (_) {}
      });
    });
  }

  // v75: Mark only the actual Undo / Next click targets by their visible label.
  // Chakra-generated css-* class names are unstable, therefore we deliberately
  // do not use them here. No DOM node is moved, cloned or removed.
  function markNativeTurnActionButtons() {
    const normalize = (value) => String(value || "")
      .normalize("NFD")
      .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
      .replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}]+/gu, " ")
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase();

    const accepted = new Set([
      "undo", "next",
      "visszavonas", "kovetkezo",
      "zuruck", "weiter"
    ]);

    document.querySelectorAll('button,[role="button"],a').forEach((el) => {
      if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
      const label = normalize(el.innerText || el.textContent);
      if (!accepted.has(label)) return;
      if (el.dataset.adCoreV75TurnAction !== "1") {
        el.dataset.adCoreV75TurnAction = "1";
      }
    });
  }

  /* v76: Align the lower Undo / Next action row using a measured gap instead
     of a hard-coded compact-mode translate. This is intentionally limited to
     the two buttons marked above; their React-owned DOM is never moved or
     removed. */
  function syncNativeTurnActionClearance() {
    const html = document.documentElement;
    if (!html) return;

    const actions = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('[data-ad-core-v75-turn-action="1"]'))
      .filter((el) => el instanceof HTMLElement);
    if (!actions.length) return;

    const compact = html.classList.contains("ad-core-vp-lowh") ||
      html.classList.contains("ad-core-vp-ultralow");
    if (!compact) {
      actions.forEach((el) => el.style.removeProperty("--ad-core-v76-turn-action-shift"));
      return;
    }

    // Reset the variable first so every measurement is based on the native
    // button location rather than on the shift calculated during a previous
    // render pass.
    actions.forEach((el) => {
      el.style.setProperty("--ad-core-v76-turn-action-shift", "0px", "important");
    });

    const playerHost = document.querySelector("#ad-ext-player-display");
    const turnHost = document.querySelector("#ad-ext-turn");
    if (!(playerHost instanceof HTMLElement) || !(turnHost instanceof HTMLElement)) return;

    const panelRects = Array.from(playerHost.children || [])
      .filter((el) => el instanceof HTMLElement && !!el.querySelector(".ad-ext-player"))
      .map((el) => el.getBoundingClientRect())
      .filter((r) => r.width > 80 && r.height > 60);
    if (!panelRects.length) return;

    // Prefer real card surfaces. The selector list intentionally matches only
    // elements already used by the v72 height rule, so the empty turn-band or
    // its non-card wrappers cannot distort the vertical target.
    const cardSelectors = [
      ".ad-ext-turn-throw",
      ".ad-total-cell",
      ".score.css-156dsds",
      ".css-rrf7rv",
      ".css-1dkgpmk",
      ".css-1wlduvp",
      ".css-sm8wdq",
      ".css-881tme"
    ];
    const cardRects = Array.from(turnHost.querySelectorAll(cardSelectors.join(",")))
      .filter((el) => el instanceof HTMLElement)
      .map((el) => el.getBoundingClientRect())
      .filter((r) => r.width > 24 && r.height > 42);

    const turnRect = turnHost.getBoundingClientRect();
    const cardTop = cardRects.length
      ? Math.min(...cardRects.map((r) => r.top))
      : turnRect.top;
    const cardBottom = cardRects.length
      ? Math.max(...cardRects.map((r) => r.bottom))
      : turnRect.bottom;

    if (!(cardBottom > cardTop)) return;

    const playerBottom = Math.max(...panelRects.map((r) => r.bottom));
    // Keep a sensible guard only for edge cases such as a transitional DOM
    // render; normal 1360x768 compact view reports about 16px here.
    const desiredGap = clamp(cardTop - playerBottom, 8, 36);
    const desiredActionTop = cardBottom + desiredGap;

    actions.forEach((el) => {
      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (r.width < 12 || r.height < 12) return;
      const shift = clamp(desiredActionTop - r.top, 0, 120);
      el.style.setProperty(
        "--ad-core-v76-turn-action-shift",
        `${Math.round(shift * 10) / 10}px`,
        "important"
      );
    });
  }

  let adCorePlayerCountMode = "";

  function getAdCorePlayerCountMode() {
    const host = document.querySelector("#ad-ext-player-display");
    if (!host) return "";
    const panels = [...host.children].filter((el) => {
      if (!(el instanceof Element)) return false;
      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      return r.width > 80 && r.height > 80 && !!el.querySelector(".ad-ext-player");
    });
    return panels.length === 2 ? "ad-core-two-player" : "";
  }

  function applyAdCorePlayerCountMode(force = false) {
    const html = document.documentElement;
    if (!html) return false;
    const next = getAdCorePlayerCountMode();
    if (!force && next === adCorePlayerCountMode) return false;
    html.classList.remove("ad-core-two-player");
    if (next) html.classList.add(next);
    adCorePlayerCountMode = next;
    return true;
  }

  function removeLegacyCanvasFitResidue() {
    const html = document.documentElement;
    if (html) {
      html.classList.remove(
        "ad-core-canvas-fit-active",
        "ad-core-canvas-fit-ready",
        "ad-core-canvas-fit-layout-on",
        "ad-core-canvas-fit-layout-off"
      );
    }

    try {
      document.querySelectorAll('style[id*="canvas-fit"], style[id*="ad-core-canvas"]').forEach((el) => {
        // v67-v70 próbaverziókból visszamaradt canvas-fit CSS eltávolítása ugyanazon az oldalon.
        if (el && el.parentNode) el.parentNode.removeChild(el);
      });
    } catch (_) {}

    try {
      const host = document.querySelector("#ad-ext-player-display");
      if (host && host.dataset) {
        Object.keys(host.dataset).forEach((key) => {
          if (/canvas|fit/i.test(key)) delete host.dataset[key];
        });
      }
    } catch (_) {}
  }


  /* ================== UPDATE SCHEDULING ================== */
  let scheduled = false;
  function scheduleUpdate() {
    if (scheduled) return;
    scheduled = true;
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      scheduled = false;

      removeLegacyCanvasFitResidue();

      // v43-diag-v1: előbb a player-szám class, utána a viewport mód.
      // A 1360x606 diagnosztikában ez döntő: ha a viewport mód a two-player class
      // előtt számolódik, ideiglenesen ultralow/félre méretezett geometria jöhet létre.
      const pcChanged = applyAdCorePlayerCountMode();
      let vpChanged = false;
      if (applyAdCoreViewportMode(pcChanged)) {
        vpChanged = true;
      }
      if (pcChanged || vpChanged) {
        dirtyPlayers();
        dirtySkin();
      }

      handleCelebrationFreezeEdge();
      const celebrationActive = isCelebrationActive();

      renderCss();
      applyHudMenuFit();
      markNativeTurnActionButtons();
      syncNativeTurnActionClearance();

      // Skin only if dirty
      if (DIRTY.skin) {
        DIRTY.skin = false;
        ensureSkinCss();
      }
      refreshLayoutPlayerExtras();
      if (!cfg().LAYOUT_ENABLED) cleanupOffLayoutV12OverlayResidue();

      if (DIRTY.board) {
        DIRTY.board = false;
        scheduleBoardMark();
      }

      if (DIRTY.bm) {
        DIRTY.bm = false;
        ensureBmBackButton();
      }

      const c = cfg();

      if (DIRTY.players) {
        if (celebrationActive) {
          adDeferredPlayersRefresh = true;
          clearActiveClasses();
        } else {
          DIRTY.players = false;

          resetLayoutOnWidthFitLimitCollection();
          annotatePlayerCardInfo();
          cleanupOffLayoutV12OverlayResidue();
          //fitGlobalChalkboardToPanel();
          updatePlayerStateOverlay();

          // v14 OFF: a régi OFF overlay csak előkészítő/fallback marad.
          // A tényleges, minden player kártyára érvényes score/name/avg vezérlést
          // ez a külön pipeline végzi, ezért az előző overlayt eltávolítja és
          // a natív elemeket egységesen rejti. Layout ON-hoz nem nyúl.
          if (!c.LAYOUT_ENABLED) forceOffLayoutPipelineV12();

          publishLayoutOnWidthFitLimits();

          if (c.ACTIVE_PLAYER_HIGHLIGHT) updateActivePlayerHighlight();
          else clearActiveClasses();
        }
      }

    //  layoutAllPlayerPointBoards();

      const turn = document.querySelector("#ad-ext-turn");
      if (turn && DIRTY.turn) {
        DIRTY.turn = false;

        withSuppressedTurnObserver(() => {
          forceCenterTotalOverlay(turn);
          updateAllThrowGroups(turn);
        // ✅ mindig visszarakjuk a kijelölt kártyára
          applyStickyThrowSelection(turn);
          markCheckoutInTurnBar(turn);
          if (c.TRIPLE_ANIM) updateTripleHighlight(turn);
        });

        if (c.WIN_MUSIC) scanWinMusic();
      } else {
        // ✅ ha nem volt turn-dirty, akkor is tartsuk életben
        //fitGlobalChalkboardToPanel();
        if (turn) applyStickyThrowSelection(turn);
        if (c.WIN_MUSIC) scanWinMusic();
      }

    syncFullHistoryOverlays();

      if (clockEl) {
        applyClockStyle();
        applyClockScale();
        applyClockPosition();
        applyClockEnabled();
      }
    });
  }

  /* ================== ACTIVE POLL TIMER ================== */
  let activePollTimer = null;
function configureActivePolling() {
  if (activePollTimer) { clearInterval(activePollTimer); activePollTimer = null; }
  const c = cfg();
  if ((c.ACTIVE_POLL_MS | 0) > 0) {
    activePollTimer = setInterval(() => {
      if (isCelebrationActive()) {
        clearActiveClasses();
        return;
      }
      updatePlayerStateOverlay();
      if (c.ACTIVE_PLAYER_HIGHLIGHT) updateActivePlayerHighlight();
    }, c.ACTIVE_POLL_MS | 0);
  }
}

  function applyToggleSideEffects() {
    const c = cfg();
    if (!c.ACTIVE_PLAYER_HIGHLIGHT) clearActiveClasses();
    if (!c.TRIPLE_ANIM) clearTripleClasses();
    if (!cfg().WIN_MUSIC) stopWinAudio();
    if (!cfg().WIN_MUSIC && scopeWin) { scopeWin.abort(); scopeWin = null; }
    configureActivePolling();

    // toggles can affect everything
    dirtyTurn();
    dirtyPlayers();
    dirtyBoard();
    dirtyBm();
    dirtySkin();

    scheduleUpdate();
  }

  /* ================== UI ================== */
  let uiBtn = null;
  let panel = null;
  let toastEl = null;
  let fileInput = null;
  let fontFileInput = null;
  let turnFontFileInput = null;

    return `

           xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true">
        <path d="M10.325 4.317c.426-1.756 2.924-1.756 3.35 0
                 .276 1.134 1.56 1.69 2.573 1.066
                 1.543-.94 3.31.826 2.37 2.37
                 -.624 1.012-.068 2.297 1.066 2.573
                 1.756.426 1.756 2.924 0 3.35

                 .94 1.543-.826 3.31-2.37 2.37

                 -.426 1.756-2.924 1.756-3.35 0
                 -.276-1.134-1.56-1.69-2.573-1.066
