  function ensureHead(cb) {
    if (document.head) return cb();
    const obs = new MutationObserver(() => {
      if (document.head) {
        obs.disconnect();
        cb();
      }
    });
    obs.observe(document.documentElement, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  }

  function clamp(n, min, max) {
    return Math.min(Math.max(n, min), max);
  }

  function isEl(n) {
    return !!(n && n.nodeType === 1 && n instanceof HTMLElement);
  }

  function rowsEqual(a, b) {
    if (!Array.isArray(a) || !Array.isArray(b)) return false;
    if (a.length !== b.length) return false;
    for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
      if ((a[i]?.a || "") !== (b[i]?.a || "")) return false;
      if ((a[i]?.b || "") !== (b[i]?.b || "")) return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  function ensureMem() {
    const key = location.pathname;
    if (historyMem.key !== key) {
      historyMem = {
        key,
        slots: Object.create(null),
      };
      hostMemo = new WeakMap();
      lastHistoryHosts = Object.create(null);
    }
  }

  function getSlotKey(index) {
    return `p${index}`;
  }

  function getSlotState(slotKey) {
    if (!historyMem.slots || typeof historyMem.slots !== "object") {
      historyMem.slots = Object.create(null);
    }
    if (!historyMem.slots[slotKey]) {
      historyMem.slots[slotKey] = { rows: [], turn: -1 };
    }
    return historyMem.slots[slotKey];
  }

  function getHistoryStorageKey() {
    return `${STORAGE_PREFIX}|${location.pathname}`;
  }

  function saveHistoryMem() {
    try {
      const safeSlots = Object.create(null);
      const srcSlots = (historyMem && historyMem.slots && typeof historyMem.slots === "object")
        ? historyMem.slots
        : {};

      for (const [slotKey, slot] of Object.entries(srcSlots)) {
        safeSlots[slotKey] = {
          rows: Array.isArray(slot?.rows) ? slot.rows : [],
          turn: Number.isFinite(slot?.turn) ? slot.turn : -1,
        };
      }

      const payload = {
        key: historyMem.key,
        slots: safeSlots,
        ts: Date.now(),
      };
      sessionStorage.setItem(getHistoryStorageKey(), JSON.stringify(payload));
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  function loadHistoryMem() {
    try {
      const raw = sessionStorage.getItem(getHistoryStorageKey());
      if (!raw) return;

      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== "object") return;

      const slots = Object.create(null);

      if (parsed.slots && typeof parsed.slots === "object") {
        for (const [slotKey, slot] of Object.entries(parsed.slots)) {
          slots[slotKey] = {
            rows: Array.isArray(slot?.rows) ? slot.rows : [],
            turn: Number.isFinite(slot?.turn) ? slot.turn : -1,
          };
        }
      } else {
        if (Array.isArray(parsed.left) || Number.isFinite(parsed.leftTurn)) {
          slots.p0 = {
            rows: Array.isArray(parsed.left) ? parsed.left : [],
            turn: Number.isFinite(parsed.leftTurn) ? parsed.leftTurn : -1,
          };
        }
        if (Array.isArray(parsed.right) || Number.isFinite(parsed.rightTurn)) {
          slots.p1 = {
            rows: Array.isArray(parsed.right) ? parsed.right : [],
            turn: Number.isFinite(parsed.rightTurn) ? parsed.rightTurn : -1,
          };
        }
      }

      historyMem = {
        key: location.pathname,
        slots,
      };
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  function clearHistoryMemStorage() {
    try {
      sessionStorage.removeItem(getHistoryStorageKey());
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  function ensureOverlayRoot() {
  let root = document.getElementById(OVERLAY_ROOT_ID);
  if (root) return root;

  root = document.createElement("div");
  root.id = OVERLAY_ROOT_ID;
  Object.assign(root.style, {
    position: "fixed",
    inset: "0",
    zIndex: "54",
    pointerEvents: "none",
    overflow: "visible",
  });

  document.body.appendChild(root);
  return root;
}

function hideNativeHost(host) {
  if (!host) return;
  if (host.getAttribute("data-ad-history-hidden") === "1") return;

  if (!("adHistOrigVisibility" in host.dataset)) host.dataset.adHistOrigVisibility = host.style.visibility || "";
  if (!("adHistOrigPointerEvents" in host.dataset)) host.dataset.adHistOrigPointerEvents = host.style.pointerEvents || "";
  if (!("adHistOrigOpacity" in host.dataset)) host.dataset.adHistOrigOpacity = host.style.opacity || "";

  host.setAttribute("data-ad-history-hidden", "1");
  host.style.visibility = "hidden";
  host.style.pointerEvents = "none";
  host.style.opacity = "0";
}

function restoreHistoryHost(host) {
  if (!host) return;

  host.style.visibility = host.dataset.adHistOrigVisibility || "";
  host.style.pointerEvents = host.dataset.adHistOrigPointerEvents || "";
  host.style.opacity = host.dataset.adHistOrigOpacity || "";

  delete host.dataset.adHistOrigVisibility;
  delete host.dataset.adHistOrigPointerEvents;
  delete host.dataset.adHistOrigOpacity;

  host.removeAttribute("data-ad-history-hidden");
}

function unhideManagedHistoryHosts() {
  document.querySelectorAll("[data-ad-history-hidden='1']").forEach(restoreHistoryHost);
}

function removeFloatingOverlayBySide(side) {
  const root = document.getElementById(OVERLAY_ROOT_ID);
  if (!root) return;
  root.querySelectorAll(`:scope > .ad-native-history-floating[data-ad-hist-side="${side}"]`).forEach(el => el.remove());
}

function historyRowsReady(rows) {
  if (!Array.isArray(rows)) return false;

  const cleaned = rows.filter(r =>
    String(r?.a || "").trim() || String(r?.b || "").trim()
  );

  if (!cleaned.length) return false;

  // már az induló 501 sort is engedjük,
  // hogy rögtön a nagy overlay jelenjen meg
  if (cleaned.length === 1) {
    const b = String(cleaned[0]?.b || "").trim();
    return /^\d{2,3}$/.test(b);
  }

  return cleaned.some(r => String(r?.a || "").trim());
}

function cleanupPanelLocalHistoryOverlays() {
  document.querySelectorAll("#ad-ext-player-display > div > .ad-native-history-floating").forEach(el => el.remove());
}

function restoreAll(root = document) {
  document.querySelectorAll(".ad-native-history-floating").forEach(el => el.remove());

  const overlayRoot = document.getElementById(OVERLAY_ROOT_ID);
  if (overlayRoot) overlayRoot.remove();

  document.querySelectorAll("[data-ad-history-hidden='1']").forEach(el => {
    el.style.visibility = "";
    el.style.pointerEvents = "";
    el.removeAttribute("data-ad-history-hidden");
  });

  document.querySelectorAll("[data-ad-history-panel-patched='1']").forEach(el => {
    el.style.position = "";
    el.removeAttribute("data-ad-history-panel-patched");
  });
}

function rectIntersects(a, b, pad = 0) {
  if (!a || !b) return false;
  return !(
    a.right < b.left - pad ||
    a.left > b.right + pad ||
    a.bottom < b.top - pad ||
    a.top > b.bottom + pad
  );
}

function scoreHistoryRows(rows) {
  if (!Array.isArray(rows) || !rows.length) return -9999;

  let score = 0;

  if ((rows[0]?.a || "") === "" && /^\d{2,3}$/.test(rows[0]?.b || "")) score += 50;
  if ((rows[0]?.a || "") === "" && (rows[0]?.b || "") === "501") score += 100;

  const rightVals = rows
    .map(r => Number(r?.b))
    .filter(Number.isFinite);

  let descending = 0;
  for (let i = 1; i < rightVals.length; i++) {
    if (rightVals[i] <= rightVals[i - 1]) descending++;
  }
  score += descending * 18;

  const pairedRows = rows.filter(r => (r?.a || "") !== "" && (r?.b || "") !== "").length;
  score += pairedRows * 8;

  return score;
}

  function collectNumericLeaves(root, includeVisibilityHidden = false) {
    if (!isEl(root)) return [];

    const out = [];
    const seen = new Set();

    for (const el of root.querySelectorAll("*")) {
      if (!isEl(el)) continue;
      if (el.closest(".ad-native-history-floating")) continue;
      if (el.children.length > 0) continue;

      const txt = (el.textContent || "").trim();
      if (!/^\d{1,3}$/.test(txt)) continue;

      const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
      if (cs.display === "none") continue;
      if (!includeVisibilityHidden && cs.visibility === "hidden") continue;

      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (r.width <= 0 || r.height <= 0) continue;

      const key = [
        txt,
        Math.round(r.left),
        Math.round(r.top),
        Math.round(r.width),
        Math.round(r.height)
      ].join("|");

      if (seen.has(key)) continue;
      seen.add(key);

      out.push({
        el,
        text: txt,
        left: r.left,
        top: r.top,
        width: r.width,
        height: r.height,
        cx: r.left + r.width / 2,
        cy: r.top + r.height / 2,
      });
    }

    return out;
  }

function findHistoryHost(panel) {
  if (!isEl(panel)) return null;

  const memo = hostMemo.get(panel);
  if (memo && memo.isConnected && panel.contains(memo)) return memo;

  const pr = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
  const panelArea = Math.max(pr.width * pr.height, 1);
  const candidates = [panel, ...panel.querySelectorAll("div, section, article, table, tbody")];

  let best = null;
  let bestScore = -Infinity;

  for (let el of candidates) {
    if (!isEl(el)) continue;
    if (el.classList.contains("ad-native-history-floating")) continue;
    if (el.closest(".ad-native-history-floating")) continue;

    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (r.width < 50) continue;
    if (r.height < 90) continue;
    if (r.width > Math.min(320, pr.width * 0.8)) continue;
    if (r.height > pr.height * 0.9) continue;

    const areaRatio = (r.width * r.height) / panelArea;
    if (areaRatio > 0.40) continue;

    const nums = collectNumericLeaves(el, false);
    if (!nums.length) continue;

    const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
    const borderSum =
      (parseFloat(cs.borderTopWidth) || 0) +
      (parseFloat(cs.borderRightWidth) || 0) +
      (parseFloat(cs.borderBottomWidth) || 0) +
      (parseFloat(cs.borderLeftWidth) || 0);

    const radiusSum =
      (parseFloat(cs.borderTopLeftRadius) || 0) +
      (parseFloat(cs.borderTopRightRadius) || 0) +
      (parseFloat(cs.borderBottomRightRadius) || 0) +
      (parseFloat(cs.borderBottomLeftRadius) || 0);

    let score = 0;
    score += nums.length * 18;
    score += borderSum * 20;
    score += radiusSum * 0.15;
    score += (1 - areaRatio) * 30;
    score += (r.height / Math.max(r.width, 1)) * 20;

    if (nums.length === 1 && nums[0].text === "501") score += 80;
    if (nums.length >= 4) score += 60;

    if (score > bestScore) {
      bestScore = score;
      best = el;
    }
  }

  if (best && best.tagName === "TABLE" && isEl(best.parentElement)) best = best.parentElement;
  if (best && best.tagName === "TBODY" && isEl(best.parentElement)) best = best.parentElement;

  if (best) hostMemo.set(panel, best);
  return best;
}

  function readRowsFromHost(host) {
    if (!isEl(host)) return [];

    const hr = host.getBoundingClientRect();

    const cells = collectNumericLeaves(host, false).filter(c => (
      c.left >= hr.left - 2 &&
      c.left + c.width <= hr.right + 2 &&
      c.top >= hr.top - 2 &&
      c.top + c.height <= hr.bottom + 2
    ));

    if (!cells.length) return [];

    const midX = hr.left + hr.width / 2;

    const left = cells
      .filter(c => c.cx < midX)
      .sort((a, b) => a.cy - b.cy)
      .map(c => c.text);

    const right = cells
      .filter(c => c.cx >= midX)
      .sort((a, b) => a.cy - b.cy)
      .map(c => c.text);

    const rows = [];

    if (right.length === left.length + 1) {
      rows.push({ a: "", b: right[0] });
      for (let i = 0; i < left.length; i++) {
        rows.push({ a: left[i] || "", b: right[i + 1] || "" });
      }
      return rows;
    }

    const len = Math.max(left.length, right.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
      rows.push({ a: left[i] || "", b: right[i] || "" });
    }

    return rows;
  }

  function mergeRows(stored, visible) {
    if (!Array.isArray(visible) || !visible.length) return Array.isArray(stored) ? stored : [];
    if (!Array.isArray(stored) || !stored.length) return visible.slice();

    if (rowsEqual(stored, visible)) return stored;

    if (visible.length <= stored.length) {
      for (let start = 0; start <= stored.length - visible.length; start++) {
        if (rowsEqual(stored.slice(start, start + visible.length), visible)) {
          return stored;
        }
      }
    }

    const max = Math.min(stored.length, visible.length);
    for (let k = max; k >= 1; k--) {
      if (rowsEqual(stored.slice(-k), visible.slice(0, k))) {
        return stored.concat(visible.slice(k));
      }
    }

    if (visible.length > stored.length && rowsEqual(stored, visible.slice(0, stored.length))) {
      return visible.slice();
    }

    return stored;
  }

function parseTurn(panel) {
  if (!panel) return -1;

  const candidates = [
    ...panel.querySelectorAll(".ad-core-visible-avg-row"),
    ...panel.querySelectorAll("[data-ad-history-hidden='1']"),
    ...panel.querySelectorAll("[data-ad-core-overlay-hidden='1']"),
  ];

  for (const el of candidates) {
    const txt = String(el.textContent || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    const m = txt.match(/#\s*(\d+)/);
    if (m) return Number(m[1]);
  }

  const allText = String(panel.textContent || panel.innerText || "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();

  const m = allText.match(/#\s*(\d+)/);
  return m ? Number(m[1]) : -1;
}

  const BOGEY_SCORES = new Set([159, 162, 163, 165, 166, 168, 169]);

  function isCheckoutScore(v) {
    const n = Number(v);
    return Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 2 && n <= 170 && !BOGEY_SCORES.has(n);
  }

  function renderHistoryRow(r, i) {
    return `
      <div class="ad-native-history-row" data-row-index="${i}">
        <div class="ad-native-history-cell">${r?.a || ""}</div>
        <div class="ad-native-history-cell">${r?.b || ""}</div>
      </div>
    `;
  }

function findInfoBlockBottom(panel, host) {
  if (!isEl(panel)) return 0;

  const panelRect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();

  // 1) Elsődlegesen a layout által kiszámolt info-bottomot használjuk
  const fromDataset = Number(panel.dataset.adCoreInfoBottom);
  if (Number.isFinite(fromDataset) && fromDataset > 0) {
    return clamp(
      Math.round(fromDataset),
      Math.round(panelRect.height * 0.22),
      Math.round(panelRect.height * 0.72)
    );
  }

  const bottoms = [];

  const pushBottom = (el) => {
    if (!isEl(el)) return;
    if (host && (el === host || host.contains(el))) return;

    const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
    if (cs.display === "none" || cs.visibility === "hidden") return;
    if ((parseFloat(cs.opacity) || 1) <= 0) return;

    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (r.width < 8 || r.height < 8) return;

    bottoms.push(r.bottom - panelRect.top);
  };

  pushBottom(panel.querySelector(".ad-core-player-score"));
  pushBottom(panel.querySelector(".ad-core-visible-meta-row"));
  pushBottom(panel.querySelector(".ad-core-visible-avg-row"));

  if (bottoms.length) {
    return clamp(
      Math.round(Math.max(...bottoms)),
      Math.round(panelRect.height * 0.24),
      Math.round(panelRect.height * 0.72)
    );
  }

  const priority = [];
  const fallback = [];
  const panelArea = Math.max(1, panelRect.width * panelRect.height);

  for (const el of panel.querySelectorAll("*")) {
    if (!isEl(el)) continue;
    if (el === host) continue;
    if (host && host.contains(el)) continue;
    if (el.closest(".ad-native-history-floating")) continue;

    const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
    if (cs.display === "none" || cs.visibility === "hidden") continue;

    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (r.width < 8 || r.height < 8) continue;
    if (r.bottom <= panelRect.top || r.top >= panelRect.bottom) continue;

    const relTop = r.top - panelRect.top;
    const relBottom = r.bottom - panelRect.top;
    if (relTop > panelRect.height * 0.72) continue;

    const area = r.width * r.height;
    if (area > panelArea * 0.35) continue;

    const txt = (el.textContent || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    const cx = r.left + r.width / 2;
    const relCx = cx - panelRect.left;

    if (relCx < panelRect.width * 0.12 || relCx > panelRect.width * 0.88) continue;

    const looksImportant =
      txt.includes("Ø") ||
      txt.includes("#") ||
      /[A-Za-zÁÉÍÓÖŐÚÜŰáéíóöőúüű]/.test(txt) ||
      el.tagName === "IMG" ||
      el.tagName === "SVG" ||
      el.tagName === "CANVAS";

    const pureScore = /^\d{1,3}$/.test(txt);

    if (looksImportant && !pureScore) {
      priority.push(relBottom);
    } else {
      fallback.push(relBottom);
    }
  }

  const best = priority.length
    ? Math.max(...priority)
    : fallback.length
      ? Math.max(...fallback)
      : panelRect.height * 0.36;

  return clamp(
    Math.round(best),
    Math.round(panelRect.height * 0.28),
    Math.round(panelRect.height * 0.72)
  );
}

  function getTextMetrics(rows, fontSize, colGap, padX) {
    const maxLeftLen = Math.max(1, ...(rows || []).map(r => String(r?.a || "").length));
    const maxRightLen = Math.max(1, ...(rows || []).map(r => String(r?.b || "").length));
    const digitW = fontSize * 0.60;

    const contentW = Math.ceil(
      (maxLeftLen * digitW) +
      (maxRightLen * digitW) +
      colGap +
      (padX * 2) +
      24
    );

    return { maxLeftLen, maxRightLen, contentW };
  }

function getFloatingBoxGeometry(panel, host, rows) {
  const panelRect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
  const panelW = Math.round(panelRect.width);
  const panelH = Math.round(panelRect.height);
  const cardScale = clamp(Math.min(panelW / 411, panelH / 620), 0.48, 1);

  const isWideCard = panelW > panelH * 1.18;
  const rowCount = Math.max(1, Array.isArray(rows) ? rows.length : 0);
  const visibleRows = clamp(rowCount, 7, 14);

  let left, top, width, height;
  let fontSize, colGap, padX, rowGap, padTop, padBottom;

  if (isWideCard) {
    // OFF/ON közös history overlay: széles kártyán legyen nagyobb és végig görgethető.
    left = Math.round(panelW * 0.715);
    top = Math.round(panelH * 0.035);
    width = Math.round(panelW * 0.255);
    height = Math.round(panelH * 0.93);

    fontSize = clamp(((height + 4) / visibleRows) * 1.02, 26, 50);
    colGap = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * 0.12), 6, 11);
    padX = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * 0.18), 6, 11);
    rowGap = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * 0.09), 4, 9);
    padTop = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * 0.12), 6, 10);
    padBottom = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * 0.16), 6, 12);
  } else {
    const infoBottom = findInfoBlockBottom(panel, host);
    const gapBelowInfo = Math.round(clamp(10 + (cardScale * 8), 10, 18));
    const bottomPad = Math.max(8, Math.round(panelH * 0.035));

    width = Math.round(clamp(panelW * (cardScale <= 0.64 ? 0.76 : cardScale <= 0.78 ? 0.80 : 0.84), 112, panelW - 14));
    left = Math.round((panelW - width) / 2);

    top = Math.max(
      infoBottom + gapBelowInfo,
      Math.round(panelH * (cardScale <= 0.58 ? 0.44 : cardScale <= 0.72 ? 0.48 : 0.52))
    );

    height = Math.max(78, panelH - top - bottomPad);

    fontSize = clamp(((height - 6) / visibleRows) * (cardScale <= 0.68 ? 0.72 : 0.84), 17, 38);
    colGap = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * 0.12), 5, 12);
    padX = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * 0.18), 5, 12);
    rowGap = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * 0.10), 4, 9);
    padTop = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * 0.12), 5, 10);
    padBottom = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * 0.16), 5, 12);
  }

  const metrics = getTextMetrics(rows, fontSize, colGap, padX);
  const fontFromWidth =
    (width - (padX * 2) - colGap - 18) /
    ((metrics.maxLeftLen + metrics.maxRightLen) * 0.60);

  const widthSafeFont = isWideCard ? (fontFromWidth * 1.02) : (fontFromWidth * 0.96);

  fontSize = clamp(
    Math.min(fontSize, widthSafeFont),
    isWideCard ? 28 : 17,
    isWideCard ? 52 : 38
  );

  colGap = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * 0.12), 5, 12);
  padX = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * 0.18), 5, 12);
  rowGap = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * 0.10), 4, 9);
  padTop = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * 0.12), 5, 10);
  padBottom = clamp(Math.round(fontSize * 0.16), 5, 12);

  return {
    left: Math.round(left),
    top: Math.round(top),
    width: Math.round(width),
    height: Math.round(height),
    font: Math.round(fontSize),
    rowGap,
    colGap,
    padX,
    padTop,
    padBottom,
  };
}

  function drawHistoryConnectors(box, rows) {
    const list = box.querySelector(".ad-native-history-list");
    if (!list) return;

    let layer = list.querySelector(":scope > .ad-native-history-connectors");
    if (!layer) {
      layer = document.createElement("div");
      layer.className = "ad-native-history-connectors";
      list.appendChild(layer);
    }

    layer.innerHTML = "";

    const rowEls = Array.from(list.querySelectorAll(":scope > .ad-native-history-row"));
    if (rowEls.length < 2) return;

    const listRect = list.getBoundingClientRect();

    for (let i = 1; i < rowEls.length; i++) {
      const prevRemain = Number(rows?.[i - 1]?.b);
      if (!isCheckoutScore(prevRemain)) continue;

      const prevRow = rowEls[i - 1];
      const currRow = rowEls[i];

      const prevRight = prevRow.children[1];
      const currLeft = currRow.children[0];
      if (!prevRight || !currLeft) continue;

      const r1 = prevRight.getBoundingClientRect();
      const r2 = currLeft.getBoundingClientRect();

      const x1 = r1.left + r1.width * 0.54 - listRect.left;
      const y1 = r1.top + r1.height * 0.56 - listRect.top;

      const x2 = r2.left + r2.width * 0.46 - listRect.left;
      const y2 = r2.top + r2.height * 0.44 - listRect.top;

      const dx = x2 - x1;
      const dy = y2 - y1;
      const baseLen = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
      if (baseLen < 1) continue;

      const ux = dx / baseLen;
      const uy = dy / baseLen;

      const extendStart = Math.max(12, Math.min(r1.width, r1.height) * CONNECTOR_EXTEND_FACTOR);
      const extendEnd = Math.max(12, Math.min(r2.width, r2.height) * CONNECTOR_EXTEND_FACTOR);

      const sx = x1 - ux * extendStart;
      const sy = y1 - uy * extendStart;
      const ex = x2 + ux * extendEnd;
      const ey = y2 + uy * extendEnd;

      const finalDx = ex - sx;
      const finalDy = ey - sy;
      const finalLen = Math.hypot(finalDx, finalDy);
      const angle = Math.atan2(finalDy, finalDx) * 180 / Math.PI;

      const line = document.createElement("div");
      line.className = "ad-native-history-connector";
      line.style.left = `${sx}px`;
      line.style.top = `${sy}px`;
      line.style.width = `${finalLen}px`;
      line.style.transform = `rotate(${angle}deg)`;

      layer.appendChild(line);
    }
  }

function mountFloatingOverlay(panel, host, rows, side, opts = {}) {
  if (!panel || !host || !side) return;

  const geometryOnly = !!opts.geometryOnly;

  ensureStyle();
  hideNativeHost(host);

  const root = ensureOverlayRoot();
  const panelRect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
  const geom = getFloatingBoxGeometry(panel, host, rows);
  const fontStack = getPlayerCardResolvedFontStack();

  let box = root.querySelector(`:scope > .ad-native-history-floating[data-ad-hist-side="${side}"]`);
  if (!box) {
    box = document.createElement("div");
    box.className = "ad-native-history-floating";
    box.dataset.adHistSide = side;
    root.appendChild(box);
  }

  box.dataset.adHistSide = side;
  box.style.setProperty("--ad-hist-font-family", fontStack);
  box.style.setProperty("font-family", fontStack, "important");
  box.style.setProperty("position", "fixed", "important");
  box.style.left = `${Math.round(panelRect.left + geom.left)}px`;
  box.style.top = `${Math.round(panelRect.top + geom.top)}px`;
  box.style.width = `${geom.width}px`;
  box.style.height = `${geom.height}px`;
  box.style.setProperty("--ad-hist-font", `${geom.font}px`);
  box.style.setProperty("--ad-hist-row-gap", `${geom.rowGap}px`);
  box.style.setProperty("--ad-hist-col-gap", `${geom.colGap}px`);
  box.style.setProperty("--ad-hist-pad-x", `${geom.padX}px`);
  box.style.setProperty("--ad-hist-pad-t", `${geom.padTop}px`);
  box.style.setProperty("--ad-hist-pad-b", `${geom.padBottom}px`);

  let scroll = box.querySelector(":scope > .ad-native-history-scroll");
  const prevScrollTop = scroll ? scroll.scrollTop : 0;

  const sig = JSON.stringify(rows || []);
  const geomSig = `${panelRect.left}|${panelRect.top}|${geom.left}|${geom.top}|${geom.width}|${geom.height}|${geom.font}|${geom.rowGap}|${geom.colGap}|${geom.padX}|${geom.padTop}|${geom.padBottom}`;
  const contentChanged = !geometryOnly && (box.dataset.adSig !== sig);
  const geomChanged = box.dataset.adGeomSig !== geomSig;

  if (!scroll || contentChanged) {
    box.innerHTML = `
      <div class="ad-native-history-scroll">
        <div class="ad-native-history-list">
          ${(rows || []).map((r, i) => renderHistoryRow(r, i)).join("")}
          <div class="ad-native-history-connectors"></div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
    scroll = box.querySelector(":scope > .ad-native-history-scroll");
    box.dataset.adSig = sig;
  }

  box.dataset.adGeomSig = geomSig;

  box
    .querySelectorAll(".ad-native-history-scroll, .ad-native-history-list, .ad-native-history-row, .ad-native-history-cell")
    .forEach((el) => {
      el.style.setProperty("font-family", fontStack, "important");
    });

  requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    drawHistoryConnectors(box, rows || []);

    // Keep this legacy helper consistent with the active own-history renderer:
    // every data or geometry refresh lands on the newest available throw.
    if (scroll && (contentChanged || geomChanged)) {
      const scrollToLatest = () => {
        scroll.scrollTop = Math.max(0, scroll.scrollHeight - scroll.clientHeight);
      };
      scrollToLatest();
      requestAnimationFrame(scrollToLatest);
      setTimeout(scrollToLatest, 0);
    }
  });

  if (scroll && !scroll.dataset.adWheelBound) {
    scroll.dataset.adWheelBound = "1";
    scroll.addEventListener("wheel", e => {
      e.stopPropagation();
    }, { passive: true });
  }
}

  function cleanupHistoryOverlays(display, leftPanel, rightPanel) {
  const panels = Array.from(display.children).filter(isEl);

  for (const panel of panels) {
    const box = panel.querySelector(":scope > .ad-native-history-floating");
    if (!box) continue;

    const side = box.dataset.adHistSide || "";

    if (panel !== leftPanel && panel !== rightPanel) {
      box.remove();
      continue;
    }

    if (side === "left" && panel !== leftPanel) {
      box.remove();
      continue;
    }

    if (side === "right" && panel !== rightPanel) {
      box.remove();
      continue;
    }
  }
}

  function getOverlaySideCenterX(side) {
  const root = document.getElementById(OVERLAY_ROOT_ID);
  if (!root) return null;

  const box = root.querySelector(`:scope > .ad-native-history-floating[data-ad-hist-side="${side}"]`);
  if (!box) return null;

  const left = parseFloat(box.style.left);
  const width = parseFloat(box.style.width);

  if (!Number.isFinite(left) || !Number.isFinite(width)) return null;
  return left + (width / 2);
}

function pickStableSidePanels(panels) {
  const sorted = panels.slice().sort((a, b) => {
    const ra = a.getBoundingClientRect();
    const rb = b.getBoundingClientRect();

    if (Math.abs(ra.left - rb.left) > 4) return ra.left - rb.left;
    return ra.top - rb.top;
  });

  if (sorted.length < 2) {
    return { left: null, right: null };
  }

  const centerX = (el) => {
    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    return r.left + r.width / 2;
  };

  const prevLeftX =
    (isEl(lastSidePanels.left) && lastSidePanels.left.isConnected)
      ? centerX(lastSidePanels.left)
      : getOverlaySideCenterX("left");

  const prevRightX =
    (isEl(lastSidePanels.right) && lastSidePanels.right.isConnected)
      ? centerX(lastSidePanels.right)
      : getOverlaySideCenterX("right");

  let left = sorted[0];
  let right = sorted[sorted.length - 1];

  if (Number.isFinite(prevLeftX)) {
    left = sorted.slice().sort((a, b) =>
      Math.abs(centerX(a) - prevLeftX) - Math.abs(centerX(b) - prevLeftX)
    )[0] || left;
  }

  const remaining = sorted.filter(p => p !== left);

  if (Number.isFinite(prevRightX) && remaining.length) {
    right = remaining.slice().sort((a, b) =>
      Math.abs(centerX(a) - prevRightX) - Math.abs(centerX(b) - prevRightX)
    )[0] || remaining[remaining.length - 1];
  } else {
    right = remaining[remaining.length - 1] || right;
  }

  if (!right || left === right) {
    left = sorted[0];
    right = sorted[sorted.length - 1];
  }

  if (centerX(left) > centerX(right)) {
    [left, right] = [right, left];
  }

  lastSidePanels = { left, right };
  return { left, right };
}

function syncHistory() {
  function normCell(v) {
    return String(v || "").trim();
  }

  function isLikelyFreshLeg(rows, turn) {
    if (!Array.isArray(rows) || !rows.length) return false;
    const first = rows[0] || {};
    const firstA = normCell(first.a);
    const firstB = normCell(first.b);

    return (
      Number.isFinite(turn) &&
      turn <= 1 &&
      firstA === "" &&
      firstB === "501"
    );
  }

  function trimStoredForUndo(stored, prevTurn, currTurn) {
    const base = Array.isArray(stored) ? stored.slice() : [];

    if (!Number.isFinite(prevTurn) || !Number.isFinite(currTurn)) return base;
    if (prevTurn < 0 || currTurn < 0) return base;
    if (currTurn >= prevTurn) return base;

    const diff = Math.max(0, prevTurn - currTurn);
    if (!diff) return base;

    return base.slice(0, Math.max(0, base.length - diff));
  }

  function reconcileHistoryWithUndo(stored, visible, prevTurn, currTurn) {
    const trimmed = trimStoredForUndo(stored, prevTurn, currTurn);
    return mergeRows(trimmed, visible);
  }

  if (location.pathname !== routeKey) {
    routeKey = location.pathname;
    storageLoaded = false;
    ensureMem();
    stopCelebrationHistoryFollow();
    restoreAll();
    lastHistoryHosts = Object.create(null);
  }

  const display = document.querySelector("#ad-ext-player-display");
  if (!display) {
    stopCelebrationHistoryFollow();
    restoreAll();
    lastHistoryHosts = Object.create(null);
    return;
  }

  const panels = Array.from(display.children)
    .filter(isEl)
    .filter((el) => {
      const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      return cs.display !== "none" &&
             cs.visibility !== "hidden" &&
             r.width > 80 &&
             r.height > 120;
    })
    .sort((a, b) => {
      const ra = a.getBoundingClientRect();
      const rb = b.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (Math.abs(ra.left - rb.left) > 4) return ra.left - rb.left;
      return ra.top - rb.top;
    });

  if (!panels.length) {
    stopCelebrationHistoryFollow();
    restoreAll();
    lastHistoryHosts = Object.create(null);
    return;
  }

  if (panels.length > 2) {
    stopCelebrationHistoryFollow();
    restoreAll();
    lastHistoryHosts = Object.create(null);
    historyMem.slots = Object.create(null);
    return;
  }

  ensureMem();

  if (!storageLoaded) {
    loadHistoryMem();
    storageLoaded = true;
  }

  unhideManagedHistoryHosts();
  cleanupPanelLocalHistoryOverlays();
  hostMemo = new WeakMap();

  const seenSlots = new Set();
  const nextHosts = Object.create(null);

  for (let i = 0; i < panels.length; i++) {
    const panel = panels[i];
    const slotKey = getSlotKey(i);
    seenSlots.add(slotKey);

    const host = findHistoryHost(panel);
    const prevHost = lastHistoryHosts[slotKey] || null;

    if (!host) {
      if (prevHost) restoreHistoryHost(prevHost);
      removeFloatingOverlayBySide(slotKey);
      delete historyMem.slots?.[slotKey];
      continue;
    }

    if (prevHost && prevHost !== host) {
      restoreHistoryHost(prevHost);
      removeFloatingOverlayBySide(slotKey);
    }

    const visibleRows = readRowsFromHost(host);
    const turn = parseTurn(panel);
    const slotState = getSlotState(slotKey);

    if (isLikelyFreshLeg(visibleRows, turn)) {
      slotState.rows = visibleRows.slice();
    } else {
      slotState.rows = reconcileHistoryWithUndo(
        slotState.rows,
        visibleRows,
        slotState.turn,
        turn
      );
    }

    if (Number.isFinite(turn) && turn >= 0) {
      slotState.turn = turn;
    }

    if (historyRowsReady(slotState.rows)) {
      hideNativeHost(host);
      mountFloatingOverlay(panel, host, slotState.rows, slotKey);
    } else {
      restoreHistoryHost(host);
      removeFloatingOverlayBySide(slotKey);
    }

    nextHosts[slotKey] = host;
  }

  for (const [slotKey, host] of Object.entries(lastHistoryHosts)) {
    if (seenSlots.has(slotKey)) continue;
    if (host) restoreHistoryHost(host);
    removeFloatingOverlayBySide(slotKey);
  }

  const prunedSlots = Object.create(null);
  for (const slotKey of seenSlots) {
    const slot = historyMem.slots?.[slotKey];
    if (slot) prunedSlots[slotKey] = slot;
  }
  historyMem.slots = prunedSlots;
  lastHistoryHosts = nextHosts;

  saveHistoryMem();
}

  let celebrationFollowRaf = 0;

  function stopCelebrationHistoryFollow() {
    if (celebrationFollowRaf) {
      cancelAnimationFrame(celebrationFollowRaf);
      celebrationFollowRaf = 0;
    }
  }

  function celebrationHistoryFollowTick() {
    celebrationFollowRaf = 0;

    if (!(typeof isCelebrationActive === "function" && isCelebrationActive())) {
      return;
    }

    const display = document.querySelector("#ad-ext-player-display");
    if (!display) {
      return;
    }

    const panels = Array.from(display.children)
      .filter(isEl)
      .filter((el) => {
        const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
        const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
        return cs.display !== "none" &&
               cs.visibility !== "hidden" &&
               r.width > 80 &&
               r.height > 120;
      })
      .sort((a, b) => {
        const ra = a.getBoundingClientRect();
        const rb = b.getBoundingClientRect();
        if (Math.abs(ra.left - rb.left) > 4) return ra.left - rb.left;
        return ra.top - rb.top;
      });

    if (panels.length > 2) {
      return;
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < panels.length; i++) {
      const panel = panels[i];
      const slotKey = getSlotKey(i);
      const host = lastHistoryHosts[slotKey] && panel.contains(lastHistoryHosts[slotKey])
        ? lastHistoryHosts[slotKey]
        : findHistoryHost(panel);
      const slotState = getSlotState(slotKey);

      if (panel && host && historyRowsReady(slotState.rows)) {
        mountFloatingOverlay(panel, host, slotState.rows, slotKey, { geometryOnly: true });
      }
    }

    celebrationFollowRaf = requestAnimationFrame(celebrationHistoryFollowTick);
  }

  function ensureCelebrationHistoryFollow() {
    if (celebrationFollowRaf) return;
    celebrationFollowRaf = requestAnimationFrame(celebrationHistoryFollowTick);
  }

  function scheduleSync() {
    if (scheduled) return;
    scheduled = true;
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      scheduled = false;

      if (typeof isCelebrationActive === "function" && isCelebrationActive()) {
        ensureCelebrationHistoryFollow();
        return;
      }

      stopCelebrationHistoryFollow();
      syncHistory();
    });
  }

  function startLegacyHistory() {
    ensureHead(() => {
      ensureStyle();

      const obs = new MutationObserver(() => {
        scheduleSync();
      });

      obs.observe(document.documentElement, {
        subtree: true,
        childList: true,
        characterData: true,
        attributes: true,
      });

      window.addEventListener("resize", scheduleSync, { passive: true });
      window.addEventListener("fullscreenchange", scheduleSync, { passive: true });

      setTimeout(scheduleSync, 60);
      setTimeout(scheduleSync, 250);
      setTimeout(scheduleSync, 700);
      setInterval(scheduleSync, 1200);
    });
  }

  // Disabled: legacy history system caused duplicate floating history overlays in 2-player mode.
  // startLegacyHistory();
})();


  /* ========================================================================
     v67 – V65 NATIVE PAGE BASE + FULL ORIGINAL CORE UI/FUNCTIONS
     ------------------------------------------------------------------------
     This block intentionally replaces only the old player-card/page-layout
     writers. The v60 panel, language system, presets, import/export, clock,
     board marker, throw cards, audio and all original controls stay intact.

     Page rules:
       - Layout OFF: factory/native Autodarts page geometry.
       - Layout ON (2 players): bounded side cards only; no #root scaling,
         no global overflow lock, no absolute inner-card coordinates.
       - Native history is never moved, cloned or removed.
     ======================================================================== */

  const AD_CORE_V67_NATIVE_PAGE_ENGINE = true;
  const AD_CORE_V67_STYLE_ID = "ad-core-v67-native-page-style";
  const AD_CORE_V67_PLAYER_STYLE_ID = "ad-core-v67-native-player-style";
  const AD_CORE_V67_OWNED_OVERLAY_IDS = [
    "ad-native-history-overlay-root-core",
    "ad-history-only-overlay-root",
    "ad-core-clean-native-v65-style",
    "ad-core-clean-native-v65-ui"
  ];
  const AD_CORE_V67_FONT_BASE_CACHE = new WeakMap();
  const AD_CORE_V67_INLINE_BACKUP = new WeakMap();

  function adCoreV67Num(v, min, max, fallback) {
    const n = Number(v);
    return Number.isFinite(n) ? Math.min(Math.max(n, min), max) : fallback;
  }

  function adCoreV67Rgb(hex) {
    const h = sanitizeHex(hex, "#ffffff");
    return `${parseInt(h.slice(1, 3), 16)}, ${parseInt(h.slice(3, 5), 16)}, ${parseInt(h.slice(5, 7), 16)}`;
  }

  function adCoreV67RememberInline(el, property) {
    if (!el || !el.style) return;
    let mem = AD_CORE_V67_INLINE_BACKUP.get(el);
    if (!mem) {
      mem = new Map();
      AD_CORE_V67_INLINE_BACKUP.set(el, mem);
    }
    if (!mem.has(property)) {
      mem.set(property, {
        value: el.style.getPropertyValue(property),
        priority: el.style.getPropertyPriority(property)
      });
    }
  }

  function adCoreV67SetInline(el, property, value) {
    if (!el || !el.style) return;
    adCoreV67RememberInline(el, property);
    el.style.setProperty(property, value, "important");
  }

  function adCoreV67RestoreInline(el, property) {
    const mem = AD_CORE_V67_INLINE_BACKUP.get(el);
    if (!mem || !mem.has(property) || !el?.style) return;
    const prev = mem.get(property);
    if (prev.value) el.style.setProperty(property, prev.value, prev.priority || "");
    else el.style.removeProperty(property);
    mem.delete(property);
  }

  function adCoreV67BaseFont(el) {
    if (!el) return 16;
    if (AD_CORE_V67_FONT_BASE_CACHE.has(el)) return AD_CORE_V67_FONT_BASE_CACHE.get(el);
    const px = Number.parseFloat(getComputedStyle(el).fontSize) || 16;
    AD_CORE_V67_FONT_BASE_CACHE.set(el, px);
    return px;
  }

  function adCoreV67Panels() {
    const host = document.querySelector("#ad-ext-player-display");
    if (!host) return [];
    return Array.from(host.children || []).filter((panel) =>
      panel && panel.nodeType === 1 && !!panel.querySelector(".ad-ext-player-score")
    );
  }

  function adCoreV67FindContentRoot(player, score) {
    let node = score?.parentElement || null;
    while (node && node !== player) {
      if (node.querySelector?.(".ad-ext-player-name") && (node.children?.length || 0) >= 2) return node;
      node = node.parentElement;
    }
    return null;
  }

  function adCoreV67DirectChild(root, target) {
    let node = target || null;
    while (node && node.parentElement !== root) node = node.parentElement;
    return node || null;
  }

  function adCoreV67FindAverageBox(root, scoreBox, nameBox) {
    if (!root) return null;
    const direct = Array.from(root.children || []);
    return direct.find((child) => {
      if (child === scoreBox || child === nameBox) return false;
      const text = String(child.textContent || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
      return /(?:∅|Ø|average|avg|#\s*\d+\s*\|)/i.test(text);
    }) || null;
  }

  function adCoreV67CleanupLegacyOwnedArtifacts() {
    // Only script-owned overlay artifacts are deleted. Native React nodes are never removed.
    document.querySelectorAll(".ad-native-history-floating:not([data-ad-core-own-history='1']), .ad-core-off-v12-overlay, .ad-core-off-v15-overlay, .ad-core-off-v40-overlay, .ad-core-player-top-overlay, .ad-core-player-top-overlay-off-v43").forEach((el) => {
      try { el.remove(); } catch (_) {}
    });
    AD_CORE_V67_OWNED_OVERLAY_IDS.forEach((id) => {
      const node = document.getElementById(id);
      if (node?.parentNode) {
        try { node.parentNode.removeChild(node); } catch (_) {}
      }
    });
    document.querySelectorAll("[data-ad-history-hidden='1']").forEach((el) => {
      if (el.dataset?.adCoreOwnHistorySource === "1") return;
      try {
        el.style.removeProperty("visibility");
        el.style.removeProperty("opacity");
        el.style.removeProperty("pointer-events");
        el.removeAttribute("data-ad-history-hidden");
        delete el.dataset.adHistOrigVisibility;
        delete el.dataset.adHistOrigPointerEvents;
        delete el.dataset.adHistOrigOpacity;
      } catch (_) {}
    });
  }

  function adCoreV67ApplyRootClasses() {
    const root = document.documentElement;
    const c = cfg();
    const panels = adCoreV67Panels();
    root.classList.add("ad-core-v67-native-page");
    root.classList.toggle("ad-core-v67-two-player", !!c.LAYOUT_ENABLED && panels.length === 2);
    root.classList.toggle("ad-core-layout-on", !!c.LAYOUT_ENABLED);
    root.classList.toggle("ad-core-layout-off", !c.LAYOUT_ENABLED);
    root.classList.toggle("ad-core-layout-name-above", c.LAYOUT_NAME_PLACEMENT === "above");
    root.classList.toggle("ad-core-layout-avg-above", c.LAYOUT_AVG_PLACEMENT === "above");
  }

  function adCoreV67PlayerExtrasCss() {
    const c = cfg();
    const fontStack = getPlayerCardResolvedFontStack();
    const scoreScale = adCoreV67Num(Number(c.LAYOUT_SCORE_FONT_PX ?? 100) / 100, 0.60, 1.80, 1);
    const nameScale = adCoreV67Num(c.LAYOUT_NAME_ROW_SCALE, 0.60, 1.80, 1);
    const avgScale = adCoreV67Num(c.LAYOUT_AVG_ROW_SCALE, 0.60, 1.80, 1);
    const nameOrder = c.LAYOUT_NAME_PLACEMENT === "above" ? 10 : 30;
    const avgOrder = c.LAYOUT_AVG_PLACEMENT === "above" ? 11 : 31;

    return `
/* v67 safe player content: native outer page/card dimensions are untouched. */
#ad-ext-player-display{
  --ad-core-v67-player-font: ${fontStack};
}

#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-v67-content,
#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-v67-content *{
  font-family: var(--ad-core-v67-player-font) !important;
}

#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-v67-hide-name .ad-ext-player-name{
  display:none !important;
}
#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-v67-hide-avg .ad-core-v67-avgbox{
  display:none !important;
}

html.ad-core-layout-off #ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-v67-content{
  font-family: var(--ad-core-v67-player-font) !important;
}

/* Two-sided/ON: only the existing native flex children are reordered. */
html.ad-core-layout-on #ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-v67-content{
  display:flex !important;
  flex-direction:column !important;
  align-items:center !important;
  justify-content:center !important;
  align-content:center !important;
  flex:1 1 auto !important;
  min-width:0 !important;
  min-height:0 !important;
  max-width:100% !important;
  box-sizing:border-box !important;
  gap:clamp(4px,.65vh,11px) !important;
  padding:clamp(8px,1.2vw,16px) !important;
  overflow:hidden !important;
}
html.ad-core-layout-on #ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-v67-scorebox{
  order:20 !important;
  flex:0 0 auto !important;
  min-width:0 !important;
  max-width:100% !important;
  display:flex !important;
  align-items:center !important;
  justify-content:center !important;
}
html.ad-core-layout-on #ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-v67-namebox{
  order:${nameOrder} !important;
  flex:0 0 auto !important;
  min-width:0 !important;
  max-width:100% !important;
  display:flex !important;
  align-items:center !important;
  justify-content:center !important;
}
html.ad-core-layout-on #ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-v67-avgbox{
  order:${avgOrder} !important;
  flex:0 0 auto !important;
  min-width:0 !important;
  max-width:100% !important;
  display:flex !important;
  align-items:center !important;
  justify-content:center !important;
}
html.ad-core-layout-on #ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-v67-scoretext{
  font-size:clamp(40px, calc(${scoreScale} * 8vw), 180px) !important;
  line-height:.92 !important;
  font-weight:900 !important;
  white-space:nowrap !important;
  max-width:100% !important;
  overflow:visible !important;
  text-align:center !important;
}
html.ad-core-layout-on #ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-v67-namebox,
html.ad-core-layout-on #ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-v67-namebox *{
  font-size:clamp(12px, calc(${nameScale} * 1.35vw), 28px) !important;
  line-height:1.05 !important;
  font-weight:800 !important;
  white-space:nowrap !important;
  min-width:0 !important;
}
html.ad-core-layout-on #ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-v67-avgbox,
html.ad-core-layout-on #ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-v67-avgbox *{
  font-size:clamp(11px, calc(${avgScale} * 1.10vw), 24px) !important;
  line-height:1.05 !important;
  font-weight:800 !important;
  white-space:nowrap !important;
  min-width:0 !important;
}

/* Explicitly suppress only former script-owned floating history copies. */
.ad-native-history-floating,
#ad-native-history-overlay-root-core,
#ad-history-only-overlay-root{
  display:none !important;
}
`;
  }

  function adCoreV67SkinCss() {
    const c = cfg();

  --ad-core-v67-overlay-alpha:${overlay};
  --ad-core-v67-player-rgb:${playerRgb};
  --ad-core-v67-player-opacity:${playerOpacity};
  --ad-core-v67-bg:url("${bgUrl}");
}

  // Replaces old Stylebot-era whole-root scale/layout CSS.
  function ensureSkinCss() {
    ensureHead(() => {
      let st = document.getElementById(EXTRA_STYLE_ID);
      if (!st) {
        st = document.createElement("style");
        st.id = EXTRA_STYLE_ID;
        document.head.appendChild(st);
      }
      st.textContent = adCoreV67SkinCss();
      const core = document.getElementById(STYLE_ID);
      if (core?.parentNode && st.parentNode === core.parentNode && core.nextSibling !== st) core.after(st);
    });
  }

  // Replaces every legacy player-card extras stylesheet with the safe v65-style engine.
  function buildLayoutPlayerExtrasCss() {
    return adCoreV67PlayerExtrasCss();
  }

  function refreshLayoutPlayerExtras() {
    ensureHead(() => {
      ensurePlayerCardFontFace();
      let st = document.getElementById(PLAYER_LAYOUT_EXTRAS_ID);
      if (!st) {
        st = document.createElement("style");
        st.id = PLAYER_LAYOUT_EXTRAS_ID;
        document.head.appendChild(st);
      }
      st.textContent = adCoreV67PlayerExtrasCss();
      const extraSkin = document.getElementById(EXTRA_STYLE_ID);
      const core = document.getElementById(STYLE_ID);
      if (extraSkin?.parentNode) {
        if (st.parentNode !== extraSkin.parentNode) extraSkin.parentNode.appendChild(st);
        if (extraSkin.nextSibling !== st) extraSkin.after(st);
      } else if (core?.parentNode) {
        if (st.parentNode !== core.parentNode) core.parentNode.appendChild(st);
        if (core.nextSibling !== st) core.after(st);
      }
      adCoreV67ApplyRootClasses();
    });
  }

  // Replaces all historical score/meta/history geometry writers.
  function annotatePlayerCardInfo() {
    const c = cfg();
    adCoreV67CleanupLegacyOwnedArtifacts();
    adCoreV67ApplyRootClasses();

    for (const panel of adCoreV67Panels()) {
      const player = panel.querySelector(".ad-ext-player") || panel;
      const score = player.querySelector(".ad-ext-player-score");
      const name = player.querySelector(".ad-ext-player-name");
      if (!score) continue;

      const root = adCoreV67FindContentRoot(player, score);
      const scoreBox = root ? adCoreV67DirectChild(root, score) : null;
      const nameBox = root && name ? adCoreV67DirectChild(root, name) : null;
      const avgBox = root ? adCoreV67FindAverageBox(root, scoreBox, nameBox) : null;
      const avgText = avgBox?.querySelector(".chakra-text") || avgBox;

      if (root) root.classList.add("ad-core-v67-content");
      if (scoreBox) scoreBox.classList.add("ad-core-v67-scorebox");
      if (nameBox) {
        nameBox.classList.add("ad-core-v67-namebox");
        nameBox.dataset.adCoreV67Place = c.LAYOUT_NAME_PLACEMENT === "above" ? "above" : "below";
      }
      if (avgBox) {
        avgBox.classList.add("ad-core-v67-avgbox");
        avgBox.dataset.adCoreV67Place = c.LAYOUT_AVG_PLACEMENT === "above" ? "above" : "below";
      }
      score.classList.add("ad-core-v67-scoretext", "ad-core-player-score");
      if (name) name.classList.add("ad-core-player-name");
      if (avgText) avgText.classList.add("ad-core-player-avg");

      panel.classList.toggle("ad-core-v67-hide-name", !c.LAYOUT_SHOW_PLAYER_NAME);
      panel.classList.toggle("ad-core-v67-hide-avg", !c.LAYOUT_SHOW_PLAYER_AVG);

      // Exact v65 principle: font scaling is local to the native text only,
      // and only while the two-sided Layout module is enabled.
      if (c.LAYOUT_ENABLED) {
        const scoreScale = adCoreV67Num(Number(c.LAYOUT_SCORE_FONT_PX ?? 100) / 100, 0.60, 1.80, 1);
        const nameScale = adCoreV67Num(c.LAYOUT_NAME_ROW_SCALE, 0.60, 1.80, 1);
        const avgScale = adCoreV67Num(c.LAYOUT_AVG_ROW_SCALE, 0.60, 1.80, 1);
        adCoreV67SetInline(score, "font-size", `${Math.round(adCoreV67BaseFont(score) * scoreScale * 100) / 100}px`);
        if (name) adCoreV67SetInline(name, "font-size", `${Math.round(adCoreV67BaseFont(name) * nameScale * 100) / 100}px`);
        if (avgText) adCoreV67SetInline(avgText, "font-size", `${Math.round(adCoreV67BaseFont(avgText) * avgScale * 100) / 100}px`);
      } else {
        adCoreV67RestoreInline(score, "font-size");
        adCoreV67RestoreInline(name, "font-size");
        adCoreV67RestoreInline(avgText, "font-size");
      }
    }
  }

  // Legacy OFF paths are deliberately disabled. Their former work is now handled
  // by annotatePlayerCardInfo() without coordinates, overlays or React DOM moves.
  function applyOffNativeMeasuredLayout(panel) { return; }
  function forceOffLayoutDirectOverlayV15() { return; }
  function cleanupOffLayoutV12OverlayResidue() { adCoreV67CleanupLegacyOwnedArtifacts(); }
  function forceOffLayoutPipelineV12() { adCoreV67CleanupLegacyOwnedArtifacts(); }

  // v80: Restore the user's own complete-history renderer. It is not a

  // session memory, and draws one script-owned box in the original native
  // chalkboard rectangle. Every refreshed history view automatically lands on
  // the newest row at the bottom.
  function syncFullHistoryOverlays() { syncFullHistoryOverlaysOwnHistory(); }

  // Disable old point-board auto placement. The native chalkboard owns itself.
  function layoutAllPlayerPointBoards() { return; }
  function autoFitPlayerBoard(panel, scoreEl, metaRow, avgRow) { return; }
  function fitGlobalChalkboardToPanel() { return; }


  /* ========================================================================
     v82 – NATIVE PLAYER-CARD LAYOUT CONTROLS REPAIR
     ------------------------------------------------------------------------
     v67 deliberately removed the former absolute-coordinate player-card
     engine to keep the page responsive. That also left the Layout panel's
     name/average placement and scaling controls without a safe writer in
     Layout OFF. This layer restores only those local controls.

     Guardrails:
       - never changes #root, host or outer card geometry;
       - never moves/removes React-owned nodes;
       - uses only the existing direct flex children of the native card;
       - keeps the native/default card untouched until a non-default layout
         setting is selected.
     ======================================================================== */

  const AD_CORE_V82_FONT_BASE_CACHE = new WeakMap();
  const AD_CORE_V82_INLINE_BACKUP = new WeakMap();

  function adCoreV82RememberInline(el, property) {
    if (!el || !el.style) return;
    let mem = AD_CORE_V82_INLINE_BACKUP.get(el);
    if (!mem) {
      mem = new Map();
      AD_CORE_V82_INLINE_BACKUP.set(el, mem);
    }
    if (!mem.has(property)) {
      mem.set(property, {
        value: el.style.getPropertyValue(property),
        priority: el.style.getPropertyPriority(property)
      });
    }
  }

  function adCoreV82SetInline(el, property, value) {
    if (!el || !el.style) return;
    adCoreV82RememberInline(el, property);
    el.style.setProperty(property, value, 'important');
  }

  function adCoreV82RestoreInline(el, property) {
    const mem = AD_CORE_V82_INLINE_BACKUP.get(el);
    if (!mem || !mem.has(property) || !el?.style) return;
    const prev = mem.get(property);
    if (prev.value) el.style.setProperty(property, prev.value, prev.priority || '');
    else el.style.removeProperty(property);
    mem.delete(property);
  }

  function adCoreV82BaseFont(el) {
    if (!el) return 16;
    if (AD_CORE_V82_FONT_BASE_CACHE.has(el)) return AD_CORE_V82_FONT_BASE_CACHE.get(el);

    // v67 may already have captured the natural font before its own writer.
    // Prefer that exact baseline, then fall back to the currently native CSS.
    let px = 0;
    try { px = Number(AD_CORE_V67_FONT_BASE_CACHE.get(el)); } catch (_) {}
    if (!Number.isFinite(px) || px <= 0) {
      px = Number.parseFloat(getComputedStyle(el).fontSize) || 16;
    }
    px = Math.min(Math.max(px, 8), 280);
    AD_CORE_V82_FONT_BASE_CACHE.set(el, px);
    return px;
  }

  function adCoreV82ApplyFontScale(el, scale) {
    if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return;

    // Remove the former v67 local font writer first. Its backup restores the
    // actual native source value, so the v82 baseline does not compound.
    try { adCoreV67RestoreInline(el, 'font-size'); } catch (_) {}

    const s = adCoreV67Num(scale, 0.60, 1.80, 1);
    if (Math.abs(s - 1) < 0.001) {
      adCoreV82RestoreInline(el, 'font-size');
      return;
    }

    const px = Math.round(adCoreV82BaseFont(el) * s * 100) / 100;
    adCoreV82SetInline(el, 'font-size', `${px}px`);
  }

  function adCoreV82NeedsLocalReflow(c) {
    const score = adCoreV67Num(Number(c.LAYOUT_SCORE_FONT_PX ?? 100), 40, 220, 100);
    const name = adCoreV67Num(c.LAYOUT_NAME_ROW_SCALE, 0.60, 1.80, 1);
    const avg = adCoreV67Num(c.LAYOUT_AVG_ROW_SCALE, 0.60, 1.80, 1);
    return c.LAYOUT_NAME_PLACEMENT === 'above' ||
      c.LAYOUT_AVG_PLACEMENT === 'above' ||
      Math.abs(score - 100) > 0.01 ||
      Math.abs(name - 1) > 0.001 ||
      Math.abs(avg - 1) > 0.001;
  }

  function adCoreV82PlayerExtrasCss() {
    const fontStack = getPlayerCardResolvedFontStack();
    return `

#ad-ext-player-display{
  --ad-core-v82-player-font:${fontStack};
}

#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-v82-content,
#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-v82-content *{
  font-family:var(--ad-core-v82-player-font) !important;
}

}
#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-v67-hide-avg .ad-core-v82-avgbox,
#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-v67-hide-avg .ad-core-v67-avgbox{
  display:none !important;

  // The existing refresh function calls this name on every scheduled update.
  // Replacing just this stylesheet keeps the original panel and all controls.
  function adCoreV67PlayerExtrasCss() {
    return adCoreV82PlayerExtrasCss();
  }

  // Replaces only v67's local player-content annotation. It does not create
  // coordinates, overlays or card/page sizing rules.
  function annotatePlayerCardInfo() {
    const c = cfg();
    adCoreV67CleanupLegacyOwnedArtifacts();
    adCoreV67ApplyRootClasses();

    const wantsReflow = adCoreV82NeedsLocalReflow(c);
    const nameOrder = c.LAYOUT_NAME_PLACEMENT === 'above' ? 10 : 30;
    const avgOrder = c.LAYOUT_AVG_PLACEMENT === 'above' ? 11 : 31;
    const scoreScale = adCoreV67Num(Number(c.LAYOUT_SCORE_FONT_PX ?? 100) / 100, 0.60, 1.80, 1);
    const nameScale = adCoreV67Num(c.LAYOUT_NAME_ROW_SCALE, 0.60, 1.80, 1);
    const avgScale = adCoreV67Num(c.LAYOUT_AVG_ROW_SCALE, 0.60, 1.80, 1);

    for (const panel of adCoreV67Panels()) {
      const player = panel.querySelector('.ad-ext-player') || panel;
      const score = player.querySelector('.ad-ext-player-score');
      const name = player.querySelector('.ad-ext-player-name');
      if (!score) continue;

      const content = adCoreV67FindContentRoot(player, score);
      const scoreBox = content ? adCoreV67DirectChild(content, score) : null;
      const nameBox = content && name ? adCoreV67DirectChild(content, name) : null;
      const avgBox = content ? adCoreV67FindAverageBox(content, scoreBox, nameBox) : null;
      const avgText = avgBox?.querySelector('.chakra-text') || avgBox;

      if (content) {
        content.classList.add('ad-core-v82-content', 'ad-core-v67-content');
        content.classList.toggle('ad-core-v82-controls-active', wantsReflow);
        content.style.setProperty('--ad-core-v82-name-order', String(nameOrder));
        content.style.setProperty('--ad-core-v82-avg-order', String(avgOrder));
      }
      if (scoreBox) scoreBox.classList.add('ad-core-v82-scorebox', 'ad-core-v67-scorebox');
      if (nameBox) nameBox.classList.add('ad-core-v82-namebox', 'ad-core-v67-namebox');
      if (avgBox) avgBox.classList.add('ad-core-v82-avgbox', 'ad-core-v67-avgbox');

      score.classList.add('ad-core-v82-scoretext', 'ad-core-v67-scoretext', 'ad-core-player-score');
      if (name) name.classList.add('ad-core-player-name');
      if (avgText) avgText.classList.add('ad-core-player-avg');

      panel.classList.toggle('ad-core-v67-hide-name', !c.LAYOUT_SHOW_PLAYER_NAME);
      panel.classList.toggle('ad-core-v67-hide-avg', !c.LAYOUT_SHOW_PLAYER_AVG);

      adCoreV82ApplyFontScale(score, scoreScale);
      adCoreV82ApplyFontScale(name, nameScale);
      adCoreV82ApplyFontScale(avgText, avgScale);
    }
  }


  /* ========================================================================
     v83 – COMPLETE NATIVE ROW CONTROLS
     ------------------------------------------------------------------------
     The v82 writer changed only the text nodes for the name/average controls.
     v83 applies visibility and sizing to the complete native flex rows:
       - name row = avatar + flag + name + badge together;
       - average row = complete average/leg row together.

     UI scale and player spacing are intentionally absent from the Layout
     panel: the v67 native responsive page no longer consumes those legacy
     coordinate-engine values.
     ======================================================================== */

  function adCoreV83FindAverageBox(content, scoreBox, nameBox) {
    if (!content) return null;
    const looksLikeAverage = (el) => {
      const txt = String(el?.textContent || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
      return /(?:∅|Ø|\bavg(?:erage)?\b|#\s*\d+\s*\||\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?\s*\/\s*\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?)/i.test(txt);
    };

    // Fast/native route: the average is normally a direct child.
    for (const child of Array.from(content.children || [])) {
      if (child === scoreBox || child === nameBox) continue;
      if (looksLikeAverage(child)) return child;
    }

    // Robust fallback for the current Autodarts variants where a small wrapper
    // sits between the content root and the average row.
    for (const candidate of Array.from(content.querySelectorAll('*'))) {
      if (candidate === scoreBox || candidate === nameBox) continue;
      if (!looksLikeAverage(candidate)) continue;
      const direct = adCoreV67DirectChild(content, candidate);
      if (direct && direct !== scoreBox && direct !== nameBox) return direct;
    }
    return null;
  }

  function adCoreV83SetWholeRowScale(row, scale) {
    if (!(row instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
    const s = adCoreV67Num(scale, 0.60, 1.80, 1);

    // CSS zoom changes the layout box as well as the visual size; unlike a
    // transform it keeps larger/smaller full rows from overlapping neighbours.
    if (Math.abs(s - 1) < 0.001) {
      adCoreV82RestoreInline(row, 'zoom');
      return;
    }
    adCoreV82SetInline(row, 'zoom', String(s));
  }

  function adCoreV83ClearTextScale(el) {
    if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
    try { adCoreV67RestoreInline(el, 'font-size'); } catch (_) {}
    try { adCoreV82RestoreInline(el, 'font-size'); } catch (_) {}
  }

  function adCoreV82NeedsLocalReflow(c) {
    const score = adCoreV67Num(Number(c.LAYOUT_SCORE_FONT_PX ?? 100), 40, 220, 100);
    const name = adCoreV67Num(c.LAYOUT_NAME_ROW_SCALE, 0.60, 1.80, 1);
    const avg = adCoreV67Num(c.LAYOUT_AVG_ROW_SCALE, 0.60, 1.80, 1);
    return !c.LAYOUT_SHOW_PLAYER_NAME ||
      !c.LAYOUT_SHOW_PLAYER_AVG ||
      c.LAYOUT_NAME_PLACEMENT === 'above' ||
      c.LAYOUT_AVG_PLACEMENT === 'above' ||
      Math.abs(score - 100) > 0.01 ||
      Math.abs(name - 1) > 0.001 ||
      Math.abs(avg - 1) > 0.001;
  }

  function adCoreV82PlayerExtrasCss() {
    const fontStack = getPlayerCardResolvedFontStack();
    return `
/* v83: complete native name/average row controls. Outer page/card geometry stays native. */
#ad-ext-player-display{
  --ad-core-v82-player-font:${fontStack};
}

#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-v82-content,
#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-v82-content *{
  font-family:var(--ad-core-v82-player-font) !important;
}

/* Full row visibility: name means avatar/flag/name/badge together. */
#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-v83-hide-name-row .ad-core-v83-namebox,
#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-v83-hide-name-row .ad-core-v82-namebox{
  display:none !important;
}
#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-v83-hide-avg-row .ad-core-v83-avgbox,
#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-v83-hide-avg-row .ad-core-v82-avgbox{
  display:none !important;
}

/* The local flex writer is only engaged when a layout setting differs from
   the native defaults. It never touches host, outer card, root or page flow. */
#ad-ext-player-display .ad-core-v82-controls-active{
  display:flex !important;
  flex-direction:column !important;
  align-items:center !important;
  justify-content:center !important;
  align-content:center !important;
  min-width:0 !important;
  min-height:0 !important;

  order:20 !important;
  flex:0 0 auto !important;
  display:flex !important;

  justify-content:center !important;

  order:var(--ad-core-v82-avg-order,31) !important;
  flex:0 0 auto !important;
  display:flex !important;
  align-items:center !important;

  min-width:0 !important;

  white-space:nowrap !important;

  overflow:visible !important;
  text-align:center !important;

  line-height:1.05 !important;
  min-width:0 !important;
}

/* Suppress only obsolete script-owned floating duplicates. v80 history stays. */
.ad-native-history-floating:not([data-ad-core-own-history="1"]),
#ad-native-history-overlay-root-core,
#ad-history-only-overlay-root{
  display:none !important;
