  }

  function adCoreV67PlayerExtrasCss() {
    return adCoreV82PlayerExtrasCss();
  }

  function annotatePlayerCardInfo() {
    const c = cfg();
    adCoreV67CleanupLegacyOwnedArtifacts();
    adCoreV67ApplyRootClasses();

    const wantsReflow = adCoreV82NeedsLocalReflow(c);
    const nameOrder = c.LAYOUT_NAME_PLACEMENT === 'above' ? 10 : 30;
    const avgOrder = c.LAYOUT_AVG_PLACEMENT === 'above' ? 11 : 31;
    const scoreScale = adCoreV67Num(Number(c.LAYOUT_SCORE_FONT_PX ?? 100) / 100, 0.60, 1.80, 1);
    const nameScale = adCoreV67Num(c.LAYOUT_NAME_ROW_SCALE, 0.60, 1.80, 1);
    const avgScale = adCoreV67Num(c.LAYOUT_AVG_ROW_SCALE, 0.60, 1.80, 1);

    for (const panel of adCoreV67Panels()) {
      const player = panel.querySelector('.ad-ext-player') || panel;
      const score = player.querySelector('.ad-ext-player-score');
      const name = player.querySelector('.ad-ext-player-name');
      if (!score) continue;

      const content = adCoreV67FindContentRoot(player, score);
      const scoreBox = content ? adCoreV67DirectChild(content, score) : null;
      const nameBox = content && name ? adCoreV67DirectChild(content, name) : null;
      const avgBox = content ? adCoreV83FindAverageBox(content, scoreBox, nameBox) : null;
      const avgText = avgBox?.querySelector('.chakra-text') || avgBox;

      if (content) {
        content.classList.add('ad-core-v82-content', 'ad-core-v67-content');
        content.classList.toggle('ad-core-v82-controls-active', wantsReflow);
        content.style.setProperty('--ad-core-v82-name-order', String(nameOrder));
        content.style.setProperty('--ad-core-v82-avg-order', String(avgOrder));
      }
      if (scoreBox) scoreBox.classList.add('ad-core-v82-scorebox', 'ad-core-v67-scorebox');
      if (nameBox) nameBox.classList.add('ad-core-v83-namebox', 'ad-core-v82-namebox', 'ad-core-v67-namebox');
      if (avgBox) avgBox.classList.add('ad-core-v83-avgbox', 'ad-core-v82-avgbox', 'ad-core-v67-avgbox');

      score.classList.add('ad-core-v82-scoretext', 'ad-core-v67-scoretext', 'ad-core-player-score');
      if (name) name.classList.add('ad-core-player-name');
      if (avgText) avgText.classList.add('ad-core-player-avg');

      // Remove v82's partial text-only toggles. v83 controls full native rows.
      panel.classList.remove('ad-core-v67-hide-name', 'ad-core-v67-hide-avg');
      panel.classList.toggle('ad-core-v83-hide-name-row', !c.LAYOUT_SHOW_PLAYER_NAME);
      panel.classList.toggle('ad-core-v83-hide-avg-row', !c.LAYOUT_SHOW_PLAYER_AVG);

      adCoreV82ApplyFontScale(score, scoreScale);
      adCoreV83ClearTextScale(name);
      adCoreV83ClearTextScale(avgText);
      adCoreV83SetWholeRowScale(nameBox, nameScale);
      adCoreV83SetWholeRowScale(avgBox, avgScale);
    }
  }

  /* ========================================================================
     v84 – RELIABLE FULL-ROW VISIBILITY TOGGLES
     ------------------------------------------------------------------------
     v83 correctly found/scaled the complete native rows, but CSS-only
     display:none lost against legacy inline display:* !important values still
     present on some Chakra wrappers. v84 takes ownership only of the display
     property on the actual complete name / average row. The prior inline
     value and priority are stored per native node and restored verbatim when
     the toggle is switched back on. No DOM node is moved, cloned or deleted.
     ======================================================================== */

  const AD_CORE_V84_DISPLAY_SNAPSHOT = 'data-ad-core-v84-display-snapshot';

  function adCoreV84EncodeDisplaySnapshot(el) {
    if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
    if (el.hasAttribute(AD_CORE_V84_DISPLAY_SNAPSHOT)) return;
    try {
      const value = el.style.getPropertyValue('display');
      const priority = el.style.getPropertyPriority('display');
      el.setAttribute(AD_CORE_V84_DISPLAY_SNAPSHOT, JSON.stringify({ value, priority }));
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  function adCoreV84RestoreDisplaySnapshot(el) {
    if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
    const raw = el.getAttribute(AD_CORE_V84_DISPLAY_SNAPSHOT);
    if (!raw) return;
    try {
      const saved = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (saved && typeof saved.value === 'string' && saved.value) {
        el.style.setProperty('display', saved.value, saved.priority || '');
      } else {
        el.style.removeProperty('display');
      }
    } catch (_) {
      try { el.style.removeProperty('display'); } catch (_) {}
    }
    try { el.removeAttribute(AD_CORE_V84_DISPLAY_SNAPSHOT); } catch (_) {}
  }

  function adCoreV84SetWholeRowVisible(row, visible) {
    if (!(row instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
    if (visible) {
      adCoreV84RestoreDisplaySnapshot(row);
      return;
    }
    adCoreV84EncodeDisplaySnapshot(row);
    try { row.style.setProperty('display', 'none', 'important'); } catch (_) {}
  }

  function adCoreV84FindNameBox(player, content) {
    if (!player || !content) return null;
    const name = player.querySelector('.ad-ext-player-name');
    if (!name) return null;
    const direct = adCoreV67DirectChild(content, name);
    if (direct && direct !== content) return direct;

    // Fallback: retain the complete native wrapper that includes the name,
    // avatar, flag and badge, never the text element alone.
    let node = name.parentElement;
    while (node && node !== content) {
      if (node.parentElement === content) return node;
      node = node.parentElement;
    }
    return null;
  }

  function adCoreV84FindAverageBox(player, content, scoreBox, nameBox) {
    const direct = adCoreV83FindAverageBox(content, scoreBox, nameBox);
    if (direct && direct !== content) return direct;
    if (!player || !content) return null;

    const looksLikeAverage = (el) => {
      const text = String(el?.textContent || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
      return /(?:∅|Ø|#\s*\d+\s*\||\bavg(?:erage)?\b|\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?\s*\/\s*\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?)/i.test(text);
    };

    let best = null;
    let bestScore = -Infinity;
    for (const candidate of Array.from(content.querySelectorAll('*'))) {
      if (!looksLikeAverage(candidate)) continue;
      const row = adCoreV67DirectChild(content, candidate);
      if (!row || row === content || row === scoreBox || row === nameBox) continue;
      const text = String(row.textContent || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
      let score = 0;
      if (/[∅Ø]/.test(text)) score += 8;
      if (/#\s*\d+\s*\|/.test(text)) score += 6;
      if (/\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?\s*\/\s*\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?/.test(text)) score += 4;
      // Prefer the most compact direct row. A parent containing score+name+avg
      // must never win over the actual average row.
      score -= Math.min(200, text.length) * 0.02;
      if (score > bestScore) {
        bestScore = score;
        best = row;
      }
    }
    return best;
  }

  function annotatePlayerCardInfo() {
    const c = cfg();
    adCoreV67CleanupLegacyOwnedArtifacts();
    adCoreV67ApplyRootClasses();

    const wantsReflow = adCoreV82NeedsLocalReflow(c);
    const nameOrder = c.LAYOUT_NAME_PLACEMENT === 'above' ? 10 : 30;
    const avgOrder = c.LAYOUT_AVG_PLACEMENT === 'above' ? 11 : 31;
    const scoreScale = adCoreV67Num(Number(c.LAYOUT_SCORE_FONT_PX ?? 100) / 100, 0.60, 1.80, 1);
    const nameScale = adCoreV67Num(c.LAYOUT_NAME_ROW_SCALE, 0.60, 1.80, 1);
    const avgScale = adCoreV67Num(c.LAYOUT_AVG_ROW_SCALE, 0.60, 1.80, 1);

    for (const panel of adCoreV67Panels()) {
      const player = panel.querySelector('.ad-ext-player') || panel;
      const score = player.querySelector('.ad-ext-player-score');
      if (!score) continue;

      const content = adCoreV67FindContentRoot(player, score);
      const scoreBox = content ? adCoreV67DirectChild(content, score) : null;
      const nameBox = content ? adCoreV84FindNameBox(player, content) : null;
      const avgBox = content ? adCoreV84FindAverageBox(player, content, scoreBox, nameBox) : null;
      const avgText = avgBox?.querySelector('.chakra-text') || avgBox;

      if (content) {
        content.classList.add('ad-core-v82-content', 'ad-core-v67-content');
        content.classList.toggle('ad-core-v82-controls-active', wantsReflow);
        content.style.setProperty('--ad-core-v82-name-order', String(nameOrder));
        content.style.setProperty('--ad-core-v82-avg-order', String(avgOrder));
      }
      if (scoreBox) scoreBox.classList.add('ad-core-v82-scorebox', 'ad-core-v67-scorebox');
      if (nameBox) nameBox.classList.add('ad-core-v84-namebox', 'ad-core-v83-namebox', 'ad-core-v82-namebox', 'ad-core-v67-namebox');
      if (avgBox) avgBox.classList.add('ad-core-v84-avgbox', 'ad-core-v83-avgbox', 'ad-core-v82-avgbox', 'ad-core-v67-avgbox');

      score.classList.add('ad-core-v82-scoretext', 'ad-core-v67-scoretext', 'ad-core-player-score');
      const name = player.querySelector('.ad-ext-player-name');
      if (name) name.classList.add('ad-core-player-name');
      if (avgText) avgText.classList.add('ad-core-player-avg');

      // Keep existing CSS classes for compatibility, but the direct inline
      // writer below is authoritative where an older inline !important value
      // would otherwise defeat CSS-only visibility.
      panel.classList.remove('ad-core-v67-hide-name', 'ad-core-v67-hide-avg');
      panel.classList.toggle('ad-core-v83-hide-name-row', !c.LAYOUT_SHOW_PLAYER_NAME);
      panel.classList.toggle('ad-core-v83-hide-avg-row', !c.LAYOUT_SHOW_PLAYER_AVG);
      panel.classList.toggle('ad-core-v84-hide-name-row', !c.LAYOUT_SHOW_PLAYER_NAME);
      panel.classList.toggle('ad-core-v84-hide-avg-row', !c.LAYOUT_SHOW_PLAYER_AVG);

      adCoreV84SetWholeRowVisible(nameBox, !!c.LAYOUT_SHOW_PLAYER_NAME);
      adCoreV84SetWholeRowVisible(avgBox, !!c.LAYOUT_SHOW_PLAYER_AVG);

      adCoreV82ApplyFontScale(score, scoreScale);
      adCoreV83ClearTextScale(name);
      adCoreV83ClearTextScale(avgText);
      adCoreV83SetWholeRowScale(nameBox, nameScale);
      adCoreV83SetWholeRowScale(avgBox, avgScale);
    }
  }



  /* ================== v85 OWN HISTORY FONT SYNC ==================
     The user-owned full-history renderer is independent of the native player
     card DOM. It therefore does not inherit the Player Card font automatically.
     Keep every current and newly-mounted own-history box on the exact same
     font stack as score / name / average, without altering history geometry,
     row sizing or scrolling. */
  const AD_CORE_V85_HISTORY_FONT_STYLE_ID = "ad-core-v85-own-history-font";

  function adCoreV85EnsureOwnHistoryFontStyle() {
    ensureHead(() => {
      let st = document.getElementById(AD_CORE_V85_HISTORY_FONT_STYLE_ID);
      if (!st) {
        st = document.createElement("style");
        st.id = AD_CORE_V85_HISTORY_FONT_STYLE_ID;
        document.head.appendChild(st);
      }
      st.textContent = `
        .ad-native-history-floating[data-ad-core-own-history="1"],
        .ad-native-history-floating[data-ad-core-own-history="1"] .ad-native-history-scroll,
        .ad-native-history-floating[data-ad-core-own-history="1"] .ad-native-history-list,
        .ad-native-history-floating[data-ad-core-own-history="1"] .ad-native-history-row,
        .ad-native-history-floating[data-ad-core-own-history="1"] .ad-native-history-cell{
          font-family: var(--ad-hist-font-family) !important;
        }
      `;
    });
  }

  function adCoreV85ApplyOwnHistoryFont() {
    try { ensurePlayerCardFontFace(); } catch (_) {}
    try { adCoreV85EnsureOwnHistoryFontStyle(); } catch (_) {}

    const fontStack = getPlayerCardResolvedFontStack();
    const selectors = [
      ".ad-native-history-scroll",
      ".ad-native-history-list",
      ".ad-native-history-row",
      ".ad-native-history-cell"
    ].join(", ");

    document
      .querySelectorAll('.ad-native-history-floating[data-ad-core-own-history="1"]')
      .forEach((box) => {
        try {
          box.style.setProperty("--ad-hist-font-family", fontStack);
          box.style.setProperty("font-family", fontStack, "important");
          box.querySelectorAll(selectors).forEach((el) => {
            el.style.setProperty("font-family", fontStack, "important");
          });
        } catch (_) {}
      });
  }

  // The history overlay is mounted after player-card annotation during the
  // normal update pass. Wrap both stages so the selected font is applied to
  // existing boxes and immediately to newly mounted/rebuilt boxes as well.
  const adCoreV85AnnotatePlayerCardInfo = annotatePlayerCardInfo;
  annotatePlayerCardInfo = function adCoreV85AnnotatedPlayerCards() {
    adCoreV85AnnotatePlayerCardInfo();
    adCoreV85ApplyOwnHistoryFont();
  };

  const adCoreV85SyncFullHistoryOverlays = syncFullHistoryOverlays;
  syncFullHistoryOverlays = function adCoreV85SyncedFullHistoryOverlays() {
    adCoreV85SyncFullHistoryOverlays();
    adCoreV85ApplyOwnHistoryFont();
  };



  /* ========================================================================
     v90 – LAYOUT ON LANDSCAPE REFERENCE
     ------------------------------------------------------------------------
     Base: v85, which is the accepted functional CORE baseline:
       • original CORE panel / HU-EN-DE / presets / import-export;
       • v76 turn-card height + measured action spacing;
       • v80 user-owned full scrollable history;
       • v81 active player marker;
       • v84 row visibility and v85 history font inheritance.

     This block intentionally changes only LAYOUT ON with exactly two players.
     It uses the supplied "AD Landscape mode" composition as the geometry
     reference:
       side card | modest gap | fixed-width centre turn lane | gap | side card

     No React node is moved or deleted. The custom history stays script-owned,
     scrollable and auto-jumps to the newest throw; it is only positioned at
     the lower part of the player card.
     ======================================================================== */


  /* ========================================================================
     v91 – LAYOUT ON / TWO-PLAYER REFERENCE REBUILD
     ------------------------------------------------------------------------
     Scope: only the enabled two-sided layout, with exactly two players.
     It replaces the v90 fixed 900px middle band with one measured three-zone
     composition:
       side card | equal clearance | full middle turn lane | equal clearance | side card

     No native React node is moved or deleted. The accepted v80 own-history
     renderer remains script-owned, scrollable and auto-scrolled to the newest
     throw. Layout OFF and all CORE controls remain outside this block.
     ======================================================================== */

  const AD_CORE_V91_STYLE_ID = "ad-core-v93-layout-on-reference-style";
  let adCoreV91Raf = 0;

  function adCoreV91Clamp(value, min, max) {
    const n = Number(value);
    return Math.min(Math.max(Number.isFinite(n) ? n : min, min), max);
  }

  function adCoreV91Panels() {
    try {
      return adCoreV67Panels().filter((panel) => panel instanceof HTMLElement);
    } catch (_) {
      const host = document.querySelector('#ad-ext-player-display');
      return host ? Array.from(host.children || []).filter((panel) =>
        panel instanceof HTMLElement && !!panel.querySelector('.ad-ext-player-score')
      ) : [];
    }
  }

  function adCoreV91IsActive() {
    const c = cfg();
    return !!c?.LAYOUT_ENABLED &&
      document.documentElement.classList.contains('ad-core-layout-on') &&
      adCoreV91Panels().length === 2;
  }

  function adCoreV91Geometry() {
    const vw = Math.max(1, Math.round(window.innerWidth || document.documentElement.clientWidth || 1));
    const vh = Math.max(1, Math.round(window.innerHeight || document.documentElement.clientHeight || 1));

    // The reference has side cards wide enough to be real panels, but leaves a
    // single uninterrupted central lane. The minimum lane prevents collisions
    // on 1360×768-class screens.
    const side = adCoreV91Clamp(Math.round(vw * 0.024), 38, 68);
    const gap = adCoreV91Clamp(Math.round(vw * 0.011), 16, 30);
    const top = adCoreV91Clamp(Math.round(vh * 0.060), 62, 88);
    const bottom = adCoreV91Clamp(Math.round(vh * 0.050), 38, 68);
    const minLane = adCoreV91Clamp(Math.round(vw * 0.32), 420, 720);
    const nominalCard = adCoreV91Clamp(Math.round(vw * 0.190), 315, 470);
    const maxCard = Math.max(260, Math.floor((vw - (side * 2) - (gap * 2) - minLane) / 2));
    const cardWidth = Math.max(260, Math.min(nominalCard, maxCard));
    const laneWidth = Math.max(300, vw - (side * 2) - (cardWidth * 2) - (gap * 2));
    const cardHeight = Math.max(180, vh - top - bottom);

    return { vw, vh, side, gap, top, bottom, cardWidth, laneWidth, cardHeight };
  }

  function adCoreV91Css() {
    return `
/* v91 Layout ON: one symmetric, full-width three-zone system. */
html.ad-core-v67-native-page.ad-core-layout-on.ad-core-v67-two-player{
  --ad-core-v91-side:60px;
  --ad-core-v91-gap:24px;
  --ad-core-v91-top:72px;
  --ad-core-v91-bottom:52px;
  --ad-core-v91-card-width:390px;
  --ad-core-v91-turn-width:min(900px, calc(100vw - 24px));
  --ad-core-v91-board-size:min(640px, calc(100vh - 360px));
  --ad-core-v92-turn-card-height:126px;

  function adCoreV91EnsureStyle() {
    ensureHead(() => {
      let style = document.getElementById(AD_CORE_V91_STYLE_ID);
      if (!style) {
        style = document.createElement('style');
        style.id = AD_CORE_V91_STYLE_ID;
        document.head.appendChild(style);
      }
      style.textContent = adCoreV91Css();
    });
  }

  function adCoreV91ClearPanel(panel) {
    if (!(panel instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
    const props = ['left','right','top','bottom','width','min-width','max-width','height','min-height','max-height'];
    props.forEach((prop) => panel.style.removeProperty(prop));
    panel.classList.remove('ad-core-v91-card');

    const player = panel.querySelector('.ad-ext-player');
    const score = player?.querySelector('.ad-ext-player-score');
    const content = player && score ? adCoreV67FindContentRoot(player, score) : null;
    const scoreBox = content && score ? adCoreV67DirectChild(content, score) : null;
    const nameBox = content ? adCoreV84FindNameBox(player, content) : null;
    const avgBox = content ? adCoreV84FindAverageBox(player, content, scoreBox, nameBox) : null;
    content?.classList?.remove('ad-core-v91-content');
    scoreBox?.classList?.remove('ad-core-v91-scorebox');
    nameBox?.classList?.remove('ad-core-v91-namebox');
    avgBox?.classList?.remove('ad-core-v91-avgbox');
  }

  function adCoreV91ApplyPanelContent(panel, geo) {
    const player = panel.querySelector('.ad-ext-player') || panel;
    const score = player.querySelector('.ad-ext-player-score');
    const content = score ? adCoreV67FindContentRoot(player, score) : null;
    const scoreBox = content && score ? adCoreV67DirectChild(content, score) : null;
    const nameBox = content ? adCoreV84FindNameBox(player, content) : null;
    const avgBox = content ? adCoreV84FindAverageBox(player, content, scoreBox, nameBox) : null;
    if (!content || !score) return;

    const c = cfg();
    const h = Math.max(180, Math.round(geo.cardHeight));
    const w = Math.max(220, Math.round(geo.cardWidth));
    const topPad = adCoreV91Clamp(Math.round(h * .040), 22, 42);
    const sidePad = adCoreV91Clamp(Math.round(w * .070), 18, 34);
    const bottomPad = adCoreV91Clamp(Math.round(h * .030), 14, 28);
    const histHeight = adCoreV91Clamp(Math.round(h * .485), 220, Math.max(220, h - 190));
    const contentBottom = histHeight + bottomPad + adCoreV91Clamp(Math.round(h * .018), 10, 18);
    const stackGap = adCoreV91Clamp(Math.round(h * .012), 8, 14);

    content.classList.add('ad-core-v91-content');
    scoreBox?.classList?.add('ad-core-v91-scorebox');
    nameBox?.classList?.add('ad-core-v91-namebox');
    avgBox?.classList?.add('ad-core-v91-avgbox');

    content.style.setProperty('--ad-core-v91-top-pad', `${topPad}px`);
    content.style.setProperty('--ad-core-v91-side-pad', `${sidePad}px`);
    content.style.setProperty('--ad-core-v91-content-bottom', `${contentBottom}px`);
    content.style.setProperty('--ad-core-v91-stack-gap', `${stackGap}px`);
    content.style.setProperty('--ad-core-v91-name-order', String(c.LAYOUT_NAME_PLACEMENT === 'above' ? 10 : 30));
    content.style.setProperty('--ad-core-v91-avg-order', String(c.LAYOUT_AVG_PLACEMENT === 'above' ? 11 : 31));
    content.style.setProperty('--ad-core-v91-score-scale', String(adCoreV91Clamp(Number(c.LAYOUT_SCORE_FONT_PX || 100) / 100, .60, 1.80)));

    // Remove only old v87/v90 presentation classes. The native DOM stays in
    // place and v84 visibility/scale controls keep ownership of the rows.
    panel.classList.remove('ad-core-v87-card-stack', 'ad-core-v90-card-stack');
    content.classList.remove('ad-core-v87-content', 'ad-core-v90-content');
    scoreBox?.classList?.remove('ad-core-v87-scorebox', 'ad-core-v90-scorebox');
    nameBox?.classList?.remove('ad-core-v87-namebox', 'ad-core-v90-namebox');
    avgBox?.classList?.remove('ad-core-v87-avgbox', 'ad-core-v90-avgbox');
  }



  function adCoreV92ApplyBoardSize(size) {
    const px = `${Math.round(size)}px`;
    const boards = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('svg.ad-board-svg, .ad-board-svg'))
      .filter((el) => el instanceof HTMLElement || el instanceof SVGElement);
    for (const board of boards) {
      try {
        board.style.setProperty('width', px, 'important');
        board.style.setProperty('height', px, 'important');
        board.style.setProperty('max-width', px, 'important');
        board.style.setProperty('max-height', px, 'important');
        board.style.setProperty('display', 'block', 'important');
        let p = board.parentElement;
        for (let i = 0; p && i < 3; i++, p = p.parentElement) {
          const r = p.getBoundingClientRect();
          if (r.width > size * 0.85 && r.height > size * 0.85) {
            p.style.setProperty('max-width', px, 'important');
            p.style.setProperty('max-height', px, 'important');
            p.style.setProperty('width', px, 'important');
            p.style.setProperty('height', px, 'important');
            p.style.setProperty('box-sizing', 'border-box', 'important');
          }
        }
      } catch (_) {}
    }
  }

  function adCoreV91ApplyGeometry() {
    adCoreV91EnsureStyle();
    const panels = adCoreV91Panels();
    const root = document.documentElement;
    if (!adCoreV91IsActive()) {
      panels.forEach(adCoreV91ClearPanel);
      ['--ad-core-v91-side','--ad-core-v91-gap','--ad-core-v91-top','--ad-core-v91-bottom','--ad-core-v91-card-width','--ad-core-v91-turn-width','--ad-core-v91-board-size','--ad-core-v92-turn-card-height','--ad-core-v92-throw-card-width','--ad-core-v92-total-card-width','--ad-core-v92-turn-y'].forEach((key) => root.style.removeProperty(key));
      return;
    }

    const geo = adCoreV91Geometry();
    root.style.setProperty('--ad-core-v91-side', `${geo.side}px`);
    root.style.setProperty('--ad-core-v91-gap', `${geo.gap}px`);
    root.style.setProperty('--ad-core-v91-top', `${geo.top}px`);
    root.style.setProperty('--ad-core-v91-bottom', `${geo.bottom}px`);
    root.style.setProperty('--ad-core-v91-card-width', `${geo.cardWidth}px`);
    root.style.setProperty('--ad-core-v91-turn-width', `${geo.laneWidth}px`);
    const boardSize = adCoreV91Clamp(Math.round(Math.min(geo.laneWidth * 0.47, geo.vh * 0.515)), 340, 660);
    const turnCardH = adCoreV91Clamp(Math.round(geo.vh * 0.102), 118, 136);
    const cardGap = 8;
    const throwW = Math.max(120, Math.floor((geo.laneWidth - cardGap * 3) / 3.68));
    const totalW = Math.max(100, Math.floor(throwW * 0.68));
    const turnY = adCoreV91Clamp(Math.round(geo.vh * 0.012), 8, 16);
    root.style.setProperty('--ad-core-v91-board-size', `${boardSize}px`);
    root.style.setProperty('--ad-core-v92-turn-card-height', `${turnCardH}px`);
    root.style.setProperty('--ad-core-v92-throw-card-width', `${throwW}px`);
    root.style.setProperty('--ad-core-v92-total-card-width', `${totalW}px`);
    root.style.setProperty('--ad-core-v92-turn-y', `${turnY}px`);
    adCoreV92ApplyBoardSize(boardSize);

    panels.forEach((panel, index) => {
      panel.classList.add('ad-core-v91-card');
      panel.style.setProperty('top', `${geo.top}px`, 'important');
      panel.style.setProperty('bottom', `${geo.bottom}px`, 'important');
      panel.style.setProperty('width', `${geo.cardWidth}px`, 'important');
      panel.style.setProperty('min-width', '0px', 'important');
      panel.style.setProperty('max-width', `${geo.cardWidth}px`, 'important');
      panel.style.setProperty('height', 'auto', 'important');
      panel.style.setProperty('min-height', '0px', 'important');
      panel.style.setProperty('max-height', 'none', 'important');
      if (index === 0) {
        panel.style.setProperty('left', `${geo.side}px`, 'important');
        panel.style.setProperty('right', 'auto', 'important');
      } else {
        panel.style.setProperty('right', `${geo.side}px`, 'important');
        panel.style.setProperty('left', 'auto', 'important');
      }
      adCoreV91ApplyPanelContent(panel, geo);
    });
  }

  /* The accepted v80 renderer remains untouched except for its Layout ON
     geometry. Its font never scales down when rows exceed the viewport; the
     native scroll container carries the overflow and jumps to the newest row. */
  const adCoreV91OwnHistoryGeometryBase = phGetOwnHistoryNativeSizedGeometry;
  phGetOwnHistoryNativeSizedGeometry = function adCoreV91OwnHistoryGeometry(panel, host, rows) {
    if (!adCoreV91IsActive() || !(panel instanceof HTMLElement)) {
      return adCoreV91OwnHistoryGeometryBase(panel, host, rows);
    }

    const rect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
    const width = Math.round(rect.width);
    const height = Math.round(rect.height);
    if (!(width > 180 && height > 260)) {
      return adCoreV91OwnHistoryGeometryBase(panel, host, rows);
    }

    const sidePad = adCoreV91Clamp(Math.round(width * .095), 22, 44);
    const bottomPad = adCoreV91Clamp(Math.round(height * .030), 14, 28);
    const boxWidth = adCoreV91Clamp(Math.round(width * .62), 170, Math.max(170, width - sidePad * 2));
    const boxHeight = adCoreV91Clamp(Math.round(height * .485), 220, Math.max(220, height - 190));
    const left = Math.round((width - boxWidth) / 2);
    const top = Math.max(sidePad, height - bottomPad - boxHeight);
    const baseFont = ((boxHeight - 16) / 7) * .76;
    const font = adCoreV91Clamp(Math.round(baseFont), 20, 42);
    const colGap = adCoreV91Clamp(Math.round(font * .12), 4, 9);
    const padX = adCoreV91Clamp(Math.round(font * .16), 5, 10);
    const rowGap = adCoreV91Clamp(Math.round(font * .08), 3, 7);
    const padTop = adCoreV91Clamp(Math.round(font * .12), 4, 8);
    const padBottom = adCoreV91Clamp(Math.round(font * .14), 4, 9);

    return { left, top, width: boxWidth, height: boxHeight, font, rowGap, colGap, padX, padTop, padBottom, centerX: false };
  };

  function adCoreV91Apply() {
    adCoreV91ApplyGeometry();
    try { adCoreV85ApplyOwnHistoryFont(); } catch (_) {}
  }

  function adCoreV91ScheduleApply() {
    if (adCoreV91Raf) return;
    adCoreV91Raf = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      adCoreV91Raf = 0;
      adCoreV91Apply();
    });
  }

  const adCoreV91EnsureSkinCssBase = ensureSkinCss;
  ensureSkinCss = function adCoreV91EnsureSkinCss() {
    adCoreV91EnsureSkinCssBase();
    adCoreV91ScheduleApply();
  };

  const adCoreV91RefreshLayoutExtrasBase = refreshLayoutPlayerExtras;
  refreshLayoutPlayerExtras = function adCoreV91RefreshLayoutExtras() {
    adCoreV91RefreshLayoutExtrasBase();
    adCoreV91ScheduleApply();
  };

  const adCoreV91AnnotatePlayerCardInfoBase = annotatePlayerCardInfo;
  annotatePlayerCardInfo = function adCoreV91AnnotatePlayerCards() {
    adCoreV91AnnotatePlayerCardInfoBase();
    adCoreV91ScheduleApply();
  };

  const adCoreV91SyncFullHistoryOverlaysBase = syncFullHistoryOverlays;
  syncFullHistoryOverlays = function adCoreV91SyncedFullHistory() {
    adCoreV91ApplyGeometry();
    adCoreV91SyncFullHistoryOverlaysBase();
    try { adCoreV85ApplyOwnHistoryFont(); } catch (_) {}
    adCoreV91ScheduleApply();
  };

  window.addEventListener('resize', adCoreV91ScheduleApply, { passive:true });
  window.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', adCoreV91ScheduleApply, { passive:true });
  adCoreV91Apply();


  /* ========================================================================
     v94 – LAYOUT ON / RUNTIME THROW-SLOT WIDTH LOCK
     ------------------------------------------------------------------------
     v93 relied on historical Chakra css-* class names. Autodarts replaces the
     empty throw placeholders with a different component tree, so those class
     selectors cannot reliably cover every visual state. The total card is
     already stable and is deliberately NOT touched here.

     This narrow runtime layer finds the four actual visual surfaces in the
     live top turn row. It preserves surface #1 (the total card) and locks
     only surfaces #2–#4 to the same measured throw-slot width. It also fixes
     grid parents when the placeholder renderer uses CSS grid rather than
     flex. No player-card, board, history, action-row or page geometry is
     changed.
     ======================================================================== */

  const AD_CORE_V94_STYLE_ID = 'ad-core-v94-throw-slot-runtime-style';
  let adCoreV94Raf = 0;
  let adCoreV94Observer = null;
  let adCoreV94ObservedTurn = null;

  function adCoreV94IsActive() {
    try {
      return !!cfg()?.LAYOUT_ENABLED &&
        document.documentElement.classList.contains('ad-core-layout-on') &&
        document.documentElement.classList.contains('ad-core-v67-two-player');
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  function adCoreV94NumCss(root, name, fallback) {
    try {
      const raw = getComputedStyle(root).getPropertyValue(name).trim();
      const n = parseFloat(raw);
      return Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0 ? n : fallback;
    } catch (_) {
      return fallback;
    }
  }

  function adCoreV94Visible(el) {
    if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement) && !(el instanceof SVGElement)) return false;
    const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
    if (cs.display === 'none' || cs.visibility === 'hidden' || Number(cs.opacity || 1) === 0) return false;
    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    return r.width > 0 && r.height > 0;
  }

  function adCoreV94SlotSurfaceFromLeaf(turn, leaf) {
    if (!(turn instanceof HTMLElement) || !(leaf instanceof Element)) return null;
    if (leaf.closest('button, [role="button"], .css-19lo6pj, [data-ad-core-v75-turn-action="1"]')) return null;

    const turnRect = turn.getBoundingClientRect();
    const maxW = Math.max(120, turnRect.width * 0.72);
    const minTop = turnRect.top - 28;
    const maxBottom = turnRect.bottom + 18;
    let best = null;
    let el = leaf instanceof HTMLElement ? leaf : leaf.parentElement;

    for (let depth = 0; el && el !== turn && depth < 10; depth += 1, el = el.parentElement) {
      if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement) || !adCoreV94Visible(el)) continue;
      if (el.closest('button, [role="button"], .css-19lo6pj, [data-ad-core-v75-turn-action="1"]')) continue;
      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      const cardLike =
        r.width >= 46 && r.width <= maxW &&
        r.height >= 68 && r.height <= 190 &&
        r.top >= minTop && r.bottom <= maxBottom;
      if (cardLike) best = el;
    }
    return best;
  }

  function adCoreV94DistinctSurfaces(turn) {
    const found = new Set();
    const leaves = Array.from(turn.querySelectorAll('p, svg, [data-adval], [class*="throw"], [class*="score"]'));

    for (const leaf of leaves) {
      const surface = adCoreV94SlotSurfaceFromLeaf(turn, leaf);
      if (surface) found.add(surface);
    }

    // Prune nested duplicates: keep the outermost card-like surface per visual
    // rectangle. A React state renderer may contain p -> div -> card wrappers.
    const raw = Array.from(found).filter(adCoreV94Visible);
    const filtered = raw.filter((el) => !raw.some((other) => {
      if (other === el || !other.contains(el)) return false;
      const a = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      const b = other.getBoundingClientRect();
      return Math.abs(a.top - b.top) < 5 && Math.abs(a.height - b.height) < 8 && b.width >= a.width - 4;
    }));

    if (!filtered.length) return [];
    const tops = filtered.map((el) => el.getBoundingClientRect().top);
    const rowTop = Math.min(...tops);
    return filtered
      .filter((el) => Math.abs(el.getBoundingClientRect().top - rowTop) <= 18)
      .sort((a, b) => a.getBoundingClientRect().left - b.getBoundingClientRect().left);
  }

  function adCoreV94FindTurnRow(turn, surfaces) {
    if (!(turn instanceof HTMLElement) || surfaces.length < 4) return null;
    const parents = new Map();
    for (const surface of surfaces) {
      const parent = surface.parentElement;
      if (!parent || parent === turn) continue;
      parents.set(parent, (parents.get(parent) || 0) + 1);
    }
    let best = null;
    let bestScore = -1;
    for (const [parent, count] of parents) {
      const kids = Array.from(parent.children || []).filter((el) => el instanceof HTMLElement && adCoreV94Visible(el));
      const rr = parent.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (kids.length < 4 || rr.width < 200) continue;
      const score = count * 10 + (kids.length === 4 ? 5 : 0);
      if (score > bestScore) { best = parent; bestScore = score; }
    }
    return best;
  }

  function adCoreV94ApplyWidth(el, width, height) {
    if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement) || !(width > 0)) return;
    el.dataset.adCoreV94ThrowSlot = '1';
    el.style.setProperty('box-sizing', 'border-box', 'important');
    el.style.setProperty('flex', `0 0 ${Math.round(width)}px`, 'important');
    el.style.setProperty('flex-grow', '0', 'important');
    el.style.setProperty('flex-shrink', '0', 'important');
    el.style.setProperty('flex-basis', `${Math.round(width)}px`, 'important');
    el.style.setProperty('width', `${Math.round(width)}px`, 'important');
    el.style.setProperty('min-width', `${Math.round(width)}px`, 'important');
    el.style.setProperty('max-width', `${Math.round(width)}px`, 'important');
    if (height > 0) {
      el.style.setProperty('height', `${Math.round(height)}px`, 'important');
      el.style.setProperty('min-height', `${Math.round(height)}px`, 'important');
      el.style.setProperty('max-height', `${Math.round(height)}px`, 'important');
    }

    // Empty state components frequently have one full-height visual child.
    // Lock that child too, but do not touch text-only descendants.
    const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    for (const child of Array.from(el.children || [])) {
      if (!(child instanceof HTMLElement) || child.matches('button, [role="button"]')) continue;
      const cr = child.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (cr.height >= rect.height * 0.72 && cr.width >= Math.max(40, rect.width * 0.30)) {
        child.dataset.adCoreV94ThrowSurface = '1';
        child.style.setProperty('box-sizing', 'border-box', 'important');
        child.style.setProperty('width', `${Math.round(width)}px`, 'important');
        child.style.setProperty('min-width', `${Math.round(width)}px`, 'important');
        child.style.setProperty('max-width', `${Math.round(width)}px`, 'important');
        child.style.setProperty('height', `${Math.round(height)}px`, 'important');
        child.style.setProperty('min-height', `${Math.round(height)}px`, 'important');
      }
    }
  }

  function adCoreV94Clear() {
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-ad-core-v94-throw-slot="1"], [data-ad-core-v94-throw-surface="1"]').forEach((el) => {
      if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreV94ThrowSlot;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreV94ThrowSurface;
      ['box-sizing', 'flex', 'flex-grow', 'flex-shrink', 'flex-basis', 'width', 'min-width', 'max-width', 'height', 'min-height', 'max-height'].forEach((key) => el.style.removeProperty(key));
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-ad-core-v94-row="1"]').forEach((el) => {
      if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreV94Row;
      ['grid-template-columns', 'grid-auto-columns', 'grid-auto-flow', 'column-gap', 'gap', 'justify-content', 'flex-wrap'].forEach((key) => el.style.removeProperty(key));
    });
  }

  function adCoreV94LockSlots() {
    const turn = document.querySelector('#ad-ext-turn');
    if (!adCoreV94IsActive() || !(turn instanceof HTMLElement) || !adCoreV94Visible(turn)) {
      adCoreV94Clear();
      return;
    }

    const surfaces = adCoreV94DistinctSurfaces(turn);
    if (surfaces.length < 4) return;

    // The first top-row surface is the stable total card. Only the following
    // three real throw slots are locked.
    const row = surfaces.slice(0, 4);
    const total = row[0];
    const throws = row.slice(1, 4);
    const root = document.documentElement;
    const totalRect = total.getBoundingClientRect();
    const height = adCoreV94NumCss(root, '--ad-core-v92-turn-card-height', Math.round(totalRect.height || 126));
    let throwWidth = adCoreV94NumCss(root, '--ad-core-v92-throw-card-width', 0);
    if (!(throwWidth > 64)) {
      const turnRect = turn.getBoundingClientRect();
      throwWidth = Math.max(90, Math.floor((turnRect.width - totalRect.width - 24) / 3));
    }

    for (const slot of throws) adCoreV94ApplyWidth(slot, throwWidth, height);

    const parent = adCoreV94FindTurnRow(turn, row);
    if (parent instanceof HTMLElement) {
      const pcs = getComputedStyle(parent);
      parent.dataset.adCoreV94Row = '1';
      parent.style.setProperty('box-sizing', 'border-box', 'important');
      parent.style.setProperty('justify-content', 'flex-start', 'important');
      parent.style.setProperty('flex-wrap', 'nowrap', 'important');
      if (pcs.display.includes('grid')) {
        parent.style.setProperty('grid-auto-flow', 'column', 'important');
        parent.style.setProperty('grid-template-columns', `${Math.round(totalRect.width)}px repeat(3, ${Math.round(throwWidth)}px)`, 'important');
        parent.style.setProperty('grid-auto-columns', `${Math.round(throwWidth)}px`, 'important');
      }
    }
  }

  function adCoreV94Schedule() {
    if (adCoreV94Raf) return;
    adCoreV94Raf = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      adCoreV94Raf = 0;
      adCoreV94LockSlots();
    });
  }

  function adCoreV94EnsureObserver() {
    const turn = document.querySelector('#ad-ext-turn');
    if (turn === adCoreV94ObservedTurn && adCoreV94Observer) return;
    try { adCoreV94Observer?.disconnect(); } catch (_) {}
    adCoreV94Observer = null;
    adCoreV94ObservedTurn = turn instanceof HTMLElement ? turn : null;
    if (!(turn instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
    adCoreV94Observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
      adCoreV94Schedule();
      setTimeout(adCoreV94Schedule, 0);
      setTimeout(adCoreV94Schedule, 90);
    });
    adCoreV94Observer.observe(turn, { childList:true, subtree:true, characterData:true });
  }

  function adCoreV94EnsureStyle() {
    ensureHead(() => {
      let st = document.getElementById(AD_CORE_V94_STYLE_ID);
      if (!st) {
        st = document.createElement('style');
        st.id = AD_CORE_V94_STYLE_ID;
        document.head.appendChild(st);
      }
      st.textContent = `
html.ad-core-v67-native-page.ad-core-layout-on.ad-core-v67-two-player
#ad-ext-turn [data-ad-core-v94-throw-slot="1"],
html.ad-core-v67-native-page.ad-core-layout-on.ad-core-v67-two-player
#ad-ext-turn [data-ad-core-v94-throw-surface="1"]{
  box-sizing:border-box !important;
  flex-grow:0 !important;
  flex-shrink:0 !important;
}
`;
    });
  }

  const adCoreV94ScheduleBase = adCoreV91ScheduleApply;
  adCoreV91ScheduleApply = function adCoreV94ScheduleWithSlotLock() {
    adCoreV94ScheduleBase();
    adCoreV94EnsureObserver();
    adCoreV94Schedule();
  };

  const adCoreV94V91ApplyBase = adCoreV91Apply;
  adCoreV91Apply = function adCoreV94V91ApplyWithSlotLock() {
    adCoreV94V91ApplyBase();
    adCoreV94EnsureStyle();
    adCoreV94EnsureObserver();
    adCoreV94Schedule();
  };

  window.addEventListener('resize', adCoreV94Schedule, { passive:true });
  window.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', adCoreV94Schedule, { passive:true });
  adCoreV94EnsureStyle();
  adCoreV94EnsureObserver();
  adCoreV94Schedule();


  /* ========================================================================
     v95 – LAYOUT ON / SYMMETRY, ACTION ROW, CHECKOUT CENTRING, ACTIVE STATE
     ------------------------------------------------------------------------
     v94 successfully stabilised the card *sizes*, but the visible turn row
     still started at the left edge of its free lane. That produced unequal
     clearance next to the player cards. Some Checkout / Guide renderers also
     use a nested stack whose native alignment is flex-start.

     This final layer deliberately does NOT change board size, player-card
     geometry, own-history geometry, card height or total-card width.
     It only:
       1) centres the already locked four-card row in its lane;
       2) centres all checkout/tip/throw content inside the locked slots;
       3) pins Undo/Next below the visual cards, right-aligned to the same row;
       4) lets the established CORE active highlight win over the restrained
          v91 reference indicator.
     ======================================================================== */

  const AD_CORE_V95_STYLE_ID = 'ad-core-v95-layout-on-finish-style';
  let adCoreV95Raf = 0;
  let adCoreV95Timer = 0;

  function adCoreV95IsActive() {
    try {
      return !!cfg()?.LAYOUT_ENABLED &&
        document.documentElement.classList.contains('ad-core-layout-on') &&
        document.documentElement.classList.contains('ad-core-v67-two-player');
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  function adCoreV95Visible(el) {
    if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return false;
    const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
    if (cs.display === 'none' || cs.visibility === 'hidden' || Number(cs.opacity || 1) <= 0) return false;
    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    return r.width > 8 && r.height > 8;
  }

  function adCoreV95TurnSurfaces() {
    const turn = document.querySelector('#ad-ext-turn');
    if (!(turn instanceof HTMLElement) || !adCoreV95IsActive()) return { turn:null, surfaces:[], row:null };

    let surfaces = [];
    try { surfaces = adCoreV94DistinctSurfaces(turn).slice(0, 4); } catch (_) {}
    surfaces = surfaces.filter(adCoreV95Visible);
    if (surfaces.length < 4) return { turn, surfaces, row:null };

    let row = null;
    try { row = adCoreV94FindTurnRow(turn, surfaces); } catch (_) {}
    if (!(row instanceof HTMLElement)) {
      const parentCounts = new Map();
      for (const surface of surfaces) {
        let p = surface.parentElement;
        for (let depth = 0; p && p !== turn && depth < 4; depth += 1, p = p.parentElement) {
          const score = (parentCounts.get(p) || 0) + 1;
          parentCounts.set(p, score);
          if (score >= 4) { row = p; break; }
        }
        if (row) break;
      }
    }
    return { turn, surfaces, row: row instanceof HTMLElement ? row : null };
  }

  function adCoreV95DirectSlotHost(surface, row) {
    if (!(surface instanceof HTMLElement) || !(row instanceof HTMLElement)) return surface;
    let el = surface;
    for (let depth = 0; el.parentElement && el.parentElement !== row && depth < 8; depth += 1) el = el.parentElement;
    return el;
  }

  function adCoreV95SlotWidths() {
    const root = document.documentElement;
    const read = (name, fallback) => {
      const n = parseFloat(getComputedStyle(root).getPropertyValue(name));
      return Number.isFinite(n) && n > 20 ? n : fallback;
    };
    return {
      total: read('--ad-core-v92-total-card-width', 160),
      throw: read('--ad-core-v92-throw-card-width', 240),
      height: read('--ad-core-v92-turn-card-height', 126),
    };
  }

  function adCoreV95LockHost(host, width, height) {
    if (!(host instanceof HTMLElement) || !(width > 0)) return;
    host.dataset.adCoreV95TurnSlotHost = '1';
    host.style.setProperty('box-sizing', 'border-box', 'important');
    host.style.setProperty('flex', `0 0 ${Math.round(width)}px`, 'important');
    host.style.setProperty('flex-grow', '0', 'important');
    host.style.setProperty('flex-shrink', '0', 'important');
    host.style.setProperty('flex-basis', `${Math.round(width)}px`, 'important');
    host.style.setProperty('width', `${Math.round(width)}px`, 'important');
    host.style.setProperty('min-width', `${Math.round(width)}px`, 'important');
    host.style.setProperty('max-width', `${Math.round(width)}px`, 'important');
    if (height > 0) {
      host.style.setProperty('height', `${Math.round(height)}px`, 'important');
      host.style.setProperty('min-height', `${Math.round(height)}px`, 'important');
      host.style.setProperty('max-height', `${Math.round(height)}px`, 'important');
    }
  }

  function adCoreV95CentreSlotContent(slot) {
    if (!(slot instanceof HTMLElement) || !adCoreV95Visible(slot)) return;
    const slotRect = slot.getBoundingClientRect();
    slot.dataset.adCoreV95TurnSlot = '1';
    slot.style.setProperty('display', 'flex', 'important');
    slot.style.setProperty('align-items', 'center', 'important');
    slot.style.setProperty('justify-content', 'center', 'important');
    slot.style.setProperty('align-content', 'center', 'important');
    slot.style.setProperty('text-align', 'center', 'important');
    slot.style.setProperty('overflow', 'hidden', 'important');

    // Find only shell-like descendants. We do not flatten the component tree
    // or touch small text leaves individually; the existing Checkout badge /
    // tip hierarchy remains intact, but its full-size stack is centred.
    const candidates = Array.from(slot.querySelectorAll(':scope > *, :scope > * > *, :scope > * > * > *'))
      .filter((el) => el instanceof HTMLElement && adCoreV95Visible(el));
    for (const el of candidates) {
      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      const shellLike = r.width >= slotRect.width * 0.58 || r.height >= slotRect.height * 0.62;
      if (!shellLike) continue;
      el.dataset.adCoreV95TurnShell = '1';
      el.style.setProperty('box-sizing', 'border-box', 'important');
      el.style.setProperty('text-align', 'center', 'important');
      el.style.setProperty('align-self', 'stretch', 'important');
      const display = getComputedStyle(el).display;
      if (display.includes('flex') || display.includes('grid')) {
        el.style.setProperty('align-items', 'center', 'important');
        el.style.setProperty('justify-content', 'center', 'important');
        el.style.setProperty('align-content', 'center', 'important');
      }
    }
    slot.querySelectorAll('p, span').forEach((el) => {
      if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
      el.style.setProperty('text-align', 'center', 'important');
      el.style.setProperty('margin-left', 'auto', 'important');
      el.style.setProperty('margin-right', 'auto', 'important');
    });
  }

  function adCoreV95FindActionRow() {
    const buttonText = (el) => String(el?.innerText || el?.textContent || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const isUndo = (el) => /\bundo\b|vissza|zurück|retour/i.test(buttonText(el));
    const isNext = (el) => /\bnext\b|következő|weiter|suivant/i.test(buttonText(el));
    const buttons = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#ad-ext-turn button, button'))
      .filter((el) => el instanceof HTMLElement && adCoreV95Visible(el));
    const undo = buttons.find(isUndo);
    const next = buttons.find(isNext);
    if (!(undo instanceof HTMLElement) || !(next instanceof HTMLElement)) return null;

    let p = undo.parentElement;
    for (let depth = 0; p && depth < 8; depth += 1, p = p.parentElement) {
      if (!p.contains(next)) continue;
      const r = p.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (r.width >= 80 && r.width <= 480 && r.height <= 110) return p;
    }
    return undo.parentElement instanceof HTMLElement ? undo.parentElement : null;
  }

  function adCoreV95PlaceActions(surfaces) {
    const actionRow = adCoreV95FindActionRow();
    if (!(actionRow instanceof HTMLElement) || surfaces.length < 4) return;

    const rects = surfaces.map((el) => el.getBoundingClientRect()).filter((r) => r.width > 10 && r.height > 10);
    if (rects.length < 4) return;
    const left = Math.min(...rects.map((r) => r.left));
    const right = Math.max(...rects.map((r) => r.right));
    const bottom = Math.max(...rects.map((r) => r.bottom));

    // Clear old v76 low-height translations and HUD transforms: v95 owns this
    // row only while Layout ON/two-player is active.
    actionRow.dataset.adCoreV95ActionRow = '1';
    actionRow.style.setProperty('position', 'fixed', 'important');
    actionRow.style.setProperty('transform', 'none', 'important');
    actionRow.style.setProperty('translate', 'none', 'important');
    actionRow.style.setProperty('margin', '0', 'important');
    actionRow.style.setProperty('right', 'auto', 'important');
    actionRow.style.setProperty('bottom', 'auto', 'important');
    actionRow.style.setProperty('width', 'max-content', 'important');
    actionRow.style.setProperty('min-width', '0', 'important');
    actionRow.style.setProperty('max-width', 'none', 'important');
    actionRow.style.setProperty('display', 'flex', 'important');
    actionRow.style.setProperty('justify-content', 'flex-end', 'important');
    actionRow.style.setProperty('align-items', 'center', 'important');
    actionRow.style.setProperty('z-index', '90', 'important');
    actionRow.style.setProperty('pointer-events', 'auto', 'important');

    const own = actionRow.getBoundingClientRect();
    const preferredGap = Math.max(14, Math.min(24, Math.round(window.innerHeight * .020)));
    const targetLeft = Math.max(left, Math.round(right - own.width));
    const targetTop = Math.round(bottom + preferredGap);
    actionRow.style.setProperty('left', `${targetLeft}px`, 'important');
    actionRow.style.setProperty('top', `${targetTop}px`, 'important');
  }

  function adCoreV95Clear() {
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-ad-core-v95-turn-slot-host="1"]').forEach((el) => {
      if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreV95TurnSlotHost;
      ['box-sizing','flex','flex-grow','flex-shrink','flex-basis','width','min-width','max-width','height','min-height','max-height'].forEach((p) => el.style.removeProperty(p));
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-ad-core-v95-turn-slot="1"], [data-ad-core-v95-turn-shell="1"]').forEach((el) => {
      if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreV95TurnSlot;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreV95TurnShell;
      ['display','align-items','justify-content','align-content','text-align','overflow','align-self','margin-left','margin-right'].forEach((p) => el.style.removeProperty(p));
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-ad-core-v95-action-row="1"]').forEach((el) => {
      if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreV95ActionRow;
      ['position','transform','translate','margin','left','right','top','bottom','width','min-width','max-width','display','justify-content','align-items','z-index','pointer-events'].forEach((p) => el.style.removeProperty(p));
    });
  }

  function adCoreV95Apply() {
    if (!adCoreV95IsActive()) {
      adCoreV95Clear();
      return;
    }
    const { turn, surfaces, row } = adCoreV95TurnSurfaces();
    if (!(turn instanceof HTMLElement) || surfaces.length < 4) return;

    const size = adCoreV95SlotWidths();
    const total = surfaces[0];
    const throws = surfaces.slice(1, 4);

    // The v94 variables are viewport-derived and stable. Lock both surface and
    // direct row child so a React renderer swap cannot briefly collapse a slot.
    for (const [index, surface] of surfaces.entries()) {
      const width = index === 0 ? size.total : size.throw;
      try { adCoreV94ApplyWidth(surface, width, size.height); } catch (_) {}
      adCoreV95LockHost(adCoreV95DirectSlotHost(surface, row), width, size.height);
      if (index > 0) adCoreV95CentreSlotContent(surface);
    }

    // This is the missing symmetry: v91/v94 intentionally used flex-start.
    // The calculated lane is wider by a small remainder, so that remainder
    // must be split equally left/right rather than left entirely at the start.
    if (row instanceof HTMLElement) {
      row.dataset.adCoreV95TurnRow = '1';
      row.style.setProperty('justify-content', 'center', 'important');
      row.style.setProperty('justify-items', 'center', 'important');
      row.style.setProperty('align-items', 'center', 'important');
      row.style.setProperty('column-gap', '8px', 'important');
      row.style.setProperty('gap', '8px', 'important');
      row.style.setProperty('flex-wrap', 'nowrap', 'important');
    }
    turn.style.setProperty('justify-content', 'center', 'important');

    adCoreV95PlaceActions(surfaces);
  }

  function adCoreV95Schedule() {
    if (adCoreV95Raf) return;
    adCoreV95Raf = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      adCoreV95Raf = 0;
      adCoreV95Apply();
      clearTimeout(adCoreV95Timer);
      adCoreV95Timer = setTimeout(adCoreV95Apply, 70);
    });
  }

  function adCoreV95EnsureStyle() {
    ensureHead(() => {
      let st = document.getElementById(AD_CORE_V95_STYLE_ID);
      if (!st) {
        st = document.createElement('style');
        st.id = AD_CORE_V95_STYLE_ID;
        document.head.appendChild(st);
      }
      st.textContent = `
/* v95: restore the configurable active-player visual, which v91 reduced to
   a thin reference line. Native and script marker are both accepted. */
html.ad-core-v67-native-page.ad-core-layout-on.ad-core-v67-two-player
#ad-ext-player-display > div.ad-core-v91-card.ad-active-player,
html.ad-core-v67-native-page.ad-core-layout-on.ad-core-v67-two-player
#ad-ext-player-display > div.ad-core-v91-card:has(.ad-ext-player-active){
  border-color:rgba(var(--ad-active-rgb),.98) !important;
  outline:var(--ad-active-outline) solid rgba(var(--ad-active-rgb),.92) !important;
  outline-offset:calc(-1 * var(--ad-active-outline)) !important;
  box-shadow:
    0 0 0 1px rgba(255,255,255,.22) inset,
    0 0 34px rgba(var(--ad-active-rgb),calc(var(--ad-active-glow) * 1.15)),
    0 0 108px rgba(var(--ad-active-rgb),calc(var(--ad-active-glow) * .72)),
    0 12px 36px rgba(0,0,0,.34) !important;
  filter:brightness(1.07) saturate(1.08) !important;
}
html.ad-core-v67-native-page.ad-core-layout-on.ad-core-v67-two-player
#ad-ext-player-display > div.ad-core-v91-card.ad-active-player::before,
html.ad-core-v67-native-page.ad-core-layout-on.ad-core-v67-two-player
#ad-ext-player-display > div.ad-core-v91-card:has(.ad-ext-player-active)::before{
  background:#22d36b !important;
}

/* The row is centred by JS as well, but keep the CSS fallback active during
   React's replacement frame. */
html.ad-core-v67-native-page.ad-core-layout-on.ad-core-v67-two-player
#ad-ext-turn [data-ad-core-v94-row="1"],
html.ad-core-v67-native-page.ad-core-layout-on.ad-core-v67-two-player
#ad-ext-turn [data-ad-core-v95-turn-row="1"]{
  justify-content:center !important;
}
html.ad-core-v67-native-page.ad-core-layout-on.ad-core-v67-two-player
#ad-ext-turn [data-ad-core-v95-turn-slot="1"]{
  text-align:center !important;
  align-items:center !important;
  justify-content:center !important;
}
`;
    });
  }

  // Let the existing v94 lock run first; v95 then recentres its final,
  // already-sized surfaces. This preserves all earlier runtime observers.
  const adCoreV95V94LockBase = adCoreV94LockSlots;
  adCoreV94LockSlots = function adCoreV95LockAfterV94() {
    adCoreV95V94LockBase();
    adCoreV95Schedule();
  };

  const adCoreV95V91ApplyBase = adCoreV91Apply;
  adCoreV91Apply = function adCoreV95ApplyAfterGeometry() {
    adCoreV95V91ApplyBase();
    adCoreV95EnsureStyle();
    adCoreV95Schedule();
  };

  window.addEventListener('resize', adCoreV95Schedule, { passive:true });
  window.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', adCoreV95Schedule, { passive:true });
  adCoreV95EnsureStyle();
  adCoreV95Schedule();


  /* ========================================================================
     v96 – CHECKOUT / GUIDE CONTENT CENTRING + REMOVE GREEN ACTIVE STRIPE
     ------------------------------------------------------------------------
     v95 centred the visual *card surfaces*. The remaining failure was deeper:
     Checkout / guide cards are state-specific React components whose inner
     content may retain `flex-start`, absolute top/left offsets or an old
     `top:-1em` state style. Normal scored throws must keep their small token
     label in the lower-right corner, therefore this correction is deliberately
     limited to cards that actually contain a CHECKOUT/GUIDE marker.

     The green vertical stripe is the v91 reference indicator. It is not part
     of the configured CORE active-player highlight, so v96 suppresses only
     that pseudo-element; the configured outline/glow from v95 remains.
     ======================================================================== */

  const AD_CORE_V96_STYLE_ID = 'ad-core-v96-checkout-center-style';
  let adCoreV96Raf = 0;
  let adCoreV96Timer = 0;

  function adCoreV96IsActive() {
    try {
      return !!cfg()?.LAYOUT_ENABLED &&
        document.documentElement.classList.contains('ad-core-layout-on') &&
        document.documentElement.classList.contains('ad-core-v67-two-player');
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  function adCoreV96Visible(el) {
    if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return false;
    const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
    if (cs.display === 'none' || cs.visibility === 'hidden' || Number(cs.opacity || 1) <= 0) return false;
    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    return r.width > 8 && r.height > 8;
  }

  function adCoreV96Text(el) {
    return String(el?.innerText || el?.textContent || '')
      .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase();
  }

  function adCoreV96IsCheckoutGuideRoot(el) {
    if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement) || !adCoreV96Visible(el)) return false;
    const text = adCoreV96Text(el);
    // Current Autodarts Checkout and guide states all expose at least one of
    // these terms in the visual card. We intentionally do not match plain
    // T20 / S20 because normal registered throws use those tokens too.
    return /\b(checkout|check out|guide|tipp|tip|auscheck|finish|bullout)\b/i.test(text);
  }

  function adCoreV96FindCheckoutRoot(slot) {
    if (!(slot instanceof HTMLElement)) return null;
    const candidates = [slot, ...Array.from(slot.querySelectorAll('*'))]
      .filter((el) => el instanceof HTMLElement && adCoreV96IsCheckoutGuideRoot(el));
    if (!candidates.length) return null;

    const slotRect = slot.getBoundingClientRect();
    // Prefer the largest meaningful state component. It is the component that
    // owns both the yellow label and its value, so we can centre them as one
    // vertical group without changing normal numeric throw cards.
    candidates.sort((a, b) => {
      const ar = a.getBoundingClientRect();
      const br = b.getBoundingClientRect();
      const aScore = Math.min(ar.width, slotRect.width) * Math.min(ar.height, slotRect.height);
      const bScore = Math.min(br.width, slotRect.width) * Math.min(br.height, slotRect.height);
      return bScore - aScore;
    });
    return candidates[0] || null;
  }

  function adCoreV96CentreCheckout(slot) {
    const root = adCoreV96FindCheckoutRoot(slot);
    if (!(root instanceof HTMLElement)) return;

    slot.dataset.adCoreV96CheckoutSlot = '1';
    root.dataset.adCoreV96CheckoutRoot = '1';

    // The root is deliberately made a normal, full-card flex column. This
    // removes only old state-specific positioning; it does not change the
    // surface width/height locked by v94/v95.
    root.style.setProperty('position', 'relative', 'important');
    root.style.setProperty('inset', 'auto', 'important');
    root.style.setProperty('top', 'auto', 'important');
    root.style.setProperty('right', 'auto', 'important');
    root.style.setProperty('bottom', 'auto', 'important');
    root.style.setProperty('left', 'auto', 'important');
    root.style.setProperty('transform', 'none', 'important');
    root.style.setProperty('translate', 'none', 'important');
    root.style.setProperty('width', '100%', 'important');
    root.style.setProperty('min-width', '0', 'important');
    root.style.setProperty('max-width', '100%', 'important');
    root.style.setProperty('height', '100%', 'important');
    root.style.setProperty('min-height', '0', 'important');
    root.style.setProperty('max-height', '100%', 'important');
    root.style.setProperty('margin', '0', 'important');
    root.style.setProperty('padding', '8px 10px', 'important');
    root.style.setProperty('box-sizing', 'border-box', 'important');
    root.style.setProperty('display', 'flex', 'important');
    root.style.setProperty('flex-direction', 'column', 'important');
    root.style.setProperty('flex-wrap', 'nowrap', 'important');
    root.style.setProperty('align-items', 'center', 'important');
    root.style.setProperty('align-content', 'center', 'important');
    root.style.setProperty('justify-content', 'center', 'important');
    root.style.setProperty('text-align', 'center', 'important');
    root.style.setProperty('gap', '3px', 'important');

    // Only neutralise positioning on actual text-bearing checkout descendants.
    // Decorative backgrounds/icons keep their native geometry untouched.
    const rootRect = root.getBoundingClientRect();
    const textNodes = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll('p, span, div, strong, b'))
      .filter((el) => el instanceof HTMLElement && adCoreV96Visible(el))
      .filter((el) => {
        const t = adCoreV96Text(el);
        if (!t) return false;
        const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
        return r.width <= rootRect.width * 0.98 && r.height <= rootRect.height * 0.92;
      });

    for (const el of textNodes) {
      el.dataset.adCoreV96CheckoutText = '1';
      const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
      if (cs.position === 'absolute' || cs.position === 'fixed') {
        el.style.setProperty('position', 'relative', 'important');
        el.style.setProperty('inset', 'auto', 'important');
        el.style.setProperty('top', 'auto', 'important');
        el.style.setProperty('right', 'auto', 'important');
        el.style.setProperty('bottom', 'auto', 'important');
        el.style.setProperty('left', 'auto', 'important');
        el.style.setProperty('transform', 'none', 'important');
        el.style.setProperty('translate', 'none', 'important');
      }
      el.style.setProperty('align-self', 'center', 'important');
      el.style.setProperty('margin-left', 'auto', 'important');
      el.style.setProperty('margin-right', 'auto', 'important');
      el.style.setProperty('text-align', 'center', 'important');
    }
  }

  function adCoreV96ClearCheckout() {
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-ad-core-v96-checkout-root="1"]').forEach((el) => {
      if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreV96CheckoutRoot;
      [
        'position','inset','top','right','bottom','left','transform','translate',
        'width','min-width','max-width','height','min-height','max-height',
        'margin','padding','box-sizing','display','flex-direction','flex-wrap',
        'align-items','align-content','justify-content','text-align','gap'
      ].forEach((p) => el.style.removeProperty(p));
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-ad-core-v96-checkout-text="1"]').forEach((el) => {
      if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreV96CheckoutText;
      [
        'position','inset','top','right','bottom','left','transform','translate',
        'align-self','margin-left','margin-right','text-align'
      ].forEach((p) => el.style.removeProperty(p));
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-ad-core-v96-checkout-slot="1"]').forEach((el) => {
      if (el instanceof HTMLElement) delete el.dataset.adCoreV96CheckoutSlot;
    });
  }

  function adCoreV96Apply() {
    if (!adCoreV96IsActive()) {
      adCoreV96ClearCheckout();
      return;
    }
    const turn = document.querySelector('#ad-ext-turn');
    if (!(turn instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
    let surfaces = [];
    try { surfaces = adCoreV94DistinctSurfaces(turn).slice(1, 4); } catch (_) {}
    for (const slot of surfaces) adCoreV96CentreCheckout(slot);
  }

  function adCoreV96Schedule() {
    if (adCoreV96Raf) return;
    adCoreV96Raf = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      adCoreV96Raf = 0;
      adCoreV96Apply();
      clearTimeout(adCoreV96Timer);
      adCoreV96Timer = setTimeout(adCoreV96Apply, 90);
    });
  }

  function adCoreV96EnsureStyle() {
    ensureHead(() => {
      let st = document.getElementById(AD_CORE_V96_STYLE_ID);
      if (!st) {
        st = document.createElement('style');
        st.id = AD_CORE_V96_STYLE_ID;
        document.head.appendChild(st);
      }
      st.textContent = `
/* v96: remove only the v91 green edge marker. The configured v95 active
   outline/glow remains fully active. */
html.ad-core-v67-native-page.ad-core-layout-on.ad-core-v67-two-player
#ad-ext-player-display > div.ad-core-v91-card.ad-active-player::before,
html.ad-core-v67-native-page.ad-core-layout-on.ad-core-v67-two-player
#ad-ext-player-display > div.ad-core-v91-card:has(.ad-ext-player-active)::before{
  content:none !important;
  display:none !important;
}

/* CSS fallback for React's brief replacement frame before v96 JS centres the
   checkout component. It deliberately applies only to marked checkout slots. */
html.ad-core-v67-native-page.ad-core-layout-on.ad-core-v67-two-player
#ad-ext-turn [data-ad-core-v96-checkout-root="1"]{
  display:flex !important;
  flex-direction:column !important;
  align-items:center !important;
  justify-content:center !important;
  text-align:center !important;
}
html.ad-core-v67-native-page.ad-core-layout-on.ad-core-v67-two-player
#ad-ext-turn [data-ad-core-v96-checkout-text="1"]{
  text-align:center !important;
  align-self:center !important;
  margin-left:auto !important;
  margin-right:auto !important;
}
`;
    });
  }

  const adCoreV96V95ApplyBase = adCoreV95Apply;
  adCoreV95Apply = function adCoreV96ApplyAfterV95() {
    adCoreV95ApplyBase();
    adCoreV96EnsureStyle();
    adCoreV96Schedule();
  };

  const adCoreV96V94LockBase = adCoreV94LockSlots;
  adCoreV94LockSlots = function adCoreV96LockAfterV94() {
    adCoreV96V94LockBase();
    adCoreV96Schedule();
  };

  window.addEventListener('resize', adCoreV96Schedule, { passive:true });
  window.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', adCoreV96Schedule, { passive:true });
  adCoreV96EnsureStyle();
  adCoreV96Schedule();


  /* ========================================================================
     v97 – LAYOUT ON / CENTRE THE COMPLETE TURN-CARD GROUP IN THE PLAYER LANE
     ------------------------------------------------------------------------
     The issue here is NOT the text inside Checkout / guide cards. Those are
     already centred. The remaining visual offset is the entire four-card
     group (total + three throw cards): its left and right clearances to the
     real player-card edges differ by a few pixels.

     This layer changes no card width, height, content alignment, board size,
     player-card geometry, history, or Checkout rendering. It measures the
     rendered player-card inner edges and the rendered four-card group after
     the existing v94/v95 fixed-width logic has run, then shifts only the row
     by the exact delta required to make both outer clearances equal.
     ======================================================================== */

  const AD_CORE_V97_STYLE_ID = 'ad-core-v97-turn-group-lane-center-style';
  let adCoreV97Raf = 0;
  let adCoreV97Timer = 0;

  function adCoreV97IsActive() {
    try {
      return !!cfg()?.LAYOUT_ENABLED &&
        document.documentElement.classList.contains('ad-core-layout-on') &&
        document.documentElement.classList.contains('ad-core-v67-two-player');
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  function adCoreV97Visible(el) {
    if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return false;
    const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
    if (cs.display === 'none' || cs.visibility === 'hidden' || Number(cs.opacity || 1) <= 0) return false;
    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    return r.width > 8 && r.height > 8;
  }

  function adCoreV97Panels() {
    let panels = [];
    try { panels = adCoreV91Panels(); } catch (_) {}
    return panels
      .filter(adCoreV97Visible)
      .sort((a, b) => a.getBoundingClientRect().left - b.getBoundingClientRect().left)
      .slice(0, 2);
  }

  function adCoreV97Clear() {
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-ad-core-v97-turn-row="1"]').forEach((el) => {
      if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreV97TurnRow;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreV97TurnDelta;
      [
        'position','left','right','transform','translate','will-change'
      ].forEach((property) => el.style.removeProperty(property));
    });
  }

  function adCoreV97Apply() {
    if (!adCoreV97IsActive()) {
      adCoreV97Clear();
      return;
    }

    const panels = adCoreV97Panels();
    const turnInfo = adCoreV95TurnSurfaces();
    const row = turnInfo?.row;
    const surfaces = Array.isArray(turnInfo?.surfaces) ? turnInfo.surfaces : [];
    if (panels.length !== 2 || !(row instanceof HTMLElement) || surfaces.length < 4) return;

    /* Always measure from an unshifted row. This keeps recalculation stable
       after every React replacement, resize and dart state transition. */
    row.dataset.adCoreV97TurnRow = '1';
    row.style.setProperty('position', 'relative', 'important');
    row.style.setProperty('left', '0px', 'important');
    row.style.setProperty('right', 'auto', 'important');
    row.style.setProperty('transform', 'none', 'important');
    row.style.setProperty('translate', 'none', 'important');
    row.style.setProperty('will-change', 'left', 'important');

    /* Force layout after clearing the previous delta. */
    void row.offsetWidth;

    const leftCard = panels[0].getBoundingClientRect();
    const rightCard = panels[1].getBoundingClientRect();
    const rects = surfaces
      .filter(adCoreV97Visible)
      .map((el) => el.getBoundingClientRect())
      .filter((r) => r.width > 8 && r.height > 8);
    if (rects.length < 4 || rightCard.left <= leftCard.right) return;

    const groupLeft = Math.min(...rects.map((r) => r.left));
    const groupRight = Math.max(...rects.map((r) => r.right));
    const laneLeft = leftCard.right;
    const laneRight = rightCard.left;
    const laneWidth = laneRight - laneLeft;
    const groupWidth = groupRight - groupLeft;
    if (!(laneWidth > 0) || !(groupWidth > 0)) return;

    const targetCenter = laneLeft + laneWidth / 2;
    const currentCenter = groupLeft + groupWidth / 2;
    const requestedDelta = targetCenter - currentCenter;

    /* Never let a pathological transient measurement push the group outside
       its available lane. Normal state needs only a small correction. */
    const maxPositive = Math.max(0, laneRight - groupRight);
    const maxNegative = Math.max(0, groupLeft - laneLeft);
    const delta = Math.round(
      Math.max(-maxNegative, Math.min(maxPositive, requestedDelta)) * 10
    ) / 10;

    row.style.setProperty('left', `${delta}px`, 'important');
    row.dataset.adCoreV97TurnDelta = String(delta);

    /* v95 owns the action buttons. Re-run its placement only after the turn
       group has been shifted, so Undo/Next stay attached to the same group. */
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      try {
        if (adCoreV97IsActive()) adCoreV95PlaceActions(surfaces);
      } catch (_) {}
    });
  }

  function adCoreV97Schedule() {
    if (adCoreV97Raf) return;
    adCoreV97Raf = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      adCoreV97Raf = 0;
      adCoreV97Apply();
      clearTimeout(adCoreV97Timer);
      adCoreV97Timer = setTimeout(adCoreV97Apply, 100);
    });
  }

  function adCoreV97EnsureStyle() {
    ensureHead(() => {
      let st = document.getElementById(AD_CORE_V97_STYLE_ID);
      if (!st) {
        st = document.createElement('style');
        st.id = AD_CORE_V97_STYLE_ID;
        document.head.appendChild(st);
      }
      st.textContent = `
/* v97 only moves the already sized, already styled complete turn row.
   It deliberately does not alter checkout/tip text or individual card sizes. */
html.ad-core-v67-native-page.ad-core-layout-on.ad-core-v67-two-player
#ad-ext-turn [data-ad-core-v97-turn-row="1"]{
  position:relative !important;
}
`;
    });
  }

  const adCoreV97V95ApplyBase = adCoreV95Apply;
  adCoreV95Apply = function adCoreV97ApplyAfterV95() {
    adCoreV97V95ApplyBase();
    adCoreV97EnsureStyle();
    adCoreV97Schedule();
  };

  const adCoreV97V94LockBase = adCoreV94LockSlots;
  adCoreV94LockSlots = function adCoreV97LockAfterV94() {
    adCoreV97V94LockBase();
    adCoreV97Schedule();
  };

  window.addEventListener('resize', adCoreV97Schedule, { passive:true });
  window.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', adCoreV97Schedule, { passive:true });
  adCoreV97EnsureStyle();
  adCoreV97Schedule();


  /* ========================================================================
     v98 – LAYOUT ON / TRUE CENTRING OF THE REAL FOUR-SLOT TURN ROW
     ------------------------------------------------------------------------
     v97 tried to shift a wrapper with `left`. In the current Autodarts DOM
     that wrapper is not the layout owner of the visible grid tracks, so the
     measured delta could be correct while the row itself never moved.

     This replacement does not translate any wrapper. It makes the *actual*
     common row of Total + Throw 1 + Throw 2 + Throw 3 fill the measured turn
     lane and centres the fixed grid/flex tracks inside that row. Therefore the
     left and right gaps are calculated by the browser from one single row,
     rather than from an ineffective visual offset.
     ======================================================================== */

  const AD_CORE_V98_STYLE_ID = 'ad-core-v98-turn-row-true-center-style';
  let adCoreV98Raf = 0;
  let adCoreV98Timer = 0;

  function adCoreV98IsActive() {
    try { return adCoreV95IsActive(); } catch (_) { return false; }
  }

  function adCoreV98Clear() {
    try { adCoreV97Clear(); } catch (_) {}
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-ad-core-v98-turn-row="1"]').forEach((el) => {
      if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreV98TurnRow;
      [
        'position','left','right','transform','translate','will-change',
        'width','min-width','max-width','box-sizing','justify-content',
        'justify-items','place-content','margin-left','margin-right'
      ].forEach((property) => el.style.removeProperty(property));
    });
  }

  function adCoreV98GetRow(turnInfo) {
    const row = turnInfo?.row;
    const surfaces = Array.isArray(turnInfo?.surfaces) ? turnInfo.surfaces : [];
    if (!(row instanceof HTMLElement) || surfaces.length < 4) return null;

    // In a few Autodarts states the immediate surface parent is one wrapper
    // deeper than the row returned by the generic finder. Prefer the first
    // common direct host parent only when all four slots agree on it.
    const hosts = surfaces.slice(0, 4).map((surface) => {
      try { return adCoreV95DirectSlotHost(surface, row); } catch (_) { return surface; }
    }).filter((el) => el instanceof HTMLElement);
    if (hosts.length === 4) {
      const parent = hosts[0].parentElement;
      if (parent instanceof HTMLElement && hosts.every((host) => host.parentElement === parent)) {
        return parent;
      }
    }
    return row;
  }

  function adCoreV98Apply() {
    if (!adCoreV98IsActive()) {
      adCoreV98Clear();
      return;
    }

    // Remove v97's old relative-left correction before measuring/centring.
    try { adCoreV97Clear(); } catch (_) {}

    let info = null;
    try { info = adCoreV95TurnSurfaces(); } catch (_) {}
    const turn = info?.turn;
    const surfaces = Array.isArray(info?.surfaces) ? info.surfaces.slice(0, 4) : [];
    const row = adCoreV98GetRow(info);
    if (!(turn instanceof HTMLElement) || !(row instanceof HTMLElement) || surfaces.length < 4) return;

    row.dataset.adCoreV98TurnRow = '1';
    row.style.setProperty('position', 'relative', 'important');
    row.style.setProperty('left', '0px', 'important');
    row.style.setProperty('right', 'auto', 'important');
    row.style.setProperty('transform', 'none', 'important');
    row.style.setProperty('translate', 'none', 'important');
    row.style.setProperty('will-change', 'auto', 'important');
    row.style.setProperty('box-sizing', 'border-box', 'important');
    row.style.setProperty('width', '100%', 'important');
    row.style.setProperty('min-width', '0px', 'important');
    row.style.setProperty('max-width', '100%', 'important');
    row.style.setProperty('margin-left', '0px', 'important');
    row.style.setProperty('margin-right', '0px', 'important');

    // Works for both native flex rows and grid rows. For grid, this centres
    // the explicitly locked tracks; for flex, it centres the fixed card items.
    row.style.setProperty('justify-content', 'center', 'important');
    row.style.setProperty('justify-items', 'stretch', 'important');
    row.style.setProperty('place-content', 'start center', 'important');

    // The action row is owned by v95. Recalculate it after the genuine card
    // row has reflowed so its right edge follows the visible four-card group.
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      try {
        if (adCoreV98IsActive()) adCoreV95PlaceActions(surfaces);
      } catch (_) {}
    });
  }

  function adCoreV98Schedule() {
    if (adCoreV98Raf) return;
    adCoreV98Raf = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      adCoreV98Raf = 0;
      adCoreV98Apply();
      clearTimeout(adCoreV98Timer);
      adCoreV98Timer = setTimeout(adCoreV98Apply, 90);
    });
  }

  function adCoreV98EnsureStyle() {
    ensureHead(() => {
      let st = document.getElementById(AD_CORE_V98_STYLE_ID);
      if (!st) {
        st = document.createElement('style');
        st.id = AD_CORE_V98_STYLE_ID;
        document.head.appendChild(st);
      }
      st.textContent = `
/* v98: centre the actual Total + 3 throw layout tracks, not an outer wrapper. */
html.ad-core-v67-native-page.ad-core-layout-on.ad-core-v67-two-player
#ad-ext-turn [data-ad-core-v98-turn-row="1"]{
  box-sizing:border-box !important;
  width:100% !important;
  min-width:0 !important;
  max-width:100% !important;
  justify-content:center !important;
  place-content:start center !important;
}
`;
    });
  }

  // Replace v97's ineffective relative-left mechanism everywhere it was
  // scheduled by v94/v95. Existing wrapper functions call this binding at run
  // time, so no other functional CORE module needs to be edited.
  adCoreV97Apply = function adCoreV98ReplacesV97Apply() {
    adCoreV98Apply();
  };
  adCoreV97Schedule = function adCoreV98ReplacesV97Schedule() {
    adCoreV98Schedule();
  };

  window.addEventListener('resize', adCoreV98Schedule, { passive:true });
  window.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', adCoreV98Schedule, { passive:true });
  adCoreV98EnsureStyle();
  adCoreV98Schedule();

  /* ========================================================================
