     v99 – LAYOUT ON / DIRECT FOUR-SLOT TURN GROUP GEOMETRY
     ------------------------------------------------------------------------
     v97/v98 tried to center a row wrapper. In the live Autodarts DOM that
     wrapper does not own the visible card positions in every state, so even a
     correct calculated delta could leave the real cards unchanged.

     This layer positions the four actual card hosts directly from the two
     player-card inner edges:
       left player right edge | equal free space | total + three throws |
       equal free space | right player left edge.

     The slot widths/heights remain the already working v92/v94 values; this
     layer only fixes their common X/Y geometry and attaches Undo/Next beneath
     the resulting visible group. No React node is moved, cloned or removed.
     ======================================================================== */

  const AD_CORE_V99_STYLE_ID = 'ad-core-v99-direct-turn-group-style';
  let adCoreV99Raf = 0;
  let adCoreV99Timer = 0;
  let adCoreV99Observer = null;
  let adCoreV99ObservedTurn = null;

  function adCoreV99IsActive() {
    try { return adCoreV95IsActive(); } catch (_) { return false; }
  }

  function adCoreV99Visible(el) {
    try { return adCoreV95Visible(el); } catch (_) {
      if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return false;
      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      return r.width > 8 && r.height > 8 && getComputedStyle(el).display !== 'none';
    }
  }

  function adCoreV99Info() {
    let info = null;
    try { info = adCoreV95TurnSurfaces(); } catch (_) {}
    const turn = info?.turn;
    const surfaces = Array.isArray(info?.surfaces) ? info.surfaces.slice(0, 4).filter(adCoreV99Visible) : [];
    const row = info?.row instanceof HTMLElement ? info.row : null;
    if (!(turn instanceof HTMLElement) || !(row instanceof HTMLElement) || surfaces.length < 4) {
      return { turn:null, row:null, surfaces:[], hosts:[] };
    }

    const hosts = surfaces.map((surface) => {
      try { return adCoreV95DirectSlotHost(surface, row); } catch (_) { return surface; }
    });

    // The direct host must be unique per visible card. If a transient React
    // tree yields a shared wrapper, position the surface itself instead.
    const unique = new Set(hosts);
    const finalHosts = unique.size === 4 ? hosts : surfaces;
    return { turn, row, surfaces, hosts:finalHosts };
  }

  function adCoreV99Clear() {
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-ad-core-v99-slot-host="1"]').forEach((el) => {
      if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreV99SlotHost;
      [
        'position','left','top','right','bottom','inset','z-index','margin',
        'transform','translate','width','min-width','max-width','height',
        'min-height','max-height','box-sizing','flex','flex-grow','flex-shrink',
        'flex-basis','pointer-events','overflow','will-change'
      ].forEach((property) => el.style.removeProperty(property));
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-ad-core-v99-action-row="1"]').forEach((el) => {
      if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreV99ActionRow;
      [
        'position','left','top','right','bottom','inset','z-index','margin',
        'transform','translate','width','min-width','max-width','display',
        'justify-content','align-items','pointer-events','will-change'
      ].forEach((property) => el.style.removeProperty(property));
    });
    try { delete window.__AD_CORE_V99_GEOMETRY__; } catch (_) {}
  }

  function adCoreV99Median(values, fallback = 8) {
    const list = values.filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 0).sort((a, b) => a - b);
    if (!list.length) return fallback;
    const mid = Math.floor(list.length / 2);
    return list.length % 2 ? list[mid] : (list[mid - 1] + list[mid]) / 2;
  }

  function adCoreV99PositionActions(groupLeft, groupRight, groupBottom) {
    let actions = null;
    try { actions = adCoreV95FindActionRow(); } catch (_) {}
    if (!(actions instanceof HTMLElement) || !adCoreV99Visible(actions)) return;

    // First make the row measurable without old v76/v95 transforms.
    actions.dataset.adCoreV99ActionRow = '1';
    actions.style.setProperty('position', 'fixed', 'important');
    actions.style.setProperty('inset', 'auto', 'important');
    actions.style.setProperty('right', 'auto', 'important');
    actions.style.setProperty('bottom', 'auto', 'important');
    actions.style.setProperty('transform', 'none', 'important');
    actions.style.setProperty('translate', 'none', 'important');
    actions.style.setProperty('margin', '0', 'important');
    actions.style.setProperty('width', 'max-content', 'important');
    actions.style.setProperty('min-width', '0', 'important');
    actions.style.setProperty('max-width', 'none', 'important');
    actions.style.setProperty('display', 'flex', 'important');
    actions.style.setProperty('justify-content', 'flex-end', 'important');
    actions.style.setProperty('align-items', 'center', 'important');
    actions.style.setProperty('pointer-events', 'auto', 'important');
    actions.style.setProperty('z-index', '120', 'important');
    actions.style.setProperty('will-change', 'left, top', 'important');

    const own = actions.getBoundingClientRect();
    const gap = Math.max(12, Math.min(22, Math.round(window.innerHeight * 0.014)));
    const left = Math.round(groupRight - own.width);
    const top = Math.round(groupBottom + gap);
    actions.style.setProperty('left', `${left}px`, 'important');
    actions.style.setProperty('top', `${top}px`, 'important');
  }

  function adCoreV99Apply() {
    if (!adCoreV99IsActive()) {
      adCoreV99Clear();
      return;
    }

    const panels = (() => {
      try { return adCoreV91Panels().filter((el) => el instanceof HTMLElement); } catch (_) { return []; }
    })();
    const { turn, row, surfaces, hosts } = adCoreV99Info();
    if (panels.length !== 2 || !(turn instanceof HTMLElement) || !(row instanceof HTMLElement) || hosts.length !== 4 || surfaces.length !== 4) return;

    const leftPanel = panels[0].getBoundingClientRect();
    const rightPanel = panels[1].getBoundingClientRect();
    if (!(rightPanel.left > leftPanel.right)) return;

    const sourceRects = surfaces.map((surface) => surface.getBoundingClientRect());
    if (sourceRects.some((rect) => rect.width < 40 || rect.height < 40)) return;

    const hostRects = hosts.map((host, index) => {
      const rect = host.getBoundingClientRect();
      return (rect.width >= sourceRects[index].width * 0.55 && rect.height >= sourceRects[index].height * 0.55)
        ? rect : sourceRects[index];
    });

    const widths = sourceRects.map((rect) => Math.round(rect.width));
    const heights = sourceRects.map((rect) => Math.round(rect.height));
    const liveGaps = [];
    for (let i = 1; i < sourceRects.length; i += 1) liveGaps.push(sourceRects[i].left - sourceRects[i - 1].right);
    const slotGap = Math.round(adCoreV99Median(liveGaps, 6));
    const groupWidth = widths.reduce((sum, width) => sum + width, 0) + slotGap * 3;
    const laneLeft = leftPanel.right;
    const laneRight = rightPanel.left;
    const laneWidth = laneRight - laneLeft;
    if (!(laneWidth > groupWidth + 2)) return;

    // Equal physical clearance from the two player-card inner edges. The card
    // row starts on the same vertical line as the player cards, as requested.
    const groupLeft = Math.round(laneLeft + (laneWidth - groupWidth) / 2);
    const groupTop = Math.round(Math.max(leftPanel.top, rightPanel.top));
    const groupRight = groupLeft + groupWidth;
    const groupBottom = groupTop + Math.max(...heights);

    row.style.setProperty('overflow', 'visible', 'important');
    row.style.setProperty('position', 'relative', 'important');

    let cursor = groupLeft;
    hosts.forEach((host, index) => {
      const width = widths[index];
      const height = heights[index];
      host.dataset.adCoreV99SlotHost = '1';
      host.style.setProperty('position', 'fixed', 'important');
      host.style.setProperty('inset', 'auto', 'important');
      host.style.setProperty('left', `${Math.round(cursor)}px`, 'important');
      host.style.setProperty('top', `${groupTop}px`, 'important');
      host.style.setProperty('right', 'auto', 'important');
      host.style.setProperty('bottom', 'auto', 'important');
      host.style.setProperty('transform', 'none', 'important');
      host.style.setProperty('translate', 'none', 'important');
      host.style.setProperty('margin', '0', 'important');
      host.style.setProperty('box-sizing', 'border-box', 'important');
      host.style.setProperty('width', `${width}px`, 'important');
      host.style.setProperty('min-width', `${width}px`, 'important');
      host.style.setProperty('max-width', `${width}px`, 'important');
      host.style.setProperty('height', `${height}px`, 'important');
      host.style.setProperty('min-height', `${height}px`, 'important');
      host.style.setProperty('max-height', `${height}px`, 'important');
      host.style.setProperty('flex', `0 0 ${width}px`, 'important');
      host.style.setProperty('flex-grow', '0', 'important');
      host.style.setProperty('flex-shrink', '0', 'important');
      host.style.setProperty('flex-basis', `${width}px`, 'important');
      host.style.setProperty('z-index', '110', 'important');
      host.style.setProperty('pointer-events', 'auto', 'important');
      host.style.setProperty('will-change', 'left, top', 'important');
      cursor += width + slotGap;
    });

    adCoreV99PositionActions(groupLeft, groupRight, groupBottom);

    try {
      window.__AD_CORE_V99_GEOMETRY__ = {
        laneLeft, laneRight, laneWidth, groupLeft, groupRight, groupWidth,
        leftGap: Math.round(groupLeft - laneLeft),
        rightGap: Math.round(laneRight - groupRight),
        top: groupTop, bottom: groupBottom, slotGap, widths
      };
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  function adCoreV99Schedule() {
    if (adCoreV99Raf) return;
    adCoreV99Raf = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      adCoreV99Raf = 0;
      adCoreV99Apply();
      clearTimeout(adCoreV99Timer);
      adCoreV99Timer = setTimeout(adCoreV99Apply, 120);
    });
  }

  function adCoreV99EnsureObserver() {
    const turn = document.querySelector('#ad-ext-turn');
    if (turn === adCoreV99ObservedTurn && adCoreV99Observer) return;
    try { adCoreV99Observer?.disconnect(); } catch (_) {}
    adCoreV99Observer = null;
    adCoreV99ObservedTurn = turn instanceof HTMLElement ? turn : null;
    if (!(turn instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
    adCoreV99Observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
      adCoreV99Schedule();
      setTimeout(adCoreV99Schedule, 0);
      setTimeout(adCoreV99Schedule, 110);
    });
    adCoreV99Observer.observe(turn, { childList:true, subtree:true, characterData:true });
  }

  function adCoreV99EnsureStyle() {
    ensureHead(() => {
      let style = document.getElementById(AD_CORE_V99_STYLE_ID);
      if (!style) {
        style = document.createElement('style');
        style.id = AD_CORE_V99_STYLE_ID;
        document.head.appendChild(style);
      }
      style.textContent = `
html.ad-core-v67-native-page.ad-core-layout-on.ad-core-v67-two-player
#ad-ext-turn [data-ad-core-v99-slot-host="1"]{
  box-sizing:border-box !important;
  pointer-events:auto !important;
}
html.ad-core-v67-native-page.ad-core-layout-on.ad-core-v67-two-player
[data-ad-core-v99-action-row="1"]{
  pointer-events:auto !important;
}
`;
    });
  }

  // v95/v98 may schedule after a React update. Let them complete their own
  // card-size work first, then this direct geometry writer is authoritative
  // for the final on-screen X/Y placement.
  const adCoreV99V95PlaceActionsBase = adCoreV95PlaceActions;
  adCoreV95PlaceActions = function adCoreV99PlaceActionsAfterBase(surfaces) {
    adCoreV99V95PlaceActionsBase(surfaces);
    adCoreV99Schedule();
  };

  const adCoreV99V98ApplyBase = adCoreV98Apply;
  adCoreV98Apply = function adCoreV99ApplyAfterV98() {
    adCoreV99V98ApplyBase();
    adCoreV99EnsureObserver();
    adCoreV99Schedule();
  };

  window.addEventListener('resize', adCoreV99Schedule, { passive:true });
  window.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', adCoreV99Schedule, { passive:true });
  adCoreV99EnsureStyle();
  adCoreV99EnsureObserver();
  adCoreV99Schedule();


  /* ========================================================================
     v100 – LAYOUT ON / VISUAL SURFACE CENTRING (AUTHORITATIVE)
     ------------------------------------------------------------------------
     v97–v99 attempted to move a common parent/host. On the current React
     tree that host is not always the element which paints the four visible
     cards, so the measured correction could be valid while nothing moved.

     This layer deliberately targets the four *visible painted card surfaces*
     returned by the already working v94/v95 discovery routine. The same
     measured horizontal correction is applied to all four surfaces, therefore
     their fixed widths and their internal state renderers remain untouched.
     It also places Undo/Next again after the translated cards have a final
     bounding rectangle.
     ======================================================================== */

  const AD_CORE_V100_STYLE_ID = 'ad-core-v100-layout-on-surface-center-style';
  let adCoreV100Raf = 0;
  let adCoreV100Timer = 0;
  let adCoreV100Observer = null;
  let adCoreV100ObservedTurn = null;

  /* v99's host writer can silently return before it reaches the painted cards.
     Neutralise only that final writer, leaving v94/v95 card-size, content and
     action helpers available for v100. */
  try {
    adCoreV99Apply = function adCoreV100DisableV99HostWriter() {
      try { adCoreV99Clear(); } catch (_) {}
    };
  } catch (_) {}

  function adCoreV100IsActive() {
    try { return adCoreV95IsActive(); } catch (_) { return false; }
  }

  function adCoreV100Visible(el) {
    try { return adCoreV95Visible(el); } catch (_) {
      if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return false;
      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
      return r.width > 8 && r.height > 8 && cs.display !== 'none' && cs.visibility !== 'hidden';
    }
  }

  function adCoreV100Clear() {
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-ad-core-v100-surface="1"]').forEach((el) => {
      if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreV100Surface;
      delete el.dataset.adCoreV100Delta;
      ['transform','translate','will-change','z-index'].forEach((property) => el.style.removeProperty(property));
    });
    try { delete window.__AD_CORE_V100_GEOMETRY__; } catch (_) {}
  }

  function adCoreV100Panels() {
    let panels = [];
    try { panels = adCoreV91Panels(); } catch (_) {}
    return panels
      .filter(adCoreV100Visible)
      .sort((a, b) => a.getBoundingClientRect().left - b.getBoundingClientRect().left)
      .slice(0, 2);
  }

  function adCoreV100TurnInfo() {
    let info = null;
    try { info = adCoreV95TurnSurfaces(); } catch (_) {}
    const turn = info?.turn;
    const surfaces = Array.isArray(info?.surfaces)
      ? info.surfaces.slice(0, 4).filter(adCoreV100Visible)
      : [];
    return { turn, surfaces };
  }

  function adCoreV100PlaceActions(surfaces) {
    if (!Array.isArray(surfaces) || surfaces.length < 4) return;
    try {
      /* Current adCoreV95PlaceActions is the v99 wrapper. It calls the original
         action placer first, then the now neutral v99 scheduler. Bounding rects
         already include v100's transform, so Undo/Next follows the visual row. */
      adCoreV95PlaceActions(surfaces);
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  function adCoreV100Apply() {
    if (!adCoreV100IsActive()) {
      adCoreV100Clear();
      return;
    }

    const panels = adCoreV100Panels();
    const { turn, surfaces } = adCoreV100TurnInfo();
    if (panels.length !== 2 || !(turn instanceof HTMLElement) || surfaces.length !== 4) return;

    /* Always calculate from an unshifted visual row. */
    for (const surface of surfaces) {
      surface.style.removeProperty('transform');
      surface.style.removeProperty('translate');
    }
    void turn.offsetWidth;

    const leftPanel = panels[0].getBoundingClientRect();
    const rightPanel = panels[1].getBoundingClientRect();
    if (!(rightPanel.left > leftPanel.right)) return;

    const rects = surfaces.map((surface) => surface.getBoundingClientRect());
    if (rects.some((rect) => rect.width < 30 || rect.height < 30)) return;

    const groupLeft = Math.min(...rects.map((rect) => rect.left));
    const groupRight = Math.max(...rects.map((rect) => rect.right));
    const laneLeft = leftPanel.right;
    const laneRight = rightPanel.left;
    const leftGap = groupLeft - laneLeft;
    const rightGap = laneRight - groupRight;

    /* Shift the four visible card surfaces as one rigid unit. Positive means
       right; negative means left. Clamp only against pathological React frames. */
    const requested = (rightGap - leftGap) / 2;
    const delta = Math.round(Math.max(-160, Math.min(160, requested)) * 10) / 10;

    for (const surface of surfaces) {
      surface.dataset.adCoreV100Surface = '1';
      surface.dataset.adCoreV100Delta = String(delta);
      surface.style.setProperty('transform', `translate3d(${delta}px, 0, 0)`, 'important');
      surface.style.setProperty('translate', 'none', 'important');
      surface.style.setProperty('will-change', 'transform', 'important');
      surface.style.setProperty('z-index', '111', 'important');
    }

    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      if (!adCoreV100IsActive()) return;
      adCoreV100PlaceActions(surfaces);
    });

    try {
      window.__AD_CORE_V100_GEOMETRY__ = {
        laneLeft: Math.round(laneLeft),
        laneRight: Math.round(laneRight),
        groupLeft: Math.round(groupLeft),
        groupRight: Math.round(groupRight),
        leftGap: Math.round(leftGap * 10) / 10,
        rightGap: Math.round(rightGap * 10) / 10,
        delta,
        finalLeftGap: Math.round((leftGap + delta) * 10) / 10,
        finalRightGap: Math.round((rightGap - delta) * 10) / 10
      };
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  /* v100.3: V100's late surface writer was demonstrably ineffective on this
     DOM branch and can conflict with the CSS-owned host shift above. Keep the
     original v100 base intact, but make only this final painter correction a
     no-op. V91–V95 still own geometry, fixed slot widths and Undo/Next. */
  adCoreV100Apply = function adCoreV1003StaticHostShiftOnly() {
    if (!adCoreV100IsActive()) adCoreV100Clear();
  };

  function adCoreV100Schedule() {
    if (adCoreV100Raf) return;
    adCoreV100Raf = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      adCoreV100Raf = 0;
      adCoreV100Apply();
      clearTimeout(adCoreV100Timer);
      adCoreV100Timer = setTimeout(adCoreV100Apply, 140);
    });
  }

  function adCoreV100EnsureObserver() {
    const turn = document.querySelector('#ad-ext-turn');
    if (turn === adCoreV100ObservedTurn && adCoreV100Observer) return;
    try { adCoreV100Observer?.disconnect(); } catch (_) {}
    adCoreV100Observer = null;
    adCoreV100ObservedTurn = turn instanceof HTMLElement ? turn : null;
    if (!(turn instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
    adCoreV100Observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
      adCoreV100Schedule();
      setTimeout(adCoreV100Schedule, 0);
      setTimeout(adCoreV100Schedule, 130);
    });
    adCoreV100Observer.observe(turn, { childList:true, subtree:true, characterData:true });
  }

  function adCoreV100EnsureStyle() {
    ensureHead(() => {
      let style = document.getElementById(AD_CORE_V100_STYLE_ID);
      if (!style) {
        style = document.createElement('style');
        style.id = AD_CORE_V100_STYLE_ID;
        document.head.appendChild(style);
      }
      style.textContent = `
html.ad-core-v67-native-page.ad-core-layout-on.ad-core-v67-two-player
#ad-ext-turn [data-ad-core-v100-surface="1"]{
  pointer-events:auto !important;
  box-sizing:border-box !important;
}
`;
    });
  }

  window.addEventListener('resize', adCoreV100Schedule, { passive:true });
  window.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', adCoreV100Schedule, { passive:true });
  adCoreV100EnsureStyle();
  adCoreV100EnsureObserver();
  adCoreV100Schedule();


  /* ========================================================================
     v100.4 – UNDO / NEXT REFERENCE PLACEMENT ONLY
     ------------------------------------------------------------------------
     The accepted v100.3 static -45px turn-host shift is intentionally left
     untouched. This layer adjusts only the existing native Undo/Next action
     row after v95 has found it:
       - its RIGHT edge equals the visible four-card group's RIGHT edge;
       - it sits 16px below the lower edge of that group.

     No player-card, board, history, surface, or slot geometry is changed.
     No React node is moved, cloned, or removed.
     ======================================================================== */
  const AD_CORE_V1004_ACTION_GAP = 16;
  let adCoreV1004ActionRaf = 0;

  function adCoreV1004PlaceActionsReference() {
    if (!adCoreV95IsActive()) return;

    let info = null;
    try { info = adCoreV95TurnSurfaces(); } catch (_) {}
    const surfaces = Array.isArray(info?.surfaces)
      ? info.surfaces.slice(0, 4).filter(adCoreV95Visible)
      : [];
    if (surfaces.length < 4) return;

    let actionRow = null;
    try { actionRow = adCoreV95FindActionRow(); } catch (_) {}
    if (!(actionRow instanceof HTMLElement)) return;

    const rects = surfaces
      .map((el) => el.getBoundingClientRect())
      .filter((r) => r.width > 10 && r.height > 10);
    if (rects.length < 4) return;

    const right = Math.max(...rects.map((r) => r.right));
    const bottom = Math.max(...rects.map((r) => r.bottom));
    const own = actionRow.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (!(own.width > 20 && own.height > 12)) return;

    const targetLeft = Math.round(right - own.width);
    const targetTop = Math.round(bottom + AD_CORE_V1004_ACTION_GAP);

    actionRow.dataset.adCoreV1004ActionReference = '1';
    actionRow.style.setProperty('position', 'fixed', 'important');
    actionRow.style.setProperty('transform', 'none', 'important');
    actionRow.style.setProperty('translate', 'none', 'important');
    actionRow.style.setProperty('margin', '0', 'important');
    actionRow.style.setProperty('right', 'auto', 'important');
    actionRow.style.setProperty('bottom', 'auto', 'important');
    actionRow.style.setProperty('left', `${targetLeft}px`, 'important');
    actionRow.style.setProperty('top', `${targetTop}px`, 'important');
    actionRow.style.setProperty('z-index', '90', 'important');

    try {
      window.__AD_CORE_V1004_ACTIONS__ = {
        groupRight: Math.round(right),
        groupBottom: Math.round(bottom),
        actionWidth: Math.round(own.width),
        actionHeight: Math.round(own.height),
        left: targetLeft,
        top: targetTop,
        gap: AD_CORE_V1004_ACTION_GAP
      };
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  function adCoreV1004ScheduleActionsReference() {
    if (adCoreV1004ActionRaf) return;
    adCoreV1004ActionRaf = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      adCoreV1004ActionRaf = 0;
      requestAnimationFrame(adCoreV1004PlaceActionsReference);
    });
  }

  /* Keep the established v95 event flow; add only a final reference-aligned
     action placement after its own native-row preparation is complete. */
  const adCoreV1004V95PlaceActionsBase = adCoreV95PlaceActions;
  adCoreV95PlaceActions = function adCoreV1004PlaceActionsAfterBase(surfaces) {
    adCoreV1004V95PlaceActionsBase(surfaces);
    adCoreV1004ScheduleActionsReference();
  };

  window.addEventListener('resize', adCoreV1004ScheduleActionsReference, { passive:true });
  window.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', adCoreV1004ScheduleActionsReference, { passive:true });
  adCoreV1004ScheduleActionsReference();
  setTimeout(adCoreV1004ScheduleActionsReference, 120);


  start();
})();
